### [1] [Migration Flows: Population Scaling and Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00192)
*Cook Hyun Kim,Sangjoon Park,Seoyoung Jin,Jaiyong Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究提出以县（county）间迁移为微观机制，解释城际平均流量的人口标度律（population scaling）与城际净流分布的重尾特征（heavy-tailed net-flow distribution），并给出一个生成相关性分布的随机模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城际迁移的平均流量随人口如何标度、以及净流分布为何呈重尾，是城市动力学与人口迁移研究中的核心经验规律，需要统一机制性解释。论文试图从更细粒度的县际迁移层面（而非直接在城市层面）寻找成因。

Method: 以密度依赖（density-dependent）的迁出与迁入活动刻画平均流量随出发地与目的地城市人口的标度关系；假设县对净流为高斯型（Gaussian），其标准差与平均方向性流量成正比；在县对净流相关性固定时其和为高斯，再对相关性分布做混合平均（mixture averaging）。此外构建随机模型生成该混合所需的相关性（方差）分布。

Result: 该混合平均机制可产生一个带高斯截断（Gaussian cutoff）的中间幂律（intermediate power law），从而同时解释平均城际流量的人口标度与城际净流分布的重尾性；随机模型可生成混合所需的相关性分布。

Conclusion: 城市间的迁移行为可还原为跨城市县际迁移的加总，密度依赖的迁出/迁入活动与县对净流的相关性混合共同决定了可观测的标度律与重尾统计特征。

Abstract: We show how migration between counties in different cities can explain both the population scaling of mean intercity flows and heavy-tailed intercity net-flow distributions. Density-dependent departure and arrival activities determine how mean flows scale with the populations of the origin and destination cities. We assume Gaussian county-pair net flows with standard deviations proportional to their mean directional flows. At a fixed correlation between county-pair net flows, their sum is Gaussian. Averaging this conditional Gaussian density over the correlation distribution can produce an intermediate power law with a Gaussian cutoff. We also present a stochastic model that generates the variance distribution used in this mixture.

</details>


### [2] [Active-Matter Battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00428)
*Yun-Qian Lin,Rui-Han Liu,Yu-Han Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从活性物质（active matter）中提取能量的极简电池模型，并解析推导其充放电特性；在计入维持活性的能量成本后，充电功率—效率权衡呈环状，效率可在有限时间内达到最大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用活性物质为功能器件供能日益受到关注，但缺乏可用于储存所提取能量的简洁理论模型与定量表征。该研究旨在建立最小化电池模型以刻画此类能量储存过程。

Method: 构建描述从活性物质提取并储存能量的极简电池模型，并在解析层面推导其充电与放电特性；分析中纳入维持活性所需的能量成本。

Result: 当计入维持活性的能量成本时，充电功率—效率权衡关系表现为环状（loop-like），且效率存在有限时间最大值（finite-time maximum）。

Conclusion: 该结果与被动能量器件（passive energy devices）形成鲜明对比，表明活性物质驱动的能量储存具有独特的功率—效率特性。

Abstract: Harnessing active matter to power functional devices has attracted growing interest. We propose a minimal battery model for storing energy extracted from active matter and derive its charging and discharging characteristics analytically. When the energetic cost of sustaining activity is included, the charging power-efficiency trade-off becomes loop-like and the efficiency attains a finite-time maximum, in sharp contrast to passive energy devices.

</details>


### [3] [Generative Modeling of Stochastic Dynamics for Long-Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00546)
*Yang-yang Tan,Jinyang Li,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作证明，无需已知运动方程，仅凭固定短时间间隔的构型对（configuration pairs），即可通过生成式扩散模型（generative diffusion model）学习有限时间转移核（transition kernel），并迭代外推至远超训练间隔的非平衡动力学（non-equilibrium dynamics）长时间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡动力学（non-equilibrium dynamics）的精确随机方程通常难以获得，因此需要一种不依赖运动方程、仅从短时观测推断长时演化的通用方法。

Method: 从固定短时间滞后（short time lag）的构型对中，用生成式扩散模型学习有限时间转移核，并通过迭代该核将动力学传播至远超训练滞后的时间尺度；在二维 Model B（守恒序参量的扩散动力学）以及周期性光势阱中受驱胶体（driven colloids）两类体系上验证。

Result: 二维 Model B 中，学习到的核再现了动态临界标度（dynamic critical scaling）与自相似的 $t^{1/3}$ 粗化（coarsening）；与直接模拟的一致性在训练规模两倍的晶格及未出现在训练集中的初始系综上依然保持。受驱胶体实验中，十分钟轨迹即可将随后二十分钟的粒子流（particle current）与平均通过时间（mean passage time）预测至实验不确定度内。

Conclusion: 短时观测已包含预测长时涌现非平衡动力学所需的信息，表明该方法可作为不依赖运动方程的长时动力学预测工具。

Abstract: Exact stochastic equations for non-equilibrium dynamics are rarely accessible. We show that the long-time evolution of stochastic dynamics can be predicted from configuration pairs at a fixed short time lag, without knowledge of the equation of motion. Generative diffusion models learn the finite-time transition kernel from these pairs, and iterating it propagates the dynamics far beyond the training lag. For two-dimensional Model B, the diffusive dynamics of a conserved order parameter, the learned kernels reproduce dynamic critical scaling and self-similar $t^{1/3}$ coarsening. Agreement with direct simulations persists on lattices twice the largest training size and for initial ensembles absent from training. For driven colloids in a periodic optical potential, ten minutes of measured trajectories suffice to predict the particle current and mean passage time over the next twenty minutes within experimental uncertainty. Short-time observations thus contain the information needed to predict emergent non-equilibrium dynamics at much longer times.

</details>


### [4] [The Ericson Transition in Time-Reversal Invariant Systems: Symplectically Invariant Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00624)
*Simon Köhnes,Thomas Guhr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在具有奇数自旋且时间反演不变（time-reversal invariant）的系统中，于 Ericson 区域（Ericson regime）内研究非对角（off-diagonal）散射矩阵元与截面的分布；在普适 Heidelberg 方法（Heidelberg approach）框架下，证明高斯（Gaussian）与指数（exponential）行为是相应分布渐近展开（asymptotic expansion）的首阶项，并推导次阶项并与数值模拟（numerical simulations）比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标为刻画 Ericson 区域内非对角散射矩阵元与截面的统计分布，特别是针对具有奇数自旋且时间反演不变的系统。该问题属于量子混沌（quantum chaos）散射统计的普适性研究范畴。

Method: 采用普适 Heidelberg 方法（Heidelberg approach）作为理论框架，对所研究分布的渐近展开进行证明，并推导其次阶项；同时借助数值模拟（numerical simulations）进行对照。

Result: 证明高斯（Gaussian）与指数（exponential）行为构成相应分布渐近展开的首阶项；此外推导出次阶项（subleading-order term），并将其与数值模拟（numerical simulations）结果进行比较。

Conclusion: 摘要未给出明确结论性陈述，仅指出首阶高斯/指数行为得到证明，且次阶项被推导并与数值模拟对照，表明理论展开与数值结果具有可比性。

Abstract: We investigate the distributions of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross-sections in the Ericson regime for time-reversal invariant systems with odd spin. Within the universal Heidelberg approach we prove that Gaussian and exponential behavior is found as the leading-order term of an asymptotic expansion of the aforementioned distributions. Furthermore, the subleading-order term is derived and compared with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [5] [CTMRG as a tensor-network message-passing algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00631)
*Illia Lukin,Andrii Sotnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作将角转移矩阵方程（corner transfer matrix equations）与角转移矩阵重整化群（corner transfer matrix renormalization group, CTMRG）推广为一般平面图（planar graph）上的局域方程组，其仅依赖局部子图并可一致拼接以描述整图；求解所得消息张量（message tensors）与角矩阵（corner matrices）可用于构造任意连通子图的矩阵乘积态环境（MPS environment）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统角转移矩阵重整化群主要针对规则晶格定义，缺乏对一般平面图的普适表述；本文旨在给出不依赖全局规则结构的局域化方程形式。同时，信念传播（belief propagation）等消息传递方法在精度上存在系统性不足，需要更精确的替代方案。

Method: 构建基于局部子图的角转移矩阵方程，并将其组织为可在一般平面图上一致拼接的局域方程组，由此得到角转移矩阵重整化群形式；求解后获得消息张量与角矩阵，并据此为任意连通子图构造矩阵乘积态环境（MPS environment）。

Result: 在铁磁伊辛模型（ferromagnetic Ising model）上进行了验证，测试范围涵盖多种无限晶格（infinite lattices，包括双曲晶格 hyperbolic ones）以及有限图（finite graphs），结果均显示相对于信念传播（belief propagation）存在系统性改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的局域角转移矩阵（重整化群）方程可统一处理一般平面图，并在无限与有限图上均优于信念传播，表明其作为通用平面图张量网络求解框架具有有效性。

Abstract: We formulate the corner transfer matrix equations, and the corner transfer matrix renormalization group built on them, as a set of local equations on general planar graphs. These equations depend only on the local subgraphs of the planar graph and are glued together consistently to describe the whole graph. Solving them yields message tensors and corner matrices, which can be used to construct an MPS environment for any connected subgraph. We test the proposed equations on the ferromagnetic Ising model, on a variety of infinite lattices (including hyperbolic ones) and on finite graphs, and find systematic improvement over belief propagation.

</details>


### [6] [Thermal entanglement transitions from strong $\textrm{SU}(2)$ symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00826)
*Siqi Mo,Ehud Altman,Samuel J. Garratt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究具有强 SU(2) 对称性的 Gibbs 态（Gibbs states）中的混合态纠缠（mixed-state entanglement），在铁磁相互作用的局域自旋系统中证明有限温度有序相变（finite-temperature ordering transitions）与纠缠相变（entanglement transitions）相关联，并给出铁磁相中纠缠度量随系统尺寸增长更快的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强对称 Lindblad 主方程（Lindbladians）稳态的纠缠性质与有限温度有序相变之间是否存在联系尚不明确；同时，铁磁相中混合态纠缠度量随系统尺寸的标度行为有待刻画。

Method: 首先建立全局 SU(2) 单态（singlet）下混合态纠缠度量与自旋关联（spin correlations）之间的关系；随后提出 SU(2) 单态热态的半导体/半经典理论（semiclassical theory），证明在大 S 极限下全局单态约束简化为对总磁化强度的 Gaussian 压制；由此构造描述单态扇区自旋关联的场论（field theory），并结合三维格点 Monte Carlo 模拟与一维自旋-1/2 系统精确数值方法进行验证。

Result: 铁磁相中可蒸馏纠缠（distillable entanglement）与对数纠缠负度（logarithmic entanglement negativity）随系统尺寸的增长显著快于顺磁相中已知的、随自旋数平方根的对数增长；并在顺磁相中确认了混合态纠缠随关联长度（correlation length）与系统尺寸的标度预测。

Conclusion: 有限温度有序相变伴随纠缠相变，因此强对称 Lindblad 主方程的稳态可展现纠缠相变；所建立的半经典场论框架与数值结果一致，为表征强对称混合态纠缠提供了可操作的理论工具。

Abstract: We study mixed-state entanglement in Gibbs states with strong $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ symmetry, focusing on locally interacting spin systems with ferromagnetic interactions. Our main result is to show that finite-temperature ordering transitions are associated with entanglement transitions, and therefore the steady state of strongly symmetric Lindbladians can exhibit entanglement transitions. While in the paramagnetic phase it is known that the distillable entanglement and logarithmic entanglement negativity between two halves of a large system grow logarithmically with the square root of the number of spins, we show that in ferromagnetic phases these quantities grow parametrically faster with system size. To arrive at this result we first establish relations between these mixed-state entanglement measures and spin correlations in states that are singlets under global $\rm{SU}(2)$ symmetry transformations. We then introduce a semiclassical theory for $\rm{SU}(2)$ singlet thermal states. While the global singlet constraint generally enters this theory as a complicated function of the full semiclassical spin configuration, we show that in large $S$ limit it simplifies drastically to a Gaussian suppression of total magnetization in disordered phases as well as in ordered phases in the vicinity of continuous thermal phase transitions. This leads us to a field theory describing spin correlations in the singlet sector. Using this we determine the behavior of various probes of mixed-state entanglement at low and at high temperatures, supporting our analytical results using numerical Monte Carlo simulations of a three-dimensional lattice realization of our semiclassical theory. We also use exact numerics in one-dimensional spin-$1/2$ systems to confirm our predictions for the scaling of mixed-state entanglement with correlation length and system size in the paramagnetic phase.

</details>


### [7] [Work fluctuation speed limit in boundary conformal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01248)
*Shihao Xia,Ahsan Nazir,Harry J. D. Miller*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究边界共形场论（boundary conformal field theory, BCFT）描述量子临界系统时有限时间驱动的基本极限，提出了一个由随机功涨落（stochastic work fluctuations）决定、可饱和的精确速度极限，并给出饱和驱动协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究量子临界系统在有限时间内被外部驱动时，速度与功精度之间存在何种基本限制，以及随机功涨落能否作为快速控制的资源。

Method: 方法上针对有限温度下弱驱动的边界共形场论进行分析，推导基于涨落的精确可饱和速度极限，并将结果完全用普适标度维数（universal scaling dimension）表示，从而在Kibble–Zurek区与绝热区之间进行插值。

Result: 结果表明，当标度维数较小时，增强的时间关联导致驱动显著偏离线性协议，并带来更大的优化优势；其界限与饱和协议均可由普适标度维数完全刻画。

Conclusion: 结论是建立了边界临界控制中普适的功精度—时间权衡（work precision–time tradeoff），可应用于量子杂质（quantum impurity）、分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall）以及超导电路（superconducting-circuit）等平台。

Abstract: We explore the fundamental limits on finite-time driving in quantum critical systems described by boundary conformal field theory. We show that stochastic work fluctuations arising from external driving are a resource for speedy control, and derive an exact, saturable fluctuation-based speed limit in weakly driven boundary conformal field theories at finite temperature. The bound and saturating protocol can be expressed entirely in terms of the universal scaling dimension, and the result interpolates between the Kibble--Zurek regime,where temporal correlations are strongly nonlocal, and an adiabatic regime where linear driving becomes optimal. For small scaling dimension, the enhanced temporal correlations produce pronounced departures from linear protocols and a larger optimization advantage. These results establish a universal work precision--time tradeoff for boundary-critical control, applicable to quantum impurity, fractional quantum Hall, and superconducting-circuit platforms.

</details>


### [8] [On thermalization in random free fermions: Statistical origins and energy-dependent ETH structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01414)
*Xiao-Kan Guo,Zhiqiang Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过随机矩阵分析研究随机自由费米子模型（random free fermion model）的热化，推导出热化函数及其衰减规律、涨落标度，并考察粒子数守恒、本征态统计与相关函数涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解随机自由费米子模型中的热化行为，尤其是本征态热化假设（eigenstate thermalization hypothesis）框架下弱热化与自平均（self-averaging）的可解析实现。

Method: 方法为随机矩阵分析（random-matrix analysis）：计算单粒子可观测量的迹的系综平均与涨落，并纳入粒子数守恒，研究能量壳本征态统计与分解定理，且引入受控的本征矢—本征值关联形变。

Result: 得到热化函数 g²(t/τ_λ) 与 τ_λ=ħ/(2η√N)，证明其 |t|^(-3) 渐近衰减；涨落方差在热力学极限下以 O(1/N) 消失；本征态统计满足分解定理；在关联形变下对角斜率与谱函数出现能量依赖；相关函数涨落与全混沌系统存在定量差异。

Conclusion: 该模型构成弱本征态热化假设型自平均的解析可解实现，并提供了可将本征基混沌（eigenbasis chaos）与谱混沌（spectral chaos）分离的可控框架。

Abstract: We study the thermalization in the random free fermion model by a detailed random-matrix analysis. By computing the ensemble average and fluctuations of $\operatorname{Tr}(Aρ(t))$ for a single-particle observable $A$, we derive the thermalization function $g^2(t/τ_λ)$ with $τ_λ= \hbar/(2η\sqrt{N})$, prove its $|t|^{-3}$ asymptotic decay, and show that the variance of the fluctuations vanishes as $O(1/N)$ in the thermodynamic limit.Particle number conservation is also incorporated into the model, by which we further study the energy-shell eigenstate statistics and prove a factorization theorem of the eigenstate statistics. We further show that under a controlled eigenvector-eigenvalue correlation deformation, the diagonal energy-resolved slope and the off-diagonal eigenstate thermalization hypothesis spectral function acquire an energy dependence.Finally, we compute the fluctuations of correlation functions and compare the fluctuation scales with those of a fully chaotic system, revealing quantitative differences rooted in the Gaussian nature of the eigenstates.Our work establishes the random free fermion model as an analytically solvable realization of weak eigenstate thermalization hypothesis type self-averaging, and as a controllable setting in which eigenbasis chaos can be separated from spectral chaos.

</details>


### [9] [Learning ab initio phase-field models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01432)
*Mengyi Chen,Peichen Zhong,Zihan Zhang,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出由第一性原理（ab initio）学习相场（phase-field）模型的框架：以 Mori-Zwanzig 投影从分子动力学导出介观方程，并用神经网络参数化非局域自由能与迁移率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观组织演化模拟需同时满足量子力学精度与介观尺度覆盖，而现有方法无法兼顾；经典相场模型虽具尺度可达性，但受现象学自由能与迁移率的精度限制。

Method: 介观方程并非假设给出，而是通过 Mori-Zwanzig 投影将分子动力学投影到组分密度场（species-density fields）并在显式假设下导出；方程中未指定的非局域自由能与迁移率由神经网络参数化，并利用机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials）生成的高精度短分子动力学轨迹进行学习。

Result: 在铁-硼熔体与行星条件下的氢-氦混合物上验证：FeB$_4$ 组分熔体在常压下呈调幅不稳定（spinodally unstable），10 GPa 下被稳定，为 FeB$_4$ 仅高压合成提供热力学解释；氢-氦模型预测不混溶边界，并在对应 220 万原子的氦雨模拟中捕捉液滴成核与生长，尺度远超同精度原子模拟。

Conclusion: 跨组分与条件训练后，此类模型有望成为第一性原理分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics）的介观对应物。

Abstract: Simulating microstructure evolution requires quantum-mechanical accuracy and mesoscopic reach in length and time scales, a combination that no current method achieves. Classical phase-field models provide this reach, but their accuracy is limited by phenomenological free energies and mobilities. Here we develop a framework for learning ab initio phase-field models, where the mesoscopic equation is not postulated but derived from a Mori-Zwanzig projection of molecular dynamics onto species-density fields under explicit assumptions. The nonlocal free energy and mobility left unspecified by this equation are parametrized by neural networks and learned from short molecular dynamics trajectories generated with machine-learning interatomic potentials of ab initio accuracy. We demonstrate the framework on an iron-boron melt and on hydrogen-helium mixtures under planetary conditions. For iron-boron, the model shows that the melt at the FeB$_4$ composition is spinodally unstable at ambient pressure but stabilized at 10 GPa, offering a thermodynamic rationale for why FeB$_4$ has been synthesized only under high pressure. For hydrogen-helium, the model predicts the immiscibility boundary and captures droplet nucleation and growth in helium-rain simulations of a column corresponding to 2.2 million atoms, far beyond the scale of atomistic modeling at comparable accuracy. Trained across compositions and conditions, such models could provide a mesoscopic counterpart to ab initio molecular dynamics.

</details>


### [10] [Gate Control Improves Routing Efficiency for Periodic Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01534)
*Hyunwook Song,Hoyun Choi,B. Kahng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种带门控（gate control）的双队列（two-queue）方案，仅利用局部队列信息调节车辆进入并整合路由协议，可将突发的、不连续的拥堵相变（jamming transition）转变为渐进的、连续的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路交通与互联网数据包传输是复杂系统中集体动力学行为（collective dynamical behavior）的典型案例。尽管复杂网络中的交通流优化已有大量研究，但多数现有工作集中于单队列模型（single-queue models）。

Method: 引入一种简单而有效的双队列方案并加入门控（gate control），将其与路由协议（routing protocol）结合：车辆到达匝道闸口（on-ramp gate）后先进入等待队列（waiting queue），待主流队列（main flowing queue）排空后方可进入。该策略基于仅使用局部队列信息（local queue information）的简单规则。

Result: 在高速入流随周期变化且波动较大（与真实交通常见情形相符）时，该双队列策略尤为有利；同时改变了系统的失效特征（failure characteristics），以渐进的、连续的拥堵相变取代突然的、不连续的拥堵相变。

Conclusion: 该方法基于简单规则、仅需局部队列信息，易于实现且适用性广；除本文特定场景外，还可应用于不同条件下的其他交通网络，或推广至其他排队系统（queuing systems），如互联网数据包路由（data packet routing）。

Abstract: Traffic movement on highways and data packet transmission across the Internet serve as prototypical examples of collective dynamical behavior in complex systems. Although enhancing traffic flow in complex networks has been extensively investigated, most existing studies focus on single-queue models. In this paper, we introduce a simple yet effective two-queue scheme with gate control that regulates vehicle entry and integrates it with the routing protocol. When vehicles arrive at a on-ramp gate, they first join a waiting queue and are permitted to enter the main flowing queue once it has emptied. This two-queue strategy is especially advantageous when highway inflow varies periodically with large fluctuations, as is commonly observed in real traffic. Moreover, it modifies the system's failure characteristics, replacing sudden, discontinuous jamming transitions with gradual, continuous ones. The approach is based on a simple rule using only local queue information, making it easy to implement and widely applicable. Beyond the specific setup studied here, it can be applied to other transportation networks under different conditions or extended to other queuing systems, such as Internet data packet routing.

</details>


### [11] [Optimal sampling strategies in event-chain Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01659)
*James Gulliford,Gareth O. Roberts,Michael F. Faulkner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，将事件链蒙特卡洛（Event-chain Monte Carlo, ECMC）中的高效应采样策略推广至环面上所有平移对称的成对相互作用模型，从而有望在计算效率上超越分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）与哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo, HMC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MD与HMC虽通过确定性轨迹优于随机游走Metropolis算法，但仍受牛顿动力学约束；ECMC可摆脱该约束，此前的集体粒子动力学在一维环面N体成对模型上实现了O(N^{3/4})效率提升，但在一般吸引/排斥相互作用及低/高平均粒子密度下前置因子过小而不实用。本文旨在解决该局限并推广该高效策略。

Method: 构建一个通用框架（universal framework），将此前的高效采样策略推广到环面（torus）上所有平移对称（translationally symmetric）的成对（pairwise）模型；并通过数值方法阐释其集体粒子动力学（collective particle dynamics）。

Result: 该框架在通用性上覆盖了环面上所有平移对称成对模型，克服了先前策略在一般吸引/排斥相互作用和低/高平均粒子密度下前置因子不实用的限制，具备超越MD与HMC成为最新技术水平的潜力；同时对集体粒子动力学给出了数值阐释。

Conclusion: 该通用框架为ECMC高效采样提供了更广适用性，并可能对物理科学与计算统计学产生广泛影响，具有成为计算采样最新技术水平的潜力。

Abstract: Event-chain Monte Carlo (ECMC) has revolutionised computational sampling over recent years, providing a powerful alternative to the molecular-dynamics (MD) and Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) algorithms. Each method outperforms the ubiquitous random-walk Metropolis algorithm by advancing particles along deterministic trajectories, but ECMC achieves this without being constrained by Newtonian dynamics. Recent advances exploited this dynamical freedom to induce a collective particle dynamics that relax local density variations on fast timescales. In a foundational model of $N$ pairwise-interacting particles on the 1D torus, this led to an $O(N^{3/4})$ improvement on the industry-leading computational efficiency of MD and HMC - but with an impractically small prefactor at low/high mean particle density for general attractive/repulsive interactions. Here we present a universal framework that generalises this high-efficiency sampling strategy to all translationally symmetric pairwise models on the torus - creating the potential to surpass MD and HMC as the state of the art. We also numerically elucidate the collective particle dynamics and discuss broad impact across the physical sciences and computational statistics.

</details>


### [12] [Scalar field theory for (chiral) active Brownian particles: bottom-up derivation revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01672)
*Yuta Kuroda,Thomas Speck*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文回访二维相互作用活性布朗粒子（active Brownian particle, ABP）与包含四阶空间梯度项标量场论之间的联系，通过压力展开与重整化群（renormalization group, RG）方法从微观粒子模型系统推导标量活性场论，并推广至手性ABP模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量活性流体的大尺度行为常用标量场论描述，但此类标量活性场论多以自顶向下（top-down）方式构造，其与粒子基模型的联系尚未充分理解。本文旨在推进标量活性场论的微观推导。

Method: 采用基于压力展开（pressure expansion）以及奇异摄动理论（singular perturbation theory）语境下的重整化群（RG）方法。RG方法被用于系统消除快变量并识别慢不变流形（slow invariant manifold）上的动力学，标量场论可作为RG流方程获得。

Result: 在包含至空间梯度四阶（fourth order in spatial gradients）所有可能项的标量场论框架下，建立了其与通过两体势（two-body potential）相互作用的ABP之间的联系。将该方法应用于手性ABP（chiral ABP, cABP）模型（其中恒定力矩使粒子旋转偏向左或右）后，所得标量场论在ABP对应场论之外额外包含两个"odd"项。

Conclusion: RG方法为从粒子基模型微观推导标量活性场论提供了系统途径，并可用于手性活性系统；该方法亦可能有助于推导其他类型活性物质系统（active matter systems）的流体力学描述。

Abstract: The active Brownian particle (ABP) model is one of the most widely used particle-based models in studies of scalar active fluids. A collection of interacting ABPs is known to exhibit intriguing phenomena that are absent in equilibrium counterparts. A prominent example is spontaneous phase separation, known as motility-induced phase separation (MIPS), which occurs even in the absence of explicit attractive interactions. In parallel, the large-scale behavior of scalar active fluids is often modeled using continuum descriptions, namely, scalar field theories. Such scalar active field theories are usually formulated in a top-down manner, and establishing their connections to particle-based models remains challenging, with these connections not yet fully understood. To advance the microscopic derivation of scalar active field theories, we revisit the connection between ABPs interacting via a two-body potential in two dimensions and scalar field theories that include all possible terms up to fourth order in spatial gradients. Our approach is based on a pressure expansion and the renormalization group (RG) method in the context of singular perturbation theory. The RG method provides a systematic way to eliminate fast variables and identify the dynamics on the slow invariant manifold. Within this framework, scalar field theories can be obtained as RG flow equations. We also apply this method to a chiral variant of the ABP model, the chiral ABP (cABP) model, in which a constant torque biases particle rotation to the left or right. We show that the scalar field theory corresponding to the cABP model contains two ``odd'' terms in addition to those present in the scalar field theory for ABPs. The method developed here may also be useful for deriving hydrodynamic descriptions of other types of active matter systems.

</details>

### [13] [Tunable flat bands and their signatures in electronic specific heat of an Aharonov-Bohm triangular quantum network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00273)
*Sanchita Nandi,Santanu K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究由三角环（triangular plaquette）组成的紧束缚（tight-binding, TB）量子网络的热响应，揭示平带（flat band）形成与电子比热（electronic specific heat, ESH）之间的直接联系，并提出以比热识别平带的替代途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环状结构的量子网络在电子输运方面已被广泛研究，但其热响应相对缺乏探索。作者因此希望考察非均匀配位、跃迁不对称与量子干涉共同作用下的热学行为，尤其是平带在热响应中的表现。

Method: 构建由有限数目三角plaquette组成的紧束缚网络，相邻plaquette以单键相连，每个三角环穿过Aharonov-Bohm（AB）磁通φ。解析推导使某条能带与电子动量无关的TB参数条件，并计算电子比热以考察能谱特征。

Result: 非均匀格点配位、跃迁不对称与量子干涉的相互作用同时产生色散能带（dispersive band）与完全平带。推导出使某一能支独立于电子动量的解析条件；AB磁通φ可用来直接调节平带位置，且该位置在电子比热中表现为可辨识的显著特征。

Conclusion: 平带的形成与电子比热之间存在直接联系，电子比热可作为识别动量无关能级的替代手段，结论与解析条件及相应能量色散一致；借助AB磁通可选择性地调控比热。该方法为识别平带提供了简便的互补途径，并可推广至其他支持平带的简单与复杂量子网络。

Abstract: Quantum networks composed of loop-like structures provide a rich platform for exploring electronic transport phenomena and have been widely studied in various contexts. However, their thermal response remains relatively unexplored, motivating us to investigate it in the present work. We consider a tight-binding (TB) quantum network composed of a finite number of triangular plaquettes, where neighboring plaquettes are connected through single bonds and each triangular loop is threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm (AB) flux $φ$. The interplay of nonuniform site coordination, hopping asymmetry, and quantum interference gives rise to both dispersive and completely flat energy levels. We derive an analytical condition involving the TB parameters under which one of the energy branches becomes independent of the electronic momentum. We further show that the AB flux $φ$ provides a direct means of tuning the position of the flat band. The characteristic features of the energy spectrum associated with this quantum geometry are reflected in the thermal response, which we investigate through the electronic specific heat (ESH). In particular, we demonstrate that the position of the flat band manifests itself as a distinct feature in the ESH, establishing a direct connection between the flat-band formation and the thermal response of the system. Thus, the ESH provides an alternative route for identifying the presence of a flat band, consistent with the analytical condition and the corresponding energy dispersion. Moreover, the AB flux allows the ESH to be selectively regulated through the tuning of the flat-band position. The proposed approach provides a simple complementary means of identifying momentum-independent energy levels and may be extended to other simple and complex quantum networks supporting flat bands.

</details>


### [14] [Interior contacts in a narrow quantum Hall bar: a two-dimensional self-consistent screening calculation of the current distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00326)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文对六端 GaAs/AlGaAs 霍尔条（Hall bar）进行全二维自洽计算，在屏蔽理论（screening theory）框架下求解 Thomas-Fermi-Poisson 方程，并考察内部方形接触 A、B 在 nu=2 平台内随磁场降低所经历的“连通—浮空—隔离”三种区间及相应电阻行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于复现并解释 Kendirlik 等（Nat. Commun. 8, 14082 (2017)）的内部接触（interior-contact）实验，需要理解整数量子霍尔效应（integer quantized Hall effect）中内部探针与电流路径、体内（bulk）和边缘态（edge strips）之间的耦合机制及其电阻特征。

Method: 采用屏蔽理论下的全器件平面计算：数值求解 Thomas-Fermi-Poisson 方程，包含探针臂（probe arms）与内部接触周围耗尽区（depletion regions），并由含填充因子（filling factor）依赖电导张量的局域欧姆定律（local Ohm's law）求电流，分别针对源漏激励与内部接触激励。

Result: 在 nu=2 平台内，随磁场降低，内部接触经历三种区间：与可压缩体内（compressible bulk）电流路径电学连通、在完全不可压缩体内的霍尔电位（Hall potential）中浮空（floating）、以及被环绕接触的闭合不可压缩环（incompressible rings）与体内及边缘条隔离。浮空与隔离区间中内部接触间两端电阻上升数个量级；隔离区间位于霍尔平台内并在其低场端前结束，高场端由不可压缩体消失突兀界定，低场端随温度与朗道能级展宽（Landau-level broadening）收缩；内部接触与周边探针间电阻约为量子化霍尔电阻（quantized Hall resistance）的一半。

Conclusion: 不可压缩区的拓扑结构与隔离区间边界对局域电导正则化（regularization）、网格和中等无序（moderate disorder）不敏感，而隔离电阻的量值则不敏感于上述因素。

Abstract: We present fully two-dimensional self-consistent calculations of the electron density, local filling factor and current distribution in a six-terminal GaAs/AlGaAs Hall bar of width W = 3 micrometers with two additional square contacts, A and B, placed on the channel axis and connected to the outside by air bridges, following the interior-contact experiment of Kendirlik et al. [Nat. Commun. 8, 14082 (2017)]. Within the screening theory of the integer quantized Hall effect, the Thomas-Fermi-Poisson equations are solved on the full device plane, including the probe arms and the depletion regions around the interior contacts, and the current is obtained from a local Ohm's law with a filling-factor dependent conductivity tensor, for source-drain and interior-contact excitation separately. Across the nu = 2 plateau the interior contacts pass through three regimes as the field is lowered: galvanically connected to the current path in a compressible bulk, floating in the Hall potential of a fully incompressible bulk, and isolated from both the bulk and the edge strips by closed incompressible rings encircling each contact. In the floating and isolated regimes the two-terminal resistance between the interior contacts rises by orders of magnitude. The isolation interval lies within the Hall plateau and ends before its low-field edge; its high-field end is sharply set by the disappearance of the incompressible bulk, while its low-field end shrinks with temperature and Landau-level broadening. The resistance between an interior contact and a perimeter probe approaches one half of the quantized Hall resistance across the plateau. The topology of the incompressible regions and the boundaries of the isolation interval are insensitive to the regularization of the local conductivity, to the grid and to moderate disorder, whereas the magnitude of the isolation resistance is not.

</details>


### [15] [From Quantized Hall Plateaus to Topological Surfaces: Quantum Capacitance as a Unifying Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00336)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文论证量子电容（quantum capacitance）并非量子霍尔效应与拓扑绝缘体的次要特征，而是探测拓扑所施加边界态密度（boundary density of states）的最清晰窗口之一，并借此揭示两者类比中"绝缘体块体"假设的局域性与场依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔效应（quantum Hall effect）、拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators）与量子电容通常被视为彼此独立的主题，但三者共享一条共同线索：块体拓扑（bulk topology）决定受保护的边界态（protected boundary state），而量子电容是少数能够探测态密度（density of states）的静电方法之一。本文旨在考察这一内在联系。

Method: 采用理论与实验相结合的分析路径：以陈数（Chern number）与Z2拓扑不变量刻画边界输运的拓扑起源，结合自洽屏蔽理论（self-consistent screening theory）描述量子霍尔块体的可压缩与不可压缩区域，并援引局域量子电容测量作为验证手段。

Result: 整数量子霍尔效应表明量子化边界输运具有由陈数给出的拓扑起源，后被推广至零场下的Z2拓扑绝缘体，其具有螺旋边缘态（helical edge states）与狄拉克表面态（Dirac surface states）。量子电容技术最初为二维电子气（two-dimensional electron gas）发展，现已成为映射朗道能级（Landau level）态密度与拓扑表面态线性狄拉克态密度的关键手段。自洽屏蔽理论显示量子霍尔块体会分裂为随磁场移动的可压缩与不可压缩区域；局域量子电容测量证实，在量子化平台内块体并非均匀绝缘，尽管平台仍保持量子化。

Conclusion: 量子电容是洞察拓扑所施加边界态密度最清晰的窗口之一，而非这些现象的附属特征；同时它揭示了量子霍尔效应与拓扑绝缘体类比中"绝缘块体"假设的局域、场依赖本质。

Abstract: The quantum Hall effect, topological insulators and quantum capacitance are usually treated as distinct topics, yet they share a common thread. Bulk topology determines a protected boundary state, and quantum capacitance is one of the few electrostatic methods able to detect the density of states. This paper examines that connection. The integer quantum Hall effect showed that quantised boundary transport has a topological origin given by the Chern number, later extended to zero field in Z2 topological insulators with helical edge and Dirac surface states. Quantum capacitance, developed for two-dimensional electron gases, has since become a key technique for mapping the Landau level density of states underlying the quantum Hall effect and the linear Dirac density of states of topological surface states. This picture is complicated by self-consistent screening theory, which shows that the quantum Hall bulk splits into compressible and incompressible regions that shift with field. Local quantum capacitance measurements confirm that the bulk is not uniformly insulating across a quantised plateau, even though the plateau stays quantised. The paper argues that quantum capacitance is not a secondary feature of these phenomena but one of the clearest windows into the boundary density of states that topology imposes, and that it reveals the local, field-dependent nature of the insulating bulk assumption underlying the analogy between the quantum Hall effect and topological insulators.

</details>

*Luojia Zhang,Andrew T. Pierce,Xuepeng Wang,Simon Reinhardt,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan,Debanjan Chowdhury,Valla Fatemi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个基于超导层的自腔结构平台，实现电磁体积压缩约 $10^7$ 倍，并结合石墨烯层在 0.04 THz 观测到欠阻尼腔等离激元极化激元及其与腔模的避免交叉。为完成测量，作者搭建了兼容毫开尔文温度的亚太赫兹光谱仪。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructures）具有从亚太赫兹到太赫兹频段的自腔共振，为研究腔修饰物态（cavity-modified phases of matter）提供了途径。然而，对此类体系的谱学研究需要在毫开尔文（millikelvin）温度下具备极高频率分辨率，现有方法难以满足。

Abstract: The coherent dynamics of lattice waves is of significant importance for exploring the nonadiabatic phenomena of the electron-phonon coupling under nonequilibrium conditions.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘涡旋（edge vortices）有望成为可飞行的非阿贝尔伊辛任意子（non-Abelian Ising anyons），且可通过任意子融合过程实现全电学探测，因此需要明确其电输运特征。已有处理多限于特定对称构型，需推广至更一般的非对称情形以判断干涉信号是否可观测。

Method: 采用引入额外复本扇区（replica sector）的玻色化（bosonization）方法，将模型推广至完全非对称构型，并分析Mach-Zehnder干涉仪中的输运。通过考察直流电导与低频响应，识别可反映边缘涡旋统计性质的物理量。

Result: 直流电导不显示边缘涡旋干涉特征，而低频干涉表现为有效电容（effective capacitance）。该电容对边缘涡旋的非阿贝尔任意子统计敏感，并携带其拓扑自旋（topological spin）与非平庸共形维度（conformal dimension）的印记。低温T下相关尺度为热长度（thermal length）$l_T = \hbar v/(\pi k_B T)$，与常规量子霍尔边缘态中速度为v的准粒子输运类似。

Abstract: Co-propagating chiral Majorana edge modes are predicted to exist in proximity-coupled quantum anomalous Hall systems. In this setup, electrical transport arises from the interference of a pair of Majorana edge states. By bosonizing the model with an additional replica sector, we generalize previous treatments to fully asymmetric configurations. We analyze a Mach-Zehnder interferometer and show that while the DC conductance does not exhibit signatures of edge-vortex interference, at low frequencies, this interference manifests as an effective capacitance. This quantity is sensitive to the non-Abelian anyon statistics of edge vortices and, in particular, carries signatures of their topological spin and nontrivial conformal dimension. We demonstrate that the thermal length $l_T = \hbar v/(πk_B T)$ is the relevant scale at low temperatures $T$, analogous to quasiparticle transport in conventional quantum Hall edge states with velocity $v$. As long as the interferometer arm lengths match within this length scale, transport signatures of the non-Abelian statistics of edge vortices remain visible.

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作以石墨烯（graphene）为平台，通过将电流响应在电流算符层面分解为局域扇区（local sector）与梯度扇区（gradient sector），揭示了结构化光（structured light）驱动直流电流的方位角绕数（winding order $m$）选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化光可产生具有方位角绕数的电子直流电流，但控制其绕数阶 $m$ 的规则此前尚不清晰，需要建立系统性的分类原则。

Method: 以石墨烯作为洁净二维平台，在电流算符层面将电流响应分解为局域扇区与梯度扇区，并结合电流算符的角向结构与沿方位方向的投影进行分析；辅以数值时间演化计算进行验证。

Conclusion: 研究提出了按扇区分解的组织原则，用于分类与调控结构化光驱动的直流电流及其定制化绕数结构。

Abstract: Structured light can generate electronic dc currents with azimuthal winding, yet the rules governing their winding order $m$ remain unclear. Here we identify sector-resolved winding selection rules using graphene as a clean two-dimensional platform. By decomposing the current response into local and gradient sectors at the current-operator level, we show that the winding order is determined not by the optical orbital angular momentum $\ell$ alone, but by the angular structure of the corresponding current operators together with projection onto the azimuthal direction. For scalar Laguerre-Gaussian beams, linear polarization yields $m=|\ell\pm1|$ in the local sector and $m=|\ell|,|\ell\pm2|$ in the gradient sector, whereas circular polarization with helicity $σ=\pm1$ selects $m=|\ell-σ|$ in the local sector and $m=|\ell+2σ|$ in the gradient sector. Numerical time-evolution calculations verify these rules and further show that helicity can select an $m=0$ branch, producing an azimuthally uniform circulating current whose radial profile determines the axial magnetic field $B_z(z)$. Our results provide a sector-resolved organizing principle for classifying and controlling structured-light-driven dc currents with tailored winding structures.

</details>


### [25] [Parafermions in fractional Chern insulator-superconductor heterostructures: the role of spin polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01486)
*Aaron Amire*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文分析自旋-谷极化 $\nu=2/3$ 分数量子反常霍尔边缘沟道中的配对与隧穿，发现中性模超导与中性模背散射两个通道各自凝聚同一中性任意子（anyon），其畴壁承载 $Z_3$ parafermion（每个界面 3 个受保护态），并给出稳定条件与 $6\pi$ 周期约瑟夫森电流预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往 $Z_3$ parafermion 方案多基于自旋非极化 $\nu=2/3$ Halperin $(1,1,2)$ 态；而扭转 MoTe$_2$ 与菱面体石墨烯中的分数量子反常霍尔（FQAH）态是自旋-谷极化的 Jain 态，拓扑序相同但自旋结构不同，因此需重新检验边缘配对与隧穿的通道选择。

Method: 对两条极化 $\nu=2/3$ 边缘之间沟道内的边缘模电子双线性（bilinear）配对与隧穿算符，分析其共形自旋（conformal spin）；比较极化与非极化情况下的通道选择，考察中性模能隙形成前后的相关性与边缘库仑耦合下的相关（relevant）性，并判定所需超导配对对称性。

Result: 配对与隧穿各恰有两个零共形自旋双线性：配对仅与中性模超导通道相容，隧穿仅与中性模背散射相容（非极化态中单态配对与隧穿选同一通道）。两通道凝聚同一中性任意子（相差局域算符），故畴壁承载 $Z_3$ parafermion 与每对 3 个受保护态，且固定费米宇称不增加计数。中性能隙形成前，配对对排斥性边缘间耦合至多临界（marginal），需吸引性中性耦合；能隙形成后，当边缘间库仑耦合弱于边缘内耦合的 $7/25$ 时两个电荷项均为相关。超导须提供偶数频率、等自旋、谷内配对，$s$ 波单态加 Ising 自旋轨道耦合在一阶不满足，手性 $p+ip$ 与 $f+if$ 满足。

Conclusion: $Z_3$ 标签由 FQAH 态准粒子携带，任何局限于非 FQAH 隧穿段结区的过程无法改变它，故预期无论费米宇称是否固定均为 $6\pi$ 周期约瑟夫森电流。

Abstract: Most proposals for $Z_3$ parafermions in fractional quantum Hall-superconductor structures used the spin-unpolarized $ν= 2/3$ Halperin $(1,1,2)$ state. The fractional quantum anomalous Hall (FQAH) states of twisted MoTe$_2$ and rhombohedral graphene are believed to be spin- and valley-polarized Jain states, with the same topological order but a different spin structure. We analyze a trench between two polarized $ν= 2/3$ edges. Among the pairing and tunneling bilinears of the edge-mode electrons, exactly two of each have zero conformal spin. Pairing is compatible only with the neutral-mode superconductivity channel and tunneling only with neutral-mode backscattering, whereas in the unpolarized state singlet pairing and tunneling select the same channel. Since the two channels condense the same neutral anyon up to a local operator, the domain walls carry $Z_3$ parafermions, with 3 protected states per pair, whether or not the neutral channel changes between regions; fixing the fermion parity does not enlarge this count. Before the neutral gap forms, pairing is at best marginal for repulsive inter-edge coupling unless an attractive neutral coupling favors its channel; once the gap has formed, both charge terms are relevant for an inter-edge Coulomb coupling below 7/25 of the intra-edge one. The superconductor must supply even-frequency, equal-spin, intravalley pairing, which an s-wave singlet superconductor with only Ising spin-orbit coupling does not provide at leading order, whereas chiral $p+ip$ and $f+if$ states do. The $Z_3$ label is carried by a quasiparticle of the FQAH state, so no process confined to a junction whose tunneling segments are not FQAH can change it, and we expect a $6π$-periodic Josephson current with or without fixed fermion parity.

</details>

Result: 实现了斯格明子极性的确定性光学反转：激光写入的斯格明子极性由小面外场选择，单个 30 fs 激光脉冲可在无磁场、无电流脉冲条件下翻转其自旋结构极性；材料图形化为线后，光学操控可与电流诱导的斯格明子运动相组合。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了斯格明子自旋结构的确定性光学切换作为一种新的操控通道，区别于此前报道的光学成核（nucleation）与湮灭（annihilation），并指向光自旋电子学（opto-spintronic）斯格明子器件路线：由光编程斯格明子内部状态，由电流控制其位置。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions are promising nanoscale information carriers because their position can be manipulated by electrical currents. The additional ability of deterministic control of the spin structure would allow the use of magnetic skyrmions as multi-state bits, expanding the capabilities of skyrmion devices further. Such control could be realized by coupling all-optical helicity independent switching of magnetization (AO-HIS) to magnetic skyrmions. This is realized in a Pt/Ir/CoB/Gd/Pt multilayer that is engineered to stabilize magnetic skyrmions and, at the same time, enable AO-HIS by single ultrafast laser pulse. Laser excitation is used to write skyrmionic textures, whose polarity is selected by a small applied out-of-plane field. A single 30 fs laser pulse is then used to toggle the magnetization of an illuminated region containing skyrmionic textures, reversing their polarities without requiring any magnetic field or current pulses. This establishes deterministic optical switching of the skyrmion spin structure as a new manipulation channel, distinct from previously reported optical nucleation and annihilation. The material is then patterned into a wire, to combine the optical manipulation with current-induced skyrmion motion. These results establish a route toward opto-spintronic skyrmion devices in which light programs the internal state of a skyrmion and electrical currents control its position.

### [27] [Flux- and tunnel-parity-controlled Josephson nonreciprocity from singlet-triplet competition in a parallel double quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01830)
*Debika Debnath,Fernando Dominguez,Patrik Recher*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究平行双量子点约瑟夫森结（parallel double-quantum-dot Josephson junction）中由相位依赖的多体谱分支竞争所引发的非互易超电流（nonreciprocal supercurrent）机制，揭示轨道磁通（orbital flux）与规范不变的隧穿符号宇称（tunnel-sign parity）对局域与非局域库珀对转移干涉的调控作用，并给出可达约 40% 的二极管效率及 10-15% 的宽响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易超电流（nonreciprocal supercurrent）可在正负临界电流探测相位依赖多体谱的不等价部分时出现。本文旨在澄清该机制在强库仑排斥的相互作用纳米结构中的具体表现，特别是局域与非局域库珀对（Cooper-pair）转移共存时的谱分支竞争。

Method: 采用零带宽哈密顿量（zero-bandwidth Hamiltonian）的精确对角化（exact diagonalization），并显式纳入超导轨道（superconducting orbitals）。研究对象为具强位内库仑排斥（intradot Coulomb repulsion）的平行双量子点约瑟夫森结，分析轨道磁通与隧穿符号宇称对局域约瑟夫森过程与非局域交换之间干涉的控制作用。

Result: 在失谐量子点（detuned dots）情形下，产生磁通可调的单重态（singlet）、三重态（triplet）与双重态（doublet）基态谱分支及两种整流（rectification）区间。在单占据电荷扇区（(1,1) sector）边界附近，双重态分支与单重态或三重态分支竞争，二极管效率（diode efficiency）接近 40%。在三重态主导的 (1,1) 扇区内，邻近单重态交叉非对称地重塑基态包络，即使两个临界电流极值均位于类三重态分支上，仍产生约 10-15% 的较宽响应。

Conclusion: 有限温度通常抑制电荷交叉整流，但在中间温度下，三重态区间中归一化效率与绝对临界电流不对称性均出现增强。结果表明，相位依赖的多体分支竞争是约瑟夫森非互易性（Josephson nonreciprocity）的一种对栅压、磁通与隧穿宇称敏感的起源。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal supercurrents in interacting nanostructures can arise when the positive and negative critical currents probe inequivalent portions of a phase-dependent many-body spectrum. We study this mechanism in a parallel double-quantum-dot Josephson junction with strong intradot Coulomb repulsion, where local and non-local Cooper-pair transfer coexist. Using exact diagonalization of a zero-bandwidth Hamiltonian with explicit superconducting orbitals, we show that orbital flux and the gauge-invariant tunnel-sign parity control the interference between local Josephson processes and non-local exchange. For detuned dots, this produces flux-tunable singlet, triplet, and doublet ground-state spectral branches and two rectification regimes. Near the boundaries of the charge sector with singly occupied dots [(1,1) sector], doublet branches compete with singlet or triplet branches, yielding diode efficiencies approaching 40%. Within the triplet-dominated (1,1) sector, nearby singlet crossings asymmetrically reshape the ground-state envelope even when both critical-current extrema lie on the triplet-like branch, producing a broader response of order 10-15%. Finite temperature generally suppresses charge-crossover rectification, however, for intermediate temperatures we find an enhancement of both the normalized efficiency and the absolute critical-current asymmetry in the triplet regime. These results establish phase-dependent many-body branch competition as a gate-, flux-, and tunnel-parity-sensitive source of Josephson nonreciprocity.

</details>

*Dmitry Budker,Angela Wittmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该展望（perspective）文章指出，手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin selectivity, CISS）效应虽已在多种体系中获得实验观测，却缺乏统一的理论机制解释；作者主张借助基础粒子物理中常用的对称性（symmetry）考量来理解 CISS 的基本性质，并强调 CISS 并不违反宇称（parity）与时间反演（time-reversal）等基本对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CISS 效应已在众多不同体系中被实验观测，但尽管有大量理论工作，统一的理论机制（mechanistic understanding）仍未达成。该文因此试图为纷杂的实验与理论结果提供一个共同的解释框架。

Method: 采用展望（perspective）体裁，将基础粒子物理（fundamental particle physics）中常用的对称性（symmetry）考量引入对 CISS 基本性质的分析，而非构建具体微观模型。

Result: 分析表明，CISS 并不违反任何基本对称性，包括宇称（parity）与时间反演（time-reversal）。

Conclusion: 通过将 CISS 置于对称性的通用语言（universal language of symmetry）之下，该文为解释实验现象、指导未来理论与实验设计提供了稳健的概念基础（conceptual foundation）。

Abstract: The chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) effect, the coupling between structural chirality and electron spin polarization, has been experimentally observed across many diverse systems. However, despite extensive theoretical effort, a unified mechanistic understanding remains elusive. In this perspective, we demonstrate how some of the basic properties of the fascinating effects of CISS can be understood based on straightforward symmetry considerations commonly employed in fundamental particle physics. In particular, we show that CISS does not violate any fundamental symmetries including parity and time-reversal. By anchoring CISS within the universal language of symmetry, we offer a robust conceptual foundation for interpreting experiments and guiding future theoretical and experimental designs.

</details>

### [29] [Hyperbolic lattices with mass disorder: Phases and phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02192)
*Sheersh Sen,Christopher A. Leong,Bitan Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在庞加莱圆盘（Poincaré disk）上的三种双曲晶格（$\{10,3\}$、$\{8,3\}$、$\{8,4\}$）中，最近邻紧束缚模型（tight-binding model, TBM）分别表现出零能处态密度（density of states, DOS）消失、有限与发散的特征，对应狄拉克液体、费米液体与平带。质量无序（mass disorder）下，双曲狄拉克液体经历半金属-金属-安德森绝缘体两次转变，其余两体系仅呈现金属-绝缘体转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察质量无序对双曲晶格中不同零能态密度行为所对应电子态的影响，并借助这些晶格在最近邻紧束缚框架下呈现的二分性（bipartite nature）来比较无序驱动的量子相变。

Method: 采用核多项式方法（kernel polynomial method）对平均态密度与典型态密度进行大规模数值计算，研究对象为庞加莱圆盘上具开放边界条件的 $\{10,3\}$、$\{8,3\}$、$\{8,4\}$ 双曲晶格的最近邻紧束缚模型。

Result: 双曲狄拉克液体对弱质量无序保持稳定，在中等无序下发生半金属到金属的量子相变，在更强无序下继而发生安德森金属到绝缘体转变；其余两个体系仅显示后一种转变。上述各转变的临界指数（critical exponent）均接近由在位势无序（on-site potential disorder）所导致的转变数值。

Conclusion: 质量无序在双曲晶格中可诱发多重量子相变，其临界行为与在位势无序情形相近，表明无序类型对临界指数的影响在双曲几何下可能较为有限，具体机制仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor (NN) tight-binding models (TBMs) on plaquette-centered $\{ 10,3\}$ (Schläfli symbol), $\{ 8,3\}$, and $\{ 8,4\}$ hyperbolic lattices on a Poincaré disk with open boundary conditions display a vanishing, a finite, and a diverging density of states (DOS) near zero energy (band center), respectively, yielding a Dirac liquid, a Fermi liquid, and a flat band. Emergent bipartite nature of these lattices within the framework of NN-TBMs, allows us to scrutinize the impact of mass disorder on the electronic states and DOS therein. From extensive numerical calculations of the average and typical DOS using the kernel polynomial method, we show that hyperbolic Dirac liquid remains stable against weak mass disorder, undergoing a semimetal-to-metal quantum phase transition at moderate disorder, followed by an Anderson metal-to-insulator transition at even stronger disorder. The remaining two systems display only the latter transition. Critical exponents near all these transitions are found to be close to the ones mediated by on-site potential disorder.

</details>

### [30] [SupraTITO: Transferable Generative Molecular Dynamics for Supramolecular Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01381)
*Weilong Chen,Nuno Costa,Julija Zavadlav*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出 SupraTITO，一个可迁移的生成式分子动力学（GenMD）框架，用于周期性超分子体系（以多肽自组装为示例），学习以序列、拓扑和周期性几何为条件的隐式迁移算子，从而在远超 MD 积分步长的物理时间尺度上推进构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多肽序列同时决定自组装形成的结构及其涌现动力学，但预测二者需解析众多相互作用分子间的慢速集体过程；分子动力学（MD）虽提供微观洞见，却受限于组装的长时标与巨大的序列空间，系统性探索计算代价高昂。

Method: SupraTITO 学习可迁移的隐式迁移算子（TITO），其条件输入包括多肽序列、分子拓扑与周期性几何，使构型能够跨越远长于单个 MD 积分步的物理区间进行传播；这是对可迁移生成式分子动力学（GenMD）向周期性超分子体系的扩展。

Result: 在综合性二肽（dipeptide）基准上，SupraTITO 可泛化至留出序列，复现序列依赖的结构与动力学，并在长时间 rollout 中保持分子完整性；相较在相同轨迹数据上训练的直接系综预测，其更准确地复现组装结构并能解析其时间演化；所学动力学可跨多肽浓度泛化，包括训练中未出现的稀溶液条件。

Conclusion: 结果将可迁移 GenMD 扩展至周期性超分子体系中的集体动力学，并为建模多肽组装之外的相关过程提供了基础。

Abstract: Peptide sequence governs both the structures formed through supramolecular assembly and the dynamics by which they emerge, but predicting either requires resolving slow collective processes among many interacting molecules. Molecular dynamics (MD) provides microscopic insight into these processes, yet the long timescales of assembly and the vast peptide sequence space make systematic exploration computationally demanding. We introduce SupraTITO, a transferable generative molecular dynamics (GenMD) framework for supramolecular systems, demonstrated through peptide self-assembly. SupraTITO learns transferable implicit transfer operators (TITO) conditioned on peptide sequence, molecular topology, and periodic geometry, allowing configurations to be propagated over physical intervals much longer than an MD integration step. On a comprehensive dipeptide benchmark, SupraTITO generalizes to held-out sequences and reproduces sequence-dependent structures and dynamics while maintaining molecular integrity over long rollouts. Compared with direct ensemble prediction trained on the same trajectory data, SupraTITO more accurately reproduces assembly structures while also resolving their temporal evolution. The learned dynamics generalize across peptide concentrations, including dilute conditions not represented during training. These results extend transferable GenMD to collective dynamics in periodic supramolecular systems and provide a foundation for modeling related processes beyond peptide assembly.

</details>

*L. Ts. Adzhemyan,D. A. Davletbaeva,D. A. Evdokimov,M. V. Kompaniets*

TL;DR: 本文检验随机湍流（stochastic turbulence）的微扰重整化群（perturbative renormalization group, RG）机制能否从小 ε 延续至物理值 ε_phys = 2，并在大维数极限（large-d limit）下将修正指数（correction exponent）ω 计算至五圈阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理湍流对应 ε_phys = 2（大尺度强迫），而 RG 微扰展开通常仅在小 ε 处受控，因此需检验该微扰 RG 机制能否外推至物理情形。

Method: 利用大维数极限（large-d limit）带来的简化，将修正指数 ω 计算至五圈阶（five-loop order），并据此推测其封闭解析形式。

Result: 五圈阶结果呈现简单结构，提示存在封闭解析形式；该形式表明在 ε = 0 附近稳定的不动点（fixed point）在到达 ε_phys = 2 之前不再同时保持实性与红外稳定性（infrared stability）。

Conclusion: 结果指向红外区域（infrared regime）可能发生改变，而非受控的微扰 RG 外推。

Abstract: We examine whether the perturbative renormalization group (RG) regime of stochastic turbulence can be continued from small $\varepsilon$ to the physical value $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{phys}}=2$, corresponding to large-scale forcing. Exploiting the simplifications of the large-$d$ limit, we calculate the correction exponent $ω$ to five-loop order and find a simple structure suggesting a closed analytic form. This form implies that the fixed point stable near $\varepsilon=0$ ceases to be simultaneously real and infrared stable before reaching $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{phys}}=2$, pointing to a possible change of infrared regime rather than a controlled perturbative RG extrapolation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RP 镍酸盐中压力诱导超导电性的发现激发了对高临界温度（high-T_c）超导体的广泛兴趣，需要厘清四层 RP 镍酸盐 La₅Ni₄O₁₃ 在应变下的电子结构以探索其超导可能性。

Conclusion: 结果表明 c 轴压缩可能为四层镍酸盐提供一条类比于双层与三层镍酸盐的有利超导路径。

Abstract: The discovery of pressure-induced superconductivity in Ruddlesden--Popper (RP) nickelates has stimulated extensive interest in high-T$_c$ superconductors. Here, we systematically study the electronic properties of the quadlayer RP nickelate La$_5$Ni$_4$O$_{13}$ under ambient pressure, 5% isotropic compressive strain, and 4% $c$-axis uniaxial strain using density functional theory (DFT) and random phase approximation (RPA) calculations. DFT calculations show that isotropic strain broadens the Ni-$e_g$ bands and induces charge transfer from O-$p$ to Ni-$d$ orbitals, whereas $c$-axis uniaxial strain selectively shifts the $d_{z^2}$-derived bonding1 band upward while leaving the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ dispersion nearly unchanged. From Wannier downfolding, we construct a quadlayer two-orbital model that reproduces the low-energy Ni-$e_g$ bands. Our model reveals that under ambient pressure and 5% isotropic strain, the Fermi surface consists of two electron pockets ($α$ and $δ$) and three hole pockets ($β$, $β^{\prime}$, and $β^{\prime \prime}$), while under uniaxial strain, a $γ$ hole pocket with $d_{z^2}$ orbital character emerges. RPA calculations reveal that the leading spin response shifts from $\mathbf{q}\approx(2π/3,2π/3)$ at ambient pressure to $\mathbf{q}\approx(π,π)$ under both strain conditions and is enhanced under $c$-axis compression. These results suggest that $c$-axis compression may provide a favorable route to superconductivity in the quadlayer nickelate analogous to that in bilayer and trilayer nickelates.

Abstract: We investigate collective modes in charge-neutral altermagnetic superconductors with $d$-wave spin-split bands, focusing on the impact of emergent Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces. In the absence of a magnetic field, the BCS state supports collective modes that inherit the underlying $d$-wave symmetry, resulting in pronounced momentum-space anisotropy. The broken time-reversal symmetry further couples these modes to spin-density fluctuations, allowing their signatures to emerge in the spin dynamical structure factor. Upon applying a magnetic field, Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces emerge and qualitatively reshape the collective-mode dynamics through gapless particle-hole excitations and strongly anisotropic Landau damping. The damping is strongest along the momentum-space diagonal, where it can suppress the low-energy phonon mode at small momenta. Remarkably, the suppressed phonon is accompanied by the emergence of an in-gap structure in the Higgs mode and an associated pronounced enhancement of the spin response. These results establish Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces as a mechanism for generating highly anisotropic collective-mode dynamics and identify spin response as a sensitive probe of in-gap Higgs excitations in altermagnetic superconductors.

Abstract: The discovery of spin-density-wave (SDW) order in bilayer nickelates has intensified interest in its interplay with superconductivity. Unlike cuprates, where doping rapidly suppresses the Néel temperature, the SDW transition temperature ($T_{\mathrm{SDW}}$) in bilayer nickelates is robust against oxygen annealing and even increases under pressure. Here, we combine oxygen annealing with isovalent rare-earth ($A$-site) substitution to effectively apply $c$-axis uniaxial pressure, realizing superconducting bilayer nickelate films with $T_{\mathrm{SDW}}$ suppressed from 150 K to 70 K. Notably, while SDW order is weakened but remains, a second charge-like anisotropy order is completely eliminated in the superconducting state. Polarization-resolved O $K$-edge X-ray absorption and electronic structure calculations show that strengthened interlayer coupling reconstructs the Fermi surface and weakens the SDW. These findings, consistent with a spin-spinless stripe ground state, provide new insight into the mechanism of density wave formation and their interplay with superconductivity.

### [35] [Quantum gates from the middle convolution of twisted Burau representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00293)
*Haru Negami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由特征扭曲 Burau 输入经 Katz-Long-Moody（KLM）构造得到、对应 KZ 型方程乘性中位卷积（multiplicative middle convolution）的辫表示。作者刻画了 M=n(n+1) 维输出的谱与不变直和项分解，并构造显式正交基给出各生成元的块结构与量子线路实现及其资源界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辫表示在量子信息中可提供非 Clifford 与纠缠生成元，但源自 KLM 构造的这类输出的谱、可约性与量子线路实现尚不明确。作者针对固定输入下酉实现唯一（至同时酉共轭）的情形，提出两个问题：输出的谱与不变直和项结构，以及能否给出可行的门级实现与资源刻画。

Method: 在 q=e^{2πia}、τ=e^{2πic}（a,c>0，nc+(n+1)a<1，n≥2）及 λ=e^{2πil}、0<l<1-nc-(n+1)a 的正定不变形式下，计算 M=n(n+1) 维输出的生成元谱与不变直和项，并分析其 Cliffod、纠缠与稠密性性质；随后构造一个正交基，将每个生成元化为特定块结构，并表示为秩一相位反射之积及由单量子比特酉门与 CNOT 构成的 O(M) 长词。

Result: 生成元谱可恢复 (q,τ)；输出分解为两个平凡与两个约化 Burau 直和项，以及一个与 Long 约化构造同构的 n(n-1) 维直和项，后者在 n=3,4 时一般不可约。若 a 或 c 无理，每个生成元在每个有限维 Weyl-Heisenberg 框架下均非 Clifford；若 1,a,c 在 Q 上线性无关，每个生成元对每个非平凡二分均是纠缠的；整体辫像是投影稠密的。对一般正定酉输入，非例外参数处整体辫输出的不可约性迫使自由群输入算子可交换。所得正交基下每个生成元含一个 2(n+1) 块、n-2 个二元块，其余为单位；每个生成元是 2n 个秩一相位反射之积，在固定二进制编码下为不含辅助量子比特的 O(M) 长单量子比特酉与 CNOT 词（含方向制备及其逆）。文中给出有限字母表逼近、条件数与误差界，并与 Quantum Shannon 参考编译器进行匹配资源比较；门界对编码量子比特数呈指数增长，且不包含经典预处理。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了 KLM 型辫表示的输出谱与不变子空间结构，揭示了其非 Clifford、纠缠与（非）稠密性判据，并给出显式块结构正交基及门级量子线路实现与资源界，同时明确了门界的指数依赖及其不含经典预处理的限制。

Abstract: We study braid representations obtained from character-twisted Burau inputs by the Katz-Long-Moody (KLM) construction, which corresponds to multiplicative middle convolution for KZ-type equations. For $n\ge2$, let $q=e^{2πia}$, $τ=e^{2πic}$, where $a,c>0$ and $nc+(n+1)a<1$. Known form and signature results give a positive-definite invariant form for $λ=e^{2πil}$, $0<l<1-nc-(n+1)a$. For fixed input, unitary realizations are unique up to simultaneous unitary conjugation. We address two questions.
  First, we determine spectra and invariant summands of the $M=n(n+1)$-dimensional output. The generator spectrum recovers $(q,τ)$. The output decomposes into two trivial and two reduced Burau summands, and an $n(n-1)$-dimensional summand isomorphic to Long's reduced construction, generically irreducible for $n=3,4$. If $a$ or $c$ is irrational, every generator is non-Clifford in every finite-dimensional Weyl-Heisenberg frame; if $1,a,c$ are linearly independent over $\mathbb{Q}$, every generator is entangling for every nontrivial bipartition. The full braid image is not projectively dense. For general positive-definite unitary inputs, irreducibility of the full braid output at a nonexceptional parameter forces the free-group input operators to commute.
  Second, we construct one orthonormal basis giving each generator one block of size $2(n+1)$, $n-2$ blocks of size two, and identity elsewhere. Each generator is a product of $2n$ rank-one phase reflections and, in a fixed binary encoding, an $O(M)$-length word in one-qubit unitaries and CNOTs, without auxiliary qubits and including direction preparation and its inverse. We give finite-alphabet approximation, conditioning and error bounds, and a matched-resource comparison with a Quantum Shannon reference compiler. The gate bound is exponential in the encoded qubit count and excludes classical preprocessing.

</details>


### [36] [Causal Trade-Offs in Superluminal Extensions of Relativity. A no-go theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00494)
*Amrapali Sen,Matthias Salzger,V. Vilasini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建因果建模（causal modelling）框架，对Dragan与Ekert提出的含超光速参考系（superluminal reference frames）的相对论扩展进行形式化研究，并证明一个no-go定理：该理论若不引发超光速信号传递（superluminal signalling），就必须严重限制可操作协议可及时空区域、使参考系选择具有可观测后果，或否定自由变量（free variables）的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dragan与Ekert近期提出以超光速参考系扩展相对论，试图调和相对论对称性与量子不确定性（quantum uncertainty）。本文旨在对该提议进行形式化检验，厘清其是否真能避免与超光速信号传递的冲突。

Method: 采用因果建模（causal modelling）框架对该提议进行形式化分析，并证明一个no-go定理。

Result: no-go定理表明：允许超光速参考系的理论要避免超光速信号传递，必须满足以下至少一项条件——严重限制可操作协议可及的时空区域；参考系选择产生可观测后果；自由变量不存在，或其存在依赖于参考系。该结果超出了“超光速变换可能导致超光速信号”的常见直觉。

Conclusion: Dragan与Ekert引入客观不确定性（objective uncertainty）的提议本身并不能消除这一冲突；由于可通过放宽其他基本假设来避免超光速信号传递，所提议的客观不确定性可能根本不必要。研究澄清了基于不确定性的解决方案的局限，以及超光速相对论扩展所面临的因果权衡（causal trade-offs）。

Abstract: Extensions of relativity that include superluminal reference frames have recently been proposed by Dragan and Ekert as a way to reconcile relativistic symmetry with quantum uncertainty. In this work, we develop a causal modelling framework to formally study this proposal and prove a no-go theorem demonstrating that a theory permitting superluminal reference frames can avoid superluminal signalling only if it severely constrains which spacetime regions are accessible to operational protocols, if the choice of reference frame has observable consequences or if free variables do not exist or their existence depends on the reference frame. Crucially, our result goes beyond the familiar intuition that superluminal transformations may lead to superluminal signalling; our no-go result shows that Dragan and Ekert's proposal of introducing objective uncertainty does not, by itself, eliminate this conflict. In fact, because superluminal signalling might instead be avoided by relaxing other fundamental assumptions in the presence of superluminal reference frames, the proposed objective uncertainty may not be necessary at all. Our results clarify the limitations of uncertainty-based resolutions and the causal trade-offs faced by superluminal extensions of relativity.

</details>


### [37] [Conditioning on Subalgebras - Entropy Duality and Generalized Quantum Stein's Lemma in von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00772)
*Bjarne Bergh,Li Gao,Mizanur Rahaman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在任意冯·诺依曼代数（von Neumann algebra）的子代数上建立了条件熵（conditional entropy）的对偶关系，涵盖 Umegaki 相对熵、夹层 Rényi 散度与 Petz-Rényi 散度，并将其从有限维张量积子系统推广至无限维一般子代数；作为应用，给出量子 Stein 引理（quantum Stein's lemma）在有限指数子代数假设下的无限维推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有条件熵对偶关系主要局限于有限维张量积子系统情形，缺乏对一般子代数与无限维设定的统一处理。论文旨在将这一对偶框架推广至任意冯·诺依曼代数的子代数，并赋予相对熵至子代数以操作性意义。

Method: 方法为对一般冯·诺依曼代数子代数建立条件熵的对偶关系，覆盖 Umegaki 相对熵（Umegaki relative entropy）以及夹层（sandwiched）与 Petz-Rényi 散度两类散度；并将框架提升至完全无限维（fully infinite-dimensional）设定。

Abstract: Analogue and digital Hamiltonian simulation represent two distinct approaches to making one quantum system replicate the physical properties of another, with consequences ranging from practical applications to complexity theory and quantum gravity. Abstract: Knill-Laflamme coefficients characterize exact quantum error detection through scalar compressions of error operators to the code space. A final three-qubit example shows additional noncommuting detection constraints reduce a tetrahedral range to its barycenter and four vertices, producing a disconnected signature spectrum. Method: 核心观察是费米子协方差矩阵的可容许性条件（admissibility condition）对两点关联施加了定量的一夫一妻约束（monogamy constraint）：固定区域仅有有界的协方差预算可分配给众多不相交区域。作者将此原理应用于三个方向：有限简单高次图上的二次费米子哈密顿量、分级费米子副本置换下的 k-拷贝高斯态，以及具有指数聚类互信息的空间费米子高斯态。

Abstract: A recurring principle in many-body physics is that sufficiently distributed interactions or correlations lead to an effectively mean-field description. Second, we prove a finite fermionic Gaussian de Finetti theorem: a \(k\)-copy Gaussian state that is \(n\)-exchangeable under graded fermionic copy permutations is \(O(k/n)\)-close in trace norm to the product of its one-copy marginal. In particular, infinite exchangeability within the Gaussian class implies exact product structure. Third, for spatial fermionic Gaussian states with exponentially clustering mutual information, we prove that the conditional mutual information across a separating buffer decays as \(O(r^{-1})\), and hence, by recoverability, the state admits an \(O(r^{-1/2})\) trace-norm approximation by a recovered state. The decisive reg-HF hypothesis is a one-mode exclusion axiom: covariance carried by response components orthogonal to the retained pair mode is excluded from the terminal scalar energy property, without asserting that the unrestricted response is one dimensional. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统态制备复杂度下界主要依赖电路光锥（circuit lightcone）论证，在稠密相互作用与允许任意可丢弃辅助比特（discardable ancillas）的情形下易失效，尤其是后接无限制 Clifford 电路时会扩散局部可观测量。因此需要一种不依赖光锥的复杂度度量。

Method: 以 Pauli 轮廓（Pauli profiles）的度量熵（metric entropy）导出类的“有效轮廓复杂度”，该轮廓记录恰支撑在 p 个量子比特上的所有 Pauli 算符期望值；证明上结合 Berta 等人非对称量子 de Finetti 定理（nonsymmetric quantum de Finetti theorem）的改造与高斯过程熵界（Gaussian process entropy bounds）。

Result: 二次有效轮廓复杂度一致有界的类，与基态能量相距至少正比于 √n（对足够大固定 p）；次二次复杂度下该类在领头阶无法优于合适基准类，乘积态（product states）为通用基准。应用上，达到近基态能量需 Ω(n²/log n) 个一、二量子比特门，且给出深度-宽度权衡、纠缠深度与矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）键维数下界，以及在 Parham 魔法层级各固定层级上双向的障碍（总宽度 O(n)）；首级逆向魔法（reverse magic）情形允许任意多干净辅助比特，且 Clifford+T 电路中 o(n) 个 T 门相对乘积稳定子态（product stabilizer states）无领头阶能量优势。

Conclusion: 有效轮廓复杂度可作为稠密 p-自旋态制备复杂度的统一下界工具，涵盖门数、深度-宽度、纠缠与魔法资源等多个维度，并适用于允许大宽度、任意辅助比特及非对称电路结构的场景。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying state preparation complexity in dense quantum $p$-spin Hamiltonians on $n$ qubits, going beyond bounds based only on circuit lightcones. The key input is the class's effective profile complexity, which is derived from the metric entropy of its Pauli profiles. These profiles record expectations of all Pauli operators supported on exactly $p$ qubits. Classes with uniformly bounded quadratic effective profile complexity remain separated from the ground-state energy by a positive multiple of $\sqrt n$ for sufficiently large fixed $p$. At subquadratic effective profile complexity, the class cannot outperform a suitable benchmark class at leading order, with product states providing a universal benchmark. The proof combines an adaptation of a nonsymmetric quantum de Finetti theorem of Berta et al. (arXiv:1810.12197) with Gaussian process entropy bounds.
  Applying this framework, we show that attaining near-ground-state energy requires $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ one- and two-qubit gates, even with arbitrary discardable ancillas. We also obtain depth-width tradeoffs, entanglement-depth and matrix product state bond-dimension lower bounds, and obstructions for both orientations at every fixed level of Parham's magic hierarchy (arXiv:2504.19966), with total circuit width $O(n)$. In first-level reverse magic, a shallow circuit is followed by an unrestricted Clifford circuit. The latter can spread local observables across the system, preventing a direct application of small-lightcone bounds. For this first-level class, our bounds also allow arbitrarily many clean ancillas at fixed shallow-circuit depth. A sharper benchmark shows that Clifford+$T$ circuits with $o(n)$ $T$-gates have no leading-order energy advantage over product stabilizer states, even with unrestricted Clifford operations and arbitrary discardable ancillas.

</details>


### [61] [Polynomial-time classical and quantum simulation of quantum impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02167)
*Jiaqing Jiang,Nathan Ju,Ojas Parekh,Chaithanya Rayudu,Andrew Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作系统刻画了量子杂质模型（quantum impurity models）模拟的计算复杂度：其静态性质可被经典计算机高效计算（基态能量达到多项式时间，配分函数首次获得严格多项式时间保证），而动态性质（如非平衡格林函数）经典难解、量子易解，从而界定了经典与量子可解性的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型既是相互作用量子物质的典型模型，也是现代电子结构方法的关键计算原语，但其模拟的经典与量子可解性边界此前未被清晰界定。论文旨在系统研究该问题的计算复杂度，划清可经典处理与需量子处理的界限。

Method: 采用计算复杂度分析的方法，针对耦合于大非相互作用浴（bath）的小型相互作用费米子子系统，分别研究静态性质（基态能量、逆温度 β 下的配分函数）与动态性质（非平衡格林函数）的模拟复杂度，并给出相应的经典算法与复杂度界。

Result: 静态性质方面：给出经典算法，可在 poly(n, δ⁻¹) 时间内以加性精度 δ 估计基态能量，并在 poly(n, β, δ⁻¹) 时间内以相对精度 δ 估计逆温度 β 下的配分函数（n 为系统规模）；基态能量复杂度由准多项式提升至多项式，且首次为热平衡模拟建立严格多项式时间保证。动态性质方面：经典计算难解，但量子计算容易，计算非平衡格林函数可捕捉量子计算的全部能力，即使在有限温度下亦如此。

Conclusion: 结果排除了计算静态性质时存在超多项式量子加速的可能，同时指出在模拟非平衡杂质物理方面存在量子优势的路径，明确了该模型经典与量子可解性的分界。

Abstract: Quantum impurity models are paradigmatic models of interacting quantum matter, as well as key computational primitives for modern electronic-structure methods. They describe a small subsystem of interacting fermions coupled to a large, noninteracting bath. We perform a comprehensive study of the computational complexity of simulating impurity models, delineating the boundary between classical and quantum tractability for this class of problems. Our main finding is that static properties of quantum impurity models can be calculated efficiently on a classical computer. Specifically, we give classical algorithms that (1) estimate the ground-state energy to additive precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,δ^{-1})$, and (2) estimate the partition function at inverse temperature $β$ to relative precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,δ^{-1})$, where $n$ is the system size. These results improve the previous best-known complexity for ground-state energy estimation from quasipolynomial to polynomial time, while establishing for the first time rigorous polynomial-time guarantees for simulating impurity models in thermal equilibrium. On the other hand, we find that simulating dynamical properties of impurity models is hard for classical computers but easy on a quantum computer. As a canonical example, we show that computing their nonequilibrium Green's functions captures the full power of quantum computation, even at finite temperature. Taken together, our results rule out superpolynomial quantum speedups for computing static properties, but provide an avenue for quantum advantage in simulating impurity physics out of equilibrium.

</details>

### [62] [Krylov complexity of a tilted extended Bose-Hubbard chain with Rydberg-dressed interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00712)
*Yifan Chen,Tianyi Yan,Lu Qin,Weibin Li*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究以倾斜扩展 Bose-Hubbard 链（tilted extended Bose-Hubbard chain）为平台，利用 Krylov 态复杂度（Krylov state complexity）研究多体混沌，发现在 U≃V 处存在一个"准混沌对角区（quasi-chaotic diagonal regime）"，其复杂度饱和值呈现尖锐脊线，而能级间距统计仅部分混沌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱诊断（spectral diagnostics）只能提供关于多体混沌的部分信息，无法反映态依赖的动力学特征。论文旨在检验 Krylov 态复杂度能否作为补充性、态敏感的混沌与热化诊断工具，并探索其在实验可及量子模拟器中的适用性。

Method: 研究对象为同时含在位相互作用 U 与最近邻相互作用 V 的倾斜扩展 Bose-Hubbard 模型，可由光晶格中的 Rydberg-dressed 相互作用实现。方法上采用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）与 Lanczos 递推（Lanczos recursion），从三个具有物理动机的 Fock 态出发，计算开链在单位填充（unit filling）下的时间依赖复杂度、长时间饱和值及 Lanczos 系数的涨落。

Result: 在 U≃V 附近识别出显著的准混沌对角区：Krylov 复杂度的饱和值出现尖锐脊线，而谱能级间距统计仍仅表现为部分混沌，二者出现解离（dissociation）。该现象源于在位与最近邻相互作用之间的竞争，产生大量近共振多体组态构成的密集流形，被态依赖动力学高效耦合，但仍保留残余谱结构。

Conclusion: Krylov 态复杂度可提供谱诊断无法捕捉的、互补且态敏感的多体混沌信息，可作为研究热化（thermalization）与信息搅乱（information scrambling）的有效诊断量，并适用于实验可及的量子模拟器。

Abstract: We investigate Krylov state complexity in a tilted extended Bose-Hubbard chain in which both the on-site interaction $U$ and nearest-neighbor interaction $V$ are present. The tilted extended Bose-Hubbard model can be realized with Rydberg-dressed interactions in optical lattices. Using exact diagonalization and Lanczos recursion from three physically motivated Fock states, we compute the time-dependent complexity, its long-time saturation value, and the fluctuations of the Lanczos coefficients for an open chain at unit filling. Crucially, we identify a pronounced quasi-chaotic diagonal regime near $U\simeq V$ in which the Krylov complexity saturation exhibits a sharp ridge while the spectral level-spacing statistics remain partially chaotic. This dissociation arises from the competition between the on-site and nearest-neighbor interaction, which generates a dense manifold of near-resonant many-body configurations that is efficiently coupled by the state-dependent dynamics but retains residual spectral structure. Our results show that Krylov state complexity provides complementary, state-sensitive information about many-body chaos that is not captured by the spectral diagnostics alone, establishing Krylov complexity as a useful diagnostic for studying thermalization and information scrambling in experimentally accessible quantum simulators.

</details>


### [63] [Study of quantum turbulence by vortex-antivortex dynamics in dipolar BECs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00857)
*S. Sabari,Lauro Tomio*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究在旋转高斯障碍势搅拌下的偶极玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（dipolar Bose-Einstein condensate）中，比较了两种搅拌协议下的涡旋成核与动力学，发现偶极相互作用显著抑制涡旋衰减，并在后期形成局部三角样有序结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在为具有长程各向异性相互作用的偶极超流体（dipolar superfluid）中的涡旋研究建立可控平台，并为未来量子湍流（quantum turbulence）实验提供基准。

Method: 采用旋转高斯障碍势（rotating Gaussian obstacle）搅拌偶极玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，对比两种协议：Case 1 为恒定幅值的持续搅拌，Case 2 为在 t = 15 ms 时移除障碍并以线性幅值衰减。两种情形均采用平滑角速度斜坡（t_acc = 3 ms）以抑制杂散声子激发（phonon excitations）。

Result: 首个涡旋对在 t ~ 3 ms 离开障碍，至 t ~ 8-10 ms 单个涡旋与反涡旋在相位图中完全分辨。Case 1 的涡旋数量增长至 t ~ 40 ms 后大致饱和；Case 2 在 t ~ 15-25 ms 达峰后下降。两种协议至 t ~ 50 ms 均在凝聚体局部区域形成三角样有序，并表现出椭圆畸变与沿极化轴的拉长涡核等偶极特征。混合涡旋-反涡旋在 100 ms 内保持稳定且无可观测湮灭。动能分解确认后期不可压缩（涡旋）能量占主导。

Conclusion: 结果表明偶极相互作用强烈抑制涡旋衰减，该体系可作为偶极超流体涡旋研究的可控平台，并为长程各向异性相互作用下的量子湍流实验提供基准。

Abstract: We investigate vortex nucleation and dynamics in a dipolar Bose-Einstein condensate stirred by a rotating Gaussian obstacle. Two stirring protocols are compared: continuous stirring with constant amplitude (Case 1), and obstacle removal at $t = 15$ ms with linear amplitude ramp-down (Case 2). In both cases, a smooth angular velocity ramp ($t_{\text{acc}} = 3$ ms) suppresses spurious phonon excitations. The first vortex pairs exit the obstacle at $t \sim 3$ ms, and by $t \sim 8$ - $10$ ms the individual vortices and antivortices are fully resolved in the phase maps. Case 1 yields a vortex population that grows up to $t \sim 40$ ms and then roughly saturates, while Case 2 yields a population that peaks around $t \sim 15$ - $25$ ms and then decreases. Remarkably, both protocols lead to local triangular-like ordering in selected regions of the condensate by $t \sim 50$ ms, exhibiting clear dipolar signatures: elliptical distortion and elongated vortex cores along the polarization axis. The mixed vortex-antivortex population remains stable up to $100$ ms with no observable annihilation, indicating that dipolar interactions strongly suppress vortex decay. Kinetic energy decomposition confirms that incompressible (vortex) energy dominates at late times. These results establish a controlled platform for vortex studies in dipolar superfluids and provide benchmarks for future experiments on quantum turbulence with long-range anisotropic interactions.

</details>
