Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出 Landau 规范下 Gribov–Zwanziger 模型的一个 BRST 不变表述，其中 Gribov 标度由标量四重态对称性自发破缺产生，而非通过传统构造中的显式破缺项引入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Gribov–Zwanziger 构造依赖显式破缺项来生成 Gribov 标度，这可能会损害模型的 BRST 不变性与物理内容。本文旨在寻找一种自发破缺机制，使该标度在保持 BRST 不变性的同时自然产生。

Method: 在 Landau 规范下构造 BRST 不变的 Gribov–Zwanziger 模型，引入标量四重态（scalar quartet）对称性的自发破缺机制，并采用代数重整化（algebraic renormalization）技术分析该模型。

Result: 所得作用量保持 Landau 规范的鬼场方程（ghost equation），并重现原始 Gribov 方案的特征性 Gribov 型规范传播子（gauge propagator），同时容纳精细型（refined type）的 BRST 不变质量项。模型可重整化至所有阶，且经典作用量之外无需额外抵消项（counterterm）。

Conclusion: 四重态凝聚不改变刻画可观测量的上同调（cohomology）：位移真空中的算子与对称相具有相同上同调，因此质量标度的产生不以牺牲理论的物理内容为代价。该结果使 Gribov–Zwanziger 框架下真正规范不变可观测量的构造可沿用通常的上同调方法。

Abstract: We propose a BRST-invariant formulation of the Gribov--Zwanziger model in the Landau gauge, in which the Gribov scale is produced by the spontaneous breaking of a scalar quartet symmetry, rather than through the explicit breaking term of the conventional construction. We show that the resulting action preserves the ghost equation of the Landau gauge and reproduces the characteristic Gribov-type gauge propagator of the original Gribov scenario, a BRST-invariant mass term of the refined type being accommodated by the same mechanism. We establish the renormalizability of the model to all orders by means of algebraic renormalization techniques, no counterterm being required outside those already present in the classical action. We further show that the condensation of the quartet leaves unchanged the cohomology through which the observables are characterized, the operator realized in the displaced vacuum having the same cohomology as that of the symmetric phase, so that the mass scale is produced at no cost in the physical content of the theory, and the construction of genuine gauge-invariant observables in the Gribov--Zwanziger setting is thereby left open to the usual cohomological methods.

</details>


### [65] [From the Old to the New Starobinsky Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00401)
*Shinji Hirano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在精确背景上统一处理 Starobinsky 模型的迹反常（trace anomaly）驱动演化与 $R+R^2$（scalaron）表述，给出标量与张量谱的反常修正。修正由 $aN_*/(18b)$ 控制，观测倾斜仅需 $b/a\simeq35$，并给出张标比降低约 17% 的定量特征；伴随论文提供完整分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Starobinsky 原始模型（由共形场迹反常驱动）与其 $R+R^2$（scalaron）表述通常被分开处理，缺乏对二者背景联系的统一谱计算。该文旨在基于已知的背景连接，量化反常修正对可观测谱的影响。

Method: 在精确背景上计算标量与张量谱，得到反常修正由 $aN_*/(18b)$ 控制（$a$、$b$ 分别为 Euler 反常系数与 $R^2$ 系数，$N_*$ 为 pivot 退出视界后的 e-folds 数）；并以 scalaron 暴胀作为该参数趋于零的极限情形。另在 de Sitter 分析中考察 Weyl 耦合的有限取值范围。

Result: 在 $N_*=60$ 时，符合观测倾斜仅需 $b/a\simeq35$，远小于早期实现的 $b/a\gtrsim10^9$。在该基准下，标量谱比固定 $N_*$ 的 scalaron 暴胀更红，且固定倾斜时张标比（tensor-to-scalar ratio）约低 17%。对最小引力再加热，$N_*\simeq53$ 时，只要 $b/a\gtrsim35$，倾斜仍处于 Planck 值的 $1\sigma$ 内。de Sitter 分析中，Weyl 耦合的有限范围可排除额外实自旋二极点并保持因果响应衰减。

Conclusion: 迹反常修正以 $aN_*/(18b)$ 为控制参数，使 scalaron 暴胀成为其消失极限，并显著放宽对 $b/a$ 的要求。标量谱变红与张标比降低约 17% 构成反常的可观测定量特征；完整分析见伴随论文。

Abstract: Starobinsky's original model of inflation, driven by the trace anomaly of conformal fields, and its $R+R^2$ (scalaron) formulation are often treated separately. The original anomaly-driven evolution connects an early de Sitter phase supported by the Euler anomaly to a scalaron regime, where the observable modes leave the horizon. Building on this known background connection, we compute the scalar and tensor spectra on the exact background and find that the anomaly corrections are controlled by $aN_*/(18b)$, where $a$ and $b$ are the Euler-anomaly and $R^2$ coefficients and $N_*$ the number of e-folds after the pivot exits the horizon; scalaron inflation is recovered as this parameter vanishes. The observed tilt at $N_*=60$ then requires only $b/a\simeq35$, rather than the $b/a\gtrsim10^9$ of early realizations. At this benchmark the scalar spectrum is redder than in scalaron inflation at fixed $N_*$, and the tensor-to-scalar ratio is about 17% lower at fixed tilt, a quantitative signature of the anomaly. For minimal gravitational reheating, $N_*\simeq53$, the tilt remains within $1σ$ of the Planck value for $b/a\gtrsim35$. In the de Sitter analysis, a finite range of the Weyl coupling excludes an extra real spin-two pole and keeps the causal response decaying. A companion paper gives the full analysis.

</details>


### [66] [How AI Agents Discover Scientific Equations: From Hydrotope Rediscovery to New Water-Wave Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00435)
*Zihan Zhou,Digvijay Wadekar,Matias Zaldarriaga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究以水波顶（hydrotope）公式为受控案例，考察 AI 智能体（AI agents）发现与验证科学公式的能力。结果显示单提示重发现成功率低，而「PI＋两名学生」的多智能体工作流（PI+two-student workflow）可成功重发现完整公式，并在更难的负波数问题中发现新的六点振幅（six-point amplitude）解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解 AI 智能体如何发现并验证科学公式，特别是面对全局公式需识别各频率区域边界、组合指数级潜在活跃项的难题。作者通过水波顶这一「看似简单实则困难」的案例，检验提示（hint）与人类引导对智能体表现的影响。

Method: 方法基于人类—智能体协作重构该公式的原始发现过程，并分析 18 次单提示重发现（single-prompt rediscovery）运行，包括无提示及两类人类引导（false hint 与 true hint）。据此失败模式设计「PI＋两名学生」工作流，由协调主导智能体向两个研究智能体分配互补的解析与数值任务并独立评估结果。对照方法包括传统及 LLM 辅助符号回归（symbolic regression）与标准机器学习回归器。

Result: 在 18 次单提示重发现运行中，仅 4 次恢复出覆盖所有运动学腔室（kinematic chambers）的公式；多数失败运行虽找到正确的腔室多项式，却未能将其组合或检验完整定义域。传统与 LLM 辅助符号回归及标准机器学习回归器同样未能恢复全局公式。PI＋两名学生团队成功重发现完整的 hydrotope 公式；将同一工作流应用于更难的三负波数问题（three negative wavenumber problem）时，发现了六点振幅 A6 的新独立且经验证的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 结论表明，单智能体在全局公式的组合与全域验证上存在系统性失败，而分工协作与独立评估的多智能体工作流可有效克服此类困难，并具备推广至更难物理问题的潜力。

Abstract: We study how AI agents discover and validate scientific formulas using a controlled case study of the hydrotope, a recently discovered geometric formula that combines the different polynomial pieces of nonlinear surface-wave scattering into one global expression. This problem is deceptively difficult: simple formulas can hold within individual frequency regions, but the global result must identify their boundaries and combine exponentially many potentially active terms. We reconstruct how the formula was originally discovered through human--agent collaboration and analyze 18 single-prompt rediscovery runs under no hint and two forms of human guidance: a false hint representing an incorrect prior and a true hint representing domain-informed insight. Only four recover the formula across all kinematic chambers (i.e., regions in which a single polynomial form applies), while most unsuccessful runs find correct chamber polynomials but fail to combine them or test their full domain. Conventional and LLM-assisted symbolic regression and standard machine-learning regressors likewise fail to recover the global formula in our experiments. Guided by these failure modes, we test a PI$+$two-student workflow in which a coordinating lead agent assigns complementary analytic and numerical tasks to two research agents and independently evaluates their results. The PI$+$two-student team successfully rediscovers the complete hydrotope formula, while the same workflow applied to the harder three negative wavenumber problem discovers a new independent verified analytic expression for the six-point amplitude $A_6$.

</details>


### [67] [No-signaling-in-time condition for three-flavor neutrino oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00439)
*Massimo Blasone,Fabrizio Illuminati,Luciano Petruzziello,Kyrylo Simonov,Luca Smaldone*

Conclusion: 将强宏观实在论的时间无信号条件推广到三味中微子振荡并包含CP破坏相位，有助于在中微子振荡语境下对宏观实在论作更完整的刻画。

Abstract: Macrorealism is a fundamental concept in quantum foundations, capturing basic features of our everyday experience. Among the main tools introduced to test violations of macrorealism are the Leggett-Garg inequalities. These inequalities provide necessary and sufficient conditions for a certain data set including the correlators measured in a number of different experimental runs (weak version of macrorealism). Leggett-Garg inequalities have proved useful for detecting violations of macrorealism in neutrino oscillation experiments. However, it is interesting to investigate a set of necessary and sufficient conditions for strong macrorealism - no-signaling-in-time and arrow-of-time conditions - in order to obtain a more complete characterization of macrorealism in the context of neutrino oscillations. In particular, here we extend previous study of the no-signaling-in-time condition to the case of three-flavor neutrino oscillations, including the CP-violating phase of the Pontecorvo-Maki-Nakagawa-Sakata matrix.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络场论（neural network field theory）以网络架构与参数密度定义欧氏场论，但其构造性（constructive）基础尚待建立。该文旨在为这类网络测度提供严格的构造性框架，并检验其能否重现已知的有限体积标量 φ⁴₂ 测度。

Method: 按自由传播子的二进动量壳（dyadic momentum shell）为场分配独立神经元组，系数取高斯、相位均匀、频率取自该壳谱。固定频率与相位后，对单个壳系数做带有 Wick 有序四次相互作用重加权的高斯有限维积分。利用重加权密度的均匀对数凹性（uniform log-concavity）与逐尺度归纳（induction over scales）控制涨落及截断间边缘分布的全变差距离。

Conclusion: 该工作以动量壳组织的网络参数化逐壳积分（integrate out one shell at a time）为核心，为 φ⁴₂ 提供了构造性实现，并指出该方法不限于 φ⁴₂，有望推广至其他理论。

Abstract: Neural network field theory specifies a Euclidean field theory by a network architecture and a density on its parameters, so that correlation functions are integrals over those parameters. We introduce constructive neural network field theory and use it to realize finite-volume scalar $φ^4_2$ as a limit of network measures. For each dyadic momentum shell of the free propagator, the field is given a separate set of neurons with Gaussian coefficients, uniform phases, and frequencies drawn from the shell's spectrum. Once the frequencies and phases are fixed, integrating out one shell's coefficients is a finite-dimensional integral against a Gaussian density reweighted by the Wick-ordered quartic interaction. At any coupling in two and three dimensions, the reweighted density is uniformly log-concave for every shell above a threshold, and the fluctuations of those shell fields are held to the free-field scale. In two dimensions, for almost every draw of the frequencies and phases, an induction over scales shows that shell $j$ contributes $O(2^{-2j}j^3)$ to the log partition function, and the total-variation distance between marginals at successive cutoffs is of the same order. Because these contributions are summable, the ultraviolet cutoff can be removed for any fixed sequence of widths that grows fast enough with the shell index. Almost surely, the ultraviolet and infinite-width limits commute, and in either order the joint laws of finitely many smeared fields converge to those of the standard finite-volume $φ^4_2$ measure. The method of parametrizing a field via a network organized into momentum shells and integrating out one shell at a time is not specific to $φ^4_2$, so we expect it to extend to other theories.

</details>

Abstract: We derive the necessary and sufficient conditions for AdS$_4$ solutions of $d=11$ supergravity to preserve ${\mathcal{N}} \leq 3$ supersymmetry. We use our setup to first impose ${\mathcal{N}}=2$ supersymmetry, recovering a previously known class of solutions, providing a non-trivial consistency of our equations. We then focus on the ${\mathcal{N}} = 3 $ case, first deriving the Tri-Sasaki class and then proving its uniqueness as the only ${\mathcal{N}} = 3$ AdS$_4$ class in eleven dimensional supergravity. Our work proves that all supersymmetric AdS$_4$ solutions preserving ${\mathcal{N}} > 2 $ supersymmetry are necessarily of Tri-Sasaki type.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Infinite-distance limits play a central role in the Swampland program, but understanding their behaviour at intersections of boundary divisors is subtle, as it requires control over simultaneous limits of several moduli. To address this, we establish a necessary and sufficient criterion for a boundary stratum of arbitrary codimension in the complex-structure moduli space of Calabi-Yau manifolds to lie at infinite Weil-Petersson distance. In particular, intersections of infinite-distance boundary divisors necessarily remain at infinite distance. The result holds for the full Weil-Petersson metric and arbitrary trajectories, including those with unrestricted hierarchies in the saxions and axion winding. In compactifications of Type IIB string theory on Calabi-Yau threefolds, our proof is based on an inequality relating the Weil-Petersson length of a trajectory to the BPS mass associated with an auxiliary charge vector. If this mass tends to zero while the charge-to-mass ratio remains uniformly bounded, every trajectory approaching the limit has infinite length. Using asymptotic Hodge theory, we construct an auxiliary charge vector satisfying these conditions and derive the criterion directly from the limiting mixed Hodge structure associated with the degeneration. More generally, our result applies to polarized variations of Hodge structure of Calabi-Yau type. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We prove that the classical part of the Nicolai map for a supersymmetric free nonrelativistic particle moving on a Kähler manifold is linear in the Kähler metric and local for its velocity. Moreover, we give an exact expression for the one-loop correction of the map in terms of the Kähler potential and point out a direct way to higher loop order. This provides another example for the rare cases of exact Nicolai maps, and the first all-order one-loop result. As a check we have verified the Nicolai-map conditions to zero and one-loop order (free-action and determinant-matching). A graphical representation is given as well.

### [72] [Algebraic structure in holographic tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00478)
*Abhisek Sahu,Jeremy van der Heijden,Mark Van Raamsdonk,Rana Zibakhsh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用全息（holography）的张量网络（tensor network）玩具模型，检验前期关于广义纠缠楔（generalized entanglement wedge）与半经典极限下可观测量子代数（subalgebra）对应关系的假设，并探讨代数熵与广义引力熵（generalized gravitational entropy）的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作（2511.21852）提出：在一般量子引力理论中，广义纠缠楔可对应于半经典极限下的可观测量子代数，其熵满足单调性（monotonicity）与强次可加性（strong subadditivity）。本文旨在用可计算的张量网络模型对这些思想进行检验。

Method: 在全息张量网络玩具模型中，将广义纠缠楔的类比定义为“不存在包含它的、边界腿（boundary legs）数目更少的区域”。在大键维（bond dimension）极限（类比半经典极限）下，论证每个此类区域存在一个典范关联的子代数及一族酉相关的冯·诺依曼因子子代数（von Neumann factor subalgebras），并研究其交、并、交换子（intersections, joins, commutants）等代数运算与张量网络区域几何运算的对应关系。

Result: 研究了广义纠缠楔广义熵的单调性与强次可加性在多大程度上可由所构造代数熵的性质推出；引入另一熵量 S(ω||ω∘E_{A'})，在适当条件下亦满足单调性与强次可加性；并说明如何基于键维递增的无限族随机张量网络构造 type II_1 冯·诺依曼代数，使广义纠缠楔对应区域可关联为该代数的精确子代数。

Abstract: In a previous work 2511.21852, we explored the hypothesis that in general theories of quantum gravity, spacetime regions known as generalized entanglement wedges (introduced by Bousso and Penington) have an associated subalgebra of observables in a semiclassical limit. We defined an entropy associated with these subalgebras that obeys the same monotonicity and strong subadditivity properties as the generalized gravitational entropy of generalized entanglement wedges. Here, we test aspects of these ideas making use of tensor network toy models for holography. In this context, the analog of a generalized entanglement wedge is a region for which no region containing it has a smaller number of boundary legs. In the limit of large bond dimension (analogous to the semiclassical limit), we argue that each such region has a canonically associated subalgebra of the full algebra of observables on the boundary Hilbert space, as well as a family of unitarily related von Neumann factor subalgebras. We investigate in detail how various algebraic operations on these subalgebras (e.g. their intersections, joins, and commutants) are related to corresponding geometrical operations on the tensor network regions. We study to what extent the monotonicity and strong subadditivity properties of generalized entropy for generalized entanglement wedges follow from properties of our algebraic entropy. We also introduce an alternative entropic quantity $S(ω|| ω\circ E_{\mathcal{A}'})$ associated with a subalgebra $\mathcal{A}$ that also obeys monotonicity and strong subadditivity properties with suitable conditions. Finally, we explain how to define a type $II_1$ von Neumann algebra based on an infinite family of random tensor networks with increasing bond dimension such that the regions corresponding to generalized entanglement wedges can be associated with exact subalgebras of this full algebra.

</details>


### [73] [Twisted BPS decoupling limits and AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00482)
*Troels Harmark*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发起对"扭曲 BPS 解耦极限（twisted BPS decoupling limits）"的研究，其中相关 BPS 态沿几何上扭曲于底空间（base space）的圆纤维（circle fibers）携带动量；此类极限由非相对论 sigma 模型（non-relativistic sigma-models）与非洛伦兹几何（non-Lorentzian geometry）描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPS 解耦极限（BPS decoupling limits）此前已为引力与非微扰量子理论（non-perturbative quantum theory）带来深刻洞见，尤以 AdS/CFT 对应（AdS/CFT correspondence）为代表；本文旨在将这一研究推广至"扭曲"情形。

Method: 通过引入 twisted BPS decoupling limits，使相关 BPS 态沿几何上扭曲于底空间的圆纤维携带动量；并以 AdS$_5\times S^5$ 上的 IIB 型弦理论（type IIB string theory）为例，涉及整体对称代数（global symmetry algebra）PSU$(2,2|4)$ 的 BPS 态。同时引入 D-膜（D-branes）的类似极限，以探测与弦相同的辛结构（symplectic structure）。

Result: 一般而言，扭曲 BPS 解耦极限中的弦涨落（string fluctuations）由涌现的非洛伦兹靶空间（non-Lorentzian target spaces）上的非相对论 sigma 模型描述，该靶空间具有自然的辛结构；D-膜极限探测到与弦相同的辛结构。

Conclusion: 结果进一步支持 BPS 极限与非洛伦兹几何之间的广泛联系；对 AdS$_5\times S^5$，扭曲 BPS 解耦极限对偶于 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 超对称 Yang-Mills 理论（supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory）的 Spin Matrix 理论极限，从而为 AdS/CFT 的非微扰区域提供了可处理且显式的量子力学描述。

Abstract: BPS decoupling limits of string and M-theory have led to profound insights into gravity and non-perturbative quantum theory, most notably with the AdS/CFT correspondence. In this paper, we initiate the study of twisted BPS decoupling limits, in which the relevant BPS states carry momentum along circle fibers geometrically twisted over a base space. Prime examples are found in type IIB string theory on AdS$_5\times S^5$, for which the twisted limits involve the BPS states of the global symmetry algebra PSU$(2,2|4)$. In general, string fluctuations in a twisted BPS decoupling limit are described by non-relativistic sigma-models on emergent non-Lorentzian target spaces with a natural symplectic structure. This further supports the broader connection between BPS limits and non-Lorentzian geometry. We introduce analogous limits of D-branes as well, probing the same symplectic structure as the string. For AdS$_5\times S^5$, twisted BPS decoupling limits are dual to Spin Matrix theory limits of $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, providing tractable and explicit quantum-mechanical descriptions of non-perturbative regimes of AdS/CFT.

</details>


### [74] [Gaugino condensates in $\mathcal{N}=1$ super-Yang-Mills Theory on Eguchi-Hanson space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00486)
*Mohamed M. Anber*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用瞬子方法研究 Eguchi–Hanson 空间上 N=1 SU(N) 超杨–米尔斯理论中的规范微子凝聚。分数拓扑荷与 RP^3 边界的谱不对称性改变了瞬子计算；半荷瞬子仅含两个伴随零模，可直接贡献双线性项。对 SU(2) 中心完整和（holonomy）情形，集体坐标积分重现平空间值；对 SU(N>2) 的单块嵌入，非中心完整和需连续行列式以保持重整化群不变性，故仅零模不足确定双线性。另研究电荷1、SU(4) 双块鞍点，给出四点关联结果并讨论聚类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弯曲空间与具有非平凡边界的流形为研究非微扰瞬子效应和凝聚现象提供了与平空间不同的背景。核心动机是考察 Eguchi–Hanson 空间上分数拓扑荷与 RP^3 边界谱不对称性如何修正瞬子演算，并据此计算规范微子双线性凝聚。

Method: 采用瞬子方法（instanton methods）。通过分析分数拓扑荷、伴随微子零模数目、以及 Eguchi–Hanson 路径积分所构造的 RP^3 边界态，将观测量表述为边界扇区投影振幅。利用超对称 Ward 恒等式（supersymmetric Ward identities）论证手征关联函数与插入点及 Eguchi–Hanson 分辨率参数无关。对 SU(2) 与 SU(4) 鞍点分别进行集体坐标积分（collective-coordinate integral）。

Result: 对 SU(2) 瞬子、中心完整和情形，完整集体坐标积分在指定边界投影下给出 ⟨tr(λλ)⟩=16π^2Λ^3，重现平空间值。对 SU(N>2) 中的单个 SU(2) 块嵌入，非中心完整和需用连续行列式（continuum determinant）以保证重整化群不变性，零模本身不足以确定双线性。对电荷1、SU(4) 双块鞍点（含中心完整和、16 个玻色模、8 个伴随零模），积分给出 ⟨∏_{i=1}^4 tr(λλ)(x_i)⟩=(1/6)(16π^2Λ^3)^4；在位置无关性与聚类所需假设下，该结果确定相应边界扇区中双线性凝聚的四次方。

Conclusion: 弯曲 Eguchi–Hanson 背景与 RP^3 边界扇区投影、分数拓扑荷共同显著改变瞬子结构与凝聚贡献。同一扇区缺乏独立双线性计算，故因子 1/6 本身不构成聚类疑难（clustering puzzle）。结果表明零模计数与连续行列式效应在确定凝聚时性质不同，边界扇区投影对可观测量的定义至关重要。

Abstract: We study gaugino condensates in $\mathcal{N}=1$ $SU(N)$ super-Yang--Mills theory on Eguchi--Hanson space using instanton methods. Fractional topological charge and the spectral asymmetry of the asymptotic $\mathbb{RP}^3$ boundary modify the instanton calculus. A charge-$\frac12$ instanton carries only two adjoint gaugino zero modes and can therefore contribute directly to the bilinear. Because the Eguchi--Hanson path integral prepares a boundary state on $\mathbb{RP}^3$, the observables are boundary-sector-projected amplitudes. Moreover, supersymmetric Ward identities ensure that chiral correlators are independent of insertion points and the Eguchi--Hanson resolution parameter. For $SU(2)$ instanton, with central holonomy, the full collective-coordinate integral gives $\langle\operatorname{tr}(λλ)\rangle=16π^2Λ^3$ for a specified boundary projection, reproducing the flat-space value. For a single $SU(2)$-block embedding in $SU(N>2)$, the noncentral holonomy requires a continuum determinant for renormalization-group invariance, so zero modes alone do not determine the bilinear. We also study a charge-$1$ $SU(4)$ two-block saddle with central holonomy, $16$ bosonic moduli, and $8$ adjoint zero modes. Its collective-coordinate integral gives $\langle\prod_{i=1}^4\operatorname{tr}(λλ)(x_i)\rangle=\frac16(16π^2Λ^3)^4$. Under the assumptions required for position independence and clustering, this result determines the fourth power of the bilinear condensate in the corresponding boundary sector. Since no independent bilinear calculation is available in the same sector, the factor $1/6$ does not by itself constitute a clustering puzzle.

</details>

Result: 体MI的外延性足以使量子修正后的边界熵向量满足MI的单配性（monogamy）以及所有其他全息熵锥不等式；该构造重现了GFF预期的短距离发散结构，且流–流关联函数沿重整化群（renormalization-group）流为正值且单调递减。

Abstract: We construct an extensive model of bulk mutual information (MI) in anti-de Sitter spacetime and use it to describe the leading quantum corrections to the MI of certain holographic theories. The equivalence between bulk and boundary MI follows from the Faulkner--Lewkowycz--Maldacena prescription. We show that the extensivity of the bulk MI is sufficient for the quantum-corrected boundary entropy vector to satisfy monogamy of MI and all other holographic entropy cone inequalities. Our model is built from a covariantly conserved current-current kernel in AdS and is realized explicitly by a free massive Dirac fermion, whose boundary MI at long distances is that of a fermionic generalized free field (GFF). Abstract: The study of continuous spin particles, as general low-energy implications of relativity and quantum mechanics, has intensified in recent years with potential experimental signatures. In this note we show that these particles are absent in perturbative super-string theories, generalising a result in arXiv:1302.4771 to the supersymmetric case. This stands out as a general low-energy prediction of all perturbative superstring constructions.

</details>


### [79] [Antipodal Self-Duality for Generalized Fishnets from Integrability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00803)
*Lance J. Dixon,Claude Duhr,Francesca C. Fernandes,Victor Mishnyakov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文以方形鱼网积分（square fishnet integrals）在反对径自对偶（antipodal self-duality, ASD）变换下的性质为研究对象，利用基于可积性的行列式表示与对称多项式理论，构造出大量新的 ASD 量，并顺带给出方形鱼网积分满足 ASD 的另一证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD 是一种将振幅经运动学映射与符号字母反转（多重对数函数 Hopf 代数（Hopf algebra of multiple polylogarithms）的对极作用）后与自身相联系的奇特性质；此前仅在平面 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 超杨-米尔斯理论（super-Yang-Mills theory）的一个过程中被观察到，其物理起源未知，且在强形变版本中方形鱼网积分也呈现 ASD，因此有必要在其他情境中寻找 ASD 量。本文即在此动机下研究梯形积分（ladder integrals）多项式在保持方形鱼网不变的 ASD 变换下的行为。

Method: 以由划分（partitions）或杨图（Young diagrams）标记的梯形积分矩阵行列式作为基本构造单元，这些行列式自动满足 Steinmann 关系；采用此类行列式的基于可积性（integrability-based）的表示（本质上是矩阵模型积分（matrix-model integrals））以及对称多项式（symmetric polynomials）理论，寻找更多 ASD 量。

Result: 发现大量新的 ASD 量，表现为行列式的线性组合，其系数由划分的组合学（combinatorics of partitions）表达；作为副产品，获得方形鱼网积分满足 ASD 的另一种证明。

Conclusion: 在强大形变平面 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 理论的框架下，梯形积分行列式构成可系统构造 ASD 量的有效基础，ASD 现象的适用范围相比此前已知的单过程显著拓宽，并为方形鱼网积分的 ASD 提供了独立验证。

Abstract: Antipodal self-duality (ASD) is a remarkable property that relates an amplitude to itself, after performing a suitable kinematic map and reversing the letters of the symbol (more precisely, acting with the antipode of the Hopf algebra of multiple polylogarithms). ASD has been seen in only one process in planar $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory, and its physical origin is unknown, motivating a search for ASD in other contexts. Recently, ASD was found to hold for square fishnet integrals in a strongly-deformed version of planar $\mathcal{N}=4$. In this paper, we investigate the properties of polynomials in ladder integrals under the ASD transformation that leaves the square fishnet invariant. Our basic building blocks are determinants of certain matrices of ladder integrals, that are labeled by partitions or Young diagrams, and automatically obey the Steinmann relations. We employ integrability-based representations of such determinants, which are essentially matrix-model integrals, and the theory of symmetric polynomials to find many more ASD quantities -- linear combinations of determinants whose coefficients are expressed using the combinatorics of partitions. As a byproduct, we provide an alternate proof that square fishnet integrals obey ASD.

</details>

*Ritam Basu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 Jackiw-Teitelboim（JT）引力中 Hartle-Hawking 态在长度基（length basis）下随时间演化的 Wigner 函数负性（negativity），发现其在亚指数时间内被冻结、晚期趋近上界且无 ramp 结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wigner 函数负性可作为量子态经典可模拟复杂度的操作性度量，作者借此考察 JT 引力中混沌量子动力学的半经典对偶描述，并检验长度基是否适合作为对偶有效框架。

Method: 在长度基下，对 JT 引力中 Hartle-Hawking 态的时间演化进行分析，于圆盘（disk）层面及半经典参数 β 领头阶计算 Wigner 函数负性，并借助精确生存振幅与谱数据（离散谱、双边界虫洞贡献）进行对比。

Result: 圆盘层面领头阶下，Hartle-Hawking 态为静止最小不确定度高斯波包，位于 x=2log(2β/π)、⟨p²⟩=π²/8β，其 Wigner 函数为正，负性为 1+o(1) 且在亚指数时间内保持不变（时间反演下为 t 的偶函数，反射波包解耦后因自由演化为 Clifford shear 而完全冻结）；晚期负性饱和至接近上界 √(2/π)√d_eff(β)，其中 d_eff(β)=Z(β)²/Z(2β)；负性无 ramp，双边界虫洞贡献被 e^{-2S_0} 压低。精确生存振幅 Z(β+it)/Z(β) 给出按 σ_E²t² 增长的 spread complexity，种子归一化负性在圆盘层面等于精确生存概率的倒数。

Conclusion: 结果表明长度基非常适合用于大 e^{S_0} 条件下、亚指数时间尺度上混沌量子动力学的对偶半经典有效描述。

Abstract: The negativity of the Wigner function gives an operationally meaningful measure of the complexity of simulating a quantum state on a classical computer. We study its growth for the Hartle-Hawking state under time evolution in Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity, in the length basis. At the disk level, and to leading order in the semiclassical parameter $β$, the Hartle-Hawking state is a minimum-uncertainty Gaussian wavepacket at rest at the turning point of the Liouville wall, centred at $x=2\log(2β/π)$ with $\langle p^2\rangle=π^2/8β$. Its Wigner function is positive, so its negativity is $1+o(1)$ for all sub-exponential times; it is even in $t$ by time-reversal symmetry, and exactly frozen once the reflected packet decouples from the wall, because free evolution is a Clifford shear. At late times the negativity saturates close to its upper bound, at $\sqrt{2/π}\sqrt{d_{\rm eff}(β)}$ with $d_{\rm eff}(β)=Z(β)^2/Z(2β)$, a value that requires the discrete spectrum. Unlike the spectral form factor, the negativity displays no ramp: the two-boundary wormhole contribution is suppressed by $e^{-2S_0}$ relative to a disk term that, unlike that of the spectral form factor, does not decay. The exact survival amplitude $Z(β+it)/Z(β)$ gives the spread complexity growing as $σ_E^2t^2$, and the seed-normalised negativity is, at the disk level, the inverse of the exact survival probability. We take this as evidence that the length basis is ideally suited for a dual, semi-classical effective description of chaotic quantum dynamics for large $e^{S_0}$ at sub-exponential times.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶共振（dual resonance）传统上通过不同通道的极点展开重构散射振幅，但其解析结构尚未与弦相互作用的唯一性建立明确联系。该工作旨在引入更严格的解析条件，以刻画对偶共振的解析结构并考察其唯一确定弦振幅的能力。

Result: ADR 的一维配对关系，与整数平面谱、联合交叉-自旋界（joint crossing-spin bound）以及可实现无减除重构的复 Regge 衰变（complex Regge decay）相结合，唯一地固定了所有 n≥5 的标量弦振幅（scalar string amplitude）。ADR 预测的高维夹点关系在五点与六点得到检验。

Conclusion: 该结果将对偶共振的解析结构与弦相互作用的唯一性联系起来，表明 ADS 型解析条件可作为确定弦振幅的强约束，并提示高维夹点关系是进一步研究的方向。

Abstract: Dual resonance reconstructs a scattering amplitude from pole expansions in different channels. We propose analytic dual resonance (ADR), requiring that the complete leading pole sum at an integer resonance admit an absolutely convergent local contour integral. ADR yields one-dimensional pairing relations between residues in arbitrary triangulations. Together with an integer planar spectrum, a joint crossing-spin bound, and complex Regge decay permitting unsubtracted reconstruction, these relations uniquely fix the scalar string amplitude for every $n\ge5$. ADR also predicts higher-dimensional pinch relations, which we examine at five and six points. These results connect the analytic structure of dual resonance to the uniqueness of string interactions.

</details>


### [82] [The Love for Three Logarithms: Universal Running of Dynamical Love Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01152)
*Sebastian Garcia-Saenz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个与模型无关（model-agnostic）的框架，用于描述动力学（dynamical）层面潮汐勒夫数（Love numbers）的跑动（running），适用于任意静态球对称渐近平坦背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论（GR）中黑洞的静态潮汐勒夫数（static tidal Love numbers）为零，但在更一般理论或超出静态极限情形下通常非零，并随尺度呈对数跑动，因此需要统一的、不依赖具体模型的刻画方式。

Method: 基于频率 ω 的近区展开（near-zone expansion）与空间无穷远处的单值性（monodromy）。通过一个主递推公式（master recurrence formula）在 ω² 的各阶确定单值性生成元（monodromy generator），其三个独立矩阵元完全编码勒夫数的跑动。

Result: 该结构解释了重整化群方程（renormalization group equation）的二次性质，并被证明对任意类型勒夫数在所有阶成立。单值性形式澄清了对数系数的方案依赖性（scheme dependence），并表明潮汐场反常指数（即 Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi 形式中的“重整化角动量（renormalized angular momentum）”）是方案不变的，且可在任意时空直接计算而无需显式解。

Conclusion: 应用包括：给出 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞轴向勒夫数（axial Love numbers）领头动力学跑动的闭式公式，并证明其在极端极限（extremal limit）下消失；对超越 GR 的一般黑洞，证明动力学跑动可探测低于静态情形的多极（multipole）度规形变，并对 Hayward 与 Bardeen 正则黑洞（regular black holes）导出若干精确结果。

Abstract: The static tidal Love numbers of black holes vanish in general relativity (GR), but are generically non-zero, and in fact 'run' logarithmically with scale, in more general theories or beyond the static limit. We expose a model-agnostic framework for the running of Love numbers at the dynamical level, valid for any tidal perturbation on any static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat background, based on the near-zone expansion in powers of the frequency $ω$ and on the monodromy at spatial infinity. A master recurrence formula determines the monodromy generator to all orders in $ω^2$, whose three independent entries fully encode the running of the Love numbers. This structure explains the quadratic nature of the renormalization group equation, here proved to all orders and for any type of Love number. We discuss the scheme dependence of the logarithmic coefficients, which is made transparent by the monodromy form. In particular, this clarifies that the anomalous exponents of the tidal field, the so-called 'renormalized angular momentum' of the Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi formalism, are scheme-invariant and directly computable on any spacetime, without need of explicit solutions. We discuss several applications to black holes, including the derivation of a closed-form formula for the leading dynamical running of axial Love numbers of Reissner-Nordström black holes, proving in particular that it vanishes in the extremal limit. For generic black holes beyond GR, we prove that dynamical running probes deformations of the metric at lower multipoles than the static one, and we illustrate the power of the method by deriving some exact results for the Hayward and Bardeen regular black holes.

</details>


### [83] [Gravity and generalised geometry from a Lie 2-algebroid perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01292)
*Athanasios Chatzistavrakidis,Chris Hull,Larisa Jonke,Sylvain Lavau,Peter Schupp*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文借助微分分次（differential graded, dg）辛流形（symplectic manifold）框架，为广义联络（generalised connection）重新定义自然挠率（torsion）与曲率（curvature）张量，并由此重构闭弦（closed string）无质量模（massless modes）的有效作用量（effective action）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献在广义几何（generalised geometry）中定义挠率与曲率时存在结构性循环（structural circularity）与歧义（ambiguity）问题，阻碍了从广义几何一致地重建闭弦有效作用量。

Method: 以 Courant algebroid 的 dg 辛流形描述为起点，通过 split dg 流形的系统化程序，将广义 Lie 括号（generalised Lie bracket）纳入更大的 Lie 2-algebroid 结构，从而无歧义地定义挠率、Riemann 曲率张量及其 Bianchi 恒等式（Bianchi identities）；并考察满足广义度规兼容（generalised metric compatible）且(共)挠率固定的广义联络。

Result: 对任意用于分裂 dg 流形的普通联络选择，均存在唯一的广义度规兼容且(共)挠率固定的广义联络；由此得到的全部 Lie 2-algebroid 在 L_{\infty} 拟同构（quasi-isomorphisms）意义下等价；给定 Lie 2-algebroid 时，该唯一兼容联络在挠率的规范选择下，可由基于新广义 Ricci 张量（generalised Ricci tensor）的几何作用量重现闭弦有效作用量。

Conclusion: 该框架消除了既往方法的结构性循环与歧义，建立了广义几何量与闭弦无质量模有效作用量之间的明确对应，并表明不同分裂选择所得结构在 L_{\infty} 拟同构下等价。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of defining natural torsion and curvature tensors for generalised connections and of using them to reconstruct the effective action for the massless modes of the closed string from generalised geometry. The starting point is the description of a Courant algebroid as a differential graded (dg) symplectic manifold. A systematic procedure for split dg manifolds singles out a generalised Lie bracket as part of a larger Lie 2-algebroid structure, with the right properties to unambiguously define torsion and Riemann curvature tensors together with their corresponding Bianchi identities. This framework eliminates the structural circularity and ambiguity problems of previous approaches in the literature. We show that a unique generalised metric compatible generalised connection with fixed (con)torsion exists for any choice of ordinary connection that splits the dg manifold, while at the same time all Lie 2-algebroids obtained in this way are equivalent up to $L_{\infty}$ quasi-isomorphisms. For a given Lie 2-algebroid we demonstrate that the unique compatible generalised connection reproduces the effective closed string action from a geometric action based on the new generalised Ricci tensor upon making a canonical choice of torsion.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔红外辐射既是QCD现象学的实用要素，也是窥探规范理论长程结构的窗口。过去十年发展出以天球上的渐近对称性与二维共形场论刻画红外现象的新视角，本文旨在对这一进路的研究现状作简要综述。

Result: 对单次辐射（single emission），偶极型圈图贡献（dipole-like loop contributions）中的所有对数均可被吸收到强耦合常数（strong coupling）的标度选择中。对多次辐射（multiple emission），新出现的混合螺旋度天球算子乘积展开（mixed-helicity celestial operator product expansion）必须按能量分数加权，从而破坏全纯因子化（holomorphic factorisation），并妨碍连续共线极限（successive collinear limits）下的结合性（associativity）。

Conclusion: 这些结果展示了熟悉的红外QCD物理与天球上二维理论动力学之间的显著联系，表明红外现象可由天球共形场论框架统一刻画。

Abstract: Non-abelian infrared radiation can be seen as both a practical ingredient for QCD phenomenology and a window on the long-distance structure of gauge theories. In this contribution, we briefly review the status of this approach. We begin with the standard bulk picture, presenting the infrared factorisation of massless scattering amplitudes, and then we show how universal infrared information gets mapped to marked points on the celestial sphere. In particular, soft colour-dipole singularities are reproduced to all orders by a correlator of vertex operators in a free-boson theory on the sphere. We then proceed to study soft-gluon currents, and show how bulk results constrain the structure of the celestial theory beyond the free-field limit. For single emission, we note that all logarithms in dipole-like loop contributions can be absorbed into the choice of scale for the strong coupling. For multiple emission, we show how the emerging mixed-helicity celestial operator product expansion must be weighted by energy fractions, breaking holomorphic factorisation and obstructing associativity under successive collinear limits. These results illustrate a remarkable connection between the familiar physics of infrared QCD and the dynamics of a two-dimensional theory on the celestial sphere.

</details>

Method: 构造不同历史路径：使系统抵达相同的当前驱动条件，随后施加相同的未来协议（future protocol），并比较其弛豫行为，特别是倒数第二个缠绕扇区（penultimate winding sector）的驻留时间。

Conclusion: 全息超流体的非线性拓扑弛豫存在显著历史依赖性，其晚期弛豫时间可由携带至共同放大阶段的模态失衡统一刻画，而非由历史的具体形状决定。

Abstract: We study how driving history affects the nonlinear topological relaxation of a holographic superfluid. Different histories are constructed to reach the same present driving conditions and then follow the same future protocol. Although they relax to the same final winding sector, their late-time dynamics remain strongly history-dependent: the residence time of the penultimate winding sector changes by about a factor of \(3.7\). This variation is organised by the modal imbalance carried into the common amplification stage. Histories with different shapes but matched modal imbalance produce nearly identical relaxation times. The same qualitative behaviour is found for a second pair of competing modes.

</details>


### [86] [On-shell renormalization of sine-Gordon by the quantum inverse scattering method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01571)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子逆散射方法中，sine-Gordon 模型的连续极限与无穷体积极限只有在箱长被重新标度后才一致，本文旨在阐明这一重新标度所隐含的重整化含义，并显式刻画可积性与重整化之间的相互作用。

Method: 以箱长重新标度配合在壳条件（on-shell condition）定义重整化方案；将截断依赖性全部归入乘在箱长上的因子 $Z_L$，其指数由顶点算子（vertex operator）的标度维数固定；要求最轻的呼吸子（breather）具有线性化理论中玻色子的质量，以此确定质量参数的有限重整化。

Result: 孤子（soliton）与呼吸子质量由单值化算子（monodromy operator）的本征值给出，且表示为不跑动的参数之函数；当 $β\rightarrow0$ 时退化为经典与半经典结果；质量比可直接由正则化构造得到，而格点参数与物理能标之间的关系被吸收进 $Z_L$ 且无需计算。

Conclusion: 该方案是对理论的重新参数化（reparameterization），显式体现可积性与重整化的相互作用；作者将其与 Coleman 正规序化（normal ordering）及共形微扰论（conformal perturbation theory）比较，并认为它可推广至具有质量谱的超局域可积理论（ultralocal integrable theories），可能包括允许超局域格点正则化的渐近自由模型。

Abstract: In the quantum inverse scattering method, the sine-Gordon model is solved on a lattice, and its continuum and infinite-volume limits become uniform only after the length of the box is rescaled. We show that this rescaling, supplemented by an on-shell condition, defines a renormalization scheme. The whole dependence on the cut-off is absorbed into a factor $Z_L$ multiplying the length of the box, with an exponent fixed by the scaling dimension of the vertex operator; neither the field nor the coupling $β$ is renormalized, no subtraction scale is introduced, and the mass parameter receives a finite renormalization, fixed by requiring that the lightest breather has the mass of the boson in the linearized theory. The soliton and breather masses follow from the eigenvalues of the monodromy operator as functions of parameters that do not run, and reduce to the classical and semiclassical results as $β\rightarrow0$. In particular, the mass ratios are produced directly from the regularized construction, while the relation between the lattice parameters and the physical scale is absorbed into $Z_L$ and never needs to be computed. The scheme is a reparameterization of the theory which makes explicit the interplay between integrability and renormalization. We compare it with Coleman's normal ordering and with conformal perturbation theory, and we argue that it extends to ultralocal integrable theories with a massive spectrum, possibly including asymptotically free models that admit an ultralocal lattice regularization.

</details>


### [87] [Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet quintessential inflation: From super-inflation to emergent warm inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01665)
*Omar Chahboun,Rachid Ahl Laamara,El Hassan Saidi*

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-高斯-博内引力（Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity）耦合三标量场框架下，构建了统一描述暴胀（inflation）与晚期宇宙的模型，仅用单一势函数与耦合函数贯通宇宙历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在以统一框架弥合暴胀与晚期宇宙演化，并避免任意指定高斯-博内耦合（Gauss-Bonnet coupling），改由场内容自然决定耦合形式。

Result: 暴胀子涨落增长受浴剪切黏度（shear viscosity）调控，此调控对模型可行性至关重要；原本在冷暴胀情景被排除的四次势（quartic potential）变得可行，n_s 落在普朗克 1σ 区间且 r 被压低；与谱指数的符合具有普适性，而内部自洽性将模型限制于 (n-1)Q 的窄带。第三标量场在物质-辐射相等时期释放，以类跷跷板机制（seesaw mechanism）的逆向逻辑将极小质量尺度转为极大耦合。

Conclusion: 该统一模型在晚期以暴胀子充当暗能量（dark energy），实现了从暴胀到晚期加速膨胀的单一框架描述。

Abstract: This work presents a unified inflationary and late-time cosmology, developed within Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity coupled to three scalar fields, governed by a single potential and coupling function throughout cosmic history. Permitting the Gauss-Bonnet coupling to depend on the field content, rather than assigning it arbitrarily, produces a two-phase inflationary scenario: the higher-curvature term initially drives expansion and later becomes a subdominant. The initial super-inflationary stage ends dynamically when the geometrically induced stabilization of the waterfall field fails, at a field value determined by the combination $(n-1)Q$. Coherent oscillations of the waterfall condensate serve as the initial radiation source, replacing conventional reheating and establishing the thermal bath necessary for the subsequent warm phase. Utilizing a two-stage dissipation mechanism with $Γ_φ\propto T^3$, the scalar and tensor spectra are derived while retaining Gauss-Bonnet contributions, demonstrating that the growth of inflaton fluctuations is regulated by the shear viscosity of the bath, which is essential for model viability. A quartic potential, previously excluded in the cold scenario, becomes viable, with $n_s$ within the Planck $1σ$ band and $r$ suppressed. Agreement with the spectral index is generic, while internal consistency confines the model to a narrow band in $(n-1)Q$. Finally, the release of the third scalar near matter-radiation equality transforms a very small mass scale into a very large coupling by a logic inverse to the seesaw mechanism. At late times, the inflaton persists as dark energy.

</details>


### [88] [Tree-Level Interaction Vertices in the Linear Sigma Model with Quarks under Global Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01760)
*Luis A. Hernández,Eduardo Lazcano,R. Zamora*

TL;DR: 该文在线性sigma模型（linear sigma model）中推导了全局旋转（global rotation）背景下的树级相互作用顶点。旋转改变了动量空间结构，纵向动量守恒伴随角动量选择定则，横向动力学由含贝塞尔函数（Bessel functions）的径向形状因子描述，为旋转系统中微扰计算提供基础。

Abstract: We derive the tree-level interaction vertices of the linear sigma model with quarks in the presence of global rotation. Starting from the scalar and fermionic field solutions in a rotating background, we construct the corresponding spectral representations and use them to evaluate the mesonic self-interactions and Yukawa couplings of the model. We show that rotation modifies the usual momentum-space structure of the interaction vertices: conservation of longitudinal momentum is accompanied by angular-momentum selection rules, while the transverse dynamics is encoded in rotation-induced radial form factors involving products of Bessel functions. The resulting expressions provide a set of interaction rules adapted to the symmetries and finite geometry of the rotating system. Although derived within the linear sigma model with quarks, the structures obtained for scalar self-interactions and scalar-fermion couplings can be extended to a broader class of quantum field theories under global rotation. These results provide the building blocks for perturbative calculations and contribute toward a microscopic quantum-field-theoretical description of interacting matter in rotating environments.

### [89] [From Hidden Zeros to Exact Splittings and New Zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01775)
*Yang Li,Laurentiu Rodina*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将散射振幅（scattering amplitudes）中的隐零点（hidden zeros）提升为显式分裂公式，发现有序 Tr(φ³)、非线性σ模型（NLSM）与宇宙学波函数共享同一分裂结构，并据此发现五类新的全多重度零点族及连续零点族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐零点对散射振幅施加了超出因式分解（factorization）性质的约束，其结构尚待系统刻画。作者希望将隐零点由被动约束转化为组织完整振幅的工具，并借此寻找新的零点位置。

Method: 提出显式分裂公式 A = Σ_α R_α J_{α,L} J_{α,R} J_{α,out}，适用于任意隐零点；其中前因子 R_α 为隐零点轨迹的条件，体现逐项消失，其余因子为低点振幅或流（currents）。该结构统一适用于有序 Tr(φ³)、NLSM 振幅与宇宙学波函数。

Conclusion: 隐零点可被提升为组织完整振幅的精确分裂结构，该结构在多种理论中普适，并成为系统搜索新零点（包括连续族）的有效框架。

Abstract: Hidden zeros impose constraints on scattering amplitudes beyond their familiar factorization properties. We explore their structure in two related directions. We first promote hidden zeros to explicit splitting formulas that organize full amplitudes at generic kinematics. Ordered $\mathrm{Tr}(φ^3)$ amplitudes, nonlinear sigma model (NLSM) amplitudes, and cosmological wavefunctions share the same defining exact splitting structure $\mathcal A=\sum_αR_α\,
  {\mathcal J}_{α,L}{\mathcal J}_{α,R}
  {\mathcal J}_{α,\mathrm{out}}$, adapted to any hidden zero. Each prefactor $R_α$ is a condition of the hidden zero locus, so this decomposition manifests the hidden vanishing term by term. The remaining factors are lower point amplitudes or currents. Using these representations, we can systematically find new zero loci beyond the usual ones. In particular, we obtain five new all-multiplicity zero families. We also find continuous families of zeros: the coefficients of their defining equations can vary continuously while the amplitude remains zero.

</details>


### [90] [DSSYK, open ASEP/TASEP and 2d dilaton gravity at strong coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01790)

Main category: hep-th

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: TASEP 的堆增长表示（growth of heaps）为强耦合引力提供了新洞见，暗示其可被解释为一种涌现现象（emerging phenomenon）；ASEP 与 q-TASEP、堆模型之间的新对偶给出了有用的增长解释。

Abstract: We discuss the triality between stochastic growth models in the KPZ universality class, the DSSYK model, and low dimensional dilaton gravity. The TASEP model, which will be our primary focus, is identified with the strong-coupling limit of two-dimensional quantum gravity. We also mention the relation of cumulants with the Tutte polynomial $T(1,q)$ of the crossing graph dual to the chord diagram, which implies the relationship of the models considered with the $Q=0$ Potts model coupled with gravity. We further exploit the realization of all these models in terms of different versions of discrete and continuous random walks on two-dimensional manifolds, which parameterize the Hilbert spaces of the respective theories. The representation of TASEP as the growth model of heaps provides new insight into strong-coupling gravity, suggesting its interpretation as an emerging phenomenon. The new dualities between ASEP and q-TASEP models and heap models providing a useful growth interpretation have been developed.

</details>

*Mohsen Alishahiha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究有限串行 Krylov 扇区（Krylov sector）向具有渐近线性 Lanczos 系数（Lanczos coefficients）的精确共形尾部（conformal tail）的相干释放，推导出精确的有限截断占据核及其局部均匀移动截断极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 Krylov 空间中有限串行扇区与共形尾部之间的相干释放过程，并厘清尾部结构、有限扇区结构以及两者耦合对释放轮廓的各自贡献，同时探索其在热 AdS$_2$ 情形中的具体实现。

Method: 方法上，通过解析推导获得精确的有限截断占据核（occupation kernel）及其局部均匀移动截断极限，该极限的导数对应经过滤的边界发射振幅（boundary-emission amplitude）的模平方；并借助连分式（continued fraction）极点分析与微扰展开至二阶，讨论一站点与两站点族及弱耦合下的热 AdS$_2$ 实现。

Result: 结果显示，尾部决定滤波核与对数渡越尺度（logarithmic transit scale），而释放轮廓还依赖于有限扇区；早期边缘记录发射前的串行步数，晚期边缘反映慢共振、干涉以及合并极点带来的对数增强。一站点与两站点族可共享同一领头连分式极点却呈现不等价轮廓；在热 AdS$_2$ 弱耦合极限下，缀饰振子测度（dressed oscillator measure）趋近于两站点浅扇区加共形尾部，领头复极点可复现至二阶。

Conclusion: 结论表明，共形尾部与有限串行扇区共同塑造相干释放的时间轮廓，其早期与晚期边缘分别编码步数信息与共振、干涉效应；且在热 AdS$_2$ 框架下，弱耦合极限能够以两站点浅扇区衔接共形尾部的方式重现领头极点结构。

Abstract: We study coherent release from a finite serial Krylov sector into an exact conformal tail with asymptotically linear Lanczos coefficients. We derive an exact finite-cut occupation kernel and its locally uniform moving-cut limit, whose derivative is the squared modulus of a filtered boundary-emission amplitude. The tail sets the filtering kernel and logarithmic transit scale, while the release profile also depends on the finite sector. Its early edge records the number of serial steps before emission, whereas its late edge reflects slow resonances, interference, and logarithmic enhancements from merged poles. A one- and two-site family can share the same leading continued-fraction pole while exhibiting inequivalent profiles. We further consider a thermal AdS$_2$ realization in which, in the weak-coupling limit, the dressed oscillator measure approaches a two-site shallow sector followed by the conformal tail, with the leading complex pole reproduced through second order.

</details>


### [92] [A baby universe from a large family: booklet cosmology states and quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02168)
*Jingshu Dai,Binye Dong,Cheng Peng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将 AS² 的宇宙态构造（cosmological-state construction）推广至三个及以上的全息 CFT（holographic CFT），提出“小册子宇宙态”（booklet cosmological state），并用复高斯随机张量（complex Gaussian random tensor）模型证明三臂中任意两臂可近似恢复指定码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AS² 原有的宇宙态构造仅针对两个全息 CFT 的情形，本文旨在将其扩展到三个或更多 CFT，以刻画多页边界与中心闭合宇宙之间的全息映射。

Method: 每个 CFT 位于一个 AdS 页（AdS page）的渐近边界，所有页通过施加多路接合条件（multiway junction conditions）沿公共界面粘合；态由带多线性插入（multilinear insertion）的欧氏演化制备。在适当的重插入极限（heavy insertion limit）下，体几何（bulk geometry）中心形成闭合宇宙。将 AS² 的有效高斯假设（effective Gaussian assumption）推广为圆形复高斯随机张量，在平坦能量窗口（flat energy windows）中给出三部分 Haar 态（tripartite Haar state），每条臂对应一个边界 CFT 的网络分支。

Result: 在三页且三个输出希尔伯特空间维度（output Hilbert-space dimension）相等为 b 的最简例子中，维度为 K 的给定码（prescribed code）可从任意两臂近似恢复，当 K/b→0 时误差消失且恢复概率趋于 1。

Conclusion: 该结果暗示多页宇宙态中任意两臂即可承载足够信息以近似重建指定码，支持中心闭合宇宙与多边界 CFT 之间信息分布的全息图景。

Abstract: We propose an extension of the cosmological-state construction of Antonini, Sasieta and Swingle (AS$^2$) to three or more holographic CFTs. Each CFT resides on the asymptotic boundary of an AdS page, and all pages are glued along a common interface by imposing the multiway junction conditions. The associated states are prepared by Euclidean evolution with a multilinear insertion. In appropriate heavy insertion limit the bulk geometry develop a closed universe in the center and we refer the state in this limit as a booklet cosmological state. Extending the effective Gaussian assumption used in AS$^2$, we model the three-page insertion by a circular complex Gaussian random tensor, yielding a tripartite Haar state in flat energy windows. In this Gaussian model of the cosmology-to-boundary map, each arm is a network branch associated with one boundary CFT. In the simplest example of three pages with three equal output Hilbert-space dimension $b$, we show that a prescribed code of dimension $K$ is approximately recoverable from any two arms, with vanishing error and high probability as $K/b\to0$.

</details>


### [93] [Leading gravitational dressing of operators and states in de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02209)
*Steven B. Giddings,Zi-Yue Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在牛顿常数领头非平凡阶，构造了de Sitter（dS）空间中的引力修饰可观测量（gravitationally dressed observables）。核心结论是：底层场论可观测量须为dS不变，方可被引力修饰；该修饰可通过求解与约束对易条件、借助特定格林函数（Green functions）实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力理论中一般协变可观测量需满足约束对易条件，而在dS背景上如何系统构造此类“修饰”可观测量尚不清晰。本文旨在给出可修饰性的判据，并显式构造dS情形下的引力修饰算符。

Method: 在牛顿常数领头非平凡阶展开；要求底层场论可观测量满足dS不变性（dS invariance），并求解修饰后可观测量与约束对易的条件；据此以特定格林函数（Green functions）构造修饰算符，重点给出位于时间对称切片上的可观测量所需格林函数。

Result: dS不变性是“可引力修饰”的必要条件。以产生两标量粒子态的算符为例，在大质量极限下两粒子呈对跖（antipodal）分布，其修饰可显式构造；相关关联函数（correlators）重现了Schwarzschild-dS解的线性化版本。

Conclusion: 此类dS不变可观测量可被视为关系型（relational）量，并可推广至具有观察者属性的系统，从而同样被引力修饰；文中还简要讨论了修饰可观测量与alpha真空（alpha vacua）的联系，以及更完整构造与其作用等遗留问题。

Abstract: We describe construction of gravitationally dressed observables in de Sitter (dS) space, to leading non-trivial order in Newton's constant. We show that for an underlying field theory observable to be "gravitationally dressable," it must be dS invariant. If it satisfies this condition, its gravitational dressing can be constructed in terms of certain Green functions, by solving the condition that the dressed observable commute with the constraints. We provide a construction of the Green functions relevant for dressing observables lying on a time-symmetric slice. A simple example of a dS invariant observable is an operator creating a two-particle state of scalars. In the large mass limit, the particles are shown to be antipodally located, and the dressing of this operator (or corresponding state) can be explicitly constructed. Its behavior is diagnosed by certain correlators involving this state, and we check explicitly that these correlators then reproduce a linearized version of the Schwarzschild-dS solution. The underlying dS-invariant observables may be thought of as relational, and we argue can be generalized to include systems with properties of observers, which then could be likewise gravitationally dressed. We also briefly describe how such dressed observables can be related to alpha vacua, and discuss other questions regarding a more complete construction and role of such operators.

</details>


### [94] [An Algorithm for Generating All Berglund-Hubsch-Type Calabi-Yau Orbifolds and Their Mirrors. Classification of Maximal Admissible Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01957)
*Sergei Aleshin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构造任意 Berglund-Hubsch 型模型所对应的全部 Calabi-Yau 轨形（Calabi-Yau orbifold）及其镜像对（mirror pairs）的算法，并证明其正确性；同时给出极大容许群的分类方法，并以 Fermat 型 Calabi-Yau 三维流形（threefold）为例完成分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注 Berglund-Hubsch 型模型所关联的 Calabi-Yau 轨形及其镜像对的系统性构造问题，并力求为定义轨形的群给出显式生成元（explicit generators），以支持此类模型的分类研究。

Method: 提出构造与任意 Berglund-Hubsch 型模型相关的全部 Calabi-Yau 轨形及其镜像对的算法，并证明算法正确性；该方法可为定义相应轨形的群给出显式生成元。同时提出极大容许群（maximal admissible groups）的分类方法，并将其应用于任意 Fermat 型（Fermat-type）Calabi-Yau 流形。

Result: 所提算法被证明正确，并可产出定义相应轨形的群的显式生成元；极大容许群的分类方法可用于任意 Fermat 型 Calabi-Yau 流形。作为示例，文中分类了 Fermat 型 Calabi-Yau 三维流形的全部极大容许群。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Berglund-Hubsch 型模型对应的 Calabi-Yau 轨形与镜像对提供了系统性构造工具，并建立了极大容许群的分类路径；其实例化结果表明该方法在 Fermat 型 Calabi-Yau 三维流形上具有可操作性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an algorithm for constructing all Calabi-Yau orbifolds and their mirror pairs associated with an arbitrary Berglund-Hubsch-type model, and prove its correctness. In particular, the proposed method yields explicit generators for the groups defining the corresponding orbifolds. The method for classifying maximal admissible groups is proposed. We illustrate the application of the classification approach to an arbitrary Fermat-type Calabi-Yau manifold. In addition, as an illustrative example, we classify all maximal admissible groups of Fermat-type Calabi-Yau threefolds.

</details>

### [95] [A Non Commutative Grauert Theorem and Fourier Mukai Duality for Generalized Complex Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01945)
*Patrick Antweiler,Jonathan Block*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一类弯曲微分分次（curved differential graded）非交换 Fréchet 代数上 Grauert 高阶相容性定理的推广，并借此将 Fourier-Mukai 演算拓展至新的导出范畴语境，最终证明对偶广义复环面的导出范畴等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grauert 高阶相容性定理（higher coherence theorem）原有适用范围有限，限制了 Fourier-Mukai 演算在更多导出范畴（derived categories）情形中的应用。作者希望把该框架推广到弯曲微分分次非交换 Fréchet 代数，以覆盖更广泛的几何与形变背景。

Method: 对一类弯曲微分分次（非交换）Fréchet 代数证明 Grauert 高阶相容性定理的推广，并利用非交换版本的 Poincaré 线丛（Poincaré line bundle）构造相应的 Fourier-Mukai 演算。

Result: 借助上述推广，Fourier-Mukai 演算被扩展至许多新语境下的导出范畴；并以非交换 Poincaré 线丛证明了广义复环面（generalized complex tori）对偶之间的导出范畴等价。

Conclusion: 该工作为广义复环面上广义复膜（generalized complex branes）范畴奠定了理论基础。其适用例子包括复环面、辛环面（symplectic tori）及其非交换形变与 B-场（B-field）形变。

Abstract: We prove a generalization of Grauert's higher coherence theorem for a class of curved differential graded (non-commutative) Fréchet algebras. This allows us to extend the Fourier-Mukai calculus to derived categories arising in many new contexts. We then apply it and prove equivalence of derived categories of dual generalized complex tori using a non-commutative version of the Poincaré line bundle. This lays the foundation for categories of generalized complex branes on generalized complex tori. Examples include complex tori, symplectic tori as well as their non-commutative and B-field deformations.

</details>

### [96] [EP-Flow: Disordered Crystal Structure Prediction without Site-Level Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01315)
*Qiuliang Liu,Liming Wu,Qi Li,Zhonglong Peng,Chang Chen,Xiaolong Chen,Wenbing Huang,Shifeng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用占据分布矩阵（Occupancy Distribution Matrix, ODM）统一描述无序晶体结构，并设计熵多面体流（Entropic Polytope Flow, EP-Flow）框架，在仅给定化学式的条件下实现无序晶体结构预测（CSP），性能优于改造后的有序晶体生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在有序晶体结构预测上进展迅速，但许多功能材料本质无序，其性质受取代混合、空位或间隙物种控制。既有晶体生成器或假设位点占据确定，或依赖通常缺失的位点级无序标注，难以在化学式为唯一输入时工作。

Method: 以ODM作为连续化的“位点—物种”表示，统一有序晶体、固溶体、空位无序与间隙占据；有效ODM需满足位点占据、质量守恒与非负性耦合约束，样本落在依赖化学式的运输多面体（transportation polytope）上。提出EP-Flow，即边缘约束流匹配（flow matching）框架：将异构多面体规范化到共享的双中心空间，学习保边缘的流，并通过Sinkhorn逆映射恢复可行占据。

Result: EP-Flow联合生成占据、分数坐标与晶格参数，在基于COD与MPDS构建的化学式条件无序CSP基准上取得最新最优（state-of-the-art）性能，显著优于适配的有序晶体生成器。

Conclusion: 分析表明EP-Flow能够恢复稀疏且具化学意义的局部无序模式，而非仅匹配全局成分统计，说明其在无序晶体结构预测中具备捕捉真实无序结构特征的能力。

Abstract: Generative models have made rapid progress in ordered crystal structure prediction, yet many functional materials are intrinsically disordered, with substitutional mixing, vacancies, or interstitial species controlling their properties. Existing crystal generators either assume deterministic site occupations or require site-level disorder annotations, which are often unavailable when the chemical formula is the primary input. We formulate disordered crystal structure prediction through an Occupancy Distribution Matrix (ODM), a continuous site-by-species representation that unifies ordered crystals, solid solutions, vacancy disorder, and interstitial occupancy. A valid ODM must satisfy coupled site-wise occupancy, mass-conservation, and non-negativity constraints, placing each sample on a formula-dependent transportation polytope. We propose Entropic Polytope Flow (EP-Flow), a marginal-constrained flow matching framework that canonicalizes heterogeneous polytopes into a shared double-centered space, learns a marginal-preserving flow, and recovers feasible occupancies through a Sinkhorn inverse map. By jointly generating occupancies, fractional coordinates, and lattice parameters, EP-Flow achieves state-of-the-art performance on formula-conditioned disordered CSP benchmarks derived from COD and MPDS, substantially outperforming adapted ordered-crystal generators. Analyses further show that EP-Flow recovers sparse and chemically meaningful local disorder patterns rather than merely matching global composition statistics.

</details>


### [97] [In-context Learning of Single-index Targets: Comparing Kernel and Feature Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01712)
*Haotian Gu,Yizhou Xu,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在单指标任务（single-index tasks）族上比较两种单层注意力（attention）架构，利用复制方法（replica method）推导其记忆与泛化误差，刻画预训练规模、任务池多样性与上下文长度对非线性上下文学习（in-context learning, ICL）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ICL 理论多聚焦线性目标函数（linear target functions），对非线性情形缺乏系统理解。本文旨在通过对比两类架构，揭示架构选择与数据集属性如何共同决定非线性 ICL 的表现。

Method: 在同一族单指标任务上比较两种单层注意力架构：核学习器（kernel learner）先用固定非线性特征映射（feature map）再施加线性注意力；特征学习器（feature learner）直接对原始输入做注意力，后接可学习的非线性读出（readout）。采用复制方法（replica method）推导二者的记忆与泛化误差，并纳入预训练规模、任务池多样性与训练/推理上下文长度的影响。

Result: 理论预测在广泛区域内与数值实验高度吻合。分析给出相图（phase diagrams），刻画随预训练数据量、任务多样性与上下文长度变化时各架构的优劣区间；并识别出两种学习器在上下文长度标度（context-length scaling）上的定性差异。

Conclusion: 架构选择与数据集属性之间存在显著交互作用，共同支配非线性上下文学习的行为；该结果有助于厘清架构设计在非线性 ICL 中的作用机理。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables a pretrained model to infer a task from demonstrations without updating its parameters. While much of the existing theory focuses on linear target functions, in this paper we study nonlinear cases by comparing two one-layer attention architectures on the same family of single-index tasks. A kernel learner first maps inputs through a fixed nonlinear feature map and then applies linear attention, whereas a feature learner applies attention to the original input, followed by a learned nonlinear readout. We derive predictions for their memorization and generalization errors using the replica method, retaining the effects of pretraining size, task-pool diversity, and training and inference context lengths. The resulting predictions closely match numerical experiments across a broad range of regimes. Our analysis yields phase diagrams that characterize when each architecture is advantageous as the amount of pretraining data, task diversity, and context lengths vary. We further identify qualitatively different context-length scalings for the two learners. Together, these results clarify how architectural choices interact with the dataset and govern nonlinear in-context learning.

</details>

### [98] [Analysis of grid instabilities in particle-in-cell codes based on a meshfree approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02052)
*J. M. Finn,E. G. Evstatiev*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分析等离子体动理学（plasma kinetic theory）中粒子模拟（particle-in-cell, PIC）代码网格/混叠（aliasing）不稳定性的新方法，从无网格（meshfree）线性化出发，经网格离散化导出动力学矩阵（dynamical matrix），并研究其稳定性、混叠来源与增长率标度，结果与PIC模拟吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIC代码中网格离散化会引入混叠（aliasing）不稳定性，其产生机制与稳定性判据尚需系统分析。本文旨在建立从无网格方程到网格离散的统一线性化框架，以刻画此类不稳定性。

Method: 以 Np 个宏粒子（macroparticles）的无网格方法为起点，在均匀格点（lattice）上的均匀、无漂移平衡态附近线性化，并对宏粒子位置施加扰动。随后在 Ng 个网格点上离散化线性方程，分别采用动量守恒（momentum conserving, MCP）与能量守恒（energy conserving, ECP）两种格式，得到冷静止等离子体的动力学矩阵，并分析其稳定性、色散关系与混叠性质。

Result: ECP格式下动力学矩阵为对称正定（symmetric and positive definite, SPD），稳定性可直接判定；MCP格式下矩阵既非对称亦非正定，无法直接得出稳定性结论。两种格式的矩阵元均相对无网格值发生偏差，即混叠所致，且该偏差随粒子格点相对网格的位移呈周期性。MCP离散中，一般格点位置下特征值以复共轭对出现，对应非负增长率。混叠性质与网格上的梯形法则（trapezoidal rule）误差相关。研究了线性增长率随 Ng 及每格粒子数 Nppc 的标度，解析结果与PIC模拟高度一致。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了PIC网格不稳定性的混叠来源及其与梯形法则误差的联系，并给出MCP与ECP的稳定性差异；文中还简要讨论了与冷漂移束（beam）的联系，即两种格式下的网格诱导不稳定性。

Abstract: A new method of analyzing grid, or aliasing, instabilities is described, in particle-in-cell (PIC) codes for plasma kinetic theory. It starts with a meshfree approach with $N_{p}$ macroparticles, with $N_{p}<\infty$. Linearization of these equations is done about a uniform density non-drifting equilibrium prescribed by macroparticles on a uniform \emph{lattice}. Macroparticle positions perturbations are induced about this lattice. The resulting linear equations are analyzed for stability and dispersion relations. The linearized equations are then discretized on a grid of $N_{g}$ points with $N_{g} \le N_{p}$, in both momentum conserving (MCP) and energy conserving (ECP) formulations. For a cold stationary plasma, this leads a \emph{dynamical matrix}. For ECP, the matrix is symmetric and positive definite (SPD) and immediately shows stability. For MCP, the dynamical matrix is neither symmetric nor positive definite, preventing immediate conclusions on stability. For both MCP and ECP and for a cold stationary plasma, the resulting matrix elements vary relative to the meshfree value, a result of aliasing. This deviation is periodic in the displacement of the particle lattice relative to the grid. In the MCP discretization, eigenvalues occur in complex conjugate pairs for general placement of the particle lattice relative to the grid. These conjugate pairs indicate nonnegative growth rates. The nature of the aliasing is studied and shown to be related to the trapezoidal rule error over the grid. The scaling of the linear growth rates with respect to $N_{g}$ and especially with respect to $N_{ppc}$ is studied. These analytical results are compared with PIC simulations and found to be in excellent agreement. The connection with a cold drifting \emph{beam} is discussed briefly, specifically, grid-induced instabilities for both the MCP and ECP.

</details>

### [99] [Smooth perturbation theory and generic Cantor spectrum for multi-dimensional monotone quasiperiodic operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00733)
*Ilya Kachkovskiy,Leonid Parnovski,Roman Shterenberg*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文研究多维准周期薛定谔算子（quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators），在单调锯齿型势（monotone sawtooth-type potentials）的微扰局域化区间（perturbative localization regime）中证明：某个谱隙（gap）闭合当且仅当经由自然谱隙标号（gap labeling）对应的本征函数在原点处取零值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画此类算子谱的拓扑结构，即判定谱隙何时闭合、谱是否成为康托集（Cantor set），并理解不同谱隙在闭合难易程度上的差异。

Method: 采用微扰局域化区间下的分析，引入谱隙与对应的自然谱隙标号（gap labeling），将谱隙闭合问题归约为相应本征函数在原点是否消失的条件。

Result: 证明谱隙闭合的充要条件；由此得出对一般（generic）采样函数（sampling function）f，谱为康托集且所有可能的谱隙均打开；同时给出某些谱隙闭合的一类函数f，并观察到部分谱隙较其他谱隙更难闭合。

Conclusion: 谱隙闭合可完全由本征函数在原点的取值判定，据此可对一般采样函数给出全开谱隙的康托谱结论，并揭示谱隙闭合难易的不一致性。

Abstract: We consider multi-dimensional quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators with monotone sawtooth-type potentials in the perturbative localization regime. We prove that a given gap is closed iff a corresponding (via a natural gap labeling) eigenfunction vanishes at the origin. Using this, we show that for a generic choice of the sampling function $f$, the spectrum of such an operator is a Cantor set, with all possible gaps being open. We also provide a class of functions $f$ for which some gaps are closed. Finally, we observe that certain gaps are more difficult to close than the others.

</details>


### [100] [Inverse spectral results for the magnetic Schrödinger operator in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01479)
*Devon Farrell,Anna Holloway,Nikhil Savale*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维与二维半经典磁薛定谔算子（semiclassical magnetic Schrödinger operator）的谱决定性问题，证明一维偶势与二维径向磁场分别由其对应的薛定谔算子谱唯一决定，并由此验证了 Guillemin–Wang 提出的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作针对半经典磁薛定谔算子中"谱是否决定位势/磁场"这一逆谱问题（inverse spectral problem）展开，旨在推广 Guillemin–Wang 的既有结果并回答其文中所提出的猜想。

Method: 研究对象为一维与二维空间中的半经典磁薛定谔算子；一维情形考虑偶位势（even potential），二维情形考虑纯磁算子下的径向磁场（radial magnetic field），通过谱分析手段建立谱量与位势/磁场之间的对应关系。

Result: 一维情形下，偶位势可由半经典薛定谔算子的谱唯一确定；二维情形下，径向磁场可由纯磁薛定谔算子（purely magnetic Schrödinger operator）的谱唯一确定。

Conclusion: 两项谱决定性结果共同扩展了 Guillemin–Wang 的先前工作，并证实了其在文中提出的猜想，表明在对称性约束下磁场相关的谱反演问题具有肯定答案。

Abstract: We consider the semiclassical magnetic Schrödinger operator in dimensions one and two. The first result is that an even one dimensional semiclassical potential is spectrally determined by the Schrödinger operator. The second result states that a two dimensional radial magnetic field is spectrally determined by a purely magnetic Schrödinger operator. This extends the earlier work of Guillemin-Wang and answers a conjecture of theirs therein.

</details>

### [101] [Stability and instability analysis of resonance-induced nonlinear bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01875)
*Jackson C. Turner,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文研究一维聚焦三次非线性薛定谔/格罗斯-皮塔耶夫斯基方程（NLS-GP），其中线性算子势为紧支撑。针对由线性共振（透射共振或散射共振）诱导的非线性束缚态，建立了其在分岔点附近轨道稳定性与不稳定性的精确判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明线性反射系数的纯虚零点或下半平面纯虚极点会诱导非线性束缚态，且这些态在严格正的 L²(R) 激发阈值处分岔，区别于从点谱诱导、在零 L² 范数处分岔的束缚态。本文动机在于确定这类共振诱导态在分岔点附近的稳定性条件，填补该稳定性理论的空白。

Method: 基于紧支撑势 H_V=-∂_x²+V(x) 的一维聚焦三次 NLS-GP 方程，在分岔点附近对共振诱导非线性束缚态进行轨道稳定性与不稳定性的精确判据推导；并结合数值模拟（numerical simulations）探索理论未覆盖的参数区域及长时间动力学。

Result: 得到共振诱导非线性态轨道稳定的充分条件：i) 底层线性（散射或透射）共振频率足够小；ii) 对应线性共振本征模严格为正。数值模拟进一步考察了理论无法覆盖的区间，以及稳定与不稳定共振诱导非线性束缚态附近初值的长时间动力学行为。

Conclusion: 本文给出了共振诱导非线性束缚态在分岔点附近的轨道稳定性/不稳定性精确判据，并证明在共振频率足够小且线性共振本征模严格为正时态是轨道稳定的；数值结果补充了理论未及的动力学区域。

Abstract: We study the focusing one-dimensional cubic nonlinear Schrödinger / Gross--Pitaevskii equation (NLS-GP), where the potential of the underlying linear Schrödinger operator, $H_V=-\partial_x^2+V(x)$, is compactly supported. In \cite{turner2026resonance} the authors proved that purely imaginary zeros (transmission resonances) or purely imaginary poles in the lower half plane (scattering resonances) of a reflection coefficient of $H_V$ seed branches of {\it resonance-induced nonlinear bound states}, and that these states bifurcate at a strictly positive $L^2(\mathbb R)$ excitation threshold. This is in contrast to nonlinear bound states which bifurcate at zero $L^2$-norm from point spectra of $H_V$ (corresponding to poles in the upper half plane). In this paper we establish precise criteria for the nonlinear orbital stability and instability of the resonance-induced states near the bifurcation point. A corollary is that resonance-induced nonlinear states are orbitally stable if i) the underlying linear (scattering or transmission) resonance frequency is sufficiently small, and ii) the corresponding linear resonance eigenmode is strictly positive. Numerical simulations are presented to explore regimes not accessible to our theory, and to explore the large time dynamics for initial conditions near stable and unstable resonance-induced nonlinear bound states.

</details>
