### [102] [Closed-form evaluation of the cyclic four-electron atomic correlation integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00036)
*Jonas Matuzas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作给出了Bonham 1965分类中case V（四电子循环Slater积分）在正指数下的有限实表达式，权重不超过三；一般情形为十八个仅含对数、二重对数与三重对数的显式求和，等指数时坍缩为权重三的周期常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类四电子Slater积分（Slater integral）属量子化学多中心积分，此前可能缺乏无需根式、广义多重对数（generalized polylogarithm）、围道规定或未求极限的有限实表达式，故需给出封闭且数值可验证的解析结果。

Method: 对正指数α₁…α₄求值Bonham分类中的case V循环拓扑积分，构造由十八个块组成的显式和，仅使用对数（logarithm）、二重对数（dilogarithm）与三重对数（trilogarithm），其自变量为指数的有理函数，并借助等指数特例、三条独立数值路线、精确二进（dyadic）求值与已发表标定值进行验证。

Result: 一般结果为十八块显式和；等指数时得I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)⁴λ⁻⁴[Li₃(1/4)/2−7ζ(3)/24+π²log2/12−2log³2/3]，为仅分歧于2与3的ℚ上权重三周期；三条独立数值路线在四个指数组合上至少32位小数一致，精确二进评估给出八个100位证书，三个已发表四电子标定值逐位复现。

Conclusion: 证明小指数与大指数极限律（含闭式系数）、二面体对称性（dihedral symmetry）以及四个指数三种不等价循环排列的严格重排律；公式仅适用于四循环，不评估两种带弦（chorded）拓扑。

Abstract: For positive exponents $α_1,\ldots,α_4$ we evaluate the cyclic four-electron Slater integral, the topology recorded as case V in Bonham's 1965 classification, in a finite real expression of weight at most three. The general answer is an explicit sum of eighteen blocks involving only logarithms, dilogarithms and trilogarithms of rational functions of the exponents; all logarithm arguments are positive and all nonconstant polylogarithm arguments may be placed in $(-1,1)$, so no radical, generalized polylogarithm, contour prescription or unevaluated limit is required. At equal exponents the value collapses to $I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)^4λ^{-4}[\mathrm{Li}_3(1/4)/2-7ζ(3)/24+π^2\log 2/12-2\log^3 2/3]$, a weight-three period over $\mathbb{Q}$ ramified only at 2 and 3. Three independently implemented numerical routes agree at four exponent tuples to at least 32 decimal places, an exact-dyadic evaluation of the classical formula gives eight 100-place certificates, and three published four-electron calibration values are reproduced to every printed digit. We also prove the small- and large-exponent limit laws with closed-form coefficients, dihedral symmetry, and a strict rearrangement law for the three inequivalent cyclic placements of four exponents. The formula applies to the four-cycle and does not evaluate the two chorded topologies.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum Reverse Hypercontractivity for KMS-Symmetric Quantum Markov Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00242)
*Jinsong Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 构造了在 p=1/2 处 p-对数 Sobolev 常数消失、且 p→0⁺ 时趋于 −∞ 的本原 KMS-对称量子 Markov 半群反例，阻碍 (0,1) 上一致反向超压缩速率；同时给出 1/2<p≤q<1 的量子反向超压缩性正结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察 KMS-对称量子 Markov 半群（KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroup）在反向超压缩性（reverse hypercontractivity）方面是否具有统一的正向速率，即 p-对数 Sobolev 常数（p-logarithmic Sobolev constant）在低 p 区间是否有界。

Method: 构造本原 KMS-对称量子 Markov 半群实例，分别使 p=1/2 处的 p-对数 Sobolev 常数消失、以及在相关图模型（graph-based）例子中令 p→0⁺ 时该常数趋于 −∞；并结合退相干扰动（dephasing perturbation）与图论条件（连通图、n≥3）进行分析。

Result: 反例表明 (0,1) 上不存在一致的正反向超压缩速率；但本原 KMS-对称量子 Markov 半群在 1/2<p≤q<1 均满足量子反向超压缩性并具显式充分时间；对任意 δ>0，足够强的退相干扰动使 [δ,1] 上成立一致正对数 Sobolev 不等式（logarithmic Sobolev inequality），从而在 δ<p≤q<1 具量子反向超压缩性；连通图（n≥3）的图基 KMS-对称量子 Markov 半群在 δ<p≤q<1 亦成立反向超压缩性。

Conclusion: 低 p 区间（尤其 p→0⁺ 与 p=1/2）存在本质性障碍，无法期望 (0,1) 上一致的正反向超压缩速率；但在中高 p 区间（1/2<p≤q<1）以及经足够强退相干扰动或特定图结构条件下，量子反向超压缩性可以成立。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant vanishes at $p=1/2$ and a correlated graph-based example for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant tends to $-\infty$ as $p\to 0^+$. These examples obstruct a positive uniform reverse-hypercontractivity rate on $(0,1)$. By contrast, we show that every primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroup satisfies quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $1/2< p\leq q < 1$ with explicit sufficient times. For each fixed $δ>0$, a sufficiently strong dephasing perturbation satisfies a uniform positive logarithmic Sobolev inequality on $[δ, 1]$ and hence quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $δ< p\leq q<1$ with fixed $0<δ<1/2$. Furthermore, we establish reverse hypercontractivity for graph-based KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for $δ< p\leq q< 1$ with fixed $0<δ<1$ for connected graphs when $n \geq 3$.

</details>


### [104] [Complete Factorial Asymptotics and Integrable Structure of Generalized Charlier Recurrence Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00396)
*Mahouton Norbert Hounkonnou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究广义 Charlier 多项式（generalized Charlier polynomials）在离散权 ρ_μ(k)=μ^k/(k!)^2 下的首一递推系数，给出偏差 β_n−n 与 μ−γ_n 的完整阶乘渐近，并通过 Bessel 比较与 Hankel 行列式的扇区分解确定了精确连接公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画广义 Charlier 多项式首一递推系数（monic recurrence coefficients）β_n、γ_n 相对于平凡尺度 n、μ 的偏差渐近行为。该问题涉及偏差是否超越所有代数阶以及其出现的精确尺度。

Method: 采用参数化 β_n−n=√μ c_n c_{n+1}、γ_n=μ(1−c_n^2)，将问题化为满足离散 Painlevé II 方程（discrete Painlevé II equation）的正序列 (c_n)；在 c_n=0 附近线性化得到离散 Bessel 方程（discrete Bessel equation），其退缩解为 J_n(2√μ)，并结合 Bessel 比较与基于 Hankel 行列式扇区分解（sector decomposition）的整体分析。另辅以把 Hankel 行列式解释为离散对数气体（discrete logarithmic gas）的方法。

Result: 证明 c_n/J_n(2√μ) 收敛到有限极限 L(μ)，并精确识别 L(μ)=1，从而得连接公式 c_n∼J_n(2√μ)；由此得到 β_n−n∼μ^{n+1}/(n!(n+1)!)、μ−γ_n∼μ^{n+1}/(n!)^2。同时证明 Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n−1)/2}T_n(μ) 的分解与 T_n(μ)=1+Σ_{q=1}^{n}S_q(μ)，其中扇区贡献与 n 无关，且 S_q(μ)∼e^μ μ^q/(q!)^2。

Conclusion: 偏差 β_n−n 与 μ−γ_n 超越所有代数阶，出现在 μ^n/(n!)^2 尺度上。Bessel 比较与扇区分解共同揭示出连接离散 Painlevé 动力学、Toda 形变（Toda deformations）与 Hankel-分拆表示（Hankel–partition representations）的丰富可积结构。

Abstract: We study the monic recurrence coefficients of the generalized Charlier polynomials associated with the discrete weight
  \[
  ρ_μ(k)
  =
  \frac{μ^k}{(k!)^2},
  \qquad
  k\in\mathbb N_0,
  \qquad
  μ>0.
  \]
  We establish complete factorial asymptotics for the deviations $β_n-n$ and $μ-γ_n$, proving that they are beyond all algebraic orders and appear at the scale $μ^{n}/(n!)^2$. The key step is a parametrization $β_n-n=\sqrtμ\,c_nc_{n+1}$, $γ_n=μ(1-c_n^2)$, which reduces the problem to a positive sequence $(c_n)$ satisfying a discrete Painlevé II equation.
  Linearization around $c_n=0$ yields a discrete Bessel equation whose recessive solution is $J_n(2\sqrtμ)$. We prove that the ratio $c_n/J_n(2\sqrtμ)$ converges to a finite limit $L(μ)$, which we identify exactly as $L(μ)=1$ by combining Bessel comparison with a global analysis based on the sector decomposition of the associated Hankel determinant. This yields the exact connection formula
  \[
  c_n \sim J_n(2\sqrtμ), \qquad n\to\infty,
  \]
  and consequently the precise asymptotics
  \[
  β_n-n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{n!(n+1)!}, \qquad
  μ-γ_n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{(n!)^2}.
  \]
  We further develop a complementary approach based on Hankel determinants interpreted as a discrete logarithmic gas. We show that the Hankel determinant factorizes as $Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n-1)/2}T_n(μ)$ and admits a sector decomposition $T_n(μ)=1+\sum_{q=1}^{n}S_q(μ)$ with sector contributions independent of $n$. We establish $S_q(μ)\sim e^μμ^q/(q!)^2$, providing an independent derivation of the factorial corrections. Together, the Bessel comparison and sector decomposition reveal a rich integrable structure linking discrete Painlevé dynamics, Toda deformations, and Hankel--partition representations.

</details>


### [105] [On spectrum of the harmonic Kronig-Penney model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00879)
*J. Gibbons,A. P. Veselov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文借助“穿衣链（dressing chain）”理论研究量子力学 Kronig-Penney 模型（Kronig-Penney model）的谐振修正形式，给出了特殊情形下基态（ground state）的显式表达，并证明所有谱隙（spectral gap）长度相等，其端点可表示为相应 Weber-Hermite 函数（Weber-Hermite functions）的零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源自穿衣链（dressing chain）理论，该理论为考察周期势（periodic potential）模型的谱结构提供了新的切入点，促使作者研究 Kronig-Penney 模型的谐振修正形式的谱性质（spectral properties）。

Method: 采用穿衣链（dressing chain）理论框架，对量子力学 Kronig-Penney 模型的谐振修正形式进行谱分析，并在特殊情形下构造基态（ground state）的显式解。

Result: 在特殊情形下，相应基态（ground state）可显式给出；所有谱隙（spectral gap）具有相同长度，其端点由相应 Weber-Hermite 函数的零点表示。

Conclusion: 谐振修正的 Kronig-Penney 模型在特殊情形下呈现等长谱隙（spectral gap）与显式可解的基态，其谱结构可通过 Weber-Hermite 函数的零点刻画，体现了穿衣链（dressing chain）方法在该类模型中的适用性。

Abstract: We study the spectral properties of the harmonic modification of the quantum mechanical Kronig-Penney model motivated by the theory of the dressing chain. In particular, we show that in the special cases the corresponding ground states can be given explicitly and all spectral gaps have the same length with the ends expressed as zeros of the corresponding Weber-Hermite functions.

</details>


### [106] [The Buchholz Algebra from the Universal Resolvent Algebra--Particle Structure, Gibbs States, and Closed-Path Expansions without Fock Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01622)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在不依赖福克表示（Fock representation）的前提下构造 Buchholz 代数（Buchholz algebra），将其实现为规范固定预解代数（gauge-fixed resolvent algebra）的内禀粒子截断商（particle-cutoff quotients）的有界逆极限（bounded inverse limit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的 Buchholz 代数描述依赖福克空间（Fock space）框架，该文旨在给出不依赖福克表示的纯代数构造，以拓展其表述基础。

Method: 以带有等距粒子数坐标（isometric particle-degree coordinates）的方式，取规范固定预解代数的内禀粒子截断商的有界逆极限；并在其有限矩阵角（finite matrix corners）上引入典范迹（canonical traces）、KMS 态（KMS states）与迹范数乘积公式（trace-norm product formulas），配合闭路展开（closed-path expansions）。

Result: 有限矩阵角上存在典范迹、KMS 态和迹范数乘积公式；闭路展开为路径积分表示（path-integral representations）奠定基础。已知的福克空间描述与一个恰当的准局域子代数（quasilocal subalgebra）作为实现（realizations）出现。

Conclusion: Buchholz 代数可在无福克表示的框架下由逆极限构造得到，且福克空间描述与准局域子代数均为其特例实现，闭路展开为进一步构建路径积分表示提供了出发点。

Abstract: We construct the Buchholz algebra without a Fock representation, as the bounded inverse limit of intrinsic particle-cutoff quotients of the gauge-fixed resolvent algebra, with isometric particle-degree coordinates. Its finite matrix corners carry canonical traces, KMS states, and trace-norm product formulas, together with closed-path expansions that lay the groundwork for path-integral representations. The known Fock-space description and a proper quasilocal subalgebra arise as realizations.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum hierarchies of the ILW and BO equations and the DR cycles on the moduli spaces of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01841)
*Alexandr Buryak,Abner Hernandez Rodriguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子 Intermediate Long Wave（ILW）方程层级（hierarchy）的特定极限与 Nazarov--Sklyanin 层级之间的关系，提出一项猜想并给出多种验证以支持该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 P. Rossi 与第一作者借助任意上同调场论（cohomological field theory）及稳定曲线模空间上的 double ramification（DR）cycles 构造了一大类量子层级，其中 ILW 方程层级的量子化是其特例。ILW 具有 KdV 与 Benjamin--Ono（BO）两个著名极限，而 BO 情形下已有 Nazarov--Sklyanin 量子层级，因此需要厘清二者关系。

Method: 基于已有的 DR cycles 构造框架，考察量子 ILW 层级的一个极限，并将其与 Nazarov--Sklyanin 层级进行比较，进而提出描述二者关系的猜想，同时给出多种检验。

Result: 提出了一项关于量子 ILW 层级之极限与 Nazarov--Sklyanin 层级之间关系的猜想，并给出了支持该猜想的多种验证（various checks）。

Conclusion: 量子 ILW 层级的一个极限与 Nazarov--Sklyanin 层级之间存在猜想性联系，摘要所列的多项验证结果为该猜想提供了支持，但并未在摘要中给出证明。

Abstract: In an earlier paper of P. Rossi and the first author, a construction of a wide class of quantum hierarchies was presented, with the input data given by an arbitrary cohomological field theory. A crucial role in the construction is played by the double ramification (DR) cycles on the moduli spaces of stable curves. As a special case, one gets a quantization of the hierarchy of the Intermediate Long Wave (ILW) equation. The ILW equation has two famous limits: the Korteweg--de Vries equation and the Benjamin--Ono (BO) equation, and for the latter one a quantum hierarchy was constructed by Nazarov and Sklyanin. In our paper, we propose a conjecture describing a relation between a limit of the quantum ILW hierarchy and the Nazarov--Sklyanin hierarchy and present various checks to support it.

</details>


### [108] [Quadratic symmetry algebras and Sturm algebraization of superintegrable systems with magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01869)
*Shams Ara,Md Fazlul Hoque,Ian Marquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究非零轴对称磁场中的两个三维量子超可积系统，揭示其在原哈密顿量、抛物分离后及分离前 Sturm 表示三个层面分别呈现二次代数、sl(2) 与 sl(3) 代数化结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究多可分超可积系统在不同求解阶段如何涌现不同的隐藏代数结构与精确或准精确可解性。

Method: 利用圆抛物坐标分离变量，将分离方程化为具有限多项式扇区的六次双合流 Heun 方程，并构造以物理能量为参数的 Sturm 本征值问题；经规范变换实现 sl(2) 代数化并化为有限三对角矩阵，同时构建分离前的 Sturm 表示并分析其积分代数。

Result: 两个模型的积分生成带 Casimir 的二次代数，有限维变形振子表示给出代数谱，且与分离变量结果一致；分离后方程经规范变换约化为 sl(2) 代数化，分离前固定轴向导数后约化变量与规范旋转使双变量 Sturm 哈密顿量及其非中心积分属于 U(sl(3))，两者归为同一奇异 2:1 振子正规形但有效参数嵌入不同。

Conclusion: 该工作给出三种互补的代数描述，说明不同隐藏代数结构与精确或准精确可解性可在多可分超可积系统的不同阶段出现，且共同的 sl(3) 结构并不意味着原始磁场系统等价。

Abstract: We study two three-dimensional quantum superintegrable systems in nonvanishing axially symmetric magnetic fields. For each model the integrals generate a quadratic algebra with a Casimir, and finite-dimensional deformed-oscillator representations yield algebraic spectra. The same spectra are recovered by separation of variables. In circular parabolic coordinates the separated equations are sextic biconfluent-Heun equations with finite polynomial sectors. We formulate them as Sturm eigenvalue problems with the physical energy as a parameter and the separation constant diagonalized. After gauge transformation, each equation admits an $sl(2)$ algebraization and reduces to a finite tridiagonal matrix. We also construct a pre-separation Sturm representation of each complete Hamiltonian, taking the inverse-square coupling as the Sturm eigenvalue. The transformed integrals remain commuting and, after fixing the axial integral, changing variables and gauge rotating, the two-variable Sturm Hamiltonian and its non-central integrals belong to the universal enveloping algebra $U(sl(3))$. We thus obtain three complementary algebraic descriptions: the quadratic-algebra/deformed-oscillator representation of the original Hamiltonian, the $sl(2)$ algebraization after parabolic separation, and the $sl(3)$ algebraization of the complete Sturm problem before separation. Although the two magnetic Hamiltonians are distinct in the original classification, fixing the axial integral reduces them to the same singular 2:1 oscillator normal form with different effective parameter embeddings. This explains the common $sl(3)$ structure without implying equivalence of the original magnetic systems. These results show how distinct hidden algebraic structures and exact or quasi-exact solvability can emerge at different stages of a multiseparable superintegrable system.

</details>


### [109] [Superintegrability of a resonant ghostly tri-Hamiltonian model: polynomial first integrals and transcendental completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01901)
*Andreas Fring,Ian Marquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究一个具有不定动能（indefinite kinetic energy）的共振三维哈密顿系统（resonant three-dimensional Hamiltonian）及其三种泊松描述（Poisson descriptions）的经典与量子超可积性（superintegrability）。结果显示该系统在多项式与有理数类中为最小超可积，而在容许超越积分（transcendental integrals）的不变开集上为最大超可积，并给出其量子实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注同一流（flow）的多个泊松描述下超可积性（superintegrability）的刻画问题，特别是含不定动能的三维共振哈密顿系统。其动机在于厘清多项式、有理与超越（transcendental）三类首次积分（first integrals）对系统功能秩（functional rank）的不同贡献，并探讨其量子对应。

Method: 方法上通过构造三个对合二次哈密顿量（commuting quadratic Hamiltonians）并识别两个四次原始生成元（degree-four generators）$I_4^{\rm o}$、$I_4^{\rm e}$（动量宇称分别为奇、偶）来刻画多项式首次积分环（polynomial first-integral ring）。求解完整首次积分方程以确定有理首次积分的函数依赖性；对三种泊松实现实施 Weyl 量子化（Weyl quantisation）以得到精确高阶微分对称性（higher-order differential symmetries）。

Result: 结果指出，单一的八次关系（octic relation）使多项式不变量环的超越次数（transcendence degree）为四；所有有理首次积分均可表示为 $H_1,H_2,H_3,I_4^{\rm o}$ 的有理函数，故有理不变量域（rational invariant field）的泛型功能秩亦为四。无分支（branch-free）超越不变量在不变开集 $Δ>0$ 上将泛型光滑功能秩提升至五。无分支第五经典不变量亦具有稠定非局域量子对应（densely defined nonlocal quantum counterpart）。

Conclusion: 结论为：该系统在多项式与有理类中属最小超可积（minimally superintegrable），在容许超越积分时于不变域上属最大超可积（maximally superintegrable）。Weyl 量子化给出三种泊松实现的精确高阶微分对称性，且超越完备化（transcendental completion）具有具体的量子实现。

Abstract: We study classical and quantum superintegrability of a resonant three-dimensional Hamiltonian with indefinite kinetic energy and three Poisson descriptions of the same flow. Besides three commuting quadratic Hamiltonians, the polynomial first-integral ring contains two primitive degree-four generators, $I_4^{\rm o}$ and $I_4^{\rm e}$, of odd and even momentum parity. A single octic relation leaves the polynomial invariant ring with transcendence degree four. Solving the complete first-integral equation shows that every rational first integral is a rational function of $H_1,H_2,H_3,I_4^{\rm o}$, so that the rational invariant field likewise has generic functional rank four. A branch-free transcendental invariant raises the generic smooth functional rank to five on the invariant open set $Δ>0$. The system is therefore minimally superintegrable in the polynomial and rational classes and maximally superintegrable on this invariant domain when transcendental integrals are admitted. Weyl quantisation yields exact higher-order differential symmetries in all three Poisson realisations. The branch-free fifth classical invariant also admits a densely defined nonlocal quantum counterpart, providing a concrete quantum realisation of the transcendental completion.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Transferable Graph Neural Network Surrogates for Molecular Dynamics Across Crystal Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00100)
*Judah Immanuel,Avik Mahata,Aniruddha Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可迁移的图神经网络（graph neural network, GNN）代理框架，直接预测原子位移并传播原子构型，无需显式力评估或数值时间积分；同一架构在 FCC 铝、BCC 铁与 HCP 镁上实现约 10^-4 Å^2 量级的位置预测误差与纳秒级稳定自回归传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心目标是验证统一的 GNN 表述能否在不做对称性特定修改的前提下，表征晶体对称性与配位环境存在本质差异的多种材料中的原子动力学。为此选取具有不同元素与晶格类型的 FCC、BCC、HCP 体系作为检验对象。

Method: 采用同一网络架构、特征表示、图构建方式与训练协议，不引入晶格专用表示；以直接预测原子位移的 GNN 代理替代显式力评估与数值时间积分，并进行自回归构型传播。

Result: 在三类体系中获得约 10^-4 Å^2 量级的位置预测误差，支持稳定自回归传播至纳秒时间尺度；预测轨迹保持热力学稳定性、径向分布函数（radial distribution function）中的特征配位壳层结构，以及温度依赖的均方位移（mean-squared displacement）行为。框架还捕捉到 BCC 铁中密集相邻的配位壳层及 HCP 镁的各向异性配位环境。

Conclusion: 直接基于 GNN 的原子传播可被构造为跨元素、晶格对称性与配位几何的可迁移框架，为加速分子动力学（molecular dynamics）的通用代理模型提供了路径。

Abstract: We present a transferable graph neural network (GNN) surrogate framework for molecular dynamics (MD) that directly predicts atomic displacements and propagates atomistic configurations without explicit force evaluation or numerical time integration. The central objective of this work is to establish whether a common GNN formulation can represent atomic dynamics across materials with fundamentally different crystal symmetries and coordination environments. The same network architecture, feature representation, graph construction, and training protocol are applied without symmetry-specific modification to face-centered cubic (FCC) aluminum, body-centered cubic (BCC) iron, and hexagonal close-packed (HCP) magnesium. Across these distinct elemental and crystallographic systems, the framework achieves position-prediction errors on the order of 10^-4 Angstrom^2 and supports stable autoregressive propagation to nanosecond time scales. The predicted trajectories preserve thermodynamic stability, characteristic coordination-shell structure in the radial distribution functions, and temperature-dependent mean-squared-displacement behavior. In particular, the framework captures the closely spaced coordination shells of BCC iron and the anisotropic coordination environment of HCP magnesium without introducing lattice-specific representations. These results demonstrate that direct GNN-based atomic propagation can be formulated as a transferable framework across different elements, lattice symmetries, and coordination geometries, providing a pathway toward generalizable surrogate models for accelerated molecular dynamics.

</details>


### [111] [Thermodynamic origins of multimode spinodal decomposition in multicomponent alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00351)
*Pravan Omprakash*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以二元相互作用参数与规则溶体自由能的成分约束曲率（composition-constrained curvature）为工具，探讨多元合金为何通常只沿单一赝二元（pseudobinary）模式发生调幅分解（spinodal decomposition），以及何种相互作用排布可产生两个独立不稳定方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元合金中调幅分解只有一个自由能下降的成分方向，而多元合金理论上存在更多方向，却往往仍以单一赝二元模式共同分离；其成因及实现两个独立不稳定方向的条件尚不清晰。

Method: 基于二元相互作用参数（binary interaction parameters）与规则溶体自由能（regular-solution free energy）的成分约束曲率分析，对等摩尔 BCC 四元合金（equimolar BCC quaternaries）在 500 K 下统计不稳定模式数目，并构造特定相互作用排布（如平衡排斥三角形、吸引-排斥对）；同时结合简化相场模型（phase-field models）与相特异性筛选（phase-specific screen）。

Result: 在 500 K 的等摩尔 BCC 四元合金中，单一不稳定模式最为常见，仅 15.2% 存在至少两个不稳定模式；异类元素相互混合的倾向有助于解释这种单一模式偏好。特定相互作用排布（包括平衡排斥三角形与吸引-排斥对）可保证两个下降方向；当两方向强度相近时，简化相场模型产生局部成分各异的宽泛织构（texture），而非两个化学区块。相特异性筛选识别出五种模式近乎平衡的候选合金，其中 Cu-In-Ir-Ti 为一个示例。

Conclusion: 研究建立了以基本二元相互作用倾向为依据、通过调幅分解工程化微观结构（microstructures）的理论框架，并指出近平衡双模式合金可作为产生宽泛局部固溶体织构的设计途径。

Abstract: In a binary alloy, spinodal decomposition has one composition direction in which the free energy can fall. A multicomponent alloy has more directions available, yet its elements usually separate together in a single, pseudobinary mode. Why does this happen, and what would allow two independent directions to become unstable? We examine these questions using binary interaction parameters and the composition-constrained curvature of the regular-solution free energy. Across equimolar BCC quaternaries, one unstable mode is the most common outcome at 500 K, while 15.2% have at least two. The tendency of unlike elements to mix helps explain the preference for one mode. We then show how particular arrangements of interactions, including a balanced repulsive triangle and an attractive-repulsive pair, guarantee two downhill directions. When those directions have similar strengths, simplified phase-field models produce a broad texture of locally different solid solutions rather than two chemical blocks. A phase-specific screen identifies five provisional alloys with nearly balanced modes, with Cu-In-Ir-Ti providing an example of this possibility. Thus, we establish a theoretical framework for engineering microstructures via spinodal decomposition derived from basic binary interaction tendencies.

</details>


### [112] [Stacking-Controlled Altermagnetism and Topological Magnons in Bilayer CrI$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00479)
*Lu Xiao,Meng-Han Zhang,Dao-Xin Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 以双层 CrI₃ 为代表性平台，第一性原理计算与线性自旋波理论揭示堆叠构型可调控交错磁（altermagnetic）序及其拓扑磁振子（topological magnon）激发，多体格林函数方法进一步量化磁振子能量修正与寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 堆叠范德华磁体（van der Waals magnets）为交错磁性（altermagnetism）及伴随的拓扑磁激发提供了可调平台；本文旨在探明堆叠几何如何控制层间磁基态及相关磁振子能带拓扑与输运响应。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory），结合能带表示分析（band-representation analysis）和与非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）相关的动态结构因子（dynamic structure factor）计算；并用多体格林函数（many-body Green's-function）方法处理磁振子–磁振子相互作用。

Result: 双层 CrI₃ 呈现受堆叠控制的交错磁序与磁振子能带拓扑，可识别手性劈裂的拓扑磁振子（chirally split topological magnons）；层间磁基态对堆叠几何高度敏感，从而调控磁振子能带拓扑与输运响应；磁振子–磁振子相互作用重整化磁振子色散与动态结构因子，并伴随磁振子衰变（magnon decay）。

Conclusion: 结果确立堆叠工程（stacking engineering）作为调控交错磁性及拓扑磁振子激发的结构途径，为堆叠可控的自旋电子学（spintronics）与磁子学（magnonics）器件开辟方向。

Abstract: Stacked van der Waals magnets provide a tunable route to altermagnetism, a compensated magnetic order characterized by momentum-dependent spin splitting, and to the topological magnetic excitations that such order can host. In bilayer CrI$_3$, first-principles calculations and linear spin-wave theory reveal stacking-controlled altermagnetic order and associated magnon band topology. Combining band-representation analysis with calculations of the dynamic structure factor relevant to inelastic neutron scattering, we further characterize the chirally split topological magnons and quantify their energy corrections and lifetimes using a many-body Green's-function approach. The interlayer magnetic ground state is highly sensitive to the stacking geometry, thereby controlling the magnon band topology and transport responses. We further show that magnon--magnon interactions renormalize the magnon dispersion and dynamic structure factor, with a particular focus on magnon decay. Using van der Waals bilayer CrI$_3$ as a representative platform, our results establish stacking engineering as a structural route for tuning altermagnetism and associated topological magnon excitations, opening avenues toward stacking-controlled spintronic and magnonic devices.

</details>


### [113] [Orbital angular momentum in crystalline solids: non-equilibrium theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00496)
*Sangeeta Sharma,Peter Elliott,Samuel Shallcross*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文建立周期性固体中轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum, OAM）的通用算符理论，统一描述平衡态与相干驱动态，并在能隙石墨烯中发现光致准稳态OAM等新颖现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨道磁性理论主要针对平衡态，缺乏能同时处理平衡与相干驱动（coherently driven）固体的统一OAM算符框架，因而需要建立更普适的算符理论。

Method: 采用Adams与Blount的晶体动量表象（crystal momentum representation），构造与对称化 $\hat{\mathbf r}\times\hat{\mathbf v}$ 可观测量严格对应的周期性固体OAM算符，并证明其可分解为局域环流（local circulation, LC）与巡游环流（itinerant circulation, IC）两个厄米且满足 $U(1)$、更一般地 $U(N)$ 规范协变的扇区。

Result: 在平衡态下，LC与IC扇区退化为已有的现代轨道磁性理论（modern theory of orbital magnetism）；在非平衡态下，同一算符结构揭示由激光写入的动量空间织构（momentum-space textures）与带间相干（interband coherence）产生的新贡献。应用于能隙石墨烯（gapped graphene）时，发现丰富的超快非平衡OAM现象，包括准稳态的光致OAM。

Conclusion: 该工作表明带流形相位织构（band-manifold phase texture）与非平衡OAM密切相关，为固体内光的OAM调控以及拓扑能带结构（topological band structures）在OAM材料设计中的应用提供了新可能。

Abstract: We develop a general operator theory of orbital angular momentum (OAM) in periodic solids that applies equally to equilibrium and coherently driven states. Employing the crystal momentum representation of Adams and Blount, we construct a periodic-solid OAM operator exactly corresponding to the symmetrized $\hat{\mathbf r}\times\hat{\mathbf v}$ observable, and show that it separates into local circulation (LC) and itinerant circulation (IC) sectors that are Hermitian and $U(1)$, and more generally $U(N)$, gauge covariant. In equilibrium, the LC and IC sectors reduce to the established modern theory of orbital magnetism; out of equilibrium, the same operator structure exposes new contributions generated by laser-written momentum-space textures and interband coherence. Applied to gapped graphene, we uncover a rich phenomenology of ultrafast non-equilibrium orbital angular momentum, including a quasi-stationary light-induced OAM. This latter finding, underpinned by a close connection between band-manifold phase texture and nonequilibrium OAM, points towards new possibilities for light control over OAM in solids as well as a potentially rich role for topological band structures in designed OAM materials.

</details>


### [114] [Reference-free certification of machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00585)
*Jonas Hänseroth,Christian Dreßler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种无需参考计算（reference calculation）即可对机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）进行评级的方法：将玻恩-奥本海默面（Born-Oppenheimer surface）在数学或物理上必然满足的性质归纳为四类，转化为十四个低成本探针（probes），每个探针给出绝对且可跨架构比较的评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势的留出集（held-out）能量与力误差已小到无法再预测模型在模拟中的行为，且固定测试集无法揭示某些失效模式，因此需要独立于参考计算的评价手段。

Method: 利用精确玻恩-奥本海默面必然满足的性质构建评价体系，将其组织为四个家族：对称性与不变性（symmetry and invariance）、自洽性与可积性（self-consistency and integrability）、统计力学平衡与正则性（statistical-mechanical equilibrium and regularity），并转化为十四个低成本探针，每个探针以精确、与化学体系及参考方法无关的目标为基准进行认证，仅使用势已提供的能量、力与应力。

Result: 在64个预训练势（pretrained potentials）上，该套件在报告精度相近的情况下区分出两个数量级的认证质量差异，并识别出一个微调势（fine-tuned potential）：其参考误差改善但势面退化，直至分子动力学（molecular dynamics）失效。全部64个模型的交互式排行榜见 https://jhaens.github.io/pescert-bench。

Conclusion: 无需参考计算的探针式评级可提供绝对、可比较的势质量评分，并揭示固定测试集无法发现的失效，为机器学习原子间势的评价提供补充性标准。

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials now reach held-out energy and force errors so small that they no longer predict how a model behaves in simulation. Here we show that a potential can be graded without any reference calculation, against properties the exact Born-Oppenheimer surface satisfies by mathematical or physical necessity. We organise these properties into four families, symmetry and invariance, self-consistency and integrability, statistical-mechanical equilibrium and regularity, and turn them into fourteen inexpensive probes, each certifying against a target that is exact and independent of chemistry and reference method. Every probe therefore returns an absolute, architecture-comparable score from the energies, forces and stresses a potential already exposes, and reveals failures a fixed test set cannot. Applied to 64 pretrained potentials, the suite resolves two orders of magnitude of certified quality at comparable reported accuracy, and flags a fine-tuned potential whose reference errors improve while its surface degrades until molecular dynamics fails. An interactive leaderboard of all 64 models is available at https://jhaens.github.io/pescert-bench.

</details>


### [115] [Complex Magnetic Phases and Unconventional Skyrmion Thermodynamics in Magnetically Intercalated Cr$_{1+x}$Te$_2$Compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00877)
*Clayton Conner,Santosh Karki Chhetri,Avinash Sah,Manoj Gadtoula,Dilan M. Gamachchi,Indeewari M. Karunarathne,Steven Kelley,Andrew C. Meng,Jacob Cook,Cheng Zhang,Jin Hu,Yue Li,Hoyeon Jeon,An-Ping Li,Zheng Gai,Guang Bian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过磁性插层（magnetic intercalation）调控过渡金属硫族化合物（TMDs）Cr$_{1+x}$Te$_2$的磁相互作用，揭示包含奈尔型斯格明子（Néel-type skyrmion）的丰富磁相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学插层（chemical intercalation）可用于调控TMDs的电子与磁性质，使其呈现原始形态难以获得的磁相与现象，但相关结构与磁相行为尚待系统研究。

Method: 结合扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy）、角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）、磁化测量（magnetization）、电输运（electrical transport）与洛伦兹透射电子显微镜（Lorentz transmission electron microscopy）对Cr$_{1+x}$Te$_2$进行结构、电子与磁性表征。

Result: 获得包含顺磁（paramagnetic）、面内自旋涨落（in-plane spin-fluctuation）、面外铁磁（out-of-plane ferromagnetic）及场诱导奈尔型斯格明子相的磁相图；洛伦兹透射电镜在居里温度（Curie temperature）略下方直接观测到奈尔型斯格明子织构，其构型随温度与磁场强烈演化，并表现出明显热滞（thermal hysteresis）且与条纹畴（stripe domains）共存。

Conclusion: 化学插层可深刻重塑TMDs的磁相互作用与相行为，Cr$_{1+x}$Te$_2$可作为探索可调磁性、斯格明子物理及低维量子材料中非常规热力学现象的多功能平台。

Abstract: Chemical intercalation provides a powerful route for tuning the electronic and magnetic properties of transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs), enabling the emergence of magnetic phases and phenomena that are otherwise inaccessible in their pristine forms. Here, we investigate the structural, electronic, and magnetic properties of magnetically intercalated Cr$_{1+x}$Te$_2$ through a combination of scanning tunneling microscopy, angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, magnetization, electrical transport, and Lorentz transmission electron microscopy measurements. We uncover a rich magnetic phase diagram comprising paramagnetic, in-plane spin-fluctuation, out-of-plane ferromagnetic, and field-induced Néel-type skyrmion phases. In particular, Lorentz transmission electron microscopy directly reveals the formation of Néel-type skyrmion textures slightly below the Curie temperature, with their configurations evolving strongly with temperature and magnetic field. The skyrmions exhibit pronounced thermal hysteresis and coexist with stripe domains, indicative of a complex magnetic energy landscape arising from the competition among disorder-induced local Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions, perpendicular magnetic anisotropy, and dipolar interactions. Our results demonstrate that chemical intercalation can profoundly reshape the magnetic interactions and phase behavior of TMDs, establishing Cr$_{1+x}$Te$_2$ as a versatile platform for exploring tunable magnetism, skyrmion physics, and unconventional thermodynamic phenomena in low-dimensional quantum materials.

</details>


### [116] [Topological robustness of thermally disordered lattices: From average structures to ensemble electronic properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00963)
*Oleg Rubel,Karekin Sadikian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以 Bi$_2$Se$_3$ 为代表性拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator），通过对从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics）快照按自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）缩放进行分类，检验"热平均结构"能否代表有限温度下的构型系综（ensemble）。结果表明平均结构在两个层面失效：遗漏瞬时对称破缺产生的 Rashba 型自旋劈裂（Rashba-like spin splitting），并显著低估带隙重整化（band-gap renormalization）；而少量谐振蒙特卡洛（harmonic Monte Carlo）构型可同时重现带隙均值与展宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能带拓扑（band topology）通常被赋予单一晶体结构，但有限温度下晶体实为热无序构型的系综，其带隙存在涨落。因此需要回答：热平均结构是否是该系综的可靠代理量（proxy）。

Method: 以 Bi$_2$Se$_3$ 为代表性拓扑绝缘体，对从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics）的每个快照，通过缩放自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）来分类能带排序（band ordering），并引入"能带反转存活概率（band-inversion survival probability）"作为判据；同时对比少量谐振蒙特卡洛（harmonic Monte Carlo）构型的统计表现。

Result: 平均结构在两处失效：一是遗漏个别构型中瞬时对称破缺（instantaneous symmetry breaking）产生的 Rashba 型自旋劈裂（Rashba-like spin splitting）；二是明显低估带隙重整化（band-gap renormalization）。相比之下，少量谐振蒙特卡洛（harmonic Monte Carlo）构型能够同时复现带隙的均值与展宽。利用能带反转存活概率发现，Bi$_2$Se$_3$ 的能带反转在所有采样构型中可维持至 600 K；超过该温度后，个别构型在平均带隙闭合之前就已转入正常能带排序。

Conclusion: 有限温度下的拓扑稳健性（topological robustness）是构型分布（distribution over configurations）的属性，而非任何单一结构的属性。

Abstract: Band topology is usually assigned to a single crystal structure, yet at finite temperature a crystal is an ensemble of thermally disordered configurations with a fluctuating band gap. We ask whether the thermally averaged structure is a faithful proxy for this ensemble, taking Bi$_2$Se$_3$ as a representative topological insulator and classifying band ordering in each ab initio molecular dynamics snapshot by scaling the spin-orbit coupling. The averaged structure fails in two ways: it misses the Rashba-like spin splitting that instantaneous symmetry breaking produces in individual configurations, and it substantially underestimates the band-gap renormalization, whereas a small ensemble of harmonic Monte Carlo configurations reproduces both the mean and the spread of the gap. Using a band-inversion survival probability, we find that the band inversion of Bi$_2$Se$_3$ holds in every sampled configuration up to 600 K, above which individual configurations cross into normal band ordering well before the average gap closes. Topological robustness at finite temperature is thus a property of the distribution over configurations rather than of any single structure.

</details>


### [117] [Layer-by-layer growth of highly aligned MoS$_2$ nanoribbon arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00987)
*Kaichi Yamamoto,Pablo Solís-Fernández,Haiming Sun,Yanlin Gao,Yeri Lee,Yushan Tseng,Aika Uchida,Masahiro Hara,Sunmin Ryu,Yung-Chang Lin,Susumu Okada,Kazu Suenaga,Hiroki Ago*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过化学气相沉积（chemical vapor deposition, CVD）在低对称蓝宝石衬底上实现 MoS₂ 纳米带（nanoribbon, NR）的逐层生长，可控层数达四层并保持宽度小于 20 nm，双层 NR 场效应晶体管（FET）性能显著优于单层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维过渡金属硫属化物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMD）纳米带是实现晶体管沟道宽度持续微缩的有前景路径，但层数这一决定器件性能的关键参数长期难以控制。

Method: 采用 CVD 方法，在低对称蓝宝石表面实现 MoS₂ NR 的逐层取向生长；通过提高 MoO₃ 前驱体供给促进第二层及后续层生长，并调控生长时间与前驱体供给以实现层数控制，同时将方法扩展至垂直 MoS₂/WS₂ 异质纳米带。

Result: 获得宽度小于 20 nm、2H 堆垛构型的单晶双层 NR；层数可增至四层。双层 NR-FET 的平均载流子迁移率与电流密度分别为单层器件的两倍和三倍，最高迁移率达 86.7 cm²V⁻¹s⁻¹。

Conclusion: 层数是优化 NR 器件性能的关键参数；该厚度可控生长策略为构建兼具可微缩性与高性能的 TMD 纳米带晶体管提供了通用路径。

Abstract: One-dimensional transition metal dichalcogenides (TMD) nanoribbons (NRs) offer a promising route to aggressive channel-width scaling in nanoscale transistors. However, controlling their layer number, a key determinant of device performance, has remained elusive. Here, we demonstrate a chemical vapor deposition (CVD) approach for layer-by-layer growth of MoS$_2$ NRs on a low-symmetry sapphire surface. This approach enables the oriented growth of single-crystalline bilayer NRs with a 2H stacking configuration, while maintaining widths below 20 nm. Increasing the MoO$_3$ precursor supply was found to be crucial for promoting the growth of the second and subsequent layers. By controlling the growth time and precursor supply, the layer number of MoS$_2$ NRs can be increased up to four layers. This method can also be extended to the growth of vertical MoS$_2$/WS$_2$ hetero-NRs. The average carrier mobility and current density of bilayer NR field-effect transistors (FETs) are two- and three-times higher than those of monolayer counterparts, respectively, and a maximum mobility of 86.7 cm$^2$V$^{-1}$s$^{-1}$ was attained with a bilayer NR-FET. These results highlight layer number as a key parameter for optimizing NR device performance. Our thickness-controlled growth strategy provides a general route towards TMD NR-based transistors with enhanced scalability and performance.

</details>


### [118] [HVPE Homoepitaxy of $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$: High-Temperature Growth and Orientation-Dependent Surface Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01003)
*Sourav Sarker,Saleh Ahmed Khan,Ahmed Ibreljic,Pronoy Kanti Das,Anhar Bhuiyan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 系统研究了(010)、(001)与($\bar{2}$01)三种同质衬底上β-Ga₂O₃的HVPE同质外延，将生长温度拓展至1150 °C，获得高生长速率（最高14.3 μm/h）与高结晶质量、相纯的外延层，并揭示表面形貌的强晶向依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在在宽广生长参数空间内考察β-Ga₂O₃卤化物气相外延（HVPE）生长动力学，并把同质外延拓展至更高温度区间，以探索高速率生长厚质外延层的工艺窗口。摘要未明确给出更具体的应用或器件背景。

Method: 在(010)、(001)和($\bar{2}$01)原生衬底上进行HVPE同质外延，生长温度高达1150 °C，并系统调节前驱体供给（HCl流量80–300 sccm）、温度（1050–1150 °C）、反应室压力（常压与100 Torr）、O₂流量（降至5 sccm）以及Ga源与衬底间距等参数。通过摇摆曲线FWHM、表面形貌与RMS粗糙度等表征评估外延层质量。

Result: 生长动力学强烈依赖前驱体供给、温度、反应室压力与Ga源-衬底距离：HCl流量由80增至300 sccm使生长速率最高提升约七倍；温度由1050升至1150 °C使沉积速率接近翻倍；1150 °C常压生长速率比100 Torr高2.5倍以上，O₂降至5 sccm时速率达14.3 μm/h，3 h可获42.9 μm厚膜。三种取向均得到相纯、结晶质量良好的外延层，摇摆曲线FWHM分别为(010) 63.4、(001) 66.6、($\bar{2}$01) 42.8 arcsec；表面形貌呈明显晶向依赖，($\bar{2}$01)面形成良好台阶-平台结构，局部RMS粗糙度低至0.17 nm。

Conclusion: 该工作将β-Ga₂O₃的HVPE同质外延推进至更高温区间，证明了可高速率生长厚、高质量外延层的宽工艺窗口，并显示表面演化具有强烈晶向依赖性。

Abstract: In this work, we systematically investigate homoepitaxial $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ growth by halide vapor phase epitaxy (HVPE) on (010), (001), and ($\bar{2}$01) native substrates over a broad growth parameter space, extending the growth temperature as high as 1150 $^\circ$C. The growth kinetics exhibit strong dependence on precursor supply, temperature, reactor pressure, and Ga-source-to-substrate distance. Increasing the HCl flow from 80 to 300 sccm enhances the growth rate by up to seven-fold, while increasing the temperature from 1050 to 1150 $^\circ$C nearly doubles the deposition rate. At 1150 $^\circ$C, atmospheric-pressure growth yields rates more than 2.5 times higher than those at 100 Torr, while reducing the O$_2$ flow to 5 sccm further increases the growth rate to 14.3 $μ$m/h, producing films up to 42.9 $μ$m thick in 3 h. Notably, phase-pure homoepitaxial layers with good crystalline quality are obtained across all three orientations, with low rocking-curve full width at half maximum (FWHM) values of 63.4, 66.6, and 42.8 arcsec for the (010), (001), and ($\bar{2}$01) epilayers, respectively. The surface morphology exhibits a pronounced crystallographic dependence and evolves strongly with the HVPE growth conditions. In particular, the ($\bar{2}$01) surface develops well-defined step-and-terrace morphology with local RMS roughness as low as 0.17 nm. These results extend $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ HVPE homoepitaxy into a higher-temperature regime and demonstrate a broad process window for high-rate growth of thick, high-quality epilayers with strongly orientation-dependent surface evolution.

</details>


### [119] [Train for Accuracy, Execute at Scale: Architecture-Preserving Inference for Equivariant Atomistic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01036)
*Lei Fu,Zihui Feng,Yongheng Li,Hongwei Du,Xin He,Junyi Wu,Kejie Bao,Yueyu Zhang,Zeyu Deng,Ziheng Lu,Bonan Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Symmetrix-XL 是一种无需重训练、蒸馏或修改权重即可扩展预训练 MACE 检查点的推理引擎，通过流式边执行、模型专用代码生成与分块执行，在 LAMMPS 完整步骤基准上将推理时间较 ML-IAP + cuEquivariance 降低 3.1-5.0 倍，并将单张 A100 80 GB 上的 MACE-OMAT-0 容量边界从 24,565 原子提升至 1,124 万原子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变原子基础模型（equivariant atomistic foundation models）可提供广泛可迁移的原子间势（interatomic potentials），但在仿真规模下反复执行仍受计算与内存限制。该工作旨在不改变已学习权重的前提下降低仿真执行的硬件门槛。

Method: Symmetrix-XL 结合三项技术：流式边执行（streamed-edge execution）以避免展开边中间量的全图物化；模型专用代码生成（model-specialized code generation）以编译检查点特定算子；分块执行（tiled execution）以复用有界设备内存工作区。

Result: 在完整 LAMMPS 步骤基准上，推理时间较 ML-IAP + cuEquivariance 降低 3.1-5.0 倍（A100 与 RTX 5090 工作负载）。单张 A100 80 GB 上，MACE-OMAT-0 容量边界由 24,565 原子增至 1,124 万原子；同一后端在 64 张 A800 上弱扩展至 7.03 亿原子，效率 93.8%。能量、力、应力、分子动力学稳定性及 Matbench Discovery 评估在测量容差内复现参考 MACE 行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，后训练执行（post-training execution）可作为与模型重设计、压缩和分布式横向扩展互补的扩展维度；实际精度-成本前沿（accuracy-cost frontier）同时取决于模型架构与推理执行。固态、界面与反应体系案例研究展示了该容量提升在真实工作流中的价值。

Abstract: Equivariant atomistic foundation models provide broadly transferable interatomic potentials trained against quantum-mechanical reference data, but their repeated execution at simulation scale remains computationally and memory intensive. We present Symmetrix-XL, an inference engine that scales pretrained MACE checkpoints without retraining, distillation, or modification of their learned weights. It combines streamed-edge execution to avoid graph-wide materialization of expanded edge intermediates, model-specialized code generation to compile checkpoint-specific operators, and tiled execution to reuse a bounded device-memory workspace. On complete LAMMPS-step benchmarks, Symmetrix-XL reduces inference time by 3.1-5.0 times relative to ML-IAP + cuEquivariance across tested A100 and RTX 5090 workloads. On a single A100 80 GB GPU, the tested MACE-OMAT-0 capacity boundary increases from 24,565 atoms to 11.24 million atoms. This substantially lowers the hardware threshold for simulations that would otherwise require spatial decomposition across many GPUs and compute nodes. The same backend weak-scales to 703 million atoms on 64 A800 GPUs at 93.8 percent efficiency. Energy, force, stress, molecular-dynamics stability, and Matbench Discovery evaluations reproduce reference MACE behavior within measured tolerances. Case studies spanning solid-state, interfacial, and reactive systems demonstrate the increased capacity in realistic workflows. These results establish post-training execution as a scaling axis complementary to model redesign, compression, and distributed scale-out, and show that the practical accuracy-cost frontier depends on both model architecture and inference execution.

</details>


### [120] [Landau-like formalism for the thermal-runaway and filamentation instabilities in switching Mott devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01055)
*Aniket Bajaj,Anudeep Tullibilli,Arup Basak,Pavan Nukala,Bhavtosh Bansal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将相关氧化物（correlated oxides）中偏压诱导的绝缘体-金属转变（insulator-to-metal transition）开关行为，从非线性动力学稳定性（non-linear dynamics stability）角度进行理论与实验研究，构建类朗道自由能（Landau free energy-like）的李雅普诺夫函数（Lyapunov functions），揭示开态与关态转变可分别描述为鞍结分岔（saddle-node bifurcation），并以细丝宽度（filament width）为序参量（order parameter）预测电流控制复位过程的一阶相变（first-order phase transition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关氧化物在电压作用下可切换至低阻态，是忆阻器（memristor）技术的候选材料；其中 VO2 等材料的开关源于热失控不稳定性（thermal-runaway instability），导致绝缘体内突然形成载流金属细丝（metallic filament）。因此需要从动力学稳定性角度理解其开关机制，为器件设计提供理论依据。

Method: 理论方面，构建类朗道自由能的李雅普诺夫函数，将开态（"set"）与关态（"reset"）转变分别刻画为鞍结分岔；从热输运的反应-扩散方程（reaction-diffusion equation）出发，将复位过程系统表述为以细丝宽度为序参量的平均场类朗道动力学（mean field Landau-like dynamics）。实验方面，在 VO2 薄膜器件上开展电学与光学显微（electrical and optical microscopy）测量，并与理论进行定量比较，同时进行多循环开关动力学实验。

Result: 理论预测存在突然的一阶相变，在电流控制复位转变中伴随有限宽度细丝的断裂（break-off）。该表述与 VO2 薄膜器件的电学及光学显微实验结果进行了定量对比。多循环开关动力学实验进一步揭示，在初始电-空间形成阶段（electro-spatial forming phase）之后出现稳定的随机区域（stable stochastic regime）。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个跨学科动力学框架，可与多种反应-扩散情境中的不稳定性相比较，同时为基于莫特转变（Mott-transition）技术的理性设计与优化提供了可预测的基础。

Abstract: Correlated oxides can switch to a low-resistance state when exposed to a voltage, making these insulator-to-metal transition materials promising candidates for memristor technology. Switching in materials such as VO2 occurs because of a thermal-runaway instability that leads to the abrupt formation of a hot current-carrying metallic filament within the insulator. We study, theoretically and experimentally, this bias-induced switching as a non-linear dynamics stability problem. We construct Landau free energy-like Lyapunov functions to show that both the on-state ("set") and the off-state ("reset") transitions can be described as separate saddle-node bifurcations. Starting from the reaction-diffusion equation for the heat transport, the abrupt snap-back (reset) to the insulating state is systematically formulated as a mean field Landau-like dynamics with the filament width playing the role of the order parameter. The formulation predicts an abrupt first-order phase transition with a break-off of a finite width filament across the current-controlled reset transition. The formulation is quantitatively compared against our electrical and optical microscopy experiments on VO2 thin film devices. Multi-cycle switching dynamics experiments further reveal the emergence of a stable stochastic regime following an initial electro-spatial forming phase. Beyond establishing a cross-disciplinary dynamical framework that may be readily compared to instabilities in various reaction-diffusion scenarios, the work also provides a predictive foundation for the rational design and optimization of Mott-transition-based technologies.

</details>


### [121] [Pressure induced structural transitions, coordination crossover, and optoelectronic response in divalent metavanadate CaV$_2$O$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01290)
*Bhagyashri Giri,Asish Kumar Mishra,Bidisha Mukherjee,Debabrata Samanta,Nico Giordano,Goutam Dev Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在室温、最高31 GPa条件下，通过高压同步辐射X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）、拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）、光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）及UV-Vis吸收测量，研究了二价偏钒酸盐（divalent metavanadate）CaV$_2$O$_6$的结构与光电响应演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究二价偏钒酸盐在高压下阳离子配位变化与光电响应之间的耦合关系，并验证该耦合是否具有普遍性。

Method: 联合高压同步辐射X射线衍射、拉曼光谱、光致发光与UV-Vis吸收测量，并与同族化合物MgV$_2$O$_6$的高压带隙测量进行对比。

Result: 约1 GPa起发生向低对称非中心对称单斜相（$C2$）的结构相变，5.2 GPa以上成为单一稳定相，伴随钒配位由VO$_5$双三角锥增至共边畸变VO$_6$八面体；拉曼显示混合相区（1.0-4.4 GPa）模软化及5.2 GPa处频率与线宽突变；约1 GPa处PL完全淬灭，间接光学带隙非单调演化；20 GPa以上发生等对称结构修饰，体压缩性改变且光学带隙稳定。

Conclusion: 高压下阳离子配位变化与光电响应在二价偏钒酸盐中存在明确耦合，该结论由MgV$_2$O$_6$的对比测量所支持。

Abstract: High-pressure synchrotron X-ray diffraction, Raman spectroscopy, photoluminescence (PL), and UV-Vis absorption measurements were carried out on the divalent metavanadate CaV$_2$O$_6$ up to 31 GPa at room temperature. Ambient CaV$_2$O$_6$ crystallizes in a centrosymmetric pseudo-brannerite monoclinic structure ($C2/m$), where vanadium atoms reside in fivefold-coordinated VO$_5$ trigonal bipyramids. Under hydrostatic compression, the system undergoes a structural phase transition starting at $\sim$1 GPa to a lower-symmetry, non-centrosymmetric monoclinic phase ($C2$), which becomes the single stable phase above 5.2 GPa. This transition involves an increase in vanadium coordination from VO$_5$ bipyramids to edge-sharing distorted VO$_6$ octahedra. High-pressure Raman scattering corroborates the structural phase boundaries, exhibiting mode softening ($γ_3 = -0.42$, $γ_9 = -0.46$) within the mixed-phase regime (1.0 - 4.4 GPa) and sharp anomalies in mode frequencies and linewidths across 5.2 GPa. The optical measurements show complete PL quenching near 1 GPa due to non-radiative recombination pathways introduced by local polyhedral distortions, alongside a non-monotonic evolution of the indirect optical band gap. Above 20 GPa, an anomaly in polyhedral distortion and internal strain drives an isosymmetric structural modification within the $C2$ phase, marked by an alteration in bulk compressibility and stabilization of the optical gap. Comparative high-pressure band-gap measurements on the sister compound MgV$_2$O$_6$ validate the explicit coupling between cation coordination changes and optoelectronic response in divalent metavanadates.

</details>


### [122] [Domain-engineered ferroelectric BiFeO$_3$ thin films for efficient bias-free THz emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01357)
*Z. Abdul Hadi,N. Rezi,M. Monti,G. Vaudel,A. Abdelsamie,C. Carrétéro,D. Sando,M. Viret,V. Garcia,S. Fusil,M. C. Weber,P. Ruello,V. Juvé*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过调控外延 BiFeO₃ 薄膜的铁电畴构型（从单畴到周期条纹畴），结合振幅与相位的太赫兹场解析，分离并识别了铁电薄膜中超快光电流的主导机制：面内极化薄膜以光载流子对铁电极化的超快屏蔽为主，面外极化则利于体光伏电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电体作为太赫兹发射源既无需光电导天线的偏置电压，也无需自旋电子发射器的磁场，但其发射所依赖的超快光电流机制长期存在争议。该研究旨在厘清这一机制，并考察铁电畴构型对发射的影响。

Method: 采用具有工程化铁电畴构型的外延 BiFeO₃ 薄膜，覆盖单畴至周期条纹畴；测量发射的太赫兹场的振幅与相位随泵浦光偏振及晶体取向的变化，并依据对称性分离共存的光电流成分。

Result: 条纹畴薄膜发射的太赫兹场比单畴构型强四倍，达到金属自旋电子参考发射器输出的一半左右，且在最高激发注量下仍未饱和。分析表明，面内极化薄膜中主导机制为光载流子对铁电极化的超快屏蔽，多畴薄膜中由畴壁处强内建电场增强；面外极化则倾向体光伏电流。

Conclusion: 铁电畴构型是可同时调控光驱动太赫兹发射强度与微观起源的设计参数，为铁电太赫兹发射器的优化提供了机理依据与设计自由度。

Abstract: Ultrashort light pulses can drive transient photocurrents in solids, the basis of modern terahertz (THz) emitters. Ferroelectrics have recently emerged in this context as sources that require neither the bias voltage of photoconductive antennas nor the magnetic field of spintronic emitters, yet the ultrafast photocurrent generating the emission has remained debated. Here, we resolve this question in epitaxial BiFeO$_3$ thin films with engineered ferroelectric domain configurations, from single-domain to periodic stripe patterns. The stripe-domain film emits a THz field four times stronger than the single-domain configurations, reaching about half the output of a metallic spintronic reference emitter while remaining unsaturated at our highest excitation fluences. Resolving the emitted THz field in amplitude and phase as a function of the pump light polarization and the crystal orientation, we separate the coexisting photocurrents by their symmetry. This identifies the ultrafast screening of the ferroelectric polarization by photocarriers as the dominant emission mechanism in the in-plane-polarized films, enhanced in multidomain films by the strong built-in fields at the domain walls, while an out-of-plane polarization favors the bulk photovoltaic current. Our results establish the ferroelectric domain configuration as a design parameter setting both the strength and the microscopic origin of light-driven THz emission.

</details>


### [123] [Benchmarking average atom potentials derived from atomic cluster expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01370)
*Deepak Somani,Lorenzo Piersante,Anirudh Raju Natarajan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作基准测试了一种从线性原子团簇展开（atomic cluster expansion, ACE）直接计算精确平均原子（average atom, A-atom）势的形式体系，并证明在小训练集下即可准确恢复无序合金的性质，前提是化学位点基（chemical site basis）选择得当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均原子势可对化学无序合金进行平均场（mean-field）描述，用于预测无短程有序固溶体的性质；但传统平均原子势由既有原子间势平均而来，精度受限于母模型，而高精度原子间势本身难以参数化且需要大规模训练数据。

Method: 将线性 ACE 分别拟合至两类数据：一是由嵌入原子方法（embedded atom method, EAM）势生成的 Fe-W 数据；二是约 1500 个平均含 7 个原子的 Mo-Nb 小结构的电子结构计算能量与力。随后从线性 ACE 直接导出精确的平均原子势，并与显式随机超胞及传统平均原子势进行对比。

Result: Fe-W 的 ACE 平均原子势重现了由显式随机超胞和同源 EAM 平均得到的传统平均原子势所计算的无序相性质。Mo-Nb 的 ACE 平均原子势重现了密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）的弹性常数、晶格参数、混合焓及特殊准随机结构（special quasirandom structures）的 Bain 路径；在三种化学位点基中，仅占据基（occupation basis）还能重现合金的 DFT 表面能。该势还预测，对 Mo-Nb 而言理想溶液熵在有限温度下超过使无序相失稳的振动贡献。

Conclusion: 当化学位点基被谨慎选择时，无序合金的性质可由小规模训练数据集恢复；从线性 ACE 可导出精确的平均原子势，为无序合金性质预测提供了一条降低对大规模训练数据依赖的途径。

Abstract: Average atom (A-atom) potentials provide a mean-field description of a chemically disordered alloy and are used to predict the properties of solid solutions without short-range order. Such potentials are usually averaged from an existing interatomic potential and are therefore only as accurate as the parent model. Accurate interatomic potentials are themselves difficult to parameterize and can require large training datasets. Here we benchmark a recently developed formalism that computes an exact A-atom potential directly from a linear atomic cluster expansion (ACE). We first fit a linear ACE to Fe-W data generated with an embedded atom method (EAM) potential. The resulting A-atom potential reproduces the properties of the disordered phase computed from an explicit random supercell and from a conventional A-atom potential averaged from the same EAM potential. We then fit a linear ACE to energies and forces computed from electronic structure calculations for about 1500 small Mo-Nb structures with an average of 7 atoms per structure. The A-atom potential derived from this ACE reproduces the DFT elastic constants, lattice parameter, mixing enthalpy, and Bain path of special quasirandom structures. Of the three chemical site bases, only the occupation basis also reproduces the DFT surface energies of the alloy. For Mo-Nb, the A-atom potential also predicts that the ideal solution entropy outweighs the destabilizing vibrational contribution to the finite-temperature free energy of the disordered phase. These benchmarks show that the properties of disordered alloys can be recovered from small training datasets when the chemical site basis is chosen carefully.

</details>


### [124] [Complex magnetic phase diagrams in Tb$_2$IrAl$_4$Ge$_2$ and Er$_2$IrAl$_4$Ge$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01714)
*Karolina Gornicka,Matthew S. Cook,Brenden R. Ortiz,Andrew D. Christianson,Andrew F. May*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 首次合成并研究 Ir 基 \(Ln_2M\)Al\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 家族成员 Tb\(_2\)IrAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 与 Er\(_2\)IrAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\)：二者均为四方 Tb\(_2\)NiAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 型结构并具易轴各向异性，但磁行为差异显著，拓展了该家族化学空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在引入 Ir 作为 \(Ln_2M\)Al\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 家族的新成员，以拓宽该体系的化学空间，并考察 Tb\(_2\)NiAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 型结构可容纳的磁性行为范围。

Method: 合成单晶（single-crystalline）Tb\(_2\)IrAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 与 Er\(_2\)IrAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\)，通过结构表征确认其四方 Tb\(_2\)NiAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 型结构（空间群 \(I4/mmm\)），并开展综合性磁性及磁输运（magnetotransport）测量。

Result: 两化合物均以晶体学 \(c\) 轴为易磁化方向，呈易轴磁各向异性（easy-axis magnetic anisotropy）；Tb\(_2\)IrAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 在 \(T_N = 25\) K 以下反铁磁有序（antiferromagnetic ordering），并出现系列场诱导变磁转变（metamagnetic transitions）与显著磁输运异常，包括非线性霍尔响应（nonlinear Hall response）；Er\(_2\)IrAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 则呈三个连续零场磁转变及更丰富的低温磁相图，含多个竞争性场诱导相。

Conclusion: Tb 与 Er 基化合物的差异表明 Tb\(_2\)NiAl\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 型结构可实现宽范围的磁性行为；Ir 基 \(Ln_2M\)Al\(_4\)Ge\(_2\) 化合物的成功实现显著扩展了该家族的可及化学空间，并为探索相关 \(4d\)、\(5d\) 基铝锗化物（aluminogermanides）开辟路径。

Abstract: We report the synthesis and comprehensive investigation of single-crystalline Tb$_2$IrAl$_4$Ge$_2$ and Er$_2$IrAl$_4$Ge$_2$, the first Ir-based members of the $Ln_2M$Al$_4$Ge$_2$ family. Both compounds crystallize in the tetragonal Tb$_2$NiAl$_4$Ge$_2$-type structure (space group $I4/mmm$) and exhibit easy-axis magnetic anisotropy with the crystallographic $c$ axis as the preferred magnetization direction. Tb$_2$IrAl$_4$Ge$_2$ undergoes antiferromagnetic ordering below $T_N = 25$ K and develops a sequence of field-induced metamagnetic transitions accompanied by pronounced magnetotransport anomalies, including a nonlinear Hall response. In contrast, Er$_2$IrAl$_4$Ge$_2$ exhibits three successive zero-field magnetic transitions and a substantially richer low-temperature magnetic phase diagram comprising multiple competing field-induced phases confined to relatively modest magnetic fields. The differences between the Tb- and Er-based compounds demonstrate the wide range of magnetic behavior that can be realized within the \ce{Tb2NiAl4Ge2}-type structure. More broadly, the successful realization of Ir-based $Ln_2M$Al$_4$Ge$_2$ compounds considerably expands the accessible chemical space of this family and opens a route toward the exploration of related $4d$- and $5d$-based aluminogermanides.

</details>


### [125] [Decoding Thermal Stability: In situ Insights into Phase Controlled Phosphine-free Colloidal Bi-Te Nanosheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01722)
*Fagui He,Kevin Oldenburg,Rostyslav Lesyuk,Christian Klinke*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报道了一种无膦（phosphine-free）胶体合成方法，可相选择性制备 Bi2Te3、BiTe 与 Bi4Te5 六方纳米片，并揭示其在约 340 °C 下向 Bi2Te3 转变及后续的 Te 升华与氧化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碲化铋（bismuth telluride）是热电（thermoelectric）与拓扑（topological）应用的典型 V-VI 族半导体，但其同系系列（homologous series）的相选择性合成与热稳定性尚缺乏深入理解。

Method: 采用简便且无膦的胶体合成（colloidal synthesis）途径，通过调控前驱体化学（precursor chemistry）与反应温度，在 (Bi2)m(Bi2Te3)n 同系系列中实现相选择性生长；并结合原位加热研究（in situ heating）、EDS 分析与 STEM 图像的 FFT 分析表征温度依赖的组分与结构变化。

Result: 获得形貌规整的六方 Bi2Te3、BiTe 和 Bi4Te5 纳米片；原位加热显示 BiTe 与 Bi4Te5 在约 340 °C 转变为 Bi2Te3，更高温度下在真空中发生 Te 优先升华、在空气中则发生氧化。EDS 与 FFT 分析为上述温度依赖变化提供了直接证据，并表明各相之间热学关系密切。

Conclusion: 研究推进了对纳米尺度碲化铋相稳定性与热行为的基础认识，并建立了理解相关同系系列结构演化的框架，为热电、自旋电子学（spintronics）与拓扑体系中的潜在应用提供启示。

Abstract: Bismuth telluride (Bi2Te3) is a prototypical V-VI semiconductor of interest for both thermoelectric and topological applications; however, phase-selective synthesis and thermal stability across the bismuth-telluride homologous series remain poorly understood. We report a facile and phosphine-free colloidal synthesis approach for a phase-selective synthesis of Bi2Te3, BiTe, and Bi4Te5 nanosheets with well-defined hexagonal morphology. By controlling precursor chemistry and reaction temperature, we achieved the selective formation of different phases within the (Bi2)m(Bi2Te3)n homologous series. Based on in situ heating studies, BiTe and Bi4Te5 nanosheets transform into Bi2Te3 at about 340 degree C, followed by preferential Te sublimation under vacuum or oxidation in air at higher temperatures. We discuss plausible mechanisms for these phase transformations. EDS analysis and FFT analysis of STEM images provide direct evidence for the temperature-dependent compositional and structural changes and highlight the close thermal relationship among these phases. These results not only advance fundamental understanding of phase stability and thermal behavior in bismuth-tellurides at the nanoscale but also establish a framework for understanding structural evolution in related homologous series, providing insights into their potential future applications in thermoelectric, spintronic, and topological systems.

</details>


### [126] [Theory of Chirality-Induced Spin Selectivity in Trefoil-knot Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01732)
*Xi Sun,Shu-Zheng Zhou,Kai-Yuan Zhang,Hua-Hua Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向分子结的离散几何自旋轨道耦合框架，揭示三叶结分子的位点分辨几何SOC可达约120 meV，并说明基底耦合苯环单元主导手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin selectivity, CISS）的起源尚未阐明，拓扑结分子中观测到的超高自旋极化（spin polarization, SP）亦缺乏解释。既有连续模型难以刻画位点特异的曲率与电流分配效应。

Method: 构建针对分子结的离散几何自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）框架，可在连续模型之外解析位点特异的曲率与电流分配效应；并以三叶结（trefoil-knot）分子为对象进行位点分辨的几何SOC量化。

Result: 三叶结分子的几何SOC量化值约120 meV，比轻原子的本征SOC高出近两个数量级。基底耦合的苯环单元承载最大电流并具有显著几何SOC，主导CISS效应。该框架定量复现了实测SP、温度依赖磁阻（magnetoresistance, MR）及ΔMR趋势。

Conclusion: 离散几何SOC可作为拓扑结分子中CISS的预测性工具，为超高自旋极化的来源提供统一解释。

Abstract: The origin of chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) remains elusive, and ultrahigh spin polarization (SP) in topologically knotted molecules is unexplained. We develop a discrete geometric spin-orbit coupling (SOC) framework for molecular junctions, resolving site-specific curvature and current-partition effects beyond continuous models. For trefoil-knot molecules, it quantifies site-resolved geometric SOC, reaching $\sim$120 meV, nearly two orders of magnitude larger than intrinsic SOC of light atoms. The substrate-coupled benzene unit, carrying the largest current and remarkable geometric SOC, dominates the CISS effect. The framework quantitatively reproduces measured SP, temperature-dependent magnetoresistance (MR) and $Δ$MR trends, establishing discrete geometric SOC as a predictive tool for CISS in topologically knotted molecules.

</details>


### [127] [Structured-Light Control of Goniopolar Thermoelectricity in NaSn\texorpdfstring{$_2$}{2}As\texorpdfstring{$_2$}{2}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01755)
*Atoumane Ndiaye,Edwin Fohtung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过空间结构化的拉盖尔--高斯矢量势（Laguerre--Gaussian vector potential）对 NaSn$_2$As$_2$ 的各向异性极性（goniopolar）相空间进行可编程重构，揭示轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum, OAM）依赖的空间标度律与倍频热电响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性极性金属（goniopolar metals）沿不同晶向呈相反热电极性，可实现零场横向热电效应，但缺乏外部调控手段。本文旨在引入结构化光场作为可编程控制自由度。

Method: 采用第一性原理导出的 Wannier 输运计算，结合键依赖的 Peierls 耦合（bond-dependent Peierls coupling），分析拉盖尔--高斯矢量势对 NaSn$_2$As$_2$ 电子输运与相空间的影响。

Result: 径向响应遵循 $r_{\max}\propto\sqrt{|\ell|}$；$|\ell|=2$--5 时主导角谐波为 $m_{\mathrm{dom}}=2|\ell|$，体现频率加倍的热电响应；$|\ell|$ 增大可使代表性各向异性极性窗口增强约 17%（$|\ell|=5$）；固定偏振下反转 $\ell$ 仅产生小修正并在偏振反转时近似互换；能量分辨输运显示结构化场重分布面内与跨面电子速度，移动确定各向异性极性态的 Seebeck 零点边界。

Conclusion: 涡旋位置与 OAM 幅度可作为各向异性极性热电性的可编程控制坐标。

Abstract: Goniopolar metals exhibit opposite thermoelectric polarities along different crystallographic directions, enabling zero-field transverse thermoelectricity but offering few means for external control. Here we show that a spatially structured Laguerre--Gaussian vector potential can programmably reconstruct the goniopolar phase space of NaSn$_2$As$_2$. First-principles-derived Wannier transport with bond-dependent Peierls coupling reveals two OAM-dependent spatial scaling laws: the radial response follows the Laguerre--Gaussian radius $r_{\max}\propto\sqrt{|\ell|}$, while calculations for $|\ell|=2$--5 yield a dominant angular harmonic $m_{\mathrm{dom}}=2|\ell|$, encoding the optical winding in a frequency-doubled thermoelectric response. Increasing $|\ell|$ simultaneously reconstructs pre-existing goniopolar windows, enhancing a representative window by approximately 17\% at $|\ell|=5$. By contrast, reversing $\ell$ at fixed polarization produces only a small correction that approximately interchanges upon polarization reversal. Energy-resolved transport reveals that the structured field redistributes in-plane and cross-plane electronic velocities, shifting the directional Seebeck-zero boundaries that define the goniopolar state. These results establish vortex position and OAM magnitude as programmable control coordinates for goniopolar thermoelectricity.

</details>


### [128] [Weak non-Landau-type contributions to the diamagnetism of graphite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01764)
*A. V. Nikolaev,A. V. Bibikov,M. Ye. Zhuravlev,L. L. Tao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于第一性原理计算了石墨（graphite）磁化率（magnetic susceptibility）中除朗道能级（Landau levels）之外各较小贡献项的大小，发现泡利顺磁性可忽略，而朗之万与范弗莱克机制给出的抗磁性贡献与石墨面内实验抗磁性及富勒烯（fullerene）C60、C70的抗磁响应相当，并呈轻微各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单晶石墨垂直于石墨烯层方向极大的抗磁化率（diamagnetic susceptibility）源自朗道能级，但磁化率还存在其他贡献：金属中以泡利顺磁性（Pauli paramagnetism）为主，电介质中则以抗磁朗之万（Langevin）项与顺磁范弗莱克（Van Vleck）项为主，这些成分在石墨中同样存在，其量级尚需厘清。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first principles）方法，计算石墨中上述各较小磁化率贡献项的大小，包括泡利顺磁项以及来自朗之万与范弗莱克机制的抗磁项。

Result: 泡利顺磁性可忽略，约为2.3 × 10^{-9} emu/g；朗之万与范弗莱克机制产生的抗磁贡献与石墨面内实验抗磁性以及C60、C70富勒烯的抗磁响应相当。该抗磁贡献呈轻微各向异性：沿x或y轴为-2.93 × 10^{-7} emu/g，沿z轴为-2.24 × 10^{-7} emu/g，平均值为-2.7 × 10^{-7} emu/g。

Conclusion: 石墨磁化率中除朗道能级主导的超大抗磁性外，朗之万与范弗莱克机制提供的抗磁贡献不可忽略，其量级与面内抗磁性及富勒烯抗磁性相当，且具有轻微各向异性。

Abstract: The exceptionally large diamagnetic susceptibility of a single-crystal graphite in the direction perpendicular to the graphene layers is caused by the Landau levels. However, there are also other contributions to the magnetic susceptibility. In particular, in metals the Pauli paramagnetism is leading, whereas in dielectrics the diamagnetic Langevin and paramagnetic Van Vleck terms are essential. These components of magnetic susceptibility are also present in graphite. In this work, we calculate the magnitudes of all these small contributions from first principles. It is found that the Pauli paramagnetism is negligible (2.3 x 10^{-9} emu/g), whereas the other diamagnetic contribution - arising from the Langevin and Van Vleck mechanisms - is comparable to the in-plane experimental diamagnetism of graphite and to the diamagnetic response of C60 and C70} fullerenes. This diamagnetic contribution proves to be slightly anisotropic (-2.93 x 10^{-7} emu/g along the x- or y-axes and -2.24 x 10^{-7} emu/g along the $z-$axis), with the averaged value -2.7 x 10^{-7} emu/g.

</details>


### [129] [GEODE: Symmetry-Preserving Cartesian Diffusion for Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01898)
*Yuchen Lou,Alex M. Ganose*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: GEODE 提出首个在笛卡尔空间（Cartesian space）联合进行坐标与晶格扩散（coordinate and lattice diffusion）的生成模型，通过先采样对称模板再联合生成晶格、原子坐标与原子类型，并引入 Wyckoff 约束损失（Wyckoff-constrained loss）保持对称性，在无条件下生成中达到 11.9% 的 mSUN 率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数已知无机晶体（inorganic crystals）呈对称原子排布，但生成模型常难以复现。显式施加对称性约束此前得到的稳定且新颖结构反而少于无约束生成，因此需要新的建模范式来兼顾对称保持与生成质量。

Method: GEODE 首先采样对称模板（symmetry templates），随后联合生成晶格、原子坐标与原子类型，并通过新颖的 Wyckoff 约束损失保持指定对称性；其在笛卡尔空间中执行坐标与晶格扩散，使坐标噪声具有一致的物理尺度（physical scale）。此外引入采样时间模板过滤（sampling time template filtering），无需重训练即可提升结果，并支持对称性与性质联合引导。

Result: 无条件生成达到 11.9% 的亚稳、独特且新颖（metastable, unique and novel, mSUN）率，优于次优对称感知模型的 7.7%。采样时间模板过滤在无需重训练下将 mSUN 提升约 6%，使 GEODE 可与领先的对称不可知（symmetry-agnostic）模型竞争。模板选择还实现了对称与性质的联合引导，通过无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）验证于介电常数（permittivity）。

Conclusion: GEODE 表明笛卡尔空间的联合扩散与 Wyckoff 约束损失可有效保持晶体对称性并提升生成质量，模板过滤与模板引导进一步扩展了对称与性质联合控制能力，为对称感知晶体生成提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Most known inorganic crystals exhibit symmetric atomic arrangements, yet generative models often fail to reproduce them. Explicitly enforcing these symmetries has so far yielded fewer stable and novel structures than unconstrained generation. We introduce Generative Equivariant Orbit Diffusion Engine (GEODE), to our knowledge the first model to combine coordinate and lattice diffusion in Cartesian space. GEODE first samples symmetry templates, then jointly generates the lattice, atomic coordinates and atom types while preserving the specified symmetry with a novel Wyckoff-constrained loss. Cartesian diffusion gives coordinate noise a consistent physical scale that we empirically demonstrate improves performance. Unconditional generation achieves a metastable, unique and novel (mSUN) rate of 11.9%, compared with 7.7% for the next best symmetry-aware model. We also introduce sampling time template filtering, which increases mSUN by ~6% without retraining, making GEODE competitive with leading symmetry-agnostic models. Template selection also enables joint symmetry and property guidance, which we demonstrate through classifier-free guidance of permittivity.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Rotation fixed points of undecorated braid varieties and fusion rings of affine Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02034)
*Yuma Mizuno*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文证明：与 Coxeter 元幂次相关联的无装饰编织簇（braid variety）上，由字循环旋转诱导的有限阶自等价（Zamolodchikov 变换）的不动点栈（fixed-point stack）的粗模空间，同构于对应仿射李代数（affine Lie algebra）融合环（fusion ring）的谱；该结论可推广至扭曲 Coxeter 元，并由此得到所有仿射李代数的融合环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注编织簇上的自等价（Zamolodchikov 变换）与仿射李代数融合环之间的几何—代数联系。其目标是将融合环实现为某类模空间的谱，从而为融合环提供几何解释，并统一刻画所有仿射李代数的融合环。

Method: 设 G 为单连通半单代数群（simply connected semisimple algebraic group）。在与 Coxeter 元（Coxeter element）的幂次相关联的无装饰编织簇上，利用字的循环旋转诱导有限阶自等价，即 Zamolodchikov 变换；随后考察该自等价的不动点栈，并计算其粗模空间（coarse moduli space）。方法进一步推广至扭曲 Coxeter 元（twisted Coxeter elements）。

Result: 该自等价不动点栈的粗模空间同构于对应仿射李代数融合环的谱（spectrum）。该结果对扭曲 Coxeter 元同样成立，并由此获得所有仿射李代数的融合环。

Conclusion: Zamolodchikov 变换的不动点栈的粗模空间为仿射李代数融合环提供了几何实现，且该构造在扭曲 Coxeter 元情形下依然有效，可统一得到全部仿射李代数的融合环。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simply connected semisimple algebraic group. On the (undecorated) braid variety associated with a power of a Coxeter element, there is a finite-order autoequivalence, called the Zamolodchikov transformation, induced by cyclic rotation of words. In this paper, we show that the coarse moduli space of the fixed-point stack of this autoequivalence is isomorphic to the spectrum of the fusion ring of the corresponding affine Lie algebra. The above results also extend to twisted Coxeter elements, and the fusion rings of all affine Lie algebras are obtained in this way.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Gromov-Hausdorff Convergence of Spectral Truncations for Noncommutative Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01132)
*Vibhor Bhatt,Satyajit Guin,Palash Sharma*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该论文证明非交换 n 环面（noncommutative n-tori）谱截断（spectral truncation）所关联算子系统的状态空间在 Connes 距离函数下于 Gromov-Hausdorff 意义下收敛，并借助环面作用与 Bochner 积分构造 C^1-近似序同构完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将经典环面的收敛结果推广至非交换几何（noncommutative geometry）框架下的谱截断情形，考察量子化变形下状态空间的几何极限行为。

Method: 方法上利用 n-环面在非交换 n-环面上的作用，并结合 Bochner 积分的适当使用，构造出 C^1-近似序同构（C^1-approximate order isomorphism）以建立收敛性。

Result: 结果为证明非交换 n-环面（n≥2）谱截断算子系统的状态空间在 Connes 距离下 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛；经典环面情形的既有收敛结果作为特例成立。

Conclusion: 结论部分研究了截断算子系统的 C^*-包络（C^*-envelopes）与传播数（propagation numbers），表明环面截断算子系统的结构性质在变形下得以保持。

Abstract: We prove the Gromov-Hausdorff convergence of the state spaces, equipped with Connes' distance function, of the operator systems associated with spectral truncations for noncommutative $n$-tori, $n\ge 2$. The action of the $n$-torus on the noncommutative $n$-torus, together with an appropriate use of Bochner integrals, enables us to construct an $C^1$-approximate order isomorphism that establishes the convergence. The earlier convergence result for the classical case of tori [20] follows as a special case of our result. We conclude by studying the $C^*$-envelopes and propagation numbers of the truncated operator systems and show that the structural properties of the truncated operator systems for tori persist under deformation.

</details>
