### [132] [Probing Matter Unification through Higgs Physics at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23708)
*Mark Brettell,Jon Butterworth,Hridoy Debnath,Pavel Fileviez Perez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: We investigate the Higgs sector phenomenology of the minimal low scale quark-lepton unification theory. In addition to the Standard Model-like Higgs boson, the theory predicts a new CP-even neutral Higgs, a CP-odd neutral Higgs, and two charged Higgses, together with heavy right-handed neutrinos and leptoquarks. We analyze the scalar spectrum, the Yukawa structure responsible for charged-fermion masses, and the neutrino sector realized through an inverse-seesaw mechanism, emphasizing that the couplings of the new Higgs bosons are directly tied to the structure of matter unification and therefore lead to characteristic decay patterns dominated by third-generation fermions. We compute the branching ratios and production cross sections of the new Higgs states at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) and assess their impact on present and future collider measurements. We find that current LHC data probe significant regions of parameter space for the neutral heavy Higgs bosons, with the strongest sensitivity arising from channels involving tau leptons, tops, and related final states, while the charged Higgs remains less constrained because of its electroweak production mechanism. Our results show that Higgs measurements provide a powerful and complementary probe of low-scale matter unification beyond direct leptoquark searches and open a new avenue for testing quark-lepton unification at present and future colliders, with discovery potential in high-luminosity LHC running.

</details>


### [133] [Full-event anomaly detection for new physics searches at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00308)
*Dimitrios Athanasakos,Sebastian Grieninger,Hongkai Liu,Tymothy Mangan,Felix Ringer,Robert Szafron*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在未来的电子-离子对撞机（Electron-Ion Collider, EIC）上利用机器学习与事件全部粒子信息进行共振异常检测（resonant anomaly detection），通过弱监督、条件生成与无监督方法提升不变质量搜寻的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来EIC为搜寻超越标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的物理提供新机遇，需要发展适配其独特运动学的异常检测方法。作者以中性规范玻色子（neutral gauge boson）与重中性轻子（heavy neutral lepton）作为示例信号。

Method: 采用弱监督学习（weakly supervised learning）利用事件全部信息，在训练中不识别单个信号事件的前提下增强不变质量（invariant-mass）搜寻；并将预训练于LHC喷注（jet）的模型改造为条件全事件生成器（conditional full-event generator）以产生EIC背景事件；此外构建无监督图自编码器（graph autoencoder）。

Result: 与高层观测量（high-level observables）比较显示，电子运动学（electron kinematics）贡献了分离性的重要部分，粒子间关联（correlations）提供额外灵敏度。基于学习背景参考的信号比例扫描显示信号显著增强；无监督图自编码器增强幅度有限，但在弱监督失效的极低信号比例下仍保持灵敏度。

Conclusion: 结果支持将全事件异常检测（full-event anomaly detection）纳入EIC物理项目，并指出需发展适配其独特运动学的方法方向。

Abstract: The future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) will offer new opportunities to search for physics beyond the Standard Model. We study resonant anomaly detection using machine learning and the full particle content of an event. We consider a new neutral gauge boson and a heavy neutral lepton as illustrative signals with different electron-jet topologies. Weakly supervised learning uses this event information to enhance the sensitivity of an invariant-mass search without identifying individual signal events during training. Comparisons with high-level observables show that the electron kinematics account for an important part of the separation, while correlations among the particles provide further sensitivity. We also construct a conditional full-event generator by adapting a model pretrained on LHC jets to produce EIC background events. A scan over signal fractions demonstrates substantial signal enhancement using the learned background reference, providing a proof of concept for this approach at the EIC. Finally, we consider a fully unsupervised graph autoencoder that provides modest signal enhancement but retains sensitivity at signal fractions too small for effective weak supervision. Our results motivate full-event anomaly detection as part of the EIC physics program and identify directions for developing methods suited to its distinct kinematics.

</details>


### [134] [Quantum Information in High-Energy Physics: a top subject](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00467)
*Yoav Afik,Juan Ramón Muñoz de Nova*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文为 Frontiers of Science Award 获奖综述，发表于 Proceedings of the ICBS，系统梳理量子信息（Quantum Information, QI）技术在高能物理（High-Energy Physics, HEP）中的应用，以顶夸克（top quark）作为相对论量子比特（relativistic qubit）的范式案例，涵盖纠缠、量子层析（quantum tomography）方案及实验验证，并讨论该方向的学科扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在综述量子信息技术向高能物理迁移的可行性与成果，并说明该交叉方向如何从个案研究发展为独立且丰富的研究领域。顶夸克被选作典型对象，因其可作为相对论量子比特的“果蝇”（drosophila）模型。

Method: 以顶夸克-反顶夸克（top-antitop quark）对在 LHC 等高能对撞机中的产生过程为研究对象，分析其中纠缠（entanglement）的存在；并提出针对该夸克对的量子层析（quantum tomography）实验方案。摘要未给出具体的理论推导或数据分析细节。

Result: 所提出的量子层析方案最终促成了 ATLAS 与 CMS 合作组的纠缠观测，构成为迄今能量最高的纠缠观测（highest-energy observations of entanglement ever）。该工作亦被选为 Frontiers of Science Award 获奖成果。

Conclusion: 文中论述的概念与技术已实际催生出一个规模可观且内容丰富的“高能物理中的量子信息”研究领域，显示 QI 与 HEP 的交叉具有持续的研究生命力。

Abstract: As a work selected for the Frontiers of Science Award, we present in the Proceedings of the ICBS an overview of how the techniques from the field of Quantum Information (QI) can be implemented in High-Energy Physics (HEP), including also some novel insights. We focus on the paradigmatic case of the top quark, a drosophila of a relativistic qubit. We study the presence of entanglement, perhaps the most genuine feature of Quantum Mechanics, in top-antitop quark production in high-energy colliders such as the LHC. We also analyze our experimental proposal of a quantum tomography protocol for the top-antitop quark pair, eventually leading to the entanglement observations by the ATLAS and CMS collaborations, which constituted the highest-energy observations of entanglement ever. We discuss how the concepts and techniques developed in our work have effectively spawned an already vast and rich field of QI in HEP.

</details>


### [135] [Neutrino trident process at a muon collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00723)
*Reinaldo Francener,Victor P. Goncalves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨TeV量级μ子对撞机（muon collider）通过μ子衰变产生高准直中微子束，并利用前向中微子探测器研究稀有中微子三叉戟过程（neutrino trident process），评估其对标准模型检验及新物理探测的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeV量级μ子对撞机可在环的直线段通过μ子衰变产生高准直高能中微子通量，已有研究表明其适用于中微子物理高精度测量；本文进一步探索其发现稀有中微子三叉戟过程的可能性。

Method: 研究基于中微子三叉戟过程，其由来自中微子的弱玻色子（weak boson）与靶核库仑场（Coulomb field）中的光子聚变所表征；针对末态不同味道带电轻子对（charged lepton）的配置，估算基线10 kg中微子探测器的年事例率，并以L_μ - L_τ模型预言的新规范玻色子（gauge boson）Z'为例推导投影灵敏度。

Result: 结果显示，在10 kg基线中微子探测器中，不同末态带电轻子味道对配置下中微子三叉戟年事例率可达$\mathcal{O}(10^3)$；以此推导出对Z'玻色子的投影灵敏度，表明可显著扩展当前参数空间的排除区域。

Conclusion: μ子对撞机配备中微子探测器可用于高精度检验标准模型（Standard Model）并搜寻新物理（New Physics），尤其在中微子三叉戟过程及L_μ - L_τ模型Z'玻色子探测方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: A TeV-scale muon collider enables the production of a highly collimated high-energy neutrino flux by muon decay in the straight sections of the collider ring. Recent studies have demonstrated that these neutrinos can be used for high-precision measurements in neutrino physics with a dedicated far-forward neutrino detector. In this study, we investigate the potentiality of the muon collider to discover the rare neutrino trident process, which is characterized by the fusion between a weak boson from the neutrino and a photon from the Coulomb field of the nucleus target. Our results indicate large neutrino trident rates per year [$\mathcal{O}(10^3)$] in a baseline 10 kg neutrino detector for a configuration of different flavour pairs of charged leptons in the final state, which will allow high-precision tests of the Standard Model and searches for New Physics. As a first example, we investigate the impact of the a new gauge boson $Z'$, as predicted by the $L_μ- L_τ$ model, and derive the projected sensitivity to this new gauge boson. Our findings show that a neutrino detector at a muon collider will significantly extend the current excluded regions of the parameter space.

</details>


### [136] [Probing an up-phobic Higgs at colliders: the role of $b$-associated production in the democratic 3HDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01412)
*Dipankar Das,Teesha Khandelwal,Ipsita Saha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出存在一类理论上动机充分的希格斯（Higgs）部门，其中常规重希格斯产生机制被压低，此时与底夸克（b-quark）伴随的产生道成为主要产生机制；民主三希格斯二重态模型（democratic three Higgs-doublet model, 3HDM）可实现该极限，且现有 b 伴随搜索是该 3HDM 情形最敏感的 LHC 探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规重希格斯（heavy-Higgs）产生机制在某些希格斯部门中可能被压低，需要刻画在此极限下占主导的产生道并寻找可检验的紫外完备框架。

Method: 通过理论构造论证存在使常规重希格斯产生被压低的希格斯部门，指出民主三希格斯二重态模型（3HDM）为实现该极限的可行 BSM 框架，并利用现有 b 伴随搜索及未来对撞机搜索投影进行参数空间约束分析。

Result: 在与底夸克伴随的产生道主导的极限下，现有 b 伴随搜索（b-associated searches）构成对该 3HDM 场景在 up-phobic 极限下最敏感的 LHC 探针，并据此导出对参数空间的约束；同时给出未来对撞机搜索的预期，显示其可探测更多 up-phobic 参数空间区域。

Conclusion: 民主 3HDM 提供了可实现“常规重希格斯产生被压低”极限的可行 BSM 框架，b 伴随产生道在此成为主要机制；现有及未来对撞机搜索可有效检验并进一步约束该模型的 up-phobic 参数空间。

Abstract: We show that there exists a theoretically well-motivated Higgs sector in which the conventional heavy-Higgs production mechanisms are suppressed. In this limit, the $b$-associated production channel assumes the role of the primary production mechanism. We demonstrate that the democratic three Higgs-doublet model (3HDM) provides a viable beyond the SM (BSM) framework in which this limit can be realized. We further show that existing $b$-associated searches provide the most sensitive LHC probe of this 3HDM scenario in the up-phobic limit and derive the corresponding constraints on its parameter space. We also present projections for future collider searches, demonstrating their potential to probe further regions of the model's up-phobic parameter space.

</details>


### [137] [Mechanisms of $D \bar{D}$ pair production in $e^{+} e^{-} \to e^{+} e^{-} D \bar{D}$ and $AA \to AA D \bar{D}$ reactions and production of exotic charmonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01420)
*Antoni Szczurek,Izabela Babiarz,Piotr Lebiedowicz,Wolfgang Schäfer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究 $e^+e^-$ 碰撞中 $D\bar{D}$ 对产生的机制，指出 Belle 与 BaBar 观测到的 $M_{D^0\bar{D}^0}\approx3.8$ GeV 宽峰更可能来源于连续谱（continuum）而非 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振，并给出 $\chi_{c2}(3930)\to D\bar{D}$ 的分支比估计及 Belle II、LHC 相关预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle 与 BaBar 合作组在 $M_{D^0\bar{D}^0}=3.8$ GeV 处观测到一个宽峰（broad bump），其本质尚不明确：可能对应宽 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振，也可能源于非共振的连续谱过程。该文旨在厘清这一结构的产生机制。

Method: 考虑 $D^0\bar{D}^0$ 连续谱机制，包含 $t/u$ 道 $D^*$ 矢量介子交换，其中 $D^*D\gamma$ 耦合常数由 $D^*\to D\gamma$ 衰变导出；对 $D^+D^-$ 道主要考虑 $D^\pm$ 交换。同时研究 $D\bar{D}$ 对的共振产生，涵盖 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 与 $\chi_{c2}(3930)$。将模型结果与 $\Gamma(\chi_{c2}(2P)\to\gamma\gamma)=0.544$ keV（Buchmüller-Tye 势对应值）同 BaBar 数据比较。

Result: $D^0\bar{D}^0$ 道的连续谱贡献相对较大，而 $D^+D^-$ 道显著较小，故 3.8 GeV 宽峰被判定为连续谱起源，而非宽 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振。$\chi_{c2}(3930)$ 是首个激发 $P$ 波粲偶素 $\chi_{c2}(2P)$ 态的良好候选者，可衰变至两个 $D\bar{D}$ 道，但其分支比目前尚不充分；由模型与 BaBar 数据比较得到 $B(\chi_{c2}(3930)\to D\bar{D})=0.58\pm0.13$。

Conclusion: 给出了若干变量下微分分布（differential distributions）的结果以适用于 Belle II 运动学（kinematics），并简要提及 LHC 上超周边重离子反应（ultraperipheral heavy-ion reaction）$PbPb\to PbPb\,D\bar{D}$ 在低不变质量（$M_{D\bar{D}}<4.3$ GeV）区的预言。

Abstract: We discuss mechanisms of $D \bar{D}$ pair production in $e^+ e^-$ collisions. We consider the $D^0 {\bar D}^0$-continuum mechanism with the $t/u$-channel $D^*$ vector-meson exchanges, where the $D^* D γ$ coupling constants are derived from the $D^* \to D γ$ decays. For the $D^+ D^-$ channel, we consider mainly the $D^{\pm}$ exchanges. We find a relatively large continuum contribution for the $D^0 {\bar D}^0$ channel and a significantly smaller one for $D^+ D^-$, concluding that the broad bump at $M_{D^0 {\bar D}^0} = 3.8$ GeV observed by the Belle and BaBar Collaborations has a continuum origin rather than corresponding to the broad $χ_{c0}(3860)$ resonance. We also investigate the resonant production of $D \bar{D}$ pairs, including $χ_{c0}(3860)$ and $χ_{c2}(3930)$. The $χ_{c2}(3930)$ meson is a good candidate for the first excited $P$-wave charmonium $χ_{c2}(2P)$ state. This resonance can decay into both $D \bar{D}$ channels; however, its branching fractions are not well known at present. Comparing our model result with $Γ(χ_{c2}(2P) \to γγ) = 0.544$ keV (corresponding to the Buchmüller-Tye potential) to the BaBar data, we find $B(χ_{c2}(3930) \to D \bar{D}) = 0.58 \pm 0.13$. We present results for differential distributions in several variables for Belle II kinematics. Finally, we shortly mention predictions for the ultraperipheral heavy-ion reaction $Pb Pb \to Pb Pb D \bar{D}$ for low invariant mass ($M_{D \bar{D}} < 4.3$ GeV) at the LHC.

</details>


### [138] [An optimal strategy for experimental searches in high-mass dilepton production at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01965)
*J. Alcaraz Maestre,O. M. Carretero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于次领头阶（next-to-leading order, NLO）精度重加权（reweighting）的方法，用于研究大型强子对撞机（LHC）高不变质量双轻子产生中的新物理效应，仅需一个标准模型样本即可覆盖多种新物理场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高不变质量双轻子产生中的新物理效应通常需要大量计算成本高昂的模拟样本；本文旨在降低这一计算负担，同时保持理论精度。

Method: 方法依赖于量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）次领头阶精度精确的重加权程序；唯一计算昂贵输入为标准模型 pp→V(*)→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻（V=Z, γ*）样本，需以相同精度生成并在高不变质量相空间有充分统计覆盖。

Result: 该方法适用于任意 q q̄ ℓ⁺ℓ⁻ 接触相互作用（contact-interaction）场景，不依赖所假设的底层结构与耦合形式；也适用于搜寻 s 通道（s-channel）产生的中等至较大宽度新中性共振。

Conclusion: 通过同一初始生成样本即可轻松实现质量、耦合或宽度的输入值变更，因此该方法在新物理搜寻中具有广泛的适用性与计算效率优势。

Abstract: We present an optimal method for studying new physics effects in high-mass dilepton production at the LHC. This method relies on a reweighting procedure that is precise to the next-to-leading order in quantum chromodynamics. The only computationally expensive input required is a standard model $\mathrm{pp\rightarrow V^{(*)}\rightarrow\ell^+\ell^-}$ sample, with $\mathrm{V=Z,γ^*}$, generated at the same level of precision and with sufficient statistical coverage of the phase space at high invariant masses. The proposed method is suitable for any $\mathrm{q\overline{q}\ell^+\ell^-}$ contact-interaction scenario, regardless of the assumed underlying structure and couplings. It is also ideal for searching for medium-to-large-width new neutral resonances produced in the s-channel, as changes to the input values for mass, coupling, or width can be easily implemented using the same initial generated samples.

</details>


### [139] [Confronting $K^-d$ dynamics with kaonic atom measurements within a novel unitary framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01977)
*Albert Feijoo,Àngels Ramos,Juan Torres-Rincon*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在固定中心近似（Fixed-Center Approximation）下构建了 $K^-d$ 三体振幅，将手征 $\bar{K}N$ 相互作用模型（OR 与 BCN）作为输入，并通过幺正化多道耦合方法（含电荷交换与同位旋破缺效应）计算散射长度与有效力程。随后利用 bootstrap 分析评估 BCN 模型与 SIDDHARTA-2 的 kaonic 氢/氘实验测量数据的相容性，结果显示需要对 BCN 参数化进行重新调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在 $K^-d$ 三体振幅构造中缺乏幺正约束（unitary constraints），难以一致地描述低能 $K^-d$ 相互作用。同时，$\bar{K}N$ 相互作用的同位旋矢量分量（isovector component）本身约束不足，而新的 kaonic 原子实验测量有望为此提供额外信息。

Method: 将 OR 与 BCN 手征 $\bar{K}N$ 相互作用模型作为输入，在 Faddeev 方程的固定中心近似（Fixed-Center Approximation）框架下构建三体 $K^-d$ 振幅，通过纳入相干多重散射过程实现幺正约束。该方法被推广至耦合道（coupled channels），包含电荷交换过程，并通过使用物理粒子质量引入同位旋破缺（isospin-breaking）效应。最后用 bootstrap 分析检验 BCN 模型与 SIDDHARTA-2 测量结果的相容性。

Result: 所构建的框架可一致地描述低能 $K^-d$ 振幅，并据此确定散射长度（scattering length）与有效力程（effective range）。与 SIDDHARTA-2 的 kaonic 氢和 kaonic 氘测量数据进行相容性检验后，BCN 参数化被重新调整，该调整在若干对同位旋敏感的 $\bar{K}N$ 散射可观测量中产生明显效应。

Conclusion: 新的 kaonic 原子测量数据为更好地约束 $\bar{K}N$ 相互作用中了解甚少的同位旋矢量分量提供了额外信息，并可用于进一步限定 $\bar{K}N$ 相互作用模型的参数化。

Abstract: We study the low-energy interaction of negative kaons with deuterons using chiral $\bar{K}N$ interaction models (OR and BCN) as input. The three-body $K^-d$ amplitude is constructed within the Fixed-Center Approximation to the Faddeev equations, where the unitary constraints missing in the conventional approach are formally implemented with the incorporation of coherent multiple-scattering processes. The unitary procedure is extended to coupled channels including charge-exchange processes, as well as isospin-breaking effects through the use of physical particle masses. This provides a consistent description of the low-energy $K^-d$ amplitude and allows for the determination of the scattering length and the effective range. The resulting framework is then used to assess the compatibility of the BCN $\bar{K}N$ interaction model with the recent SIDDHARTA-2 measurements of kaonic hydrogen and kaonic deuterium through a bootstrap analysis. The procedure leads to a readjustment of the BCN parameterization which produces visible effects in several isospin-sensitive $\bar{K}N$ scattering observables, hence highlighting the additional information provided by the new kaonic-atom measurements to better constrain the poorly known isovector component of the ${\bar K}N$ interaction.

</details>


### [140] [A consistent $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD description of decays and high-$P_T$ prompt production of charmonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02096)
*Valerio Bertone,Jean-Philippe Lansberg,Kate Lynch,Tanjona R. Rabemananjara*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于 LHC 高 $P_T$ 数据，在 NRQCD 框架下至 $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ 全局确定六个长程矩阵元（LDMEs），并验证其能良好描述 $J/\psi$、$\psi(2S)$ 与 $\chi_c$ 的大 $P_T$ 强子产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确描述 $J/\psi$ 等高 $P_T$ 夸克偶素产生需确定非相对论量子色动力学（NRQCD）中的长程矩阵元（LDMEs），而此前对多个态及极化数据的全局约束尚不充分。

Method: 采用 NLO+NLL 精度的领头功率碎裂框架（leading-power fragmentation framework），拟合 ATLAS 与 CMS 的 $\psi(2S)/J/\psi$ 产额比及 CMS 在 $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV 的 $\psi(2S)$ 极化数据，并对每个粲偶素引入一个衰变宽度。

Result: 拟合所得 LDMEs 能极好预言 ATLAS 与 CMS 直至 $P_T=360$ GeV 的 $J/\psi$ 截面数据，以及 CMS 的 $J/\psi$ 极化数据。

Conclusion: 与产生及衰变数据的高度一致性表明，$\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ 的 NRQCD 足以描述 $J/\psi$、$\psi(2S)$ 与 $\chi_c$ 在大 $P_T$ 下的强子产生。

Abstract: We present a global determination of the six long-distance matrix elements (LDMEs) within non-relativistic QCD (NRQCD) appearing in the production of prompt $J/ψ$ up to $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ using high-$P_T$ LHC data for direct and feed-down contributions from $χ_c$ and $ψ(2S)$. Using a leading-power fragmentation framework at NLO+NLL, we fit $ψ(2S)/J/ψ$ yield ratios from ATLAS and CMS and $ψ(2S)$ CMS polarization data at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV, injecting one decay width per charmonium. The fit LDMEs provide excellent predictions of the CMS and ATLAS cross-section data up to $P_T=360$ GeV for the $J/ψ$, as well as the $J/ψ$ polarization data from CMS. The obtained excellent agreement with production and decay data demonstrates that $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD is sufficient to describe $J/ψ$, $ψ(2S)$, and $χ_c$ hadroproduction at large $P_T$.

</details>


### [141] [The Scalar MSW Effect: Compact analytical formulas for neutrino oscillations with large matter effects, including $μ-μ$ and $τ-τ$ dominant matter potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02107)
*Sandhya Choubey,Andreas Lund*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文扩展了 Jacobi 对角化方法，通过对 PMNS 混合矩阵采用特定 Euler 旋转参数化，导出物质中三味中微子振荡（neutrino oscillation）的有效混合参数映射，并首次系统应用于标量非标准相互作用（scalar non-standard interactions, SNSI）。结果显示所有对角 SNSI 耦合均可导致类似 MSW 效应的能量无关共振增强，即标量 MSW（scalar MSW, SMSW）效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推导简单且精确的物质中三味中微子振荡概率公式，对于理解未来实验的物理现象学（phenomenology）至关重要，而现有方法在处理奇异物质势时适用性有限。

Method: 扩展 Jacobi 对角化方法，其核心是对 PMNS 混合矩阵采用特定排列的 Euler 旋转（Euler rotations）进行参数化，从而可导出奇异物质势下的有效混合参数映射；先以标准物质效应为例验证推广，再应用于 SNSI 的三种单对角耦合情形。

Result: 导出的公式表明，所有对角 SNSI 耦合均可产生 SMSW 效应，即类似 MSW 效应的能量无关共振增强；对当代及未来实验的精度检验显示，δm_ee 情形精度优异，而 δm_μμ 与 δm_ττ 情形因需忽略 A_CC 而精度较差，需引入一阶微扰修正加以改善。

Conclusion: 该推广方法可为奇异物质势提供简洁精确的有效参数映射，并揭示对角 SNSI 耦合的共振增强特性；后续工作需借助一阶微扰修正提升部分耦合情形的精度。

Abstract: Deriving simple and accurate three-flavor neutrino oscillation probability formulas in matter is critical for understanding the phenomenology of upcoming experiments. In this article, we extend the Jacobi diagonalization method for deriving effective parameter mappings for neutrino oscillations in matter. The key insight behind the extension is to purposefully parameterize the mixing matrix, $U_{\text{PMNS}}$, using particular arrangements of Euler rotations. This novel extension allows for deriving simple and accurate effective mixing parameter mappings for exotic types of matter potentials. First, we revisit the Jacobi diagonalization method for neutrino oscillations with standard matter effects and use this to demonstrate our generalization. Subsequently, we apply the Jacobi diagonalization method to scalar non-standard interactions (SNSI) and derive effective parameter mappings for all three single diagonal coupling cases. The resulting formulas reveal that all diagonal SNSI couplings could give rise to a scalar MSW (SMSW) effect, that is an energy independent resonant enhancement analogous to the MSW effect. Finally, we study the accuracy of the derived SNSI formulas for various contemporary and upcoming experiments. For $δm_{ee}$, the accuracy is excellent for all considered experiments. For $δm_{μμ}$ and $δm_{ττ}$ the accuracy is worse since $A_{CC}$ must be neglected, and first order perturbative corrections are applied to amend this.

</details>


### [142] [Drell-Yan lepton pair production from low to high transverse momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02169)
*Benoît Assi,Enrico Bothmann,John Campbell,Christian Gütschow,Stefan Höche,Wan-Li Ju,Max Knobbe,Marek Schönherr,Jesse Thaler,Michael L. Wagman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种在粒子层面（particle-level）计算Drell-Yan轻子对产生的技术，在中小横向动量区域达到N³LO与近似N⁴LL′精度，并整合了高横向动量下的电弱修正与多喷注树图精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Drell-Yan过程的高精度理论预言需要同时兼顾不同运动学区域，而横向动量分布涉及非微扰输入，且高精度计算结果难以直接转化为可用的正定事件权重。

Method: 采用非微扰的Collins-Soper核（Collins-Soper kernel），其数值来自连续极限格点QCD（continuum-limit lattice QCD）结果；并基于最大熵原理（maximum entropy principle）的信息论方法，将高精度理论结果重构为严格为正的事件权重。

Result: 在中小横向动量区域实现N³LO与近似N⁴LL′精度；对Z+4、5、6喷注达到树图精度，并在高横向动量区域实现近似NLO电弱（EW）精度。

Conclusion: 该技术将微扰与非微扰计算、多喷注与电弱修正整合于统一的粒子层面框架，并通过信息论方法输出正定事件权重，适用于事件生成器级别的高精度模拟。

Abstract: We present a technique for computing Drell-Yan lepton pair production at particle-level that achieves N$^3$LO and approximate N$^4$LL$'$ accuracy at small to moderate transverse momentum, tree-level precision for $Z+4$, $5$, and $6$ jets, and approximate NLO EW precision at high transverse momentum. A crucial non-perturbative input, the Collins-Soper kernel, is obtained from continuum-limit lattice QCD results. High-precision theory results are recast into strictly positive event weights using an information-theoretic approach based on the maximum entropy principle.

</details>


### [143] [Dark-State Interference in an Effective Confined Three-Level Color System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00146)
*S. D. Campos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受量子色动力学（QCD）启发的有效模型，用暗态干涉（dark-state interference）描述受限三态系统中的色电响应，并借助马尔可夫型 Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad 主方程（Markovian GKSL master equation）给出稳态探针磁化率被强烈压制的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的在于在量子光学中的暗态干涉与受限色动力学（confined color dynamics）之间建立受控的有效联系，而非进行第一原理 QCD 计算。该动机源于将电磁诱导透明（EIT）类机制在色电自由度上的类比推广。

Method: 模型包含两个低能 \(Q\overline{Q}\) 态 \(|1\rangle\)、\(|2\rangle\) 与一个胶子激发的杂化态（hybrid state）\(|H\rangle\)；未微扰哈密顿量含静态色电通量管（chromoelectric flux-tube）背景，时变色电微扰以 \(\Lambda\) 型构型耦合三态。在双"光子"共振条件下，采用马尔可夫 GKSL 主方程求解动力学。

Result: 系统形成与 \(|H\rangle\) 解耦的相干叠加态，当低态退相干（lower-state decoherence）趋于消失时，稳态探针磁化率的虚部 \(\operatorname{Im}\chi_{\text{CE}}\) 被强烈压制，即出现透明窗口。唯象的非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）杂化能修正主要使透明窗口移动并改变其形状，而非产生干涉本身。

Conclusion: 该模型并非对介质不透明度的第一原理 QCD 计算，而是量子光学暗态干涉与约化受限色动力学之间的受控有效桥梁；唯象非阿贝尔修正仅起修饰透明窗口的作用。

Abstract: In this work, a QCD-inspired effective model describes dark-state interference in a confined three-state system with two low-energy $Q\overline{Q}$ states, $|1\rangle$ and $|2\rangle$, and a gluon-excited hybrid state, $|H\rangle$. A stationary chromoelectric flux-tube background enters the unperturbed Hamiltonian, while a time-dependent chromoelectric perturbation couples $|1\rangle$ and $|2\rangle$ to $|H\rangle$ in a $Λ$-type configuration. Under two-photon\footnote{We retain standard terms like ``two-photon'' from the EIT literature only by mathematical analogy, but they do not refer to electromagnetic photons but to the effective frequencies and energy mismatches of chromoelectric perturbations and gluonic excitations that connect confined states.} resonance and within a Markovian Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad master equation, the system forms a coherent superposition that decouples from $|H\rangle$, strongly suppressing the steady-state probe susceptibility $\operatorname{Im}χ_{\text{CE}}$ as lower-state decoherence vanishes. A phenomenological non-Abelian correction to the hybrid energy mainly shifts and reshapes the transparency window rather than creating the interference itself. The model is not a first-principles QCD calculation of medium opacity but a controlled effective link between dark-state interference in quantum optics and reduced confined color dynamics.

</details>


### [144] [The Great Wall of the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00461)
*Yang Bai,Yiming Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 摘要探讨标准模型（Standard Model, SM）规范群全局形式未定的问题：当商群取 Γ=ℤ_p 时，SM 具有残余整体电一形式对称性（electric one-form symmetry）ℤ_{6/p}；在热圆上紧致化后产生可自发破缺的离散零形式对称性（zero-form symmetry），进而可能形成宇宙畴壁（domain walls）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型规范群的全局形式 [SU(3)_c×SU(2)_L×U(1)_Y]/Γ（Γ⊆ℤ_6）尚未被确定，需要寻找可观测的宇宙学效应以区分不同商群（quotient）选择。

Method: 计算电弱能标以下 ℤ_{6/p} 畴壁的温度依赖张力（tension）与宽度（width）；并开发一个唯象的温度依赖、速度依赖的单尺度模型（velocity-dependent one-scale model），纳入温度依赖的表面张力、惯性与等离子体拖曳（plasma drag）以追踪网络演化。

Result: 除 Γ=ℤ_6 外每个商群均存在该类畴壁；基准情形下其厚度在再电离时期（reionization）附近可达千米量级；若形成足够早，基准估计在太初核合成（Big Bang nucleosynthesis, BBN）起始附近壁能量分数约为 10^{-14} 量级，并在更晚时期进一步被压低。不确定性来自表面自由能与物理壁能量的关系，以及晚期等离子体演化与结构形成。

Conclusion: 这些宏观热界面若产生即构成标准模型的“长城”（"Great Walls"）；尽管能量分数极小，它们仍可探测 SM 规范群的全局结构，值得针对其宇宙学信号开展专门搜寻。

Abstract: The global form of the Standard Model (SM) gauge group, $[SU(3)_c\times SU(2)_L\times U(1)_Y]/Γ$, with $Γ\subseteq\mathbb{Z}_6$, remains undetermined. For $Γ=\mathbb{Z}_p$, the SM possesses a residual global electric one-form symmetry $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$. Compactification on the thermal circle yields an ordinary discrete zero-form symmetry whose spontaneous breaking can produce cosmological domain walls. We calculate the temperature-dependent tension and width of the $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$ domain walls below the electroweak scale, which exist for every quotient except $Γ=\mathbb{Z}_6$, finding that their thickness can reach the kilometer scale around reionization in our benchmarks. These macroscopic thermal interfaces, if produced, would constitute the "Great Walls" of the Standard Model. We develop a phenomenological thermal velocity-dependent one-scale model to follow their network evolution, incorporating temperature-dependent surface tension, inertia, and plasma drag. For sufficiently early formation, our benchmarks give an estimated wall energy fraction of order $10^{-14}$ near the onset of Big Bang nucleosynthesis, with further suppression at later times. Uncertainties arise from relating surface free energy to physical wall energy and from late-time plasma evolution and structure formation. Despite their small energy fraction, these walls could probe the global structure of the SM gauge group, motivating dedicated searches for their cosmological signatures.

</details>


### [145] [Fractional anomalous determinants and the chiral phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00472)
*Robert D. Pisarski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个显式“超越朗道（beyond Landau）”的协同模型，探讨QCD类理论中反常U(1)_A对称性破缺与手征相变（chiral phase transition）阶数，并给出可经格点QCD检验的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温下瞬子（instanton）形成稀薄气体，反常U(1)_A破缺由反常行列式的整数次幂给出；作者希望将这一图像推广至手征相变温度以下及任意N_f/N_c情形，并澄清手征相变的阶数问题。

Method: 构造协同模型：在手征极限且T>T_χ时仅保留反常行列式的整数次幂；在T_χ以下假设拓扑荷Q为分数（fractional），即整数乘以1/N_c，从而在手征有效拉格朗日量中引入反常行列式的分数次幂，并推广Witten-Veneziano项至任意N_f/N_c。

Result: 模型预言手征相变一般为二阶（second order）；例外为单味（可能为跨越crossover）与三味（可能为弱一阶）。提出用2+1味格点QCD，在T_χ至约2T_χ区间比较π^a与a_0^a传播子差值的温度依赖与奇异夸克手征凝聚，加以检验；1至4味简并情形亦可作类似测量。最后提出对称相中重子数（baryon number）的算符。

Conclusion: 分数拓扑荷与反常行列式分数次幂的引入，使有效拉格朗日量超越传统朗道框架，并对相变阶数给出可证伪的格点预言；同时给出对称相中重子数算符的构造建议。

Abstract: At high temperature instantons form a dilute gas, so in QCD-like theories the breaking of the anomalous $U(1)_A$ symmetry is given by integral powers of the anomalous determinant, $\sim (\det Φ)^{Q}$, where $Φ\sim \overline{q}_L q_R$ is bilinear in the quark fields, and with untwisted boundary conditions, the topological charge, $Q$, is an integer. A syncretic model is constructed, which is manifestly "beyond Landau". In the chiral limit, at temperatures above the chiral phase transition, $T > T_χ$, only integral powers of the anomalous determinant appear. Below $T_χ$, following 't Hooft et al. I assume that the topological charge $Q$ is fractional, as an integer times $1/N_c$, where $N_c$ is the number of colors. I suggest that consequently, fractional powers of the anomalous determinant appear in the chiral effective Lagrangian. For $N_f$ degenerate flavors, this generalizes the Witten-Veneziano term, valid for small $N_f/N_c$, to arbitrary $N_f/N_c$. In this model the chiral phase transition is generically of second order. The two exceptions are for one flavor, where it is probably crossover, and three flavors, where it could well be weakly first order. This can be tested in lattice QCD with $2+1$ flavors by comparing the (known) temperature dependence of the difference of the $π^a$ and $a_0^a$ propagators, to the chiral condensate of the strange quark, between $T_χ$ and $\sim 2 \, T_χ$. Analogous measurements are possible for one to four degenerate flavors about $T_χ$. Lastly, I propose an operator for baryon number in the symmetric phase.

</details>


### [146] [Glueballs and fractional anomalous determinants at nonzero $θ$, and the decays of the X(2370)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00484)
*Francesco Giacosa,Shahriyar Jafarzade,Győző Kovács,Péter Kovács,Robert D. Pisarski,Fabian Rennecke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用分数反常行列式（fractional anomalous determinants）构建 SU(3) 规范理论在非零 θ 角（θ angle）下的迹反常与轴反常（trace and axial anomalies）模型，通过标量与赝标量胶球（glueball）耦合来描述，并与格点（lattice）数据及 BESIII 实验测量进行对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立统一描述规范理论迹反常与轴反常的有效框架，从而刻画 θ 依赖的真空能量、胶球性质以及胶球候选态的多体衰变，弥补反常与胶球谱之间联系的缺失。

Method: 采用分数反常行列式，将 SU(3) 规范理论的迹反常与轴反常通过标量及赝标量胶球的耦合进行建模；并在纯规范理论与 QCD 两种情形下分别讨论其物理后果。

Result: 在纯规范理论中复现了格点给出的真空能量 θ 依赖与标量胶球质量，并估算了 θ=π 处一阶相变（first-order transition）伴随的拓扑荷密度（topological charge density）跃变。在 QCD 中，赝标量胶球与分数反常行列式的耦合描述了 BESIII 测量的 0^{-+} 胶球候选态 X(2370) 的全部四个三赝标量衰变通道。

Conclusion: 该模型为胶球候选态 X(2370) 的三赝标量衰变提供了统一解释；测量 ηηη 与 ηηη' 的衰变率可对模型构成严格检验（stringent test）。

Abstract: Using fractional anomalous determinants, we construct a model of the trace and axial anomalies of a $SU(3)$ gauge theory at nonzero $θ$ angle through the couplings to scalar and pseudoscalar glueballs. In the pure gauge theory, we reproduce the $θ$ dependence of the vacuum energy and the scalar-glueball mass from the lattice, and estimate the jump in the topological charge density for the first-order transition at $θ= π$. In QCD, the coupling of a pseudoscalar glueball to fractional anomalous determinants describes all of the four three-pseudoscalar decay channels of the $0^{-+}$ glueball candidate $X(2370)$ measured by the BESIII collaboration. Measuring the decay rates for $ηηη$ and $ηηη'$ can provide a stringent test of the model.

</details>


### [147] [Analyticity Bootstrap of Feynman Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01154)
*Xiang Li,Dao-Ming Mu,Yan-Qing Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出解析性 bootstrap（analyticity bootstrap）方法，利用费曼积分（Feynman integrals, FIs）泰勒展开区域（analytic region）的有限性约束约化系数，从而以极少积分分部（integration-by-parts, IBP）样本完成积分约化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费曼积分的约化（reduction）通常依赖大量 IBP 关系，计算成本高。本文试图找到一种更高效的约化途径，利用积分本身的解析结构而非纯粹代数恒等式来约束约化系数。

Method: 核心观察是维度正规化（dimensionally regularized）的费曼积分在任意运动学点存在泰勒展开区域，该区域有限性对约化系数（运动学变量的有理函数）形成强约束。方法为写出与奇异性结构相容的最一般拟设（ansatz），再用少量 IBP 样本或渐近展开（asymptotic expansions）固定剩余参数，并在单变量与多变量情形系统展开。

Result: 在单变量与多变量情形给出了大量显式算例；发现主积分（master integrals）的微分方程（differential equations, DEs）可确定全部解析性约束，反之这些约束也有助于构建 DEs；在辅助质量流（auxiliary mass flow）方法中，DEs 的构建成本显著降低，甚至可完全不使用 IBP 而完全确定。

Conclusion: 解析性约束与微分方程之间存在双向关系，二者可互相促进构建。该方法为费曼积分约化提供了低成本的替代框架，并可用于辅助质量流方法中 DEs 的全新构造方式。

Abstract: We propose an analyticity bootstrap method to determine the reduction of Feynman integrals (FIs). The key observation is that, at any kinematic point, a dimensionally regularized FI possesses a Taylor expansion region (analytic region), and the finiteness of the Taylor region strongly constrains the integral reduction coefficients, which are rational functions of the kinematic variables. By writing the most general ansatz compatible with the singularity structure and then fixing the remaining parameters with only a few integration-by-parts (IBP) samples or asymptotic expansions, one can obtain the full reduction. We develop the method systematically in both single-variable and multi-variable cases, with many explicit examples. Interestingly, the differential equations (DEs) of the master integrals determine all these analyticity constraints and, conversely, the constraints facilitate the construction of the DEs. In particular, we show that the DEs in the auxiliary mass flow method can be constructed at significantly reduced cost and can even be fully determined without using IBP.

</details>


### [148] [Generalized fluctuation-dissipation theorem and Einstein relation in rotating equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01797)
*Shuo Fang,Shi Pu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文从精确的相空间KMS关系出发，为旋转热平衡中的矢量场推导出广义涨落耗散定理（FDT），揭示旋转诱导的张量贡献、新型爱因斯坦关系及零点模机制，并应用于重夸克偶素的轨道极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在旋转量子物质（rotating quantum matter）研究中，旋转热平衡下的涨落与耗散关系缺乏一般性的微观约束。论文旨在建立适用于矢量场的广义FDT，以刻画旋转对输运与谱结构的非平凡影响。

Method: 从精确的相空间Kubo-Martin-Schwinger（KMS）关系出发，推导旋转热平衡下矢量场的广义涨落耗散定理；并在局域输运极限下分析热涡度（thermal vorticity）二阶展开及谱函数零点模。

Result: 所得FDT在标量热因子之外含旋转诱导的张量贡献；局域极限下给出含旋转的广义爱因斯坦关系，对称动量扩散在热涡度二阶依赖超出耗散拖曳所编码的低频谱信息；发现连接谱函数零点模与非旋转拖曳系数的新爱因斯坦型关系，零点模构成重夸克偶素轨道极化的新机制；FDT还给出修正的细致平衡关系，其一阶涡旋修正由跃迁极化决定且不显式依赖浴模型。

Conclusion: 研究建立了旋转量子物质中微观平衡约束，所获广义FDT、爱因斯坦型关系与修正细致平衡关系具有模型独立性，可推广至多个物理领域的研究。

Abstract: We derive a generalized fluctuation-dissipation theorem (FDT) for vector fields in rotating thermal equilibrium from an exact phase-space Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) relation. The resulting FDT contains rotation-induced tensorial contributions beyond a scalar thermal factor. In the local transport limit, we obtain a model-independent generalized Einstein relation with rotation in which, through second order in thermal vorticity, symmetric momentum diffusion depends on low-frequency spectral information beyond that encoded in the rotation-dependent dissipative drag. Remarkably, we find a new Einstein-type relation linking zero modes in the spectral function to the nonrotating drag coefficient. These zero modes underlie a new mechanism for the orbital polarization of heavy quarkonium. The same FDT constraint yields a modified detailed-balance relation whose first-order vortical correction is governed by transition polarization, without explicit dependence on the bath model. Our findings establish microscopic equilibrium constraints for studying rotating quantum matter in various fields.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

### [149] [Emergence of electronic modes and triplet pairing from spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulators in the Kanamori-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00110)
*Masanori Kohno*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过双轨道 Kanamori-Hubbard 模型（two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model）研究自旋为 1 的反铁磁绝缘体（antiferromagnetic insulator）在掺杂与温度变化下能带结构的演化，揭示了由自旋、电荷与轨道自由度耦合产生的、具有动量偏移自旋色散关系的涌现电子模式（emergent electronic modes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能带绝缘体（band insulator）在掺杂或升温时能带结构基本不变，而 Hubbard 与 Kondo 晶格模型等描述的 Mott 与 Kondo 绝缘体中会从带边向能隙内出现动量偏移的电子模式。本文旨在厘清多轨道、大自旋情形下这类涌现模式出现的机制与成因，填补对轨道简并体系中能带随掺杂与温度演化的系统理解。

Method: 采用双轨道 Kanamori-Hubbard 模型作为研究框架，结合弱跃迁有效理论（weak-hopping effective theory）、选择定则分析（selection-rule analysis）与数值计算三种手段，对涌现电子模式的性质进行判定，并考察掺杂、温度以及非平衡态下自旋或电荷扰动的影响。

Result: 在典型参数区间内，掺杂诱导的模式在各轨道分别对应不同类型的自旋模式，而温度诱导的模式在两个轨道中主要反映低能自旋模式，两者均表现出动量偏移的自旋模式色散关系；当轨道间跃迁参数符号不同时，涌现模式的动量区间可在轨道间存在差异。轨道间排斥较小时，掺杂后可形成在位自旋三重态配对（onsite spin-triplet pair），产生破对能隙（pair-breaking gap），而温度诱导的电子模式仍反映低能自旋模式且不伴随破对能隙。

Conclusion: 研究给出了大自旋反铁磁绝缘体中自旋、电荷与轨道自由度相互作用导致涌现电子模式的整体图景，并阐明自旋或电荷扰动下非平衡态中此类模式的出现及其特征，为在强关联体系中探索非常规量子现象提供了新路径。

Abstract: The electronic band structures of conventional band insulators generally remain unchanged under doping via chemical-potential shifts or increasing temperature. However, in Mott and Kondo insulators described by models such as the Hubbard and Kondo lattice models, distinct electronic modes with momentum-shifted magnetic dispersion relations have been shown to emerge from band edges into the gap. Here, by investigating the two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model, this study elucidates how and why the band structures of spin-1 antiferromagnetic insulators change with doping and temperature, revealing emergent spectral features characteristic of orbitally degenerate systems. The nature of the emergent electronic modes is clarified using weak-hopping effective theory, selection-rule analysis, and numerical calculations. In a typical parameter regime, the doping-induced modes in each orbital reflect distinct types of spin modes, while the temperature-induced modes primarily reflect the low-energy spin mode in both orbitals; in both cases, the emergent modes exhibit momentum-shifted spin-mode dispersion relations. The momentum regimes of these emergent modes can differ between orbitals if signs of the hopping parameters differ. For small inter-orbital repulsion, onsite spin-triplet pairs can form after doping, leading to a pair-breaking gap, while the temperature-induced electronic modes continue to reflect the low-energy spin mode without a pair-breaking gap. Moreover, the emergence and characteristics of electronic modes in nonequilibrium states by spin or charge perturbations are elucidated. These findings provide a comprehensive understanding of how the interplay among the spin, charge, and orbital degrees of freedom in large-spin antiferromagnetic insulators leads to emergent electronic modes, opening avenues for exploring unconventional quantum phenomena in strongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [150] [Causality violations in cluster GW+DMFT: Exact Lehmann moments and the necessity of non-local vertex corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00324)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过对最小哈伯德团簇（Hubbard cluster）进行严格对角化（exact diagonalization），该工作严格检验了 GW 与 GW+DMFT 自能（self-energy）的解析结构，证明二者在阻挫团簇上均违反松原因果性条件（Matsubara causality condition），并揭示局域自能修正不足以恢复因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW 近似已知会破坏因果性（causality），在复频率上半平面产生非物理极点；GW+DMFT 通过注入精确局域自能试图修正该问题，但其有效性缺乏严格基准检验。该研究旨在严格检验这些方法的解析结构。

Method: 在最小哈伯德团簇上使用精确对角化，对 GW 与 GW+DMFT 自能的解析结构进行基准测试；以无插值的实轴精确自能评估作为主要因果性证书（causality certificate），有理延拓（rational continuation）作为辅助证据；并解析计算精确的 Lehmann 矩（Lehmann moments）。

Result: 发现“二聚体反常（dimer anomaly）”：在两格点二聚体上，反演对称性迫使非局域自能为纯实数，掩盖了因果性破坏。在非二分的三格点哈伯德环（hopping frustration 导致非局域自能出现有限虚部）上，证明 GW 与 GW+DMFT 均违反松原因果性条件。推导出高频尾部闭式表达式 C = U²·Var(d)·I，并表明标准方法无法捕捉执行该条件所需的精确等时双粒子关联函数（equal-time two-particle correlator）。

Conclusion: 控制高频尾部的等时关联函数正是编码于全不可约顶点（fully irreducible vertex）Λ 的高频渐近中的那些；中频分析表明 GW+DMFT 中仅局域的 Λ 截断未修正非局域 RPA 共振。两个诊断量在逻辑上相互独立：GW+DMFT 满足必要的高频条件 C⪰0，却仍在有限频率处违反松原符号条件。局域自能修正不足以保证阻挫团簇上的因果解析结构。

Abstract: The $GW$ approximation is known to violate causality, yielding self-energies with unphysical poles in the upper half of the complex frequency plane. The combined $GW$+DMFT approach attempts to cure this by injecting the exact local self-energy. Using exact diagonalization on minimal Hubbard clusters, we rigorously benchmark the analytic structure of the $GW$ and $GW$+DMFT self-energies. We demonstrate a ``dimer anomaly'': on the two-site dimer, inversion symmetry forces the non-local self-energy to be purely real, masking the causality violation. Moving to the non-bipartite three-site Hubbard ring, where hopping frustration yields a finite imaginary part in the non-local self-energy, we prove that both $GW$ and $GW$+DMFT violate the Matsubara causality condition. By analytically evaluating the exact Lehmann moments, we derive a closed-form expression for the high-frequency tail of the self-energy, $C = U^2\,\mathrm{Var}(d)\,I$, and show that standard methods fail to capture the exact equal-time two-particle correlators required to enforce it. The equal-time correlators that govern this tail are precisely those encoded in the high-frequency asymptotics of the fully irreducible vertex $Λ$; nevertheless, our intermediate-frequency analysis demonstrates that the local-only truncation of $Λ$ in $GW$+DMFT leaves non-local RPA resonances uncorrected. The two diagnostics are logically independent: $GW$+DMFT satisfies the necessary high-frequency condition $C\succeq0$ yet still violates the Matsubara sign condition at finite frequencies. Taking the interpolation-free real-axis evaluation of the exact self-energy as the primary causality certificate, with rational continuation as supporting evidence, we establish a rigorous benchmark demonstrating that local self-energy corrections are insufficient to guarantee a causal analytic structure on frustrated clusters.

</details>


### [151] [Anyon Proliferation and Anyon Superconductivity in Higgsing Transitions via Conformal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00452)
*Diego García-Sepúlveda,Da-Chuan Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于共形嵌入（conformal embedding）讨论 (2+1) 维 Chern-Simons Higgsing 相变及其与任意子（anyon）增殖的关系；这类相变在缩小规范群的同时保持或扩大内禀拓扑序（intrinsic topological order），并给出 $SO(N)_2 \hookrightarrow SU(N)_1$ 家族与 $\mathrm{Spin}(16)_1 \to \mathrm{Spin}(9)_2$ 的具体构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Chern-Simons Higgsing 相变如何在规范群缩小的情况下仍保持或扩大内禀拓扑序，并厘清其与任意子凝聚（anyon condensation）及任意子增殖的联系。

Method: 以共形嵌入为基础分析 (2+1) 维 Chern-Simons Higgsing 相变；考察 $SO(N)_2 \hookrightarrow SU(N)_1$（$N \geq 3$）家族，指出标量表示相对 UV Chern-Simons 能级非整可积（non-integrable），并借助启发式伴随屏蔽（adjoint screening）提出未 Higgs 相的候选任意子，通过 Higgs 子群下的分支（branching）识别可凝聚任意子（condensable anyon）对应的通道。此外构造 $\mathrm{Spin}(16)_1 \to \mathrm{Spin}(9)_2$ 相变及其可凝聚代数（condensable algebra）。

Result: 拓扑序被扩大时两相由任意子凝聚区分，相变将阿贝尔拓扑序（Abelian topological order）变为非阿贝尔拓扑序（non-Abelian topological order）。在 $SO(N)_2 \hookrightarrow SU(N)_1$ 家族中，两个半经典区间具有等价的内禀拓扑序但全局 $U(1)$ 对称性的实现不同；对奇数 $N$，这对应于从费米子 $1/N$ Laughlin 态到电荷 $2e$ 任意子超导的相变，二者共存相同的 chiral $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 拓扑序。$\mathrm{Spin}(16)_1 \to \mathrm{Spin}(9)_2$ 的可凝聚代数包含一个非阿贝尔任意子，但其自融合（self-fusion）并未包含全部允许的可规范（gaugable）通道。

Conclusion: 共形嵌入为描述保持或扩大内禀拓扑序的 Chern-Simons Higgsing 相变提供了框架；标量表示的非整可积性使其不能直接标记未 Higgs 相的任意子，需借助伴随屏蔽与 Higgs 子群分支来确定相变两侧的任意子谱与凝聚结构。

Abstract: We discuss (2+1)d Chern-Simons Higgsing transitions based on conformal embeddings and their relation to the proliferation of anyons. These transitions preserve or enlarge the intrinsic topological order despite reducing the gauge group. When the topological order is enlarged, the two phases differ by anyon condensation, and the transition takes an Abelian topological order to a non-Abelian one. In the $SO(N)_{2} \hookrightarrow SU(N)_{1}$ family with $N \geq 3$, the two semiclassical regimes have equivalent intrinsic topological orders but distinct realizations of a global $U(1)$ symmetry. For odd $N$, these are non-Abelian realizations of transitions from fermionic $1/N$ Laughlin states to charge-$2e$ anyon superconductivity coexisting with the same chiral $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ topological order. The scalar representations are always non-integrable with respect to the UV Chern-Simons level and therefore do not directly label anyons of the un-Higgsed phase, despite defining Wilson lines in the UV. Using heuristic adjoint screening, we propose candidate anyons in the un-Higgsed phase, while branching under the Higgs subgroup reveals channels corresponding to condensable anyons of the Higgs phase. We further construct a $\mathrm{Spin}(16)_{1} \to \mathrm{Spin}(9)_{2}$ transition admitting a condensable algebra that contains a non-Abelian anyon but not all of the allowed gaugable channels in its self-fusion.

</details>


### [152] [Near-infrared magnetic linear and non-reciprocal directional dichroism in the chiral antiferromagnet SmFe3(BO3)4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00534)
*B. Beke,B. Szász,I. A. Gudim,D. Szaller,S. Bordács*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过偏振分辨磁光光谱研究了反铁磁序对SmFe3(BO3)4近红外光学响应的影响，观测到4f-4f跃迁的磁线性二色性和非互易方向二色性，并实现磁场调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic order, AFM order）如何影响稀土铁硼酸盐的光学响应，并寻找探测反铁磁序参量面内取向的光学手段。

Method: 采用偏振分辨磁光光谱（polarization-resolved magneto-optical spectroscopy）测量SmFe3(BO3)4在近红外波段的响应，并通过磁场旋转易平面反铁磁基态的序参量。

Result: 在奈尔温度（Néel temperature）以下，Sm3+的4f-4f跃迁出现明显的磁线性二色性（magnetic linear dichroism）；磁场可调控该线性二色性，并据此推导出偏振选择定则；若干4f-4f跃迁还表现出非互易方向二色性（non-reciprocal directional dichroism），其非互易吸收出现在面内磁场垂直于二重旋转轴时的环面几何（toroidal geometry）中。

Conclusion: 结果表明电偶极与磁偶极激发之间存在强干涉；所观测的光学各向异性为探测反铁磁序的面内取向提供了可行途径，可用于稀土铁硼酸盐中反铁磁序参量的成像与时间分辨研究。

Abstract: We investigated the influence of antiferromagnetic (AFM) order on the near-infrared optical re- sponse of SmFe3(BO3)4 by polarization-resolved, magneto-optical spectroscopy. Below the Néel temperature, we observed pronounced magnetic linear dichroism at the 4f -4f transitions of Sm3+. By rotating the order parameter of the easy-plane AFM ground state using magnetic fields, we demonstrated the magnetic field control of linear dichroism, and deduced polarization selection rules. In addition, we detected non-reciprocal directional dichroism for several 4f -4f transitions. The non-reciprocal absorption appears in the symmetry-allowed toroidal geometry when the in- plane magnetic field is normal to one of the two-fold rotation axes. These results indicate a strong interference between electric- and magnetic-dipole excitations for transitions showing non-reciprocal absorption. The observed optical anisotropies provide a promising path to detect the in-plane orien- tation of the AFM order, enabling imaging and time-resolved studies of the AFM order parameter in rare-earth ferroborates.

</details>


### [153] [Mechanism of Incommensurate Magnetic Order in $\mathrm{BaCo_2(AsO_4)_2}$: Interplay of Frustrated Further-Neighbor Exchanges and Bond-Directional Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00626)
*Mohammad-Hossein Zare,Mehdi Biderang,Hamid Mosadeq*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过拓展的 \(J_1\text{--}K\text{--}Γ\text{--}Γ'\text{--}J_2\text{--}J_3\) 模型，结合第一性原理、Luttinger-Tisza 方法与精确对角化，证明 \(\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2\) 的零场不可公度磁序无需异常强的 Kitaev 耦合，而源于交换阻挫与键各向异性的协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝状钴酸盐 \(\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2\) 零场不可公度磁序（incommensurate magnetic order）的微观机制尚未解决，核心争议在于键方向性 Kitaev 型相互作用与交换阻挫（exchange frustration）的相对贡献。

Method: 构建含 XXZ 型交换各向异性的拓展 \(J_1\text{--}K\text{--}Γ\text{--}Γ'\text{--}J_2\text{--}J_3\) 模型，最近邻参数由从头算电子结构计算约束；结合解析的 Luttinger-Tisza 方法与扭曲边界条件下的精确对角化（exact diagonalization），绘制 \((J_2, J_3)\) 参数空间的经典与量子相图。

Result: 实验观测的沿 \(Γ\to M\) 方向传播的不可公度螺旋相，其稳定化不需要占主导的 Kitaev 耦合，而是由进一步邻耦合（\(J_2, J_3\)）驱动的交换阻挫与中等非对角键各向异性（\(Γ, Γ'\)）协同产生；量子区域中，该不可公度流形与面外铁磁相（\(\mathrm{FM}_z\)）竞争，后者经量子无序选择机制（order-by-disorder）被选择性稳定。

Conclusion: 研究调和了对 \(\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2\) 磁相互作用相互矛盾的解释，并确立了其不可公度基态的微观起源与稳定范围。

Abstract: The microscopic mechanism governing the zero-field incommensurate magnetic order in the honeycomb cobaltate $\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2$ remains a significant unresolved problem, particularly concerning the relative contributions of bond-directional Kitaev-type interactions and exchange frustration. This study investigates an extended $J_1\text{--}K\text{--}Γ\text{--}Γ'\text{--}J_2\text{--}J_3$ model, which incorporates XXZ-type exchange anisotropies on the honeycomb lattice. Nearest-neighbor parameters are constrained by ab initio electronic structure calculations. By integrating the analytical Luttinger-Tisza approach with exact diagonalization calculations under twisted boundary conditions, we delineate the classical and quantum phase diagrams across the $(J_2, J_3)$ parameter space. We demonstrate that the stabilization of the experimentally observed incommensurate spiral phase, propagating along the $Γ\to M$ direction, does not necessitate an anomalously dominant Kitaev coupling. Instead, this phase arises naturally from the synergistic interplay between exchange frustration, driven by further-neighbor couplings ($J_2, J_3$), and intermediate off-diagonal bond anisotropies ($Γ, Γ'$). In the quantum regime, we elucidate the competition between this incommensurate manifold and an out-of-plane ferromagnetic ($\mathrm{FM}_z$) phase, which is selectively stabilized via a quantum order-by-disorder mechanism. Our findings reconcile conflicting interpretations of the magnetic interactions in $\mathrm{BaCo}_2(\mathrm{AsO}_4)_2$ and establish the microscopic origin and stability range of its incommensurate ground state.

</details>


### [154] [The half-filled optical Su-Schrieffer-Heeger-Hubbard model with uniaxial strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00681)
*Jonah Huang,James Neuhaus,Benjamin Cohen-Stead,Steven Johnston,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本工作以各向异性跃迁建模单轴应变（uniaxial strain）下的 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）电子-声子耦合模型，并加入在位 Hubbard U，给出半填充（half-filling）下的相图、键序波（bond-order-wave, BOW）形成的临界温度及谱函数中各向异性的能隙特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变（strain）通过改变键长、跃迁振幅与晶格对称性直接调控电子相，使电子-声子（electron-phonon, e-ph）耦合体系成为研究应变效应的自然平台；而 SSH 型耦合本身就调制跃迁，预期对 strain 场高度敏感，但其在半填充双分晶格上的相行为与临界性质尚需系统刻画。

Method: 将单轴应变以各向异性跃迁（anisotropic hopping）表示，破坏 90° 旋转对称性，使 BOW 相简并度由四降至二；同时在 SSH 电子-声子相互作用基础上引入在位 Hubbard U 以促成反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）关联。在半填充条件下，于 e-ph 与 Hubbard 耦合构成的参数平面内绘制相图，确定 BOW 形成的临界温度，并通过解析延拓（analytic continuation）计算谱函数（spectral function）。

Result: 半填充下 SSH 相互作用在低温驱动以 BOW 关联为主的相，其特征为短路键（高动能）与长路键（低动能）沿四种可能图案交替排列；引入各向异性跃迁后 90° 旋转对称性被破坏，BOW 简并度降低为两种；Hubbard U 促进反铁磁关联，而 SSH 相互作用本身也（弱）驱动该关联。研究给出 e-ph 与 Hubbard 耦合平面内的半填充相图、BOW 形成的临界温度，并在谱函数中展示 BOW 相内方向依赖的能隙形成。

Conclusion: 结果表明，单轴应变通过各向异性跃迁解除 BOW 相的部分简并，并主导形成具有方向依赖能隙的键序波相；该框架将应变、e-ph 耦合与 Hubbard 关联统一于同一半填充相图中，为理解应变调控的电子相提供了可计算的描述方式。

Abstract: Strain offers a direct route to control electronic phases by altering bond lengths, hopping amplitudes, and lattice symmetries. These aspects make electron-phonon (e-ph) coupled systems a natural setting to study strain-induced effects. The Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) Hamiltonian describes the coupling between itinerant electrons and lattice degrees of freedom that modulate hopping and is thus expected to be strongly affected by strain fields. At half-filling and on a bipartite lattice, the SSH interactions drive dominant bond-order-wave (BOW) correlations at low- temperature in which short bonds with high kinetic energy alternate with long bonds with low kinetic energy along four possible patterns. We model uniaxial strain through anisotropic hopping, which breaks the $90^\circ$ rotational symmetry and reduces the BOW phase degeneracy to two. We also include an on-site Hubbard U that promotes antiferromagnetic correlations that are also (weakly) driven by the SSH interaction itself. We map out the half-filled phase diagram in the plane of the e-ph and Hubbard couplings, and determine the critical temperature of BOW formation. Using analytic continuation, we also evaluate the spectral function and demonstrate directionally dependent gap formation in the BOW phase

</details>


### [155] [Robustness of the Verwey transition against remanent strain-induced defects in magnetite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01337)
*M. A. Gala,K. J. Grzywa,K. Komędera,R. Zalecki,A. Baczmański,Z. Kąkol,A. Pacanowska,C. M. N. Kumar,R. Rodriguez Lamas,C. Detlefs,C. Yildirim,C. Marin,J. Debray,G. Beutier,L. Ortega,A. Kozłowski,N. Barišić,J. E. Lorenzo,W. Tabiś*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究利用暗场X射线显微术（DFXM）与交流磁化率测量，考察单轴压缩及卸载产生的应变诱导缺陷对化学计量比单晶磁铁矿Verwey转变的影响，发现线状扩展缺陷与残余应变场并未可测量地改变转变温度及其锐度，与点缺陷的强烈抑制效应形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁铁矿的Verwey转变是对晶格不完美与外部扰动高度敏感的电子驱动相变，已知掺杂、氧非化学计量与辐照点缺陷会降低转变温度。然而，单轴应力产生的残余应变场与扩展缺陷结构所起的作用此前基本未被探索。

Method: 结合暗场X射线显微术（dark-field x-ray microscopy, DFXM）与交流磁化率（ac magnetic susceptibility）测量。对化学计量比单晶磁铁矿沿同一[011]方向压缩并卸载：加载至200 MPa产生稳定线状缺陷与残余应变场，超过断裂阈值则产生更致密缺陷网络与机械断裂；另对沿[001]压缩断裂的样品进行交流磁化率测量。采用实空间DFXM成像、晶格取向映射与残余应变映射表征结构无序。

Result: 压缩后样品中观察到显著残余结构无序，但所有压缩或断裂样品的转变温度T_V及转变锐度均无可测量的变化。该行为与点状无序对T_V的强烈抑制形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 塑性变形产生的线状扩展缺陷与残余应变场不会可测量地扰动导致Verwey转变的宏观电子有序，从而在磁铁矿中区分了点缺陷与滑移相关的扩展缺陷的不同效应。

Abstract: The Verwey transition in magnetite is a benchmark electronically driven phase transition that is highly sensitive to lattice imperfections and external perturbations. Doping, deviations from oxygen stoichiometry, and irradiation-induced point defects are known to lower the Verwey transition temperature, $T_{\rm V}$. By contrast, the role of remanent strain fields and extended defect structures generated by uniaxial stress has remained largely unexplored. Here we combine dark-field x-ray microscopy (DFXM) and ac magnetic susceptibility to determine how strain-induced defects affect the Verwey transition in stoichiometric single-crystalline magnetite after uniaxial compression and unloading. The DFXM measurements were performed on two samples compressed along the same $[011]$ direction: loading to $200~\mathrm{MPa}$ generated stable line-like defects and remanent strain fields, whereas loading beyond the fracture threshold produced denser defect networks and mechanical fracture. To test the effect of a different loading geometry, we also measured the ac susceptibility of a sample fractured by compression along $[001]$. Real-space DFXM imaging, lattice-orientation mapping, and residual strain mapping show substantial remanent structural disorder after compression. Nevertheless, neither the transition temperature $T_{\rm V}$ nor the sharpness of the transition changes measurably in any of the compressed or fractured samples. This behavior contrasts with the strong suppression of $T_{\rm V}$ by point-like disorder. Our results show that extended line-like defects and remanent strain fields produced by plastic deformation do not measurably perturb the macroscopic electronic ordering responsible for the Verwey transition, thereby distinguishing the effects of point defects from those of extended slip-related defects in magnetite.

</details>


### [156] [Na$_2$Ir$^\text{IV}$Cl$_6$: The Missing Member of the Perfectly Cubic Vacancy Ordered $A_2$IrCl$_6$ Family, Another Potential $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ Ground State Candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01371)
*Siddhartha Sankar Soren,Aditya Patra,Rounak Das,Ritwik Das,Tanmay Chanda,Jens Buck,Indra Dasgupta,Sugata Ray*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 首次合成并表征了具有面心立方（fcc）结构与1.91 K反铁磁转变的无水Na₂IrCl₆单晶，纠正了此前“无水”单斜相的认识，为研究J_eff = 1/2与Kitaev相互作用提供新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空位有序双钙钛矿（VODP）铱酸盐虽研究逾百年，但其精确晶体结构与磁性基态仍存争议；Na基体系尤其存在水合依赖的结构与磁性不确定性，导致立方Na₂IrCl₆始终无法稳定获得，因此亟需明确其结构与物性关系。

Method: 采用合成与综合表征手段制备无水Na₂IrCl₆单晶，并通过热重分析（thermogravimetric analysis）确认无晶格水及热稳定性，结合结构与磁性测量确定其晶体相与磁转变行为。

Result: 所获单晶为面心立方（fcc）结构，反铁磁转变温度为1.91 K；热重分析表明无晶格水，晶体热稳定至453 K；该立方相与先前报道的“无水”单斜Na₂IrCl₆形成对比。

Conclusion: 结果凸显Na基VODP铱酸盐中结构与物理性质的强关联，该新材料为探索潜在的完美J_eff = 1/2八面体及最近邻（nn）Ir离子对间的强Kitaev相互作用提供了理想平台。

Abstract: Vacancy ordered double perovskites (VODPs) have been known for more than 100 years, but their exact crystal structures and magnetic ground state remained controversial till date. Anhydrous VODP iridates ($A_2$Ir$X_6$; $A$ = K$^+$, (NH$_4$)$^+$, Cs$^+$, Rb$^+$, and $X$ = F$^-$, Cl$^-$, Br$^-$), having $face-centered~cubic$ (fcc) antiferromagnetic ground state, offer a chemically well-defined platform to investigate the influence of on-site Coulomb repulsion, crystal field, spin-orbit coupling, and competing Heisenberg and Kitaev interactions. Interestingly, among these, the Na-based VODP iridates seem to possess an unusual hydration dependent structural and magnetic uncertainty. As a result, a cubic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ system having fcc antiferromagnetism could not be stabilized ever. Here, for the first time, we report the synthesis and comprehensive characterization of anhydrous Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ single crystals having fcc structure with antiferromagnetic transition temperature of 1.91 K. Thermogravimetric analysis establishes the absence of lattice water and also demonstrates thermal stability of the crystals up to 453 K. The cubic phase reported here contrasts with previously described 'anhydrous' monoclinic Na$_2$IrCl$_6$ and highlights the strong structural and physical property relationship in Na-based VODP iridates. This new material offers an exciting playing ground for exploring another potential perfect $J_{eff}$ = $\frac{1}{2}$ octahedra and the consequent strong Kitaev interaction within the nearest-neighbor (nn) pairs of Ir ions.

</details>


### [157] [Room-temperature Magnetoelastic Coupling in UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01551)
*Mingyu Xu,Tomasz Klimczuk,M. Brian Maple,Weiwei Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究报道了铀基金属间化合物 UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$ 在略高于室温处存在巨大磁弹耦合（giant magnetoelastic coupling）。通过变温单晶 X 射线衍射与各向异性磁化率测量，观察到晶格参数与特定化学键键长在磁转变区附近的显著异常，且无晶体学对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料中电子自由度与结构自由度的相互作用是诸多涌现现象的基础，但金属体系中的磁弹耦合通常较弱，且局限于低温或微观尺度。该研究旨在寻找并表征近室温下强磁弹耦合的块体金属间化合物。

Method: 采用变温单晶 X 射线衍射（single-crystal X-ray diffraction）结合各向异性磁化率（anisotropic magnetic susceptibility）测量，直接解析与磁有序取向相耦合的细微但可重复的结构畸变。

Result: 在晶体学对称性未破缺的情况下，晶格参数与选定化学键键长在磁转变区附近出现显著异常，显示晶体结构对磁取向具有异常敏感性；磁弹耦合使原子间距与局域化学键合对电子态与磁态的调控作用可被直接探测。

Conclusion: UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$ 被确立为一种磁性（magnetism）与晶格畸变（lattice distortion）在近室温强耦合的罕见平台，为磁性响应量子材料（magnetically responsive quantum materials）与功能化磁传感应用提供了新机遇。

Abstract: The interplay between electronic and structural degrees of freedom underpins many emergent phenomena in quantum materials, yet magnetoelastic coupling in metallic systems is typically weak and confined to low temperatures or microscopic length scales. Here, we report giant magnetoelastic coupling slightly above room temperature in the uranium-based intermetallic compound UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$. Using temperature-dependent single-crystal X-ray diffraction together with anisotropic magnetic susceptibility measurements, we directly resolve subtle but reproducible structural distortions coupled to magnetic alignment. Despite the absence of crystallographic symmetry breaking, pronounced anomalies emerge in lattice parameters and selected chemical bond distances near the magnetic transition region, revealing an unusual sensitivity of the crystal structure to magnetic orientation. The coupling enables direct probing of how atomic distances and local chemical bonding govern the electronic and magnetic states in a bulk intermetallic material. Our results establish UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$ as a rare platform in which magnetism and lattice distortions are strongly intertwined near room temperature, opening new opportunities for magnetically responsive quantum materials and functional magnetic sensing applications.

</details>


### [158] [Mass-asymmetry-controlled exciton dressing and dissociation in a quantum lattice model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01594)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在极性材料中，中性激子（neutral exciton）因电荷中性导致形变势源项抵消，其与声子的弹性耦合受电子-空穴干涉项正则化。本文通过 Holstein-激子模型的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）确定该干涉的非微扰结果：等质量情形下对称基态受精确交换选择定则（exchange selection rule）保护而免于声子缀饰，质量不对称增大后该保护被破坏并形成强局域极化子云；解离边界由库仑束缚与极化子稳定化的全局能量平衡决定，且几乎与内部缀饰无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性激子的电声耦合源自电子与空穴形变势之和，但该源项因电荷中性而总体抵消，实际耦合由电子-空穴干涉（electron-hole interference）残余主导，其非微扰行为缺乏清晰刻画。已有晶格激子-极化子（exciton-polaron）模型对该干涉的最终归宿以及激子解离（dissociation）判据未给出统一图像，同时实验上质量对称与不对称极性半导体中激子谱线宽窄差异也需理论解释。

Method: 采用 Holstein-激子模型，通过精确对角化（exact diagonalization）进行非微扰求解；以质量不对称度（mass asymmetry）为参数将其与小极化子原子极限（small-polaron atomic limit）解耦，从而构建包含内部缀饰交叉（internal dressing crossover）与解离边界的相图。对等质量的对称激子基态保护机制给出解析证明并辅以数值验证，同时分析解离边界的能量平衡来源。

Result: 等质量情形下，若声子源项在电子-空穴交换下为奇（即中性情形），对称激子基态被精确交换选择定则保护，不发生声子缀饰；质量不对称增大时该选择定则被破坏，激子获得强局域极化子云。解离边界由库仑束缚能与极化子稳定化能之间的全局能量平衡设定，几乎不依赖内部缀饰过程。由此出现反直觉结果：保护激子免受缀饰的对称性同时剥夺了其极化子稳定化，使其在强耦合下趋于解离。

Conclusion: 该工作为晶格激子-极化子模型提供了非微扰相图框架，揭示交换对称性对激子缀饰与稳定化的双重作用：对称性既抑制声子缀饰又取消极化子稳定化，推动强耦合解离。据此可解释质量对称与不对称极性半导体中激子谱线呈现锐线（sharp line）与宽线（broad line）的差异。

Abstract: In a polar material, a neutral exciton couples to phonons through the sum of the electron and hole deformation potentials. Because the total source vanishes by charge neutrality, the elastic exciton-phonon vertex is regularized by electron-hole interference. Here we determine the non-perturbative fate of this interference by exact diagonalization of a Holstein-exciton model. By parameterizing the mass asymmetry to decouple it from the small-polaron atomic limit, we map a regime map comprising an internal dressing crossover and a dissociation boundary. We prove analytically and verify numerically that for equal masses, the symmetric exciton ground state is protected from phonon dressing by an exact exchange selection rule, provided the phonon source is odd under electron-hole exchange (the neutral case). As mass asymmetry increases, this selection rule is broken and the exciton acquires a strong local polaronic cloud. We show that the dissociation boundary, conversely, is set by a global energy balance between Coulomb binding and polaronic stabilization, and is nearly independent of the internal dressing. Paradoxically, the very symmetry that protects the exciton from dressing denies it polaronic stabilization, driving it toward dissociation at strong coupling. We discuss these results in the context of lattice exciton-polaron models and their implications for sharp versus broad excitonic lines in mass-symmetric versus mass-asymmetric polar semiconductors.

</details>


### [159] [Why polar excitons stay sharp: parity protection of the center-of-mass recoil channel in exciton-phonon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01600)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作指出，冻结质心（center-of-mass, COM）近似遗漏了激子—声子问题的决定性运动学自由度；恢复质心反冲后出现一个普适、无参数的 $1s\to1s$ 吸收通道，其速率由电—空穴相消干涉（destructive electron-hole interference）控制，并据此建立散射机制层级，用于解释卤化物钙钛矿中异常尖锐的激子共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极性半导体（polar semiconductors）中，Fröhlich 相互作用（Fröhlich interaction）是主导的电子—声子耦合，然而卤化物钙钛矿（halide perovskites）等材料中的激子共振却异常尖锐，这一矛盾需要解释。作者认为标准处理冻结质心（center-of-mass, COM），因而缺失了关键的质心反冲自由度。

Method: 恢复精确的质心反冲（COM recoil），在氢原子型 Fröhlich 模型（hydrogenic Fröhlich model）框架内解析推导反冲通道，并引入反冲线宽 $\gamma_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}\propto\mathcal{F}_{1s,1s}(q_*)^2$ 与弹性缀饰抑制律 $S_X/S_{\rm ind}=\eta^2(6-\eta^2)/5$；随后以质量不对称度 $\eta$、反冲动量 $q_*$ 及 Rydberg 失谐为控制参数，对 GaAs、FAPbI$_3$、MAPbI$_3$ 等体系进行定量比较。

Result: 理论给出反冲动量为 $q_*=\sqrt{2M_{\rm ex}\hbar\omega_{\rm LO}}/\hbar$ 的普适 $1s\to1s$ 通道，速率随 $N_{\rm LO}(T)$ 变化。质量不对称体系（GaAs, $\eta=-0.74$）中该通道活跃，$\gamma_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}=2.2$~meV；质量对称体系（FAPbI$_3$, $\eta=0$；MAPbI$_3$, $\eta=-0.11$）中因干涉而几近消失（0.00 与 0.12 meV）。故实验观测到的 27–40 meV 钙钛矿线宽无法由质心反冲解释，需借助内态改变等非弹性通道，其中建设性且对 $\eta$ 稳健的 $1s\to np$ 共振在当前模型内为首要候选。

Conclusion: 仅用 Fröhlich 常数 $\alpha$ 不足以作为品质因数（figure of merit）；投影到关联激子（correlated exciton）之后，真正的控制参数为质量不对称度 $\eta$、$q_*a_X$ 与 Rydberg 失谐。该理论确立了激子—声子散射机制的层级划分。

Abstract: In polar semiconductors the Fröhlich interaction is the dominant electron--phonon coupling, yet excitonic resonances in materials such as halide perovskites remain anomalously sharp. We show that standard frozen-center-of-mass treatments of the exciton--phonon problem miss the decisive kinematic degree of freedom: restoring the exact center-of-mass (COM) recoil reveals a universally open, parameter-free $1s\to1s$ absorption channel at recoil momentum $q_*=\sqrt{2M_{\rm ex}\hbarω_{\rm LO}}/\hbar$, whose rate scales as $N_{\rm LO}(T)$. We prove that this recoil channel is controlled by destructive electron--hole interference: the recoil linewidth vanishes with the mass asymmetry as $γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}\propto\mathcal{F}_{1s,1s}(q_*)^2$, and the elastic dressing obeys the exact suppression law $S_X/S_{\rm ind}=η^2(6-η^2)/5$ within the hydrogenic Fröhlich model. The theory establishes a hierarchy of scattering regimes. In mass-asymmetric materials (GaAs, $η=-0.74$) the recoil channel is active ($γ_{\rm LO}^{\rm recoil}=2.2$~meV); in mass-symmetric materials (FAPbI$_3$, $η=0$; MAPbI$_3$, $η=-0.11$) it is killed by interference (0.00 and 0.12 meV), showing that the observed 27--40 meV perovskite linewidths cannot be accounted for by COM recoil and therefore require internal-state-changing and other inelastic channels, of which the constructive, $η$-robust $1s\to np$ resonance is the leading candidate within the present model. The Fröhlich constant $α$ alone is therefore insufficient as a figure of merit: after projection onto the correlated exciton, the controlling parameters are $η$, $q_*a_X$, and the Rydberg detuning.

</details>


### [160] [Frustration-induced multiferroicity in hauerite MnS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01990)
*Vilmos Kocsis,Harish K. Singh,Kranthi K. Bestha,Yaqian Guo,Maxim Mostovoy,Jeroen van den Brink,Oleg Janson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 黄铁矿型矿物 hauerite（MnS$_2$）是一种具有局域 $S=5/2$ 磁矩的磁性绝缘体，在 48 K 以下形成共线反铁磁序，并伴随晶胞加倍与反演对称性破缺，从而产生最高 360 μC/m$^2$ 的铁电极化，属于 type-II 多铁性（multiferroic）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示 MnS$_2$ 中磁有序与电极化之间的耦合机制，特别是反演对称性破缺如何导致铁电性，以及其是否构成 type-II 多铁性（type-II multiferroic）。

Method: 论文通过分析磁有序引起的局域对称性变化，从微观上追踪极化的电子起源，指出 S$_2$ 二聚体（S$_2$ dimers）在邻近 Mn 位点阻挫磁有序（frustrated magnetic ordering）诱导下获得偶极矩；并考察时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）对铁电畴的影响及畴翻转所需能量尺度。

Result: 在 48 K 以下，MnS$_2$ 形成共线反铁磁序（collinear antiferromagnetic order），伴随晶胞加倍并破坏反演对称性，产生最高 360 μC/m$^2$ 的铁电极化。时间反演对称性不破坏铁电畴，而畴翻转需克服由磁交换能尺度（magnetic exchange energy scale）设定的高能垒。

Conclusion: MnS$_2$ 因磁有序诱导的电极化而被确认为 type-II 多铁性材料；其铁电畴受时间反演对称性保护，畴翻转受高磁交换能垒限制，体现出磁电耦合的独特特征。

Abstract: Pyrite-type mineral hauerite MnS$_2$ is a magnetic insulator with localized $S=\frac52$ moments. Below 48 K, it develops a collinear antiferromagnetic order, accompanied by unit-cell doubling and breaking the inversion symmetry. We demonstrate that the latter gives rise to a ferroelectric polarization of up to 360 $μ$C/m$^2$ rendering MnS$_2$ a type-II multiferroic. Microscopically, the electronic polarization originates from S$_2$ dimers that acquire a dipole moment induced by the frustrated magnetic ordering of neighboring Mn sites. We show that time-reversal symmetry leaves ferroelectric domains intact, and that domain switching necessitates overcoming a high energy barrier set by the magnetic exchange energy scale.

</details>


### [161] [Magnetic Phase Diagrams and Spin Hamiltonian of Monoclinic $α$-RuCl$_3$ from Angle-Dependent Torque Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02006)
*Daniel Antoniou,Danrui Ni,John S. Pearce,Robert J. Cava,Amalia I. Coldea,Radu Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了亚100微米尺寸、低温下保持单斜（monoclinic）结构的α-RuCl₃单晶磁性，发现其14 K的尖锐磁转变及与菱方（rhombohedral）样品显著不同的磁相图，并结合力矩测量与平均场（mean-field）计算提出磁结构模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-RuCl₃被视为自旋轨道纠缠的j_eff=1/2 Ru³⁺磁矩在强阻挫Kitaev及键依赖各向异性相互作用下呈现非常规协同磁性的候选材料。已有研究多集中于较大单晶，其降温至150 K以下发生单斜到菱方的结构相变并于约7 K进入zigzag磁序，而对低温仍保持单斜结构的微小单晶的磁性认识不足。

Method: 采用压电悬臂梁（piezo-cantilever）高灵敏磁力矩（magnetic torque）测量，在最高16 T磁场下，将磁场分别旋转于三个正交晶面内；通过近乎连续改变磁场角度与强度，利用原始力矩数据及其对角和场强的高阶导数异常来识别相变，并与适合α-RuCl₃、考虑单斜对称性的磁哈密顿量平均场计算进行直接对比。

Result: 高质量亚100微米单晶在低温下保持单斜结构并显示14 K的尖锐磁转变。所得磁相图与菱方样品存在诸多差异：旋转对称性降低、抑制自发磁序所需磁场显著更高，并出现不同的场诱导相。力矩数据与平均场计算对比后，为相图所有区域提出了最小磁结构模型。

Conclusion: 低温保持单斜结构的微小α-RuCl₃单晶表现出与菱方样品不同的磁性相图，凸显晶格对称性对磁基态与场诱导相的调控作用，所提出的最小磁结构模型为理解该材料的非常规磁性提供依据。

Abstract: The layered honeycomb $α$-RuCl$_3$ has been much explored as a candidate to display unconventional cooperative magnetism between spin-orbit entangled $j_{\rm eff}=1/2$ Ru$^{3+}$ magnetic moments stabilized by strongly-frustrated, Kitaev and other bond-dependent anisotropic interactions. Most studies so far have focused on relatively large single crystals that undergo a structural phase transition from monoclinic to rhombohedral upon cooling below 150 K and a magnetic transition to zigzag order around 7 K. Here we study the magnetism of high-quality, very small (sub 100 $μ$m diameter) single crystals, which remain monoclinic upon cooling to low temperatures and display a sharp magnetic transition at 14 K. Using highly-sensitive piezo-cantilever magnetic torque measurements in fields up to 16 T, we report a comprehensive study of the magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic field rotated in three orthogonal crystallographic planes. Our extensive torque data collected upon varying almost continuously the angular orientation of the field as well as the field magnitude, allows clear detection of phase transitions via anomalies in the raw torque data and its higher order derivatives with respect to both angle and field magnitude. The obtained magnetic phase diagrams show many differences compared to rhombohedral samples, displaying reduced rotational symmetry, substantially higher fields required to suppress the spontaneous magnetic order, and distinct field-induced phases. We provide a direct comparison of the torque data with mean-field calculations for magnetic Hamiltonians appropriate for $α$-RuCl$_3$ allowing for the monoclinic lattice symmetry and propose minimal magnetic structure models for all explored regions of the magnetic phase diagrams.

</details>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [A Combinatorial Approach to the CUE Joint Moment Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00599)
*George Snape*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文给出单位圆上 CUE（circular unitary ensemble，圆酉系综）在所有指数均为偶数正整数情形下，全部联合矩（joint moments）的精确组合表达式，并导出相应渐近性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献中关于 CUE 联合矩的诸多结果彼此割裂，缺乏统一框架，且 Basor 等（2018）关于不规则共形块结构（irregular conformal block structure）的猜想尚待证明。

Method: 采用组合方法，给出两种等价的矩表达形式：其一为涉及 Kostka 数（Kostka numbers）的求和；其二与整数矩阵计数（幻方，magic squares）相关。

Result: 在上述偶数正整数指数条件下，得到全部 CUE 联合矩的精确组合表达式及其渐近展开，并由此证明 Basor 等（2018）的猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了文献中此前相互独立的多项结果，并建立了 CUE 联合矩与不规则共形块结构之间的联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we give exact combinatorial expressions for all CUE joint moments on the unit circle, in the case that all exponents are even positive integers, yielding the corresponding asymptotics. We offer two equivalent expressions for these quantities: the first is a sum involving Kostka numbers; the second is related to counting integer matrices (magic squares). As an application of our results, we unify many results in the literature, which were previously disconnected. Moreover, we prove a conjecture of Basor et al. (2018) regarding an irregular conformal block structure.

</details>


### [163] [Wetting Transition of the Two-Dimensional Solid-on-Solid Model with Quenched Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01157)
*Seokun Choi*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究二维固上固（Solid-On-Solid, SOS）界面在淬火无序（quenched disorder）与硬壁作用下的润湿转变（wetting transition），证明淬火临界点与均匀润湿点一致，且近临界自由能的首阶渐近行为与均匀模型相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画淬火无序是否改变润湿转变的临界点与临界自由能渐近行为，并将其与已知的无序 SOS 钉扎模型（pinning model）作对比，以厘清无序在不同界面模型中的作用差异。

Method: 以非负整数值高度函数 φ 表示界面，哈密顿量包含界面张力项 β∑|φ(x)-φ(y)| 与壁接触项（含 i.i.d. 中心化无序场 ω_x、无序强度 α 及归一化 λ(α)）；该归一化使退火（annealed）模型等同于相应均匀润湿模型，随后在淬火测度下分析临界点与超出自由能。

Result: 对任意固定 α≥0，淬火临界点等于均匀润湿点，即 h_c(β,α)=h_w(β)=-log(1-e^{-4β})；令 u=h-h_w(β)，淬火超出自由能满足 \overline F_β(α,u)=\overline F_homo(β,u)+o(u^3)（u↓0），其中 \overline F_homo(β,u)≍u^3。

Conclusion: 在临界点与近临界自由能首阶渐近两个层面，淬火模型与均匀 SOS 润湿模型行为一致；此结果与无序 SOS 钉扎模型形成对比——后者淬火无序不改变临界点，但会修改自由能的首阶临界行为。

Abstract: We study the wetting transition for a two-dimensional Solid-On-Solid (SOS) interface above a hard wall in the presence of quenched disorder. The interface is represented by a nonnegative integer-valued height function $φ$, with Hamiltonian $\mathcal H^ω(φ)=β\sum_{x\sim y}|φ(x)-φ(y)|-\sum_x(h+αω_x-λ(α))\mathbf 1_{\{φ(x)=0\}}$, where $(ω_x)_{x\in\mathbb Z^2}$ is an i.i.d. centered field, $α\ge0$ is the disorder strength, and $λ(α)=\log\mathbb E[e^{αω_0}]$. This normalization is chosen so that the annealed model coincides with the corresponding homogeneous wetting model. As the wall attraction $h$ increases, the interface undergoes a transition from a delocalized phase, in which contacts with the wall have vanishing density, to a localized phase with a positive density of contacts. For sufficiently large $β$, the homogeneous wetting point and its sharp near-critical free-energy behavior are known. We prove that, for every fixed $α\ge0$, the quenched critical point coincides with the homogeneous wetting point, $h_c(β,α)=h_w(β)=-\log(1-e^{-4β})$.
  Moreover, writing $u=h-h_w(β)$, we show that the quenched excess free energy satisfies $\overline F_β(α,u)=\overline F_{\mathrm{homo}}(β,u)+o(u^3)$ as $u\downarrow0$, where $\overline F_{\mathrm{homo}}(β,u)$ denotes the leading homogeneous wetting asymptotic and satisfies $\overline F_{\mathrm{homo}}(β,u)\asymp u^3$. Thus, at the level of both the critical point and the leading near-critical free-energy asymptotics, the quenched model has the same behavior as the homogeneous SOS wetting model. This contrasts with the disordered SOS pinning model, in which quenched disorder leaves the critical point unchanged but modifies the leading critical behavior of the free energy.

</details>


### [164] [The critical Ising magnetization field can be reconstructed from its $+/-$ interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02064)
*Paul Cahen,Christophe Garban,Avelio Sepúlveda*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明临界 Ising 磁化场（IMF）可由嵌套 CLE₃ 直接重构，补全了此前从着色 CLE₁₆∕₃ 构造 IMF 的结果；主要困难在于 IMF 的重整化指数 15/8 小于 CLE₃ 地毯的 Hausdorff 维数 187/96，故无法用直接 Minkowski 内容构造法恢复场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界 Ising 模型（critical Ising model）存在若干自然的标度极限（scaling limits）：重整化自旋场收敛为 IMF，自旋界面收敛为嵌套 CLE₃，FK–Ising 表示收敛为着色 CLE₁₆∕₃。已有工作（[CGN15]）从着色 CLE₁₆∕₃ 构造 IMF，本文旨在补全另一条路径，即直接从嵌套 CLE₃ 重构 IMF。

Method: 通过证明 IMF 可由嵌套 CLE₃ 直接重构来建立构造。作者指出该重构路径的主要障碍为：IMF 的重整化指数（renormalization exponent）15/8 小于 CLE₃ 地毯（CLE₃ carpet）的 Hausdorff 维数 187/96，因此不能像 CLE₁₆∕₃ 情形那样采用直接的 Minkowski 内容（Minkowski-content）构造。摘要未给出更具体的技术手段细节。

Result: 成功证明 IMF 可从嵌套 CLE₃ 直接重构，与已知的着色 CLE₁₆∕₃ 构造形成互补。此外，若借助 CLE 渗流（CLE percolation，[MSW17]）由嵌套 CLE₃ 构造着色 CLE₁₆∕₃，该构造恢复出同一磁化场。值得注意的是，因重整化指数 15/8 < 187/96 这一维数比较上的困难，无法沿用 CLE₁₆∕₃ 情形的直接 Minkowski 内容构造来实现该恢复。

Conclusion: 本文确立了从嵌套 CLE₃ 到 IMF 的重构，与着色 CLE₁₆∕₃ 的构造相辅相成，并经由 CLE 渗流与后者一致。作者进一步猜想反向可测性（converse measurability）亦成立，即嵌套 CLE₃ 本身也可由 IMF 重构。

Abstract: The critical Ising model admits several natural scaling limits. For an Ising model on a lattice approximation $D^a$ of a planar domain $D$, the rescaled spin field converges, as $a\to0$, to the critical Ising magnetization field (IMF), a rough random distribution on $D$, while the spin interfaces converge to a nested $CLE_3$ and the FK--Ising representation converges to a colored $CLE_{16/3}$.
  In this work, we prove that the IMF can be directly reconstructed from the nested $CLE_3$. This complements the previously known construction of the IMF from the colored $CLE_{16/3}$ [CGN15]. Our result also implies that %We further show that if one constructs a colored $CLE_{16/3}$ from the nested $CLE_3$ through CLE percolation [MSW17], the resulting construction recovers the same magnetization field.
  A main difficulty in this present reconstruction comes from the fact that the renormalization exponent of the IMF, $15/8$, is smaller than the Hausdorff dimension of the $CLE_3$ carpet, $187/96$. Consequently, the field cannot be recovered by a direct Minkowski-content construction from the nested $CLE_3$ as is the case for $CLE_{16/3}$.
  We conjecture that the converse measurability also holds, namely that the nested $CLE_3$ can itself be reconstructed from the IMF.

</details>


### [165] [Convergence of stationary distributions for a class of zero-range processes and its application to a wealth distribution model with debt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00901)
*Hironobu Sakagawa*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文在含中央银行集体债务上限的主体基模型中引入随机交易机制，将系统刻画为取值为整数（含负值表示债务）的零程过程，证明稳态财富分布在适当标度下收敛为双边 Gamma 分布或 Gamma 分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宏观财富分布如何从微观交易规则中涌现是经济物理学（econophysics）的核心问题，而既有模型多将财富限制为非负，忽略了债务这一现实经济系统的基本特征。

Method: 在带有中央银行中介的集体债务上限的主体基模型（agent-based model）中引入随机交易机制：主体依其当前资产或债务水平概率性地决定是否转移一枚硬币；并将所得系统表述为位点占据数为 $\mathbb{Z}$ 值的零程过程（zero-range process），负值代表债务。

Result: 证明在适当标度下，稳态财富分布收敛为双边 Gamma 分布（two-sided Gamma distribution）或 Gamma 分布，具体取决于控制硬币转移的函数的渐近行为。

Conclusion: 结果严格识别了此前未被考虑的上述极限分布，并为理解随机货币交换如何产生宏观财富分布提供了数学框架。

Abstract: Understanding how macroscopic wealth distributions emerge from microscopic transaction rules among agents is a central problem in econophysics. While many traditional models restrict agents' wealth to non-negative values, debt is an essential feature of realistic economic systems. In this paper, we introduce a stochastic transaction mechanism into the agent-based model with a central-bank-mediated collective debt limit, as studied in [14]. In our model, each agent probabilistically determines whether to transfer a coin, depending on their current asset or debt level. From a probabilistic perspective, the resulting system can be formulated as a zero-range process with $\mathbb{Z}$-valued site occupation numbers, where negative values represent debt. We prove that, under an appropriate scaling, the wealth distribution in the stationary state converges to either a two-sided Gamma distribution or a Gamma distribution, depending on the asymptotic behavior of the function governing coin transfers. Our results rigorously identify these previously unconsidered limiting distributions and provide a mathematical framework for understanding how stochastic monetary exchanges give rise to macroscopic wealth distributions.

</details>
