### [166] [Loading history and window geometry bound compact-state slip ranking during granular shear startup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00124)
*Ruixin Zhou,Boliang Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究将颗粒体系滑动预测中的材料状态、加载历史与事件中心采样几何三种贡献分离，利用紧凑神经评分在缓慢剪切二维摩擦圆盘体系中评估近滑窗预测能力，并指出加载历史基线与窗口几何敏感性会限制证据强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒滑动预测（granular slip forecasting）常将材料状态（material state）、加载进度（loading progress）与事件中心采样几何（event-centered sampling geometry）混为一谈，需厘清各自贡献以判断是否存在真正的短时前兆信号。

Method: 在缓慢剪切（slowly sheared）二维摩擦圆盘（two-dimensional frictional disks）体系中，构建由应力（stress）、压力（pressure）、配位数（coordination）、非仿射运动（non-affine motion）与力网络可观测量（force-network observables）组成的紧凑神经评分（compact neural score）。模型在36条轨迹上开发并冻结，随后在18条新轨迹上以两种嵌套应力降（stress-drop）定义测试，并采用恢复感知（recovery-aware）描述性分析与轨迹等权处理。

Result: 等权情形下，紧凑评分在两种定义下均将近滑窗（near-slip windows）排序于流行率（prevalence）和轨迹内循环相位对照（within-trajectory circular-phase controls）之上（代表平均精度0.310，流行率0.173，相位零模型上界0.257）。加载历史坐标排序更强，因果累计应变（causal elapsed strain）达0.534。恢复感知规则保留78.4%的活动门控事件（activity-gated events），并偏好更长的前序间隔；按距上一编目事件时间排序仍与计数条件化几何零模型（count-conditioned geometry null）相容。

Conclusion: 紧凑可观测量确实包含时间对齐的滑动信息，但更强的加载历史基线与窗口几何敏感性限定了该证据的边界；这些启动阶段数据（startup data）未能分离出超越加载历史的、状态特异的短时前兆（state-specific short-horizon precursor），也不支持对累计应变排序作更新过程（renewal）解释。

Abstract: Granular slip forecasting can conflate material state, loading progress, and the geometry of event-centered sampling. We separated these contributions in slowly sheared two-dimensional frictional disks using a compact neural score of stress, pressure, coordination, non-affine motion, and force-network observables. The model was developed on 36 trajectories and frozen efore testing on 18 new trajectories under two nested stress-drop definitions. Inspection of held-out results revealed post-event sampling asymmetry; recovery-aware analyses are therefore descriptive. With trajectories weighted equally, the compact score ranked near-slip windows above both prevalence and within-trajectory circular-phase controls under both definitions (representative average precision 0.310 versus prevalence 0.173; phase-null upper bound 0.257). Loading-history coordinates ranked more strongly, reaching 0.534 for causal elapsed strain. The recovery-aware rule retained 78.4\% of activity-gated events and preferentially selected longer preceding intervals; ranking by time since the previous catalogued event remained compatible with a count-conditioned geometry null. Compact observables thus contain temporally aligned slip information, but stronger loading-history baselines and window-geometry sensitivity bound that evidence. These startup data do not isolate a state-specific short-horizon precursor beyond loading history or support a renewal interpretation of elapsed-strain ranking.

</details>


### [167] [Temperature dependence of surface tension in aqueous sodium dodecyl sulfate solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00532)
*Jong Cheon Lee,Ildoo Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文用 Wilhelmy 板法测量了十六种浓度的十二烷基硫酸钠（sodium dodecyl sulfate, SDS）水溶液在 26–83 °C 下的表面张力，发现温度系数在约 0.3–0.8 mM（约 cmc 的十分之一）处变号，并在约 3 mM 处达到 +0.53 mN m⁻¹ K⁻¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面张力的温度依赖性（temperature dependence）对热毛细应力（thermocapillary stress）行为至关重要，而 SDS 溶液在低于临界胶束浓度（critical micelle concentration, cmc）区间的该性质尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 采用 Wilhelmy 板法（Wilhelmy plate method）测量 SDS 水溶液表面张力；浓度范围 0.17–43 mM，覆盖 0.02 至 5 倍室温 cmc，共十六个浓度点；温度范围 26–83 °C。

Result: 最低浓度下表面张力随温度升高而下降，与纯水一致；约 0.4 mM 以上转为升高，温度系数在 0.3–0.8 mM 之间变号，并在约 3 mM 处达 +0.53 mN m⁻¹ K⁻¹，量值为纯水的三倍且符号相反。超过 cmc 后表面张力几乎与温度无关，饱和于 35.2 ± 0.3 mN m⁻¹。

Conclusion: 该行为无法用 cmc 的温度依赖性解释（后者量级弱一个数量级），而与随温度升高而变薄的表面活性剂层（surfactant layer）图像一致；结果表明 SDS 溶液热毛细应力的符号与大小可通过浓度进行调控。

Abstract: We report measurements of the surface tension of aqueous sodium dodecyl sulfate solutions at sixteen concentrations from 0.17 to 43$\,$mM, spanning 0.02 to 5 times the room-temperature critical micelle concentration (cmc), and at temperatures from 26 to 83$\,$$^\circ{\rm C}$, using the Wilhelmy plate method. At the lowest concentration the surface tension decreases with temperature, as it does for pure water. Above about 0.4$\,$mM, however, it increases: the temperature coefficient changes sign at a concentration of 0.3 to 0.8$\,$mM, roughly a tenth of the cmc, and reaches $+0.53\,\rm mN\,m^{-1}\,K^{-1}$ near 3$\,$mM, three times the magnitude of that of pure water and of the opposite sign. Above the cmc the surface tension is nearly independent of temperature, saturating at $35.2\pm0.3\,\rm mN\,m^{-1}$. The behavior cannot be attributed to the temperature dependence of the cmc, which is an order of magnitude too weak. It is instead consistent with a surfactant layer that thins as the temperature rises. The results imply that the sign and magnitude of thermocapillary stresses in SDS solutions can be selected through the concentration.

</details>


### [168] [Enhanced Long-Wavelength Fluctuations and Interaction-Stress Correlations in Active Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00634)
*Antik Bhattacharya,Smarajit Karmakar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该摘要通过二维与三维活性布朗晶体（active Brownian crystal）的位移协方差与应力谱分析，揭示活性如何使固体长波密度涨落超出 Mermin-Wagner-Hohenberg 定理的平衡预言，并将该增强归因于活性对普通声学模式的异常占据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性可增强固体长波密度涨落，其幅度超过平衡态预言，但这一增强的微观起源尚不清楚，本文旨在阐明其机制。

Method: 研究二维活性布朗晶体中纵、横位移协方差随波矢的标度行为，并对比被动情形；同时计算协方差定义的模量、直接力学响应、迁移率与积分活性力关联，以及 Irving-Kirkwood 相互作用应力谱与应力—应力关联。另在三维活性 FCC 晶体中验证。

Result: 位移协方差标度为 C^u_λ(q)∼q^{-3}（被动为 q^{-2}），预言 MSD 平台发散在二维正比于 L、三维对数、四维有限，与近期 Dey 等的结果吻合。协方差倒数给出 H^cov_λ∼|q|^3、色散 ω_cov,λ∼q^{3/2}，而直接力学响应为 K_resp,λ∼q^2、声学模 ω_0,λ∼q，说明非线性色散由协方差而非力学定义。纵、横 Irving-Kirkwood 应力谱标度为 q^{-1}；横向应力—应力关联与异常位移场吻合。

Conclusion: 活性通过异常占据普通声学模式，并由保守相互作用网络将涨落作为长程应力传递，从而产生协方差定义的非线性色散；迁移率与积分活性力关联保持非奇异，有限持续时间的交叉尚不能完全排除。三维活性 FCC 晶体呈现相同配对低 q 趋势。

Abstract: Activity can enhance the long-wavelength density fluctuations of a solid beyond the equilibrium fluctuations predicted by the Mermin-Wagner-Hohenberg theorem, but the microscopic origin of this enhancement remains unclear. We study a two-dimensional active Brownian crystal whose longitudinal and transverse displacement covariances scale as $C^u_λ(q)\sim q^{-3}$ as compared to the passive counterpart with $q^{-2}$ law. This spectrum predicts an MSD-plateau divergence proportional to $L$ in two dimensions, logarithmic in three dimensions, and finite in four dimensions, in close correspondence with recent results reported in Dey et al. [Nat. Commun. 16, 5498 (2025)]. Inverse of the Covariance gives $H^{\mathrm{cov}}_λ\sim |q|^3$ with the corresponding dispersion $ω_{\mathrm{cov},λ}\sim q^{3/2}$, whereas direct mechanical response gives $K_{\mathrm{resp},λ}\sim q^2$ and acoustic modes with $ω_{0,λ}\sim q$. The nonlinear dispersion is therefore covariance-defined rather than mechanical. The mobility and integrated active-force correlations remain non-singular, although a finite-persistence crossover cannot be ruled out completely with the existing data. Microscopically, the longitudinal and transverse Irving-Kirkwood interaction-stress spectra scale as $q^{-1}$. The transverse stress-stress correlation connects well with the anomalous displacement field, indicating that activity anomalously populates ordinary acoustic modes, while the conservative interaction network transmits the resulting fluctuations as long-ranged stress. A three-dimensional active FCC crystal exhibits the same paired low-$q$ trends.

</details>


### [169] [Fatigue failure in two-dimensional glasses under cyclic shear deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00655)
*Himangsu Bhaumik,Srikanth Sastry*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过原子模拟（atomistic simulations）研究二维（2D）模型玻璃在循环剪切（cyclic shear）下的疲劳失效，发现失效循环数随应变幅值趋近疲劳极限呈幂律发散（power-law divergence），指数接近 -1，与三维（3D）报道的 -2 不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维模型中失效指数 -1 与三维中的 -2 存在差异，且二维弹塑性（elastoplastic）模型近期也观测到 -1，提示失效行为可能依赖空间维度（spatial dimensionality），需加以解释。同时需厘清损伤累积与失效时间变异性（variability）之间的关系。

Method: 采用原子模拟（atomistic simulations）对二维模型玻璃施加循环剪切变形（cyclic shear deformation）；测量累积塑性活动（plastic activity），包括耗散功（dissipated work）与非仿射位移（non-affine displacements）；并开展等构型"播种"（isoconfigurational seeded）模拟，人为引入局部软区（soft region）。

Result: 失效循环数随应变幅值趋近疲劳极限以幂律发散，指数接近 -1（二维），不同于三维的 -2。累积塑性活动指标随失效时间呈标度关系（scaling），与三维结果一致，表明损伤累积与失效之间存在稳健联系。播种模拟虽约束了失效的空间位置，失效时间分布仍很宽泛，显示其表观随机性（apparent stochasticity）。

Conclusion: 该工作将三维关于疲劳失效时间、累积塑性及表观随机性的结论推广至二维玻璃，同时揭示关键差异：描述失效时间发散的幂律指数（exponent）在二维与三维间不同，提示其可能依赖空间维度，尚待理论解释。

Abstract: We investigate fatigue failure in two-dimensional (2D) model glasses under cyclic shear deformation using atomistic simulations. We find that the number of cycles to failure diverges as a power law as the strain amplitude approaches the fatigue limit, with an exponent close to $-1$, in contrast to the exponent of $-2$ reported in three dimensions (3D). A failure exponent of $-1$ has also recently been observed in a 2D elastoplastic model, suggesting an interesting dependence on spatial dimensionality that needs to be rationalized. Measures of accumulated plastic activity, including dissipated work and non-affine displacements, exhibit scaling with the failure time that is consistent with results in 3D, and indicate a robust connection between damage accumulation and failure. To probe the origins of the variability of failure times, we perform isoconfigurational {\it seeded} simulations in which a localized soft region is introduced. While such seeding constrains the spatial location of failure, the distribution of failure times remains broad. These findings extend recent results in 3D concerning fatigue failure times to 2D glasses, including their relation to accumulated plasticity and apparent stochasticity, while also revealing a key difference, namely the exponent describing the divergence of the failure times.

</details>


### [170] [Polymer viscoelasticity from an orientational closure with implicit stretch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00892)
*Souta Miyamoto,Takeshi Sato,Wanxiang Jin,Katsuaki Tanabe,John J. Molina,Takashi Taniguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文从取向动力学（orientational kinetics）出发，隐式保留构象拉伸（conformational stretch）贡献，提出一个偏应力（deviatoric-stress）本构方程，并验证其在多种剪切与拉伸流场下的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建基于偏应力状态、可由标准流变测量（standard rheometry）重构的本构描述，并给出拉伸如何进入聚合物黏弹性（polymer viscoelasticity）的物理解释。

Method: 从哑铃模型（dumbbell model）出发，考虑构象拉伸通过有效应力尺度与旋转扩散率（rotational diffusivity）影响取向动力学，并假定取向各向异性与拉伸状态存在近似关系，从而推导基于取向的本构方程。

Result: 常数有效系数下，稳态-振荡剪切对应关系与经验性的 Cox-Merz 及 Gleissle-Osaki 关系密切相关；对特定模型系数，上随体 Maxwell 模型（upper-convected Maxwell model）表现为恒定松弛时间与稳态剪切下模量增加，而含 Peterlin 预平均（Peterlin preaveraging）的有限可延展性会缩短松弛时间并抑制模量增加。

Conclusion: 在由稳态剪切状态确定系数的条件下，所提方程可成功重现瞬态剪切、大振幅振荡剪切（large-amplitude oscillatory shear）与单轴拉伸（uniaxial extension）下的应力响应，表明构象拉伸对取向动力学的影响可被隐式保留。

Abstract: We propose a deviatoric-stress constitutive equation derived from orientational kinetics, while implicitly retaining contributions from conformational stretch. Starting from the dumbbell model, we show that conformational stretch affects orientational dynamics through an effective stress scale and rotational diffusivity, which determine an effective relaxation time. The orientation-based equation is derived by assuming an approximate relation between orientational anisotropy and the stretch state. Within the proposed framework, constant effective coefficients yield steady-oscillatory shear correspondences closely related to the empirical Cox-Merz and Gleissle-Osaki relations. For model-specific effective coefficients, the upper-convected Maxwell model is characterized by a constant relaxation time and an increasing modulus under steady shear, whereas finite extensibility with Peterlin preaveraging shortens the relaxation time and suppresses the modulus increase. With coefficients determined from steady-shear states, the proposed equation successfully reproduces stress responses under transient shear, large-amplitude oscillatory shear, and uniaxial extension. These results show that the effects of conformational stretch on orientational kinetics can be retained implicitly, providing a constitutive description based on a deviatoric-stress state reconstructable from standard rheometry and a physical interpretation of how stretch enters polymer viscoelasticity.

</details>


### [171] [Nucleation and phase transition of spherical crystals and quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00931)
*Tiejun Zhou,Aibei Xie,Gang Cui,Xianbo Huang,Li Peng,Kai Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究利用Lifshitz-Petrich模型，解析了球面六重对称晶体（C6）与十重对称准晶（DQC）的成核、生长及固-固相变路径，揭示了相变多步性与几何失配协调机制之间的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究球形六重对称晶体与十重对称准晶在成核、生长和固-固相变过程中如何协调几何失配（geometric mismatch），并理解相变路径多步性的结构根源。

Method: 采用Lifshitz-Petrich模型，结合球谐赝谱方法（spherical-harmonic pseudospectral method）与保零空间鞍点搜索技术（nullspace-preserving saddle-point search），解析临界核结构与相变路径。

Result: 均相到有序态的转变均涉及局域临界核的形成，但生长阶段失配协调模式不同：C6形成分支畴结构及与取向/相失配相关的持久线缺陷，DQC则通过局部配位重排弛豫碰撞诱导缺陷；DQC至C6路径经由一系列亚稳中间态逐步连接两种失配协调模式。

Conclusion: DQC-to-C6路径的多步特征反映了局部配位调整与新生成C6畴间相容性的耦合，揭示了相变多步性与几何失配协调的结构要求之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We investigated the nucleation, growth, and solid-to-solid phase transition processes of spherical six-fold symmetric crystals (C6) and ten-fold symmetric quasicrystals (DQC) using the Lifshitz-Petrich model. By combining the spherical-harmonic pseudospectral method with a nullspace-preserving saddle-point search technique, we resolved the critical nucleus structures and phase transition pathways. Although both transitions from the homogeneous state to the ordered state involve the formation of localized critical nuclei, their subsequent growth processes exhibit distinct modes of accommodating geometric mismatch: C6 forms branched domain structures and persistent line defects associated with orientation and phase mismatches, whereas DQC relaxes collision-induced defects through local coordination rearrangements. The resolved DQC-to-C6 pathway proceeds through a series of metastable intermediates that progressively connect these distinct modes of mismatch accommodation. Its multi-step character reflects the coupled adjustment of local coordination and compatibility between the emerging C6 domains. These results reveal the intrinsic link between the multi-step nature of the phase transition and the distinct structural requirements for accommodating geometric mismatch.

</details>


### [172] [Anisotropic medium-range order uncovers dynamic crossovers in glass-forming liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01051)
*Kamlesh Mishra,Rajesh Ganapathy,Walter Kob*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文引入一种新型四点关联函数（four-point correlation function），用于确定表征笼（cage）各向异性的结构长度尺度，并在分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）中发现该尺度及其差值在两个交叉温度处出现峰值，从而将玻璃态动力学的两个动力学交叉（dynamical crossover）统一到单一结构观测量上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体降温接近玻璃转变温度（glass-transition temperature）时，弛豫时间（relaxation time）急剧增大，并伴随两个不同温度处的动力学变化：高温交叉（high-T crossover）处粒子被邻近粒子暂时笼蔽，低温交叉（low-T crossover）处笼逃逸机制改变。前者已有局部粒子排列变化的证据，但后者尚未检测到结构变化，引发了对结构在玻璃态动力学中相关性的质疑。

Method: 引入一种新型四点关联函数（four-point correlation function），用于确定表征笼各向异性（cage anisotropy）的结构长度尺度；并开展大规模分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）进行验证。

Result: 该各向异性结构尺度与玻璃形成体（glass-former）的平均结构长度尺度均延伸至中程（medium range），显著超过粒子尺寸。二者之差——作为笼各向异性程度的度量——在两个交叉温度处均出现峰值。这些峰值可用于理解两个温度下微观输运机制变化的本质。

Conclusion: 两个动力学交叉（dynamical crossover）可被关联到同一结构观测量，即各向异性的中程有序（anisotropic medium-range order）。该结果证明，远超局部笼范围的结构是理解深度过冷液体（deeply supercooled liquids）弛豫动力学的必要组成。

Abstract: On cooling liquids towards their glass-transition temperature, the dramatic increase of their relaxation times is accompanied by changes in particle dynamics at two distinct temperatures: A high-T crossover, where particles become temporarily caged by their neighbors, and a low-T crossover, where the cage escape mechanism changes. While there is some evidence that the former is associated with a change in local particle arrangement, no structural modification has so far been detected across the low-T crossover, fueling scepticism about the relevance of structure for glassy dynamics. Here, we introduce a novel four-point correlation function which allows to determine a structural length scale characterizing cage anisotropy. Extensive molecular dynamics simulations reveal that this scale, as well as the mean structural length scale of the glass-former, extends into the medium range, i.e., significantly exceeds the particle size. Strikingly, the difference between these two scales - a measure of the degree of cage anisotropy - peaks at both crossover temperatures. We discuss how the presence of these peaks enables understanding the nature of the change in the microscopic transport mechanism at the two temperatures, thereby linking both dynamical crossovers to a single structural observable, the anisotropic medium-range order. This fundamental insight demonstrates that structure, extending well-beyond the local cage, is an essential ingredient for understanding the relaxation dynamics of deeply supercooled liquids.

</details>


### [173] [Inclined surfaces bias bacterial swarming expansion and edge organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01281)
*Pengxi Gu,Zixiang Lin,Hui Ren,Jin Zhu,Anqi Li,Omar Said,Weijie Chen,Zijie Qu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究以倾斜琼脂表面为手段，考察重力方向对 Enterobacter sp. SM3 群游（swarming）扩张的影响；发现扩张随倾角呈非单调变化，且上/下朝向构型响应不同，并借助 PIV 与扩展的两相薄膜模型加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准水平实验通常将重力视为固定背景条件，忽略表面相对重力的朝向可能对细菌群游扩张的影响。该研究旨在通过改变琼脂表面倾角，检验重力取向对群游扩张的作用。

Method: 采用表面倾斜法，在多个倾角下观察 Enterobacter sp. SM3 群落在水合软表面上的扩张；设置倾角相同但上/下朝向相反（锐角向上与钝角向下）的配对构型进行对比；使用粒子图像测速（PIV）分析群体流动速度与空间速度相关长度；并将两相薄膜（two-phase thin-film）模型扩展，引入沿琼脂表面法向与切向投影的有效重力耦合（effective gravity coupling）进行数值模拟。

Result: 菌落扩张与倾角呈非单调关系，中等倾角扩张最强，浅倾角或近倒置条件下扩张较弱；相同倾角幅度下，上朝向与下朝向构型扩张响应不同，表明扩张同时取决于倾角大小与琼脂面朝上的一侧。倾斜还导致菌落形态偏向下方（downhill）。PIV 显示倾斜条件下平均活动普遍低于水平参照，角度响应非单调，速度与空间速度相关长度向内陆方向增加；下坡尖端（downhill tip）较上坡区域运动更慢、空间相关更短、涡核更小。

Conclusion: 宏观趋势可通过引入沿表面法向与切向投影的有效重力耦合的两相薄膜模型解释：切向分量主要驱动横向再分布，法向分量改变薄膜压力，进而影响压力依赖的水分补充，从而区分上朝向与下朝向构型。

Abstract: Bacterial swarming is the collective expansion of dense populations of motile cells across hydrated soft surfaces, yet gravity is usually treated as a fixed background condition in standard horizontal assays. We used surface inclination to examine how agar-surface orientation relative to gravity affects the expansion of Enterobacter sp. SM3 swarms. Colony expansion depended non-monotonically on inclination, with the strongest spreading at intermediate inclinations and weaker expansion under shallow or near-inverted conditions. Paired up-facing acute and down-facing obtuse configurations with the same tilt magnitude showed different expansion responses, suggesting that swarm expansion depended on both tilt magnitude and which side of the agar surface faced upward relative to gravity. Inclination also produced downhill-biased colony morphologies. PIV analysis showed generally lower mean activity under inclination than in the flat references, with a non-monotonic angular response and inward increases in speed and spatial velocity-correlation length. The downhill tip showed slower motion, shorter spatial correlations and smaller vortex cores than the upper region. To interpret the macroscopic trends, we extended a two-phase thin-film model by introducing an effective gravity coupling projected normal and tangential to the agar surface. Simulations suggest that the surface-tangential component mainly drives lateral redistribution, whereas the surface-normal component modifies film pressure and thereby pressure-dependent water replenishment, helping distinguish up-facing from down-facing configurations.

</details>


### [174] [Ideal gas of active particles in a box](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01287)
*Siran Li,Ralf Eichhorn,Lennart Dabelow*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文将热力学基本概念推广至活性物质（active matter）的非平衡领域，推导出受限非相互作用活性粒子悬浮液的理想气体定律。该定律对一维跑跳粒子（run-and-tumble particles, RTPs）严格成立，并适用于三类主流理论模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将热力学的基本概念（如理想气体定律）从平衡态推广到活性物质的非平衡领域，以建立宏观层面描述活性物质热力学过程的框架，而无需依赖微观粒子轨迹或自驱动涨落。

Method: 通过解析方法推导：对一维跑跳粒子（RTPs）给出精确结果；对任意维度的活性 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 粒子（active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck particles）通过稳态概率密度的系统性解析展开建立该定律；并与活性布朗粒子（active Brownian particles）及高维 RTPs 的数值结果进行对比验证。

Result: 所得理想活性气体定律对一维 RTPs 严格成立，在任意维度与活性 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 粒子的解析展开一致，并与高维活性布朗粒子和 RTPs 的数值结果吻合良好；同时可退化为大系统尺寸极限下的已知关系。

Conclusion: 结果证明该理想活性气体定律适用于三类最广泛研究的胶体活性物质理论模型，并可作为应用，在宏观层面分析涉及活性物质的热力学过程，而无需借助微观粒子轨迹或自驱动涨落。

Abstract: Extending a fundamental concept of thermodynamics to the nonequilibrium realm of active matter, we derive an ideal gas law for a suspension of noninteracting active particles confined to a finite rectangular box. The law is exact for run-and-tumble particles (RTPs) in one dimension and established by a systematic analytical expansion of the steady-state probability density for active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck particles in any dimension. It also agrees well with numerical results for active Brownian particles and RTPs in higher dimensions and recovers previously known relations for the limit of large system sizes. Together, these results demonstrate the applicability of the ideal active gas law to the three most widely studied theoretical models of colloidal active matter. As an application, we analyze thermodynamic processes involving active matter on a macroscopic level, without recourse to microscopic particle trajectories or self-propulsion fluctuations.

</details>


### [175] [Characterization and Quantification of Immiscible Polymer Blend Compatibilization by Phyllosilicate Clays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01576)
*Ankit Patidar,Gaurav Goel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作开发了面向层状硅酸盐黏土（phyllosilicate clays）的 MARTINI-3 粗粒化（coarse-grained, CG）力场参数，覆盖不同表面极性，并用其模拟有机改性蒙脱土（MMT）对 TPS-PE 共混物形貌的增容作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状硅酸盐黏土因高各向异性（anisotropy）和可调表面极性广泛用于聚合物纳米复合材料，其在聚合物共混物中的分布与界面定位可调控材料性能；而适用于黏土的粗粒化力场可促进聚合物-黏土纳米复合材料（polymer-clay nanocomposites, PCNCs）的分子模拟开发。

Method: 采用 MARTINI-3 框架构建高化学特异性（chemical specificity）的 CG 力场：以 Lifshitz 理论结合实验表面张力数据得到的hydration free energies 确定黏土官能团初始相互作用，再利用全原子（all-atom, AA）分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟获得的热塑性淀粉（thermoplastic starch, TPS）-黏土复合材料的结构、热力学与动力学性质进行微调。

Result: CG 参数能准确重现未参与参数化的 TPS 组分径向分布函数（radial distribution function）与两体过量熵（two-body excess entropy），表明参数稳健；进而用大时间与空间尺度 MD 模拟研究十二烷基三甲基铵（C12TAB）改性蒙脱土（montmorillonite, MMT）对 TPS-聚乙烯（polyethylene, PE）共混形貌的影响，观察到两亲性黏土颗粒对 TPS-PE 界面的增容作用，界面张力由 45 mN/m 降至 13.06 mN/m。

Conclusion: MARTINI-3 对模型 MMT 基 PCNCs 性质的估计与 AA 模拟及实验数据吻合良好，为参数向其他体系的可迁移性（transferability）提供了依据；同时揭示了黏土表面极性对聚合物链段动力学及结构-性能关系的影响。

Abstract: Phyllosilicate clays are widely used in polymer nanocomposites owing to their high anisotropy and tunable surface polarity. Their distribution and interface localization in polymer blends can be used to tune the properties of polymer-clay nanocomposites (PCNCs). A coarse-grained (CG) force field for clays can aid molecular simulations in PCNC development. Here, we developed MARTINI-3 parameters, a CG force field with high chemical specificity, for phyllosilicate clays with diverse surface polarities. Initial interactions for the clay functional groups were determined from hydration free energies, obtained by applying the Lifshitz theory to experimental surface tension data. These were fine-tuned using the structural, thermodynamic, and dynamic properties of thermoplastic starch (TPS)-clay composites from all-atom (AA) molecular dynamics (MD) simulations. The radial distribution function and two-body excess entropy of TPS components, not used in CG parameterization, were accurately estimated, establishing the robustness of the parameters. We investigated the effect of clay surface polarity on polymer segmental dynamics and structure-property relationships. The CG parameters were then used to study the effect of dodecyltrimethylammonium (C12TAB)-modified montmorillonite (MMT), an organically-modified MMT, on TPS-polyethylene (PE) blend morphology using large time- and length-scale MD simulations. We observed compatibilization of the TPS-PE interphase by the amphiphilic clay particle, reducing the TPS-PE interfacial tension from 45 mN/m to 13.06 mN/m. We found good agreement between MARTINI-3 estimates for properties of model MMT-based PCNCs and those from AA simulations and experimental data, establishing grounds for the transferability of the parameters to other systems.

</details>


### [176] [Learning to Classify Threading in Melts of Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01621)
*Filippo Conforto,Roman Staňo,Jan Smrek,Davide Michieletto*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于环状构象缠绕数（writhe）的机器学习框架，用一维卷积神经网络（CNN1D）从缠绕曲线中直接检测环聚合物间的穿线（threading）纠缠，并在未重新训练的情况下推广至不同密度与链长的致密熔体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非串联环聚合物（nonconcatenated ring polymers）致密熔体呈现反常动力学与非线性流变学，部分被认为与长寿命的环间穿线纠缠（threading entanglement）相关；但现有几何约束检测方法计算成本高，且各方法依赖为特定模型调参的几何特征，通用性受限。

Method: 在孤立的、受距离约束的短环对上训练模型：以一维卷积神经网络（CNN1D）处理环构象的缠绕数（writhe）曲线，从而对穿线状态进行分类；随后将训练好的模型不加再训练地应用于平衡态致密熔体。

Result: 在留出的环对上，分类准确率超过98%；模型零再训练推广至宽密度范围与链长 N = 100 至 1600 的平衡致密熔体，真阳性率（true-positive rate）保持在91%以上，假阳性率（false-positive rate）接近零。误分类仅限物理上模糊的浅层穿线事件，被认为不影响环动力学。该方法相较最先进的最小曲面（minimal-surface）检测实现约3倍加速。

Conclusion: 缠绕数有效刻画了两个环之间的相互作用，使基于缠绕数的分类器成为表征环聚合物熔体纠缠的准确、可扩展工具，并提升了大环聚合物体系拓扑分析的效率。

Abstract: Dense melts of nonconcatenated ring polymers exhibit anomalous dynamics and nonlinear rheology, in some cases linked to long-lived inter-ring threading entanglement. Detecting these geometric constraints remains computationally demanding, with different methods being based on different geometric features tuned to specific models. Here, we introduce a machine-learning framework that detects threading directly from the writhe of ring conformations and we demonstrate its broad generalisability. Trained on simulations of isolated, distance-constrained pairs of short rings, a one-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN1D) operating on the writhe profile classifies threading states with over 98% accuracy on held-out ring pairs. Remarkably, this model generalises without retraining to equilibrium dense melts spanning a wide range of densities and chain lengths (N = 100 to 1600), maintaining true-positive rates above 91% with false-positive rates near zero. Misclassified conformations are limited to physically ambiguous, shallow threading events which are arguably not impacting the dynamics of the rings. Effectively, writhe captures the interaction between the two rings, allowing writhe-based classifiers to improve the topological analysis of large ring-polymer systems with a 3-fold speed-up over the state-of-the-art minimal-surface detection. These results establish the writhe-trained neural networks as an accurate, scalable tool for characterising entanglement in ring polymer melts.

</details>


### [177] [Microscopic origin of the chiral pressure in a two-dimensional fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02003)
*Francisco Vega Reyes*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文证明二维手性流体（chiral fluid）存在压力的反对称贡献，其微观起源为宇称破缺赝标量（pseudo-scalar）的平均值，并由微极性流体力学（micropolar hydrodynamics）的旋转黏度（rotational viscosity）决定；二者在梯度零阶即存续，对应一种均匀且稳定的"静止手性态（Quiescent Chiral State, QCS）"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规压强张量（pressure tensor）的动力学与碰撞通道无法解释手性流体中压力的反对称部分，需建立超出标准框架的微观描述，并厘清其在均匀极限下的行为。

Method: 通过考察宇称破缺赝标量的平均，识别手性压力的微观来源；借助微极性流体力学框架，将手性压力与旋转黏度相联系，并以高斯阶（Gaussian order）计算粗糙圆盘体系的旋转黏度动力学值。

Result: 手性压力由旋转黏度设定，且手性压力与旋转黏度均在梯度零阶（zeroth order in the gradients）下存活，即在完全均匀流体中依然存在；该均匀手性态被证明定义良好且为稳态。

Conclusion: 由于该平均赝标量在手性流体中不为零，其分布函数（distribution function）的任何展开都必须包含该赝标量变量的项，从而要求对常规分布函数展开方式进行扩展。

Abstract: We show that a two-dimensional chiral fluid supports an antisymmetric contribution to the pressure, and determine its microscopic expression. Its origin lies outside the standard kinetic and collisional channels of the normal pressure tensor, stemming instead from the average of a parity-breaking pseudo-scalar. This chiral pressure is set by the rotational (vortex) viscosity of micropolar hydrodynamics, whose kinetic value for rough disks we compute here, to Gaussian order. Crucially, both the chiral pressure and the rotational viscosity survive at zeroth order in the gradients, meaning they persist even in the fully homogeneous fluid -- which we show is not only well defined but steady (the Quiescent Chiral State, QCS). Since this averaged pseudo-scalar does not vanish in a chiral fluid, any expansion of its distribution function must contain terms in this pseudo-scalar variable.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [From Energy-Force Weighting to Primal-Dual Optimization of Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00876)
*Chenyu Wang,Yangshuai Wang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将能量-力平衡（energy–force balancing）重新定义为依赖拟合协议（protocol-dependent）的物理模型选择问题，并以力约束（force-constrained）拟合替代直接权重选择，在三个体系上取得一致的低误差区域与约 6.7–8.9 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）通常以加权求和标量化（weighted-sum scalarization）拟合，但名义权重只有相对于完整拟合协议才有意义，导致能量与力误差的平衡缺乏明确的物理选择准则。作者因此把该平衡视为协议依赖的物理模型选择问题。

Method: 对熔融 LiCl、液态 H$_2$O 与 Si 的固定基组原子簇展开（atomic cluster expansion）模型构建标量化路径，并改用受约束形式：最小化正则化能量目标（regularized energy objective），同时对归一化力损失（normalized force loss）施加上界。求解采用投影对偶上升（projected dual ascent）依据力约束残差调整 Lagrange 乘子，再以冻结乘子的有限内存拟牛顿（limited-memory quasi-Newton）精修得到最终模型。

Result: 标量化路径在极端权重下存在被支配状态（dominated states），其非支配子集（nondominated subsets）依赖求解器，且分布外（out-of-distribution）的 Si 路径呈非单调行为。受约束流程在三体系中均达到求解器匹配的低误差区域，在给定计时约定下相对完整标量化扫描获得 6.7–8.9 倍实测加速。物理性质计算显示第一壳层几何（first-shell geometry）对所选取的平衡相对不敏感，而输运（transport）与固态观测量变化更明显。

Conclusion: 结果表明，拟合模型与协议的组合（model–protocol pair）才是能量-力模型选择的相关对象，并支持将力约束拟合（force-constrained fitting）作为一项显式的选择规则（explicit selection rule）。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials are commonly fitted by weighted-sum scalarization, which combines energy and force errors in a single loss. A nominal weight, however, identifies a potential only relative to the complete fitting protocol. We therefore treat energy--force balancing as a protocol-dependent problem of physical model selection. For fixed-basis atomic cluster expansion models of molten LiCl, liquid H$_2$O, and Si, the resulting scalarization paths contain dominated states at extreme weights. Their nondominated subsets depend on the solver, and the out-of-distribution Si path is nonmonotone. We replace direct weight selection by minimizing the regularized energy objective subject to an upper bound on the normalized force loss. Projected dual ascent adjusts the Lagrange multiplier from the force-constraint residual. A frozen-multiplier limited-memory quasi-Newton refinement then returns the final model. Across the three systems, the constrained procedure reaches the solver-matched low-error region and gives measured speedups of $6.7$--$8.9$ over completed scalarization scans under the stated timing convention. Physical-property calculations show that first-shell geometry is comparatively insensitive to the selected balance, whereas transport and solid-state observables vary more strongly. These results identify the fitted model--protocol pair as the relevant object of energy--force model selection and support force-constrained fitting as an explicit selection rule.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Wasserstein Gradient Flows and Forward-Only Diffusion Are Not Enough for Multimodal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02081)
*Daniel McBride,Pratik Khandagale,Cristina Garcia-Cardona,Yen Ting Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文反驳“基于 Wasserstein 梯度流（Wasserstein gradient flow, WGF）与前向扩散过程（forward-only diffusion process, FODP）的采样算法可高效处理复杂多峰分布”这一常见解读。作者借助 JKO 格式与 Otto  calculus 证明该类动力学与过阻尼前向扩散共享同一密度演化，因而继承非平衡统计物理中已知的亚稳态（metastability）与慢混合现象；谱分析与平均首次通过时间（mean first-passage time, MFPT）分析显示，分离良好的多峰性会带来指数级长的混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期大量采样算法（WGF、FODP）常以“指数级快速收敛到目标分布”的理论保证被解读为能够高效采样复杂多峰分布。作者认为这种解读具有根本性误导，需澄清指数收敛保证与多峰采样效率之间的区别。

Method: 引入 Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto（JKO）格式与 Otto 演算（Otto calculus），论证典型 WGF 采样动力学与过阻尼前向扩散具有相同的密度演化，从而共享亚稳态与慢混合特性；并使用两种互补工具进行分析：谱分析（spectral analysis）与平均首次通过时间（mean first-passage time, MFPT）分析，针对常用的对数线性退火调度（log-linear annealing schedule）考察中间分布的作用。

Result: 分离良好的多峰性可导致指数级长的混合时间，表现为小谱隙（small spectral gaps）与稀少的跨模态跃迁（inter-mode transitions）。在所考察的对数线性退火调度下，引入中间分布并不能消除总输运时间的指数级标度。

Conclusion: 该局限是结构性的而非实现细节：纯局部、梯度驱动的输运机制在跨分离模态输送概率质量时可能需要指数级长的时间。这构成 WGF 与 FODP 类采样在其标准形式下的根本性限制，并提示未来需发展本质非局部（nonlocal）机制以实现高效多峰采样。

Abstract: There has been a proliferation of sampling algorithms based on Wasserstein gradient flows (WGF) and forward-only diffusion processes (FODP), often accompanied by theoretical guarantees of exponentially fast convergence to the target distribution. These guarantees are frequently interpreted as evidence that such methods can efficiently sample complex multimodal distributions, often supported by empirical results. In this work, we argue that this interpretation is fundamentally misleading. By invoking the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme and Otto calculus, we establish that the canonical WGF sampling dynamics and overdamped forward diffusion share the same density evolution and therefore inherit the same metastability and slow-mixing phenomena long understood in nonequilibrium statistical physics. We analyze this family of samplers using two complementary tools -- spectral analysis and mean first-passage time (MFPT) analysis -- and show that well-separated multimodality can induce exponentially long mixing times associated with small spectral gaps and rare inter-mode transitions. For the commonly adopted log-linear annealing schedule studied here, we find that introducing intermediate distributions does not remove the exponential scaling of the total transport time. The limitation is structural rather than implementation-specific: purely local, gradient-driven transport mechanisms can require exponentially long times to transport probability mass across well-separated modes. We argue that this represents a fundamental limitation of WGF- and FODP-based sampling in their standard forms, and motivates future development of fundamentally nonlocal mechanisms for efficient multimodal sampling.

</details>


### [180] [The hidden advantage of mask resampling: a theory of masked autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01578)
*Jorge Medina Moreira,Lorenzo Bardone,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究通过高维掩码自编码器（masked autoencoder, MAE）模型，从理论上分离了掩码预测目标（masked prediction objective）与掩码多样性（mask diversity）各自的统计收益，并用实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心疑问是：为何掩码预测能学到有用表征，而非掩码重建（等价于 PCA）却无法做到。研究旨在区分掩码预测目标本身与掩码重采样带来的收益，并解释标准训练流程是否掩盖了这些优势。

Method: 在具有共享潜在结构（shared latent structure）与异质噪声（heterogeneous noise）的高维数据上构建掩码自编码器模型，给出掩码线性重建与未掩码线性重建的样本复杂度（sample complexity）对比；并引入每样本固定的 K 个掩码集合，刻画掩码多样性对特征恢复与下游性能的影响，随后在 CNN 自编码器、视觉 Transformer（vision transformer）与 BERT 上进行验证。

Result: 理论上证明掩码线性重建可在未掩码线性重建（即 PCA）失败的区间内以线性样本复杂度恢复潜在特征，并量化了掩码重采样的统计优势及更大掩码多样性降低样本复杂度的区间；实验发现随机裁剪与翻转通过更新预测任务会掩盖掩码重采样的优势，去除这些变换后动态掩码优于静态掩码，BERT 试点亦显示更大掩码多样性的下游收益。

Conclusion: 结果将掩码预测目标与掩码多样性的收益加以区分，表明可处理的理论能够指导实验设计，从而揭示被标准训练实践所掩盖的优势。

Abstract: Why can masked prediction learn useful representations that unmasked reconstruction misses? We study this question in a high-dimensional model of a masked autoencoder (MAE) trained on data with shared latent structure and heterogeneous noise. We prove that masked linear reconstruction can recover the latent feature at linear sample complexity in regimes where unmasked linear reconstruction, equivalent to PCA, fails. The analysis also quantifies the statistical advantage of mask resampling, an established ingredient of masked pretraining. By introducing a fixed collection of $K$ masks per sample, we characterize its effect on feature recovery and downstream performance, identifying regimes where greater mask diversity lowers sample complexity. Guided by this prediction, we find that random cropping and flipping in standard image-training pipelines can obscure the advantage of mask resampling by renewing the prediction task even when the patch mask is fixed. Removing these transformations reveals a downstream advantage for dynamic over static masking in CNN autoencoders and vision transformers. A complementary BERT pilot finds benefits from greater mask diversity on downstream language tasks. Our results separate the benefit of the masked prediction objective from that of mask diversity, and show how a tractable theory can guide experiments that uncover advantages hidden by standard training practices.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Scaling Collider Event Generation with Residual-Quantized Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00569)
*Dan Godi,Dmitrii Kobylianskii,Eilam Gross*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于残差量化（residual quantization）的粒子级离散生成模型，用于对撞机全事件的条件生成，并系统刻画其规模化行为与数据重复暴露效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度大型强子对撞机（High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, HL-LHC）下全探测器模拟与重建预计成为主要瓶颈，需要快速的、基于机器学习（machine learning, ML）的替代方案；同时自回归 Transformer（autoregressive transformer）在离散生成建模上已取得领先进展，为事件生成提供新范式。

Method: 将离散建模范式扩展至粒子层级，在残差量化（residual-quantized）全事件数据上训练生成模型，并实现以探测器稳定粒子（detector-stable particles）为条件的条件生成。

Result: 模型族具备条件生成能力；研究覆盖多种数据集规模与模型规模的规模化（scaling）行为，刻画了数据重复暴露（repeated data exposure）的影响，并表明 token 级损失（token-level loss）可系统性预测下游物理保真度（physical fidelity）。

Conclusion: 结果为基于残差量化表示的可扩展对撞机全事件生成提供了实证框架。

Abstract: Full detector simulation and reconstruction of collider events are projected to become major bottlenecks at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, motivating the development of fast, ML-based surrogates. At the same time, LLMs have driven fast progress in generative discrete modeling: autoregressive transformers trained on tokenized data now represent the state of the art across a range of generative tasks. We extend the discrete modeling paradigm by introducing a particle-level generative model trained on residual-quantized full-event data. We demonstrate the ability of this model family to perform conditional generation from detector-stable particles; we study its scaling behavior across a range of dataset and model sizes, characterize the effects of repeated data exposure and demonstrate that token-level loss systematically predicts downstream physical fidelity. These results provide an empirical framework for scalable collider full-event generation based on residual-quantized representations.

</details>


### [182] [Search for dark matter produced in association with a low-multiplicity jet in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00995)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用 CMS 实验 13 TeV 质子-质子碰撞数据，首次采用低组分喷流特征并借助监督机器学习搜索暗物质，未观测到超出标准模型预期的信号，并对矢量与轴矢中介子简化模型给出截面上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质粒子（dark matter particles）的直接探测依赖对特征性末态信号的搜寻；本文针对大缺失横向动量（missing transverse momentum）与低组分喷流（low-multiplicity jet）这一困难末态，旨在提升对该类信号的探测灵敏度。

Method: 分析使用 CMS 实验在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）13 TeV 下记录的质子-质子碰撞（proton-proton collision）数据，对应积分亮度（integrated luminosity）138 fb$^{-1}$（2016$-$2018 年 LHC 取数期）。这是 LHC 上首个采用低组分喷流特征的搜寻，并利用监督机器学习（supervised machine learning）最大化对该末态潜在信号的灵敏度。

Result: 未观测到相对标准模型（standard model）背景预期的事件超出。结果被用于在含矢量与轴矢中介子（vector and axial-vector mediators）的简化模型（simplified models）中，以 95% 置信水平（confidence level, CL）设定暗物质产生截面（production cross section）的上限。

Conclusion: 当暗物质粒子质量约为 150 (550) GeV 时，中介子质量分别被排除至最高 4250 (3500) GeV（95% CL）。

Abstract: A search for dark matter particles is presented using events with large missing transverse momentum and a single energetic jet containing few constituents. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data recorded by the CMS experiment at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV. This corresponds to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, recorded during the 2016$-$2018 LHC data-taking period. This is the first search at the LHC to employ the low-multiplicity jet signature. Supervised machine learning is used to maximize sensitivity to potential signals in this challenging final state. No excess of events over the standard model background prediction is observed. The results are used to set upper limits on the dark matter production cross section at 95% confidence level (CL) within simplified models featuring vector and axial-vector mediators. For a dark matter particle mass of approximately 150 (550) GeV, mediator masses up to 4250 (3500) GeV are excluded at 95% CL.

</details>


### [183] [Full Event Interpretation at Belle II: Current Status and Recent Developments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01189)
*R. Tiwary,M. Marfoli,V. Vobbilisetti,K. Trabelsi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 全事件解释（Full Event Interpretation, FEI）算法是 Belle II 分析框架中用于高效重建专属 B 介子（B-meson）衰变的核心组件；本文介绍其设计、实现、近期算法改进以及新开发的标定与性能评估技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FEI 对涉及缺失能量（missing energy）的信号侧衰变研究尤为重要：通过标记一个 B 介子，可严格约束另一信号侧 B 介子的运动学，从而改善本底抑制（background rejection）。因此需要不断改进其重建流程与性能评估手段。

Method: FEI 对强子（hadronic）与半轻子（semileptonic）末态进行分层重建（hierarchical reconstruction），采用多变量分类（multivariate classification）技术标记电子-正电子碰撞中产生的两个 B 介子之一；其多阶段多变量训练基于梯度提升决策树（gradient boosted decision trees）。

Result: 近期改进涵盖三方面：用于训练的模拟数据（simulated data）、中间粒子（intermediate particles）的重建，以及多变量输入的稳健性（robustness）。此外，本文还报告了用于标定（calibration）与性能评估的新技术，包括效率标定的改进流程、跨衰变通道与取数条件的性能检验，以及应用重加权技术（reweighting techniques）诊断并缓解模拟与数据（simulation and data）之间的差异。

Conclusion: 论文概述了 FEI 的重建策略、候选选择（candidate selection）与多阶段多变量训练，并简要讨论了旨在进一步提升重建性能与计算效率（computational efficiency）的在研及未来发展方向。

Abstract: The Full Event Interpretation (FEI) algorithm is a central component of the Belle II analysis framework, designed for the efficient reconstruction of exclusive B-meson decays. The FEI performs a hierarchical reconstruction of hadronic and semileptonic final states, using multivariate classification techniques to tag one of the two B mesons produced in electron-positron collisions. The FEI is particularly important for studies of signal-side decays involving missing energy. By reconstructing one B meson with the FEI, the kinematics of the other, signal-side B meson are tightly constrained, leading to improved background rejection. In this contribution, we present the design, implementation, and recent developments of the FEI. We outline the reconstruction strategy, candidate selection, and multistage multivariate training based on gradient boosted decision trees. Recent improvements to the FEI workflow, including improvements in the simulated data used for training, reconstruction of intermediate particles, and improved robustness of the multivariate inputs, are discussed in detail. Beyond algorithmic updates, we also discuss newly developed techniques for the calibration and performance evaluation of the FEI. These include refined procedures to calibrate the efficiency, check the performance across decay channels and data-taking conditions, and application of reweighting techniques to diagnose and mitigate discrepancies between simulation and data. Finally, we briefly discuss ongoing and future developments aimed at further improving reconstruction performance and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [184] [Search for charged-lepton-flavour violating $eμtq$ interactions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01308)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS 利用 140 fb⁻¹ 的 13 TeV 质子-质子对撞数据，搜寻顶夸克产生与衰变中的带电轻子味破坏（cLFV），未观察到显著偏离标准模型（Standard Model）的信号，并在有效场论（Effective Field Theory）框架下给出六个 Wilson 系数的 95% 置信水平上限及相应的 cLFV 顶夸克衰变分支比上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电轻子味破坏（cLFV）在标准模型（Standard Model）中受到高度压低，其可观测信号通常被视为新物理（new physics）的敏感探针。本文旨在通过对顶夸克产生与衰变过程的搜寻，检验 cLFV 相互作用是否在顶夸克能标下存在。

Method: 分析使用大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider）上 ATLAS 探测器记录的 13 TeV 质子-质子对撞数据，对应积分亮度 140 fb⁻¹。研究两类 cLFV 机制：一是单顶夸克（single-top quark）轻子衰变伴随一对反号异味轻子（oppositely-charged different-flavour leptons）的产生；二是顶夸克对（top-quark pairs）产生，其中一个顶夸克衰变为反号异味轻子对与 u 或 c 夸克，另一个按标准模型衰变为轻子衰变的 W 玻色子与 b 夸克。信号过程包含三个带电轻子（电子或缪子），结果在有效场论（Effective Field Theory）框架下进行解释。

Result: 未观察到相对标准模型（Standard Model）预言的显著偏离。在 95% 置信水平（confidence level）下对六个 Wilson 系数给出上限，范围从张量（tensor）u 夸克耦合的 |C^tensor_{eμtu}|/Λ² < 0.018 TeV⁻² 到标量（scalar）c 夸克耦合的 |C^scalar_{eμtc}|/Λ² < 0.32 TeV⁻²。

Conclusion: 上述 Wilson 系数上限被转换为 cLFV 顶夸克衰变分支比（branching ratio）的 95% 置信水平上限，范围从标量 u 夸克耦合对应的 B(t → eμu) < 6·10⁻⁹ 到张量 c 夸克耦合对应的 B(t → eμc) < 2.7·10⁻⁷。结果未提供 cLFV 在顶夸克过程中存在的证据，但给出了具有约束力的参数上限。

Abstract: A search for charged-lepton-flavour violation (cLFV) in top-quark production and decay is presented. The data analysed correspond to 140 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collisions recorded at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}= 13$ TeV by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider. The possible cLFV contribution is studied via two mechanisms. The first is via the production of a leptonically-decaying single-top quark in association with a pair of oppositely-charged different-flavour leptons. The second is the production of top-quark pairs, where one top quark decays into a pair of oppositely-charged different-flavour leptons and a $u$- or $c$-quark, while the other decays into a leptonically-decaying $W$ boson and a $b$-quark, according to the Standard Model. The signal processes contain three charged leptons, taken to be electrons or muons. No significant deviation from the Standard Model prediction is observed, and these results are interpreted within an Effective Field Theory framework. Upper limits are placed at 95% confidence level on six Wilson coefficients, ranging from $|C^{\mathrm{tensor}}_{eμtu}|/Λ^2 < 0.018$ TeV$^{-2}$ to $|C^{\mathrm{scalar}}_{eμtc}|/Λ^2 < 0.32$ TeV$^{-2}$ for the tensor $u$-quark and scalar $c$-quark couplings, respectively. These Wilson coefficient limits are translated into 95% confidence level upper limits on cLFV top-quark decay branching ratios, ranging from $B(t \to eμu) < 6 \cdot 10^{-9}$ to $B(t \to eμc) < 2.7 \cdot 10^{-7}$ for the scalar $u$-quark and tensor $c$-quark couplings, respectively.

</details>


### [185] [A-GHOST: High-rate streaming of trigger-level data to programmable GPU inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01761)
*I. Xiotidis,N. Clarke Hall,M. S. Larson,R. Gurunathan,C. Burdick,T. Du,N. Konstantinidis,K. Kordas,D. Leshchev,D. W. Miller,V. A. Petrovic,D. Sampsonidou,A. Thompson,T. Wengler*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: A-GHOST 是一项面向高能物理（high-energy physics, HEP）实验的高速率流式读出（streaming readout）研发工作，将前端从决策平台转变为聚合与流式数据源，并通过 NVIDIA IGX Thor 平台验证了 GPU 后端在 40–100 Gbps 下稳定推理的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硬件触发（hardware trigger）受固定延迟与资源上限约束，难以部署生成式与时间感知神经网络等复杂算法。该工作旨在突破确定性前端处理的局限，把复杂计算迁移到 GPU 后端。

Method: 采用软件限速发射器经 QSFP 接口与外部环回电缆连接第二 QSFP 接口，在 FPGA 源集成前先研究网络到 GPU 路径；使用 NVIDIA DAQIRI 将数据直接接收至 GPU 可访问内存，并以自定义 CUDA 内核将数据包重组为持久连续的 TensorRT 输入窗口，数据类型转换由模型处理。

Result: 在由十个主导量能器簇（calorimeter clusters）构成的 HEP 事件表示下，后端在保持恒定输入速率的同时从 40 Gbps 扩展至 100 Gbps，p95 推理延迟为 0.118 ms，系统连续运行数小时无丢包或推理错误。

Conclusion: 生成式与时间感知神经网络展示了超越常规固定延迟 FPGA 触发系统的工作负载，结果确立了 A-GHOST 作为未来 FPGA 到 GPU 流式读出系统基础的地位。

Abstract: A-GHOST (A Global Heterogeneous Online Scouting Trigger) is an R&D effort investigating high-rate streaming readout architectures for high-energy physics (HEP) experiments. The central idea is to convert the deterministic front-end and fixed-latency processing close to the detector from a decision-making platform to an aggregation and streaming source. Compact trigger-level data are streamed to a GPU-enabled backend, where substantially more complex algorithms can run beyond the resource and latency envelope of the hardware trigger. This paper presents a proof-of-concept backend using the NVIDIA IGX Thor development kit. A software rate-controlled transmitter sends data through a QSFP interface and external loopback cable to a second QSFP interface, allowing the network-to-GPU path to be studied before integration with FPGA sources. NVIDIA DAQIRI enables reception directly into GPU-accessible memory, while a custom CUDA kernel reassembles packet payloads into persistent, contiguous TensorRT input windows without data-type conversion, which is handled by the models. Using a HEP-derived event representation consisting of the ten leading calorimeter clusters, the backend scales from 40 to 100 Gbps while sustaining a constant input rate and an inference latency of 0.118 ms at the p95 interval. The system operates for multiple hours without drops or inference errors. Generative and temporally aware neural networks demonstrate workloads beyond those normally deployed in fixed-latency FPGA trigger systems. The result establishes A-GHOST as a basis for future FPGA-to-GPU streaming readout systems.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Parallax Depth Sectioning and 3D Reconstruction in 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01057)
*Desheng Ma,Chia-Hao Lee,Zixiao Shi,David A. Muller,Steven E. Zeltmann*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文建立四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D-STEM）深度信息编码/解码与层析成像（tomography）、光场摄影（light-field photography）之间的联系，推导出三维相位衬度传递函数，并证明可在小角近似下以非迭代方式实现深度切片重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D-STEM 通过不同探测器像素处虚拟像之间的视差（parallax）编码三维信息，但其深度信息传递机制与现有三维成像方法的关系尚不清晰，缺乏统一的几何与波动光学视角描述。

Method: 将 4D 数据视为角度切片（angular slices）的集合以得到 sinogram，并在小角近似下分析倾斜校正明场（tcBF）深度切片与非迭代层析重建的等价性；同时将数据映射到光场（plenoptic）成像，借用其算法从单次四维快速傅里叶变换（4D FFT）中提取二维切片获得深度截面，并与深度分辨的像差校正明场（acBF）成像对比。

Result: tcBF 深度切片可由 4D 数据集的单次 4D FFT 中提取二维切片得到，在重构大量深度切片时显著提速；acBF 相比 tcBF 可消除衬度振荡并减小响应随深度的变化，在模拟与实验数据中均分辨出堆叠氧化物薄膜与纳米颗粒组装体中的不同层。

Conclusion: 基于单一 4D-STEM 数据集的非迭代体成像（volumetric reconstruction）对厚样品、弱散射样品成像以及低剂量采集（low-dose acquisition）的重构具有应用前景。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) information is encoded in four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) through parallax between the virtual images formed at each detector pixel. In this paper we connect the encoding and retrieval of depth-dependent information in 4D-STEM to two other widely-used 3D imaging methods, tomography and light-field photography, and derive its 3D phase contrast transfer function. Viewing the 4D data as a collection of angular slices yields a collection of sinograms, providing an intuitive visual representation of the 3D information transfer. Within the small-angle approximation, tilt-corrected bright-field (tcBF) depth sectioning is equivalent to non-iterative tomographic reconstruction. Alternatively, the 4D data also can be mapped to light-field (or plenoptic) imaging, which acquires spatially resolved diffraction data in parallel using an array of lenslets. Adapting a light-field imaging algorithm, we show that tcBF depth sections can be obtained by extracting two-dimensional slices from a single four-dimensional fast Fourier transform (4D FFT) of the dataset, yielding a substantial speedup when reconstructing a large number of depth slices. These differing perspectives distinguish the geometric aspects of depth resolution from wave-optical effects that are present in phase-contrast imaging. Depth-resolved aberration-corrected bright-field (acBF) imaging yields improved 3D reconstructions compared to tcBF, removing contrast oscillations and reducing the variation in response with depth. We demonstrate acBF depth sectioning using simulated and experimental data and resolve distinct layers in stacked oxide films and nanoparticle assemblies. These non-iterative volumetric reconstructions from a single 4D-STEM dataset show promise for improved imaging of thick, weakly-scattering samples and for reconstructing low-dose acquisitions.

</details>


### [187] [Thin-capping layer epitaxial quantum dots for near-field quantum photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02080)
*Yuting Guo,Jonathan Bar-David,Pasquale Cilibrizzi,Sung-Yul L. Park,Jin Dong Song,Luca Sapienza*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作通过将 InAs/GaAs 外延量子点（epitaxial quantum dots, QDs）的 GaAs 盖层（capping layer）厚度减薄至 10nm 与 20nm（并对比标准 95nm），在保持激子寿命（excitonic lifetimes）不受扰动的前提下，获得比此前报道最多小 5 倍的发射线宽（emission linewidths），证明外延量子点靠近表面时仍能维持高光学质量，为近场光-物质相互作用（near-field light-matter interactions）研究提供可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外延量子点被视为优质量子光源（quantum light sources），具备稳定性、亮度、量子效率（quantum efficiency）与相干性（coherence）优势。但其通常依赖较厚盖层（一般超过 50nm）来屏蔽表面态（surface states）的不利影响，这阻碍了等离激元（plasmonic）等近场效应的实现——因为自发辐射动力学（spontaneous emission dynamics）与发射方向性（directionality）的调控要求发光体与金属结构距离在数十纳米以内。

Method: 本文报告了 InAs/GaAs 量子点的生长与光学表征（optical characterisation），所采用的 GaAs 盖层厚度分别为 10nm、20nm 以及更标准的 95nm，并对不同厚度下的光学性质进行比较。

Result: 观测到发射线宽最多比先前报道小 5 倍，且激子寿命未受扰动（unperturbed）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使发光体与表面距离缩短，外延量子点的高光学质量仍可维持，从而为利用相干且稳定的发光体探索近场光-物质相互作用开辟道路，适用于量子技术（quantum technology）应用。

Abstract: Epitaxial quantum dots (QDs) are widely recognised as one of the best quantum light sources, given their good stability, brightness, quantum efficiency and coherence. To reach such properties, QDs are protected from potentially detrimental surface states by a relatively thick capping layer, typically exceeding 50nm. This prevents the implementation of near-field effects, like plasmonic-based ones, that can dramatically increase the light-matter interaction, since the control of the spontaneous emission dynamics and directionality of the emission can only occur if the emitter is in close proximity (typically tens of nanometers or less) from the metallic structures. In this work, we report the growth and optical characterisation of InAs/GaAs QDs with GaAs capping layer thickness of 10nm, 20nm, and a more standard 95nm. Remarkably, we observe emission linewidths up to 5 times smaller than previously reported, with unperturbed excitonic lifetimes. These results demonstrate that the epitaxial QD's high optical quality can be maintained even when the emitters are at reduced distances from the surface, opening the path for the exploration of near-field light-matter interactions with coherent and stable emitters, suitable for quantum technology applications.

</details>

### [188] [Contraction Algebras, Cyclotomic Forms, and Reflection Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00263)
*Xiaobin Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文为有限分块规模数据 $\bm n$ 构造正定分圆形式（cyclotomic form）与 Householder 反射表示，证明关于反射群算术刚性的主定理：其有限性当且仅当保持满秩格（full rank lattice），等价于支撑集为 $\{1,N\}$ 且 $n_1=N^2n_N$；并在三折收缩代数（contraction algebras of threefold flops）与 BPS/Riemann–Hilbert 框架中给出应用与实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图在有限数据序列、分裂半单代数（split semisimple algebra）与反射群之间建立统一的算术框架，并刻画反射群何时有限。其动机之一是利用该形式的不变量性质（Fourier 谱、整除宽度）识别代数同构类型，并将该判据应用于三折收缩代数等几何场景。

Method: 以数据 $\bm n=(n_d)_{d\ge1}$（$n_1>0$）、$N=\operatorname{lcm}\{d:n_d\ne0\}$ 与 $\wt=\sum_d d^2n_d$ 构造 $C_N$ 上的形式 $B_{\bm n}(g,h)=\frac1{\wt}\sum_{(g^{-1}h)^d=1}d^2n_d$，并给出相应的 Householder 反射表示。通过带标签的 Fourier 谱与 Möbius 反演（Möbius inversion）重建完整重数序列；在 $S=\prod_d M_d(k)^{n_d}$ 情形，将整除宽度解释为 Wedderburn 块特征和的维数。主定理通过“有限性 $\Leftrightarrow$ 保持满秩格”的算术刚性论证完成，随后结合 Toda 宽度公式（Toda's width formulas）处理三折 flop 的收缩代数，并给出 BPS/Riemann–Hilbert 实现。

Result: 主定理表明：对 $N\ge2$，反射群有限当且仅当其保持满秩格，等价于 $\supp(\bm n)=\{1,N\}$、$n_1=N^2n_N$；此时 $B_{\bm n}=\frac12(I+J)$，群为 $W(A_N)\cong S_{N+1}$。在分裂半单代数语言下，有限性等价于 $S\cong k^{N^2m}\times M_N(k)^m$（$m\ge1$）。$N=2$ 时，每个非有限情形对应正交群中 $D_\infty$ 的稠密拷贝。对三折 flop 的收缩代数，Toda 宽度公式将总尺度与原始 Fourier 模分别识别为 $\dim A_{\con}$ 与 $\dim A_{\con}^{\ab}$，从而推出长度至少为 3 的所有不可约 flop 均对应无限群，而长度 2 时 $\Gamma_f$ 有限当且仅当 $\dim A_{\con}=2\dim A_{\con}^{\ab}$。

Conclusion: 论文给出了反射群有限性的算术刚性刻画，并将其与分裂半单代数的同构类型、三折 flop 收缩代数的不变量联系起来；最后提供了同一反射矩阵的 BPS/Riemann–Hilbert 互补实现，说明该形式具有跨代数、几何与物理表述的统一性。

Abstract: We associate to finite block size data \[ \bm n=(n_d)_{d\ge1},\qquad n_1>0, \] a positive definite cyclotomic form and a Householder reflection representation. Writing \[ N=\operatorname{lcm}\{d:n_d\ne0\},\qquad \wt=\sum_d d^2n_d, \] the form on the cyclic group $C_N$ is \[ B_{\bm n}(g,h)=\frac1{\wt}\sum_{(g^{-1}h)^d=1}d^2n_d. \] Its labelled Fourier spectrum reconstructs the complete multiplicity sequence by Möbius inversion. If the $n_d$ are the Wedderburn multiplicities of a split semisimple algebra $S=\prod_d M_d(k)^{n_d}$, the divisibility widths are dimensions of characteristic sums of Wedderburn blocks, and the form together with $\dim_kS$ recovers the isomorphism type of $S$.
  The main theorem is an arithmetic rigidity result. For $N\ge2$, the reflection group is finite if and only if it preserves a full rank lattice, equivalently \[ \supp(\bm n)=\{1,N\},\qquad n_1=N^2n_N. \] In this case $B_{\bm n}=\frac12(I+J)$ and the group is $W(A_N)\cong S_{N+1}$. In semisimple algebra terms, finiteness is equivalent to \[ S\cong k^{N^2m}\times M_N(k)^m \] for some $m\ge1$. For $N=2$, every nonfinite case is a dense copy of $D_\infty$ in the corresponding orthogonal group.
  For contraction algebras of threefold flops, Toda's width formulas identify the total scale and primitive Fourier mode with $\dim A_{\con}$ and $\dim A_{\con}^{\ab}$. This yields infinitude for all irreducible flops of length at least three and, in length two, \[ Γ_f\text{ finite}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad \dim A_{\con}=2\dim A_{\con}^{\ab}. \] The final part gives a complementary BPS/Riemann--Hilbert realization of the same reflection matrices.

</details>


### [189] [Supermoduli spaces are non-projected in every genus $g\geq 3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00490)
*Mauricio Corrêa,Ron Donagi,Simone Noja*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明对于任意亏格 $g\geq 3$，光滑无标点超黎曼曲面模超叠的每个宇称分支，其主障碍在代数与全纯意义下均非零，从而两个分支都非投射且非分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究光滑无标点超黎曼曲面（smooth unpunctured super Riemann surface）模超叠（moduli superstack）各宇称分支（parity component）的几何结构性质，特别是其相对约化模叠的可投射性与分裂性。

Method: 对每个亏格 $g\geq 3$ 的每个宇称分支，计算并证明其主障碍（primary obstruction）在代数与全纯（holomorphic）两种范畴下均非零。

Result: 主障碍非零这一结论推出两个宇称分支均为非投射（non-projected），并因此非分裂（non-split）：两者都不存在到其约化自旋曲线模叠（moduli stack of spin curves）的代数或全纯收缩（retraction）。

Conclusion: 在 $g\geq 3$ 情形下，超黎曼曲面模超叠的宇称分支无法通过收缩退化到约化模叠，表明其超结构不可被代数或全纯地平凡化。

Abstract: We prove that, for every genus $g\geq 3$, the primary obstruction of each parity component of the moduli superstack of smooth unpunctured super Riemann surfaces is nonzero, both algebraically and holomorphically. Consequently, both components are non-projected and, in particular, non-split: neither admits an algebraic or holomorphic retraction onto its reduced moduli stack of spin curves.

</details>


### [190] [Conifold factorization in topological recursion and Gromov--Witten theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01557)
*Juping Chen,Linji Chen,Bohan Fang,Zhengyu Zong*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文在谱曲线（spectral curve）的规则结点退化（regular nodal degeneration）附近，为普通拓扑递归（topological recursion）证明了一个因子化定理（factorization theorem），允许对数谱坐标。闭合亏格 $g\ge2$ 的配分函数（partition function）分解为归一化（normalization）的配分函数、一个普适高斯真空（Gaussian vacuum）以及一个连通图求和（connected graph sum）的指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解拓扑递归在谱曲线结点退化处的行为，并刻画消失周期（vanishing period）展开的结构，从而统一 conifold gap 现象、自由能与图展开之间的关系。

Method: 方法上，作者对允许对数谱坐标的普通拓扑递归证明因子化定理，将闭合亏格配分函数分解为归一化配分函数、普适高斯真空与连通图求和指数，并将高斯颈张量（Gaussian neck tensors）用参数化 $\mathbb P^1$ 的相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量（relative Gromov–Witten invariants）表示；示例中借助 remodeling 得到局部 $\mathbb P^2$、局部 $\mathbb F_0$ 与局部 $\mathbb F_1$ 的 Gromov–Witten 因子化。

Result: 主要结果是：该图求和在消失周期中具有严格正阶，因而因子化可恢复 conifold gap，将消失周期中度数为零的项等同于归一化的自由能（free energy），并给出周期正幂次系数的有限图公式；在固定亏格下，规则级数在周期与结点轨迹（nodal locus）上的参数中联合收敛。示例得到局部 $\mathbb P^2$ 与局部 $\mathbb F_0$ 在 conifold frame 下的 Gromov–Witten 因子化，其背景分别为 $\mathbb C^3$ 与 resolved conifold；局部 $\mathbb F_1$ 的类似因子化背景为 $\mathbb P^1$ 上的 $\mathcal O(1)\oplus\mathcal O(-3)$。

Conclusion: 结论是：在谱曲线规则结点退化附近，普通拓扑递归的闭合亏格配分函数确实具有上述因子化结构，且该结构可恢复 conifold gap 并由相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量给出显式图公式，同时可应用于局部 $\mathbb P^2$、局部 $\mathbb F_0$ 与局部 $\mathbb F_1$ 的 Gromov–Witten 因子化。

Abstract: We prove a factorization theorem for ordinary topological recursion near a regular nodal degeneration of a spectral curve, allowing logarithmic spectral coordinates. The partition function for closed genera $g\ge2$ factors into the partition function of the normalization, a universal Gaussian vacuum, and the exponential of a connected graph sum. This graph sum has strictly positive order in the vanishing period, and thus the factorization recovers the conifold gap, identifies the term of degree zero in the vanishing period with the free energy of the normalization, and gives finite graph formulas for the coefficients of positive powers of the period. At fixed genus, the regular series converges jointly in the period and the parameters along the nodal locus. The Gaussian neck tensors can be expressed in terms of relative Gromov--Witten invariants of a parametrized $\mathbb P^1$. As examples, we use remodeling to obtain Gromov--Witten factorizations for local $\mathbb P^2$ and local $\mathbb F_0$ in the conifold frame, with backgrounds $\mathbb C^3$ and the resolved conifold, respectively. The analogous local $\mathbb F_1$ factorization has background $\mathcal O(1)\oplus\mathcal O(-3)$ over $\mathbb P^1$.

</details>
