### [191] [On the spectra of strong turbulence on water surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00777)
*Gregory Falkovich*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文以综述兼论证的形式梳理了水面重力波（gravity waves）直接级联谱理论的历史及其曲折，并给出若干可检验的谱预测。核心论断为：弱湍流（weak turbulence）向强湍流（strong turbulence）的转变由谱非局域相互作用（spectrally nonlocal interactions）驱动，发生尺度早于量纲估计；强湍流谱量级非普适，依赖于泵浦条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水面重力波直接级联的谱形式长期存在分歧，尤其是破碎波（wave breaking）主导的高频段。Phillips 关于波数谱与频率谱的经典方案被认为在量级与形状上均存在问题，而 Kuznetsov 给出的频率谱斜率形式更为合理，因此需要重新评估各谱段的幅度、斜率及其衔接方式。

Method: 采用理论分析与历史梳理相结合的方式：以 Zakharov 学派建立的弱湍流理论描述长波；在能量级联向短尺度推进、平均表面陡度（mean surface steepness）增大并出现破碎的条件下，对表面尖点（surface cusps）情形下的波数谱与频率谱进行量级估计与形式判定，并据此讨论弱—强湍流转变的尺度与非局域性来源。

Result: 论证Phillips 的频率谱整体形式不正确，其波数谱幅度亦有误；破碎波所致频率谱斜率的正确形式应归于 Kuznetsov。本文进一步估计了相应幅度，并主张频率谱在整个级联过程中保持不变。波数与时空谱中弱湍流向强湍流的转变因谱非局域相互作用而提前（在更大尺度）发生，导致强湍流谱幅度不具普适性，在弱湍流级联足够长时取决于泵浦。

Conclusion: 非局域性的可能成因在于：较短波的破碎由其所在波峰上最长波的量级所决定。由此，破碎波段的频率谱形状可在整个直接级联中保持稳定，而强湍流谱的量级则依赖外部泵浦条件，而非由量纲分析给出的普适常数决定。

Abstract: Here, I give a brief history of the theory, with twists and turns, and list possible predictions for the spectra of the direct cascade of gravity waves on the water. The description of long waves is straightforward within the weak-turbulence theory developed by Zakharov and his school. As the energy cascade proceeds to shorter scales, the mean surface steepness grows, and wave-breaking events appear with increasing frequency. Considering surface cusps, Phillips suggested a wavenumber spectrum (with a wrong magnitude, as I argue here) and a frequency spectrum whose whole form is incorrect. The right form for the frequency spectral slope due to breaking waves was suggested by Kuznetsov. Here we estimate the magnitude and argue that the frequency spectrum is expected to continue unchanged through the whole cascade. The transition from weak to strong turbulence in the wavenumber and spatio-temporal spectra is due to spectrally nonlocal interactions and happens earlier (at larger scales) than the dimensional estimate suggests. As a result, the magnitude of the strong-turbulence spectrum is not universal but depends on the pumping for a sufficiently long weak-turbulence cascade. The reason for nonlocality is likely that breaking of shorter waves is determined by the magnitude of the longest wave on whose crest they are running.

</details>


### [192] [Direct Modeling of Pore Size Evolution and Microcollapse in Lyophilization by Population Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01851)
*Isaac Stonewall Wheeler,Vivek Narsimhan,Alina A. Alexeenko,Davide Fissore*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究提出群体平衡模型（population balance model）描述冻干饼孔隙尺寸分布以刻画微塌陷（microcollapse），并通过矩方法（method of moments）与插值闭合（interpolative closure）控制计算成本，实现对一次干燥（primary drying）过程中传质阻力变化的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一次干燥（primary drying）步骤中，无定形赋形剂（amorphous excipients）接近玻璃化转变温度（glass transition temperature）时易发生微塌陷（microcollapse）。既有建模方法要么回避该区间，要么依赖实验负担沉重的经验评估以确定传质阻力的下降。

Method: 建立冻干饼孔隙尺寸分布（pore size distribution）的群体平衡模型（population balance model），将孔隙尺寸与传质阻力（mass transfer resistance）相关联；采用矩方法（method of moments）配合插值闭合（interpolative closure）处理群体平衡，使计算负担与文献标准一次干燥模型相当。

Result: 在传质阻力随产物温度明显变化的文献案例中，模型对高、低产物温度情形拟合后，能以同等精度模拟中间情形；在无微塌陷配方的事后孔隙尺寸分布测量数据中，仅需拟合单一参数即可达到与事后经验评估相当的产物温度预测精度；对某一配方跨条件进行多实验拟合，产物温度呈定量一致。

Conclusion: 微塌陷的速率常数（rate constant）可能是一种材料参数（material parameter），与具体的多孔几何无关。所提出的模型为微塌陷区间的一次干燥提供了兼顾精度与计算效率的预测手段。

Abstract: In the primary drying step of pharmaceutical freeze drying, it is well known that amorphous excipients are prone to "microcollapse" as they approach their glass transition temperature. Existing modeling approaches for this phenomenon generally entail either avoidance of this regime or an experimentally burdensome empirical assessment of the resulting drop in resistance to mass transfer. In this work, we approach the phenomenon head-on by developing a population balance model for the pore size distribution of a freeze-dried cake during microcollapse and relating the pore size to mass transfer resistance. By treating the population balance with the method of moments and an interpolative closure, we keep the model's computational burden on par with the literature-standard model for primary drying.
  For a literature example when mass transfer resistance clearly varies as a function of product temperature during drying, we fit the population balance model to cases with high and low product temperature and show that it can then simulate intermediate cases with equal accuracy. The validity of the expression for mass transfer resistance is demonstrated with another literature data set, where pore size distributions were measured after the fact for a formulation without microcollapse; only a single parameter from this model needs to be fit to those cases to yield predictive accuracy in product temperature on par with post-hoc empirical assessment. Finally, a sophisticated multi-experiment fit for one formulation across a range of conditions yields quantitative agreement in product temperatures, suggesting that the rate constant for microcollapse behavior may be a material parameter and independent of the precise porous geometry.

</details>


### [193] [Rapid generation of relaxed centimeter-sized drops by capillary auto-ejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01857)
*Patric Mueller,Kai Ihrig,Achim Sack,Jonathan E. Kollmer,Thorsten Poeschel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究探讨利用毛细自喷（capillary auto-ejection，又称 puddle jumping）作为一种被动机制，在短时微重力条件下生成自由漂浮的厘米级液滴，并通过调控支撑腔形状来最小化喷射后的弛豫时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力实验常需厘米级球形液滴，而落塔（drop-tower）实验仅能提供数秒微重力，液滴须在极短时间内生成并接近弛豫状态，因此需要可快速产生无支撑自由液滴的被动机制。

Method: 针对填充固定液体积的抛物线截面（parabolic cross-section）腔体，通过最小化初始液面面积求得唯一形状假设；采用光滑粒子流体动力学（smoothed particle hydrodynamics, SPH）模拟评估其弛豫时间，并考察在腔体中引入浅中心凹陷的影响。

Result: 在抛物线族腔体中，最小化初始液面面积得到的形状同时给出最短弛豫时间；引入浅中心凹陷可进一步缩短弛豫时间，尽管其初始液面面积更大。

Conclusion: 研究结果为毛细驱动液滴发生器（capillary-driven drop generator）提供设计准则，使其能在落塔可用的短微重力时段内快速生成已弛豫的厘米级液滴。

Abstract: Many microgravity experiments require centimeter-sized spherical liquid drops. In drop-tower experiments, only a few seconds of microgravity are available, so the drops must be generated and reach a nearly relaxed state within a very short time. We investigate capillary auto-ejection (puddle jumping) as a passive mechanism for producing free-floating drops and investigate how the supporting cavity can be shaped to minimize the relaxation time after ejection.
  For cavities with a parabolic cross-section filled with a fixed liquid volume, minimization of the initial liquid surface area yields a unique shape, and smoothed particle hydrodynamics simulations show that this shape also gives the shortest relaxation time within the parabolic family. Introducing a shallow central depression into the cavity reduces the relaxation time further, despite producing a larger initial liquid surface area. These results provide design principles for capillary-driven drop generators that enable the rapid production of relaxed centimeter-sized drops during the short microgravity intervals available in drop towers.

</details>


### [194] [Helical pipes homogenize particle transport in pneumatic conveying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01920)
*Wing To Ku,Patric Mueller,Thorsten Poeschel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过CFD-DEM模拟研究螺旋管（helical pipe）作为被动手段，在中等气速下实现颗粒输送均质化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中等气速下的气力输送（pneumatic conveying）易出现强非均质颗粒输运，慢颗粒在管底累积、快颗粒在上方输送，需在不提高气速的前提下改善均质性。

Method: 采用计算流体力学-离散元耦合（CFD-DEM）模拟，对比直管与螺旋管中的气固两相流动，分析螺旋结构诱导的气固旋流（swirling motion）对颗粒横向再分布的作用。

Result: 螺旋管诱导气体与颗粒的旋流，使颗粒在管道横截面上持续再分布；直管中的非均质输运转变为更均质状态，颗粒速度分布变窄。螺旋管可在中等气速下保持高效输送，同时降低过高的颗粒速度。

Conclusion: 螺旋管可作为一种无需提高气速的被动均质化方案，在中等气速下兼顾输送效率并抑制颗粒超速。

Abstract: Pneumatic conveying at moderate gas velocities can result in strongly heterogeneous particle transport, with slow particles accumulating near the bottom of the pipe while faster particles are transported above. Here, we investigate helical pipes as a passive means of homogenizing particle transport without increasing the gas velocity. CFD-DEM simulations show that the helical pipe induces swirling motion of the gas and particles, continuously redistributing particles across the pipe cross-section. This transforms the heterogeneous transport observed in a straight pipe into a more homogeneous state with a narrower particle-velocity distribution. The results demonstrate that helical pipes can maintain efficient pneumatic conveying at moderate gas velocities while reducing excessive particle velocities.

</details>


### [195] [Spiral motion enables simultaneous homogenization and enhanced transport in granular pipe flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02105)
*Wing To Ku,Patric Mueller,Thorsten Poeschel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究利用离散元模拟（DEM）揭示，螺旋壁面纹理（helical wall texture）通过诱导颗粒的螺旋运动（spiral motion）实现流动均匀化，并据此提出中心线呈螺旋路径的螺旋管（helical pipe）设计，在适宜参数下可同时提升流动均匀性与平均流速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重力驱动颗粒管流（gravity-driven granular pipe flow）中的密度波（density waves）会降低输运效率并造成局部固相分数（solid fraction）剧烈波动。传统螺旋壁面纹理虽能抑制此类不稳定性，但以牺牲吞吐量为代价，因此需寻找兼顾均匀化与输运性能的方案。

Method: 采用离散元模拟（discrete element simulations, DEM）研究螺旋壁面纹理下的颗粒运动，并以平均角速度（mean angular velocity）量化螺旋运动，分析其与流动均匀化及输运性能的相关性；在此基础上设计中心线沿螺旋路径、横截面保持恒定圆形的新型螺旋管。

Result: 螺旋运动与流动均匀化、输运性能之间存在强相关性。所提出的螺旋管设计可产生比传统螺旋壁面纹理更强的螺旋运动，且在合适设计参数下同时增强流动均匀性，并使平均流速超过普通直管（plain straight pipe）。

Conclusion: 在所考察的参数范围内，螺旋运动可作为同时改善流动均匀性与输运效率的有效诊断量与设计变量。

Abstract: Density waves in gravity-driven granular pipe flow reduce transport efficiency and produce large fluctuations in the local solid fraction. Helical wall textures suppress these instabilities but at the expense of reduced throughput. Using discrete element simulations (DEM), \RefA{we find that this homogenization is associated with} spiral particle motion induced by the helical wall texture. Quantifying this motion by the mean angular velocity reveals a strong correlation between spiral motion, flow homogenization, and transport performance. Motivated by this insight, we introduce a helical pipe whose centerline follows a helical path while maintaining a constant circular cross section. This design generates stronger spiral motion than conventional helical wall textures. For suitable design parameters, the helical pipe simultaneously enhances flow homogeneity and increases the mean flow velocity beyond that of a plain straight pipe. These results support spiral motion as a useful diagnostic and design variable for simultaneously improving flow homogeneity and transport efficiency within the investigated parameter range.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Symmetric Thin-Shell Wormholes from a Regularized String-Cloud Black Hole: Energy Conditions, Multi-Fluid Stability, and Optical Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00131)
*Zijun Zhong,Jonathan A. Reboucas,Edson Otoniel,Minyong Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文由正则化 Letelier–Alencar 弦云黑洞（string-cloud black hole）正时延外部构造对称薄壳虫洞（thin-shell wormhole），用 Darmois–Israel 形式推导表面应力与稳定性判据，并分析能量条件、五类表面流体模型稳定性及后向光线追踪成像特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以具有有限曲率反德西特核（anti–de Sitter core）与弦云渐近的黑洞为种子，构建可稳定化检验的薄壳虫洞模型，并借助正则化与未正则化对照，辨明正则子（rational regulator）对能量条件结构与稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用 Darmois–Israel 形式（Darmois–Israel formalism）推导表面应力、能量条件组合、守恒律与有效势；给出依赖密度与半径的状态方程局部稳定性判据，并应用于五种表面流体模型（每个静态喉标定一个参数）；另以未正则化精确对照及后向光线追踪（backward ray tracing）开展成像分析。

Result: 所有可达静态喉均具有负表面能量密度，弱与主能量条件（weak/dominant energy conditions）被违反；切向零条件及内在强条件仅在内侧采样外部成立。径向真空关系消除外部动量流。正压壳（barotropic shells）始终不稳定，其余闭合模型可出现依赖参数的稳定分支。未正则化对照显示前三种闭合的能量条件结构与径向稳定性符号保持不变，而宇宙 Chaplygin 稳定性符号可改变。光线追踪表明喉位置主要重组高阶与穿喉像，弦云强度与倾角控制环状结构与多普勒不对称性。

Conclusion: 该正则化弦云黑洞薄壳虫洞在能量条件上普遍违背弱与主条件，稳定性取决于表面流体闭合形式与参数标定；正则子主要影响特定闭合（如宇宙 Chaplygin）的稳定性符号，而几何与成像特征表明中心暗区依赖源分布，单/双侧源对比可分离真实远侧像。

Abstract: We construct a symmetric thin-shell wormhole from two copies of the positive-lapse exterior of a rationally regularized Letelier--Alencar string-cloud black hole with a finite-curvature anti--de Sitter core and string-cloud asymptotics. Using the Darmois--Israel formalism, we derive the surface stresses, energy-condition combinations, conservation law, and effective potential. Every admissible static throat has negative surface energy density, so the weak and dominant energy conditions are violated, whereas the null condition restricted to directions tangent to the shell, and consequently the intrinsic strong condition, holds only in the inner sampled exterior. The radial-vacuum relation of the bulk source removes the external momentum flux. We derive a general local-stability criterion for density- and radius-dependent equations of state and apply it to five surface-fluid models, calibrating one parameter at each static throat. Across the sampled families, barotropic shells remain unstable, whereas the other closures can admit parameter-dependent stable branches. An exact unregularized control shows that the energy-condition structure and the radial-stability signs of the first three closures persist without the rational regulator, whereas the cosmic-Chaplygin stability sign can change while the selected calibration branch remains real and simple. Backward ray tracing shows that throat location primarily reorganizes higher-order and through-throat images, while string-cloud strength and inclination control annular structure and Doppler asymmetry. Comparisons of one- and two-sided source configurations isolate genuine far-side images and show that the central dark region depends on the source distribution.

</details>


### [197] [Normalizing Flow Emulator for Gravitational Wave Detection Probability in the LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA O4 Observing Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00306)
*Julius Gassert,Ignacio Magaña Hernandez,Ariel J. Amsellem,Antonella Palmese,Barnabas Póczos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作提出使用归一化流（Normalizing Flows, NFs）构建机器学习模拟器，用于在 LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA O4 类探测器网络下快速估计引力波事件的探测概率，并以公开注入数据集进行训练与测试，性能可比甚至优于蒙特卡洛重要采样（Monte Carlo importance sampling），且模型与代码公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，给定源参数估计引力波事件探测概率需要代价高昂的蒙特卡洛注入（Monte Carlo injection）campaign。因此需要一个高效且准确的替代方案。

Method: 采用基于归一化流（Normalizing Flows, NFs）的机器学习方法，针对 LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA O4 类探测器网络配置，使用公开注入数据集作为训练与测试数据。通过评估 NF 作为生成模型恢复被探测事件正确参数分布的能力来检验原始性能，并将 NF 模拟器预测的探测效率与蒙特卡洛重要采样结果进行对比。

Result: NF 模拟器预测的探测效率与蒙特卡洛重要采样结果吻合良好；对于包括具有尖锐特征（sharp features）在内的多种族群模型，其探测效率估计可比蒙特卡洛重要采样更精确。

Conclusion: NF 模拟器可像此前的神经网络方法一样用于引力波源族群分析（population analysis）；本工作的训练代码与训练好的模型已公开。

Abstract: Efficient and accurate estimation of the detection probability of a gravitational wave event given its source parameters remains a challenging problem because it conventionally requires expensive Monte Carlo injection campaigns. We present a machine learning-based approach using Normalizing Flows (NFs) to estimate the detection probability of gravitational wave events for a LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA O4-like detector network configuration. We utilize the publicly available injection dataset as training and test data. We test our method and implementation by evaluating the raw NF performance as a generative model based on its ability to recover the correct parameter distributions of detected events. In addition, we compare the detection efficiencies predicted by the NF emulator with those from Monte Carlo importance sampling and find good agreement. Furthermore, estimating the detection efficiency for a wide variety of population models, including those with sharp features, can be more precise than estimates obtained via Monte Carlo importance sampling. We show that the NF emulator can be used in population analysis of gravitational wave sources, similar to previous neural network approaches. The training code and trained models from this work are publicly available.

</details>


### [198] [Quasinormal Modes of Reissner-Nordström Black Holes in Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00393)
*Xia-Tong Tan,Yu Zhang,Zhi-Hong Nie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在标量-张量-矢量引力（STVG）框架下，本文计算了 Reissner-Nordström 修正引力（MOG）黑洞在无质量标量、电磁与引力扰动下的准正规模（quasinormal modes）及时域演化，揭示了 MOG 参数 α 与电荷 Q 对衰减行为的抑制效应，并为黑洞光谱学检验修正引力提供理论参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究动机在于检验修正引力理论：MOG 黑洞的准正规模与环降（ringdown）信号是黑洞光谱学（black hole spectroscopy）的重要观测量，可用于区分修正引力与广义相对论预言。摘要未进一步展开更具体的动机陈述。

Method: 准正规模频率通过将三阶至六阶 WKB 方法扩展并结合至 13 阶的 Padé 近似（Padé approximation）计算；完整时域波形则采用基于光锥坐标（light-cone coordinates）的有限差分法获得。三类扰动分别对应无质量标量场、电磁场与引力场。

Result: 增大 MOG 参数 α 会提升黑洞有效质量，使阻尼率 |ω_I| 下降、环降衰减变慢；电荷 Q 增大产生类似效应，并在近极端（near-extremal）极限下阻尼趋近于零。三类扰动的环降衰减时长排序为：引力场最长，电磁场居中，标量场最短。时域波形中的振荡与衰减同频域结果定性一致，晚期尾（late-time tail）的幂律指数与渐近平坦时空一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果为利用黑洞光谱学检验修正引力理论提供了系统性的理论参考。摘要未给出超出上述内容的进一步结论。

Abstract: In the framework of Scalar-Tensor-Vector Gravity (STVG), this paper investigates the quasinormal modes and time-domain evolution of Reissner-Nordström Modified Gravity (MOG) black holes under massless scalar, electromagnetic, and gravitational perturbations. Quasinormal frequencies are calculated by extending the 3rd- to 6th-order WKB method and combining it with Padé approximation to the 13th order, while complete time-domain waveforms are obtained using a finite-difference method based on light-cone coordinates. The results indicate that increasing the MOG parameter $α$ raises the effective mass of the black holes, leading to a decrease in the damping rate $|ω_I|$ and a slower ringdown decay. An increase in charge $Q$ produces a similar effect, with the damping approaching zero in the near-extremal limit. Regarding the ringdown decay times for the three types of perturbations, the gravitational field has the longest duration, the electromagnetic field is intermediate, and the scalar field is the shortest. The oscillations and decay observed in the time-domain waveforms are qualitatively consistent with the frequency-domain results, and the power-law exponent governing the late-time tail aligns with that of asymptotically flat spacetimes. This study provides a systematic theoretical reference for testing modified gravity theories using black hole spectroscopy.

</details>


### [199] [Fermionic Particle Creation in Curved Spacetime: Physical Bloch-Vector Dynamics and Exact de Sitter Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00410)
*Samak Boonpan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文将含时弯曲时空（time-dependent curved spacetime）中的费米子粒子产生表述为物理布洛赫矢量（Bloch vector）的精确演化，把每个狄拉克动量模（Dirac momentum mode）约化为二能级系统，并以此在紧凑实几何中完整刻画有限时间的态轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是为费米子粒子产生提供一个能够完整保留有限时间态演化轨迹的紧凑几何描述，同时在不把费米子占据数上限（fermionic occupation bound）的微观来源单纯归因于几何的前提下，使该上限自然显现。

Method: 将每个狄拉克动量模约化为二能级系统，通过把物理态投影到瞬时能量基（instantaneous energy basis）获得瞬时占据数；该构造与标准博戈柳博夫描述（Bogoliubov description）等价。针对德西特时空（de Sitter spacetime）中的大质量狄拉克场（massive Dirac field），动力学化为以哈勃单位（Hubble units）表示的物理动量与质量—哈勃比（mass-to-Hubble ratio）的普适函数。

Result: 识别出由质量控制的非绝热标度（mass-controlled nonadiabatic scale），以及奇异的极小质量边界层（singular small-mass boundary layer），后者解决了无质量极限与晚期极限不对易的问题。独立的笛卡尔布洛赫、角布洛赫与博戈柳博夫积分（Cartesian Bloch, angular Bloch, Bogoliubov integrations）验证了解析结果，并重现了标准的吉本斯—霍金费米—狄拉克平台（Gibbons–Hawking Fermi–Dirac plateau）。

Conclusion: 以物理布洛赫矢量刻画费米子粒子产生，能够在与标准博戈柳博夫描述等价的同时给出紧凑的实几何与完整有限时间轨迹，并为德西特时空中的质量依赖非绝热行为及极小质量边界层提供解析与数值一致的描述。

Abstract: We formulate fermionic particle creation in a time-dependent curved spacetime as the exact evolution of a physical Bloch vector. Each Dirac momentum mode reduces to a two-level system, and its instantaneous occupation is obtained by projecting the physical state onto the instantaneous energy basis. This construction is equivalent to the standard Bogoliubov description while retaining the complete finite-time state trajectory in a compact real geometry. It also makes the fermionic occupation bound manifest without attributing its microscopic origin to geometry alone. For a massive Dirac field in de Sitter spacetime, the dynamics reduces to a universal function of the physical momentum in Hubble units and the mass-to-Hubble ratio. We identify a mass-controlled nonadiabatic scale and a singular small-mass boundary layer that resolves the noncommuting massless and late-time limits. Independent Cartesian Bloch, angular Bloch, and Bogoliubov integrations verify the analytical results and recover the standard Gibbons--Hawking Fermi--Dirac plateau.

</details>


### [200] [Black hole sensitivity in Einstein--aether theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00503)
*Morgan Kidnew,Andrew Kovachik,Sergey Sibiryakov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-以太理论（Einstein–aether theory）中数值求解了缓慢运动的史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）解，并据此计算描述黑洞与以太场耦合的敏感度参数（sensitivity parameter），同时给出该参数对理论参数的简单解析近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在点粒子有效理论（point-particle effective theory）框架下，黑洞与以太场的耦合由敏感度参数刻画，因此需要获得缓慢运动黑洞的具体解以确定该参数。摘要未进一步说明更广泛的研究背景。

Method: 采用数值方法求解爱因斯坦-以太理论中缓慢运动的史瓦西黑洞解，并基于这些解在点粒子有效理论框架内计算敏感度参数，进而推导该参数的解析近似表达式。

Result: 获得了缓慢运动史瓦西黑洞的数值解，并计算出相应的敏感度参数；同时得到了敏感度对爱因斯坦-以太理论参数依赖关系的简单解析近似。

Conclusion: 结果表明可通过数值解结合解析近似来刻画爱因斯坦-以太理论中黑洞与以太场的耦合强度，为点粒子有效理论中的相关计算提供参数输入。摘要未提及其他结论。

Abstract: We numerically find the solutions for slowly moving Schwarzschild black holes in Einstein--aether theory. We use these solutions to compute the sensitivity parameter which describes the coupling of the black hole to the aether field within point-particle effective theory. A simple analytic approximation for the dependence of the sensitivity on the Einstein--aether parameters is derived.

</details>


### [201] [Dynamics of Regge calculus with torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00593)
*Ruijue Yan,You Ding,Yongge Ma,Cong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 n 维单纯形流形（simplicial manifold）上构造离散几何，将挠率（torsion）纳入 Regge 演算（Regge calculus），并建立可视为 Einstein--Cartan 理论离散类比的单纯形 Einstein--Cartan 作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为把挠率引入 Regge 演算，需要在离散框架中同时编码边长、方向与相邻单纯形界面上的联络信息，从而推广无挠的离散引力描述。

Method: 在每个单纯形中为边赋予边向量以记录长度与方向信息，并用内禀规范群元素（internal gauge group elements）表示跨相邻单纯形界面曲线的和乐（holonomy）；挠率表现为同一条边在一个单纯形中的边向量与经和乐平行移动后属于相邻单纯形的边向量之差。据此在三维与四维、欧几里得与洛伦兹号差（Euclidean and Lorentzian signatures）下构造单纯形 Einstein--Cartan 作用量。

Result: 所构造作用量在无挠情形下回到相应的 Regge 作用量；对四维离散作用量分别关于边向量与和乐变分得到两个运动方程，前者与连续理论的对应方程一致，无挠和乐满足后者，与连续情形相同。

Conclusion: 所得单纯形流形上的理论可视为 Einstein--Cartan 理论的离散类比（discrete analogue）。

Abstract: The discrete geometry on an $n$-dimensional simplicial manifold are studied, in order to incorporate torsion into Regge calculus. In each simplex, the edge vectors are assigned to the edges to encode the information of their lengths and directions. In addition, the holonomies along the curves across the interfaces of two adjacent simplices are represented by the internal gauge group elements. The torsion manifests itself as the difference between an edge vector on an interface belonging to one simplex and the parallel transported edge vector, via the holonomy, of the same edge but belonging to the adjacent simplex. The simplicial Einstein--Cartan actions are then constructed as functions of the edge vectors and holonomies in three and four dimensions with Euclidean and Lorentzian signatures, respectively. It is shown that they return to the corresponding Regge actions for the torsion-free cases. The variations of the 4-dimensional discrete action with respect to the edge vectors and holonomies, respectively, give two equations of motion. It is shown that the former is consistent with the corresponding equation in the continuum theory, and the torsion-free holonomies satisfy the latter equation as in the continuous case. Thus, the resulting theory on the simplicial manifold can be regarded as a discrete analogue of Einstein--Cartan theory.

</details>


### [202] [Naked Singularities and Black Hole Thermodynamics from Shadows and Photon Rings in the Quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00640)
*Jose Miguel Ladino,Anuar Idrissov,Carlos E. Romero-Figueroa,Hernando Quevedo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究量子奥本海默-斯奈德（quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder, qOS）时空在黑洞与裸奇点（naked singularity）两种情形下的光学与热力学性质，通过试验粒子运动分析与薄吸积盘（thin accretion disk）数值光线追踪给出可观测判据，并借助几何热力学（Geometrothermodynamics, GTD）建立光子环（photon ring）观测量与热力学稳定性之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要区分量子修正时空下黑洞与裸奇点的观测特征，并寻找可用于实际观测检验的具体判据；同时探讨此类时空光学性质与热力学性质之间的内在关联。

Method: 分析试验粒子运动并对薄吸积盘模型进行数值光线追踪；采用半解析方法（semi-analytical approach）计算高阶光子环碰撞参数（impact parameters）与间隙参数（gap parameters）；在黑洞情形下应用几何热力学（GTD）识别Davies点处的比热发散（specific-heat divergence）。

Result: 量子修正参数增大会减小视阴影（apparent shadow）尺寸；qOS黑洞呈现暗的内阴影，而裸奇点因无限排斥核心势垒（infinite repulsive core barrier）产生中心亮环，但仍保留光子环等关键光学特征。半解析得到的高阶光子环参数与数值结果高度一致；qOS裸奇点的间隙参数区间偏离史瓦西（Schwarzschild）情形，可作为观测检验判据。

Conclusion: 通过将光子环观测量与GTD曲率标量（curvature scalars）相联系，揭示qOS黑洞阴影、热力学稳定性与微观相互作用结构之间存在直接联系，qOS裸奇点的间隙参数偏离史瓦西情形提供了具体的观测区分标准。

Abstract: We investigate the optical and thermodynamic properties of quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder (qOS) spacetimes in both the black hole and naked singularity regimes. By analyzing test-particle motion and employing numerical ray tracing of thin accretion disk models, we identify their characteristic optical signatures. We find that increasing the quantum correction parameter reduces the apparent shadow size. While qOS black holes exhibit dark inner shadows, naked singularities produce central bright rings, driven by an infinite repulsive core barrier, yet still retain key optical features such as photon rings. Furthermore, using a semi-analytical approach, we obtain higher-order photon-ring impact and gap parameters that closely align with our numerical findings. Notably, the gap parameter intervals for qOS naked singularities deviate from the Schwarzschild case, providing a concrete criterion for observational tests. Finally, in the black-hole sector, the application of Geometrothermodynamics (GTD) identifies the specific-heat divergence at the Davies point. By linking photon-ring observables to GTD curvature scalars, we reveal a direct connection between qOS black hole shadows, thermodynamic stability, and microscopic interaction structures.

</details>


### [203] [Reconstruction of $f(Q)$ Gravity from Geodesic Congruence Dynamics: Raychaudhuri Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01254)
*Tuhina Ghorui,Prabir Rudra,Farook Rahaman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文以 Raychaudhuri 方程为核心几何工具，在对称远平行引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）框架下重构 f(Q) 引力的函数形式，从而无需额外暗能量成分即可解释宇宙加速膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在利用 Raychaudhuri 方程刻画宇宙测地线汇（geodesic congruence）的聚焦/散焦机制，建立非度规标量 Q 与宇宙运动学演化之间的关联，以重构可解释加速膨胀的 f(Q) 引力模型。

Method: 在 FLRW 时空中引入常减速参数（deceleration parameter）与常急动参数（jerk parameter），通过 Raychaudhuri 方程将膨胀标量（expansion scalar）与汇动力学同非度规标量 Q 联系起来，实现 f(Q) 引力的逆重构（inverse reconstruction）。

Result: 获得了 f(Q) 的显式形式，可在无额外暗能量成分条件下解释观测到的宇宙加速膨胀；所重构模型在特定情境下可外推至无限过去以描述早期宇宙。

Conclusion: 进一步讨论了广义相对论极限（general relativity limit）、Friedmann 一致性（Friedmann consistency）及背景物理可行性。分析表明，作为支配宇宙学测地线汇聚焦/散焦的方程，Raychaudhuri 方程可作为 f(Q) 引力逆重构的核心方程。

Abstract: In this study, we use the Raychaudhuri equation as the essential geometrical tool to reconstruct the functional form of $f(Q)$ gravity within the context of symmetric teleparallel gravity. The expansion scalar and the congruence dynamics recorded in the Raychaudhuri equation are used to relate the non-metricity scalar $Q$ with the kinematical evolution of the Universe in order to establish the reconstruction method. We obtain explicit forms of $f(Q)$ that can explain the observed accelerated expansion of the Universe without the need for an additional dark energy component by taking into account appropriate cosmological assumptions. Moreover, the reconstructed model can also be extended to the infinite past to model the early universe for certain scenarios. In an FLRW spacetime we have considered a constant deceleration parameter and a constant jerk parameter to facilitate our reconstruction analysis. We further discuss the, general relativity limit, Friedmann consistency and background physical viability of the reconstructed models. The analysis indicates that Raychaudhuri equation, interpreted as an equation governing the focusing/defocusing of cosmological geodesic congruences, can be used as the central equation in an inverse reconstruction of $f(Q)$ gravity.

</details>


### [204] [Charged Hawking Mass on CMC Collars with Charged Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01255)
*Sehong Park*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究带宇宙学常数、允许带电磁物质的三维时间对称带电初值数据中严格稳定极小视界。沿从视界出发的常平均曲率叶层，证明带电霍金质量的精确一阶变分恒等式，并在电荷相容条件下给出质量单调性与刚性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在带电且含宇宙学常数的情形下，推广霍金质量单调性与极小视界的局部刚性结果，并理解带电磁物质如何影响这些性质。

Method: 在三维时间对称带电初值数据中，沿从极小视界发出的常平均曲率（constant-mean-curvature）叶层，且叶层具有正雅可比算子（Jacobi operator），推导带电霍金质量（charged Hawking mass）的精确一阶变分恒等式。

Result: 当电荷密度符号与视界电荷相容时，霍金质量非减；固定电荷质量两端边界值相等时，整个领域等距于无源常曲率 Reissner--Nordström--(anti-)de Sitter 模型中的环带。极小端点处，进入恒等式的缺陷沿归一化 lapse 计算固定电荷霍金质量的海森（Hessian），其消失刻画模型视界数据。

Conclusion: 由此在局部极大性条件下恢复局部刚性结果；缺陷量的消失可作为模型视界数据的刻画。

Abstract: We study strictly stable minimal horizons in three-dimensional time-symmetric charged initial data sets with cosmological constant, allowing charged matter. Along the constant-mean-curvature foliation issuing from the horizon through leaves with positive Jacobi operator, we prove exact first-variation identities for the charged Hawking mass. When the charge density has one sign compatible with the horizon charge, Hawking masses are nondecreasing, and equality between the two boundary values of the fixed-charge mass forces the entire collar to be isometric to an annulus in a source-free constant-curvature Reissner--Nordström--(anti-)de Sitter model. From this we recover the local rigidity results under a local maximality condition. At the minimal endpoint the defect entering these identities computes the fixed-charge Hawking-mass Hessian along the normalized lapse, and its vanishing characterizes the model horizon data.

</details>


### [205] [Graceful Exit to Radiation Domination in the Starobinsky Model: A Dynamical Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01407)
*Bhargabi Saha,Malay K. Nandy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在Starobinsky模型框架内构建标量子（scalaron）衰变产生辐射的耦合动力学方程组，并保证膨胀背景下的精确能量守恒，数值演化显示慢滚暴胀约产生60个e折，随后实现优雅退出（graceful exit）与向辐射主导时期的平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Starobinsky模型是成功的暴胀实现方案及替代模型的基准，其观测成功促使作者在包含标量子衰变产生辐射的前提下，考察早期宇宙的动力学演化，以自洽地描述暴胀后的退出与再加热过程。

Method: 引入标量子衰变产生辐射的机制，建立描述标量子耗损与辐射累积的耦合动力学方程组，并在膨胀背景中保证精确能量守恒；Friedmann方程中同时计入标量子与辐射的引力贡献，从而涵盖所生成辐射的反作用（backreaction）。以无辐射宇宙为初始状态，并采用标准慢滚近似给出的初始条件进行精确数值演化。

Result: 数值演化表明暴胀动力学主要由标量子主导，尽管辐射持续产生但始终处于次要地位，慢滚条件（slow-roll conditions）在暴胀期间持续成立，标量子约产生60个e折；暴胀结束后标量子进入阻尼振荡阶段，辐射迅速增长，随后在再加热过程中随标量子能量耗损而持续增长，辐射能量密度最终显著超过残余标量子能量密度。

Conclusion: 该过渡实现了暴胀的优雅退出并平滑进入再加热阶段，辐射能量密度占优标志着热辐射主导阶段的开始，满足Kofman-Yi再加热成功判据。结果表明耦合的标量子-辐射动力学为Starobinsky模型中的优雅退出及向辐射主导的过渡提供了自洽实现。

Abstract: The Starobinsky model provides a successful realization of inflation and serves as a benchmark for alternative inflationary models. Motivated by its observational success, we investigate the dynamical evolution of the early Universe within this framework. Incorporating radiation production from scalaron decay, we formulate a coupled system of dynamical equations describing scalaron depletion and radiation buildup while ensuring exact energy conservation in the expanding background. In the Friedmann equation, both scalaron and radiation components gravitate, thereby accounting for the backreaction of the generated radiation.
  Starting with a radiation-free Universe and initial conditions from the standard slow-roll approximations, exact numerical evolution shows that inflationary dynamics is predominantly governed by the scalaron. Despite continuous radiation production, the radiation component remains subdominant, and the slow-roll conditions persist throughout inflation, yielding approximately 60 e-folds. Following inflation, the scalaron enters a damped oscillatory phase accompanied by rapid radiation growth. This transition provides a graceful exit from inflation and a smooth entry into reheating. During reheating, radiation grows continuously as the scalaron energy is depleted, with the radiation energy density ultimately becoming substantially larger than the residual scalaron energy density. This indicates the onset of a hot, radiation-dominated phase, satisfying the Kofman-Yi criterion for successful reheating. Our results demonstrate that the coupled scalaron-radiation dynamics provides a self-consistent realization of graceful exit and the transition to radiation domination in the Starobinsky model.

</details>


### [206] [Identifying Exceptional Points in Black Hole Ringdowns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01416)
*Zhen-Xiao Zhang,Chen Lan,Yi Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出在黑洞（black hole, BH）铃宕（ringdown）信号中识别例外点（exceptional points, EPs）的方法，基于时间加权的若尔当项（Jordan term）与劈裂界限（splitting bound）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铃宕光谱学（ringdown spectroscopy）可用于探测黑洞动力学，而例外点（EPs）有望揭示其背后的自由度。摘要未给出更具体的动机背景。

Method: 采用时间加权若尔当项与劈裂界限来识别铃宕中的例外点；在相对若尔当激发（Jordan excitation）正比于分离量（separation）的情形下，对共振中心（resonance center）进行拟合后，推导劈裂波形与例外点波形之间的六阶平方距离（sixth-order squared distance）。

Result: 推导出劈裂与例外点波形间的六阶平方距离；若对这些量施加独立约束，可将其恢复为四阶（fourth order）。完整波形（complete waveforms）表明，忽略普通响应（ordinary responses）会强化例外点证据，并虚假地收紧劈裂界限。

Conclusion: 在铃宕中判定例外点需谨慎处理普通响应，否则可能夸大例外点证据并导致劈裂界限被不当收紧。

Abstract: Ringdown spectroscopy probes black hole (BH) dynamics, and exceptional points (EPs) can reveal underlying degrees of freedom. We develop a procedure to identify EPs in ringdowns using a time-weighted Jordan term and a splitting bound. For relative Jordan excitation proportional to separation, we derive a sixth-order squared distance between split and EP waveforms after fitting the resonance center. Independent constraints on these quantities can restore fourth order. Complete waveforms show that omitting ordinary responses can strengthen EP evidence and spuriously tighten splitting bounds.

</details>


### [207] [Can structure formation distinguish between holographic dark energy models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01430)
*Qihong Huang,Bing Xu,Kaituo Zhang,Hao Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于 Pantheon+ SN Ia、OHD、DESI DR2 与 CMB 距离先验联合数据约束 HDE、THDE、BHDE 模型参数，并利用 MCMC 样本分析 fσ8(z) 与 S8(z) 演化；结果表明结构形成可区分 THDE 与 HDE/BHDE，并可作为区分 HDE 类模型的有效探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构形成（structure formation）此前被用于检验 THDE 模型中红外截断（IR cutoff）的可行性，发现未来事件视界（future event horizon）是可行的红外截断；本文受此启发，进一步考察结构形成对 HDE 类模型的区分能力。

Method: 使用 Pantheon+ SN Ia、OHD、DESI DR2 以及 CMB 距离先验（CMB distance prior）的联合数据集，对 HDE、THDE 与 BHDE 模型参数进行约束，并采用 MCMC 采样得到的参数集分析各模型中 fσ8(z) 与 S8(z) 的演化。

Result: HDE 与 BHDE 的 fσ8(z) 和 S8(z) 演化曲线几乎不可区分，THDE 模型曲线最低；χ² 统计显示 THDE 模型在 fσ8 与 S8 测量中均给出最低 χ² 值。在 (fσ8, S8) 平面上，THDE 模型轨迹能够进入当前 (fσ8, S8) 数据区域。

Conclusion: 结构形成能够将 THDE 与 HDE、BHDE 区分开来，表明其可作为区分 HDE 模型的有用探针，并可推广至其他 HDE 情景。

Abstract: Structure formation has been used to test the viability of the IR cutoff in the THDE model, and the future event horizon was found to be a viable IR cutoff (Phys. Rev. D {\bf 113}, 123531 (2026)). Motivated by this, in this paper, using the combined Pantheon+ SN Ia, OHD, DESI DR2, and CMB distance prior datasets, we constrain the model parameters for the HDE, THDE, and BHDE models, and use parameter sets from the MCMC samples to analyze the evolution of $fσ_{8}(z)$ and $S_{8}(z)$ for these models. We find that the evolutionary curves of both $fσ_{8}(z)$ and $S_{8}(z)$ for HDE and BHDE are nearly indistinguishable, while the curves for the THDE model lie lowest. The $χ^{2}$ statistic shows that the THDE model yields the lowest $χ^{2}$ value for both $fσ_{8}$ and $S_{8}$ measurements. Finally, we construct the ($fσ_{8}$,$S_{8}$) plane as a diagnostic to analyze the trajectories of these models, and find that the trajectory of the THDE model can achieve the current ($fσ_{8}$,$S_{8}$) data region. These results demonstrate that structure formation can distinguish THDE from HDE and BHDE, and suggest that structure formation can serve as a useful probe for distinguishing HDE models and can be extended to other HDE scenarios.

</details>


### [208] [Closed timelike curves in modified theories of gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01476)
*Agnik Sen Roy,Sashideep Gutti,Shailesh Kulkarni*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究三种修改引力（modified gravity）旋转时空中的闭合类时曲线（closed timelike curves, CTCs），通过赤道面上 g_φφ 的符号确定违反时序区域，并解析给出临界参数值，表明额外参数可调控甚至消除赤道 CTC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修改引力理论中，旋转时空常引入额外参数，其对因果结构与时序保护的影响尚需系统刻画，尤其是这些参数能否控制或消除闭合类时曲线（CTCs）区域。

Method: 选取三类旋转时空：潮汐带电膜世界黑洞（tidally charged braneworld black hole）、Rastall 引力中的 Kerr-Newman 黑洞、带电 Johannsen-Psaltis 时空；利用赤道面上度规分量 g_φφ 的符号判定 CTC 区域，并对各几何的额外参数解析求取临界值。

Result: 膜世界情形下，正 r 的 CTC 区域仅存在于潮汐荷为正时，且当 q≤0 时完全消失；Rastall 引力中 CTC 区域范围由 Rastall 耦合与周围物质共同决定，对各种物质类型均存在使 CTC 区域消失的耦合有限区间；带电 Johannsen-Psaltis 时空中形变参数依径向位置可增大或减小 g_φφ，并得到在给定径向范围内消除 CTC 区域的参数窗口。

Conclusion: 在三种修改引力旋转时空中，额外参数均可用于控制赤道闭合类时曲线的范围，甚至将其完全消除，说明这些参数可作为调控时序违反区域的自由度。

Abstract: We study closed timelike curves (CTCs) in three rotating spacetimes arising in modified gravity: the tidally charged braneworld black hole, the Kerr-Newman black hole in Rastall gravity, and the charged Johannsen-Psaltis spacetime. Using the sign of $g_{φφ}$ on the equatorial plane, we show that an additional parameter of each geometry controls the chronology-violating region, and we obtain critical parameter values analytically. In the braneworld case, the positive $r$ CTC region exists only for positive tidal charge and disappears completely for $q\leq 0$. In Rastall gravity, the extent of the CTC region depends jointly on the Rastall coupling and the surrounding matter. For each matter type we find a finite range of the coupling for which the CTC region is absent. In the charged Johannsen-Psaltis spacetime, the deformation parameter can either increase or decrease $g_{φφ}$ depending on the radial position, and we derive a window of its values for which the CTC region is removed from the radial domain considered. Thus, in all three examples, the additional parameters can be chosen to control the extent of equatorial CTCs, and even to eliminate them entirely.

</details>


### [209] [Where Chaos Pauses: Sliding-Window Frequency Ratios Reveal Transient Resonances in Relativistic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01604)
*Wenfu Cao,Ying Wang,Hongsheng Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出滑动窗口频率比（SWFR），仅用径向与极向转向事件恢复单个相对论轨道访问局部结构的顺序，并把候选区间切出重绘为独立庞加莱截面；在可积Kerr基准、磁化Kerr及Schwarzschild–Melvin时空中，计数与相空间几何一致表明全局混沌轨道正在临时驻留共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间庞加莱截面（Poincaré section）会把混沌轨道压缩成静态模糊像，丢掉轨道访问其局部结构的先后顺序；因此需要一种能从单条轨道中恢复该顺序、且不依赖频谱或参考中心的工具。

Method: 构造滑动窗口频率比（sliding-window frequency ratio, SWFR），仅由径向与极向转向事件（radial and polar turning events）构成；每个窗口跨固定数目的径向周期，只统计其中的极向事件数，因保留事件时刻，任一候选区间都可被切出并重绘为自身的庞加莱截面，无需频谱、参考中心或基函数。

Result: 可积Kerr基准恢复已知频率比，且对计数偏差一致有界的规则运动，计数误差界以O(W^{-1})衰减。磁化Kerr时空中单条带电轨道先接近3/5、后接近4/7，恰由这些区间展开为五重与七重截面结构；第二条轨道接近1/2并具两瓣。Schwarzschild–Melvin时空中光子以五重图案维持4/5。各情形截面恰覆盖SWFR所选区间。

Conclusion: 整数计数与相空间几何一致表明，全局混沌轨道会对共振进行临时访问；SWFR可在不借助频谱分析的前提下，从单条相对论轨道中识别并定位共振驻留。

Abstract: A long-time Poincaré section flattens a chaotic orbit into one static blur, erasing the order in which the orbit visits its own local structures. We introduce the sliding-window frequency ratio (SWFR), built from nothing but radial and polar turning events, to recover that order along individual relativistic orbits. Each window spans a fixed number of radial cycles and simply counts the polar events inside it; because event times are kept, any candidate interval can be sliced out and replotted as its own Poincaré section. No spectrum, no reference center, no basis functions. Integrable Kerr benchmarks recover the known frequency ratios, and for regular motion with uniformly bounded count deviations the counting-error bound falls off as $O(W^{-1})$. The same counts then pay off in chaos. In magnetized Kerr spacetime a single charged orbit dwells near $3/5$ and later near $4/7$, and exactly those intervals open into fivefold and sevenfold section structures; a second orbit dwells near $1/2$ with two lobes. In Schwarzschild--Melvin spacetime a photon holds $4/5$ across a fivefold pattern. In every case the section covers exactly the interval SWFR selected: integer counts and phase-space geometry agree that a globally chaotic orbit is paying a temporary visit to a resonance.

</details>


### [210] [Analogue black hole lightring and its resonances in an optical fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01706)
*R. Terrier,J. Fatome,B. Kibler,T. Torres*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在光学类比系统中实现了黑洞光环共振（black-hole lightring resonance）的完整观测，方法上通过将光子球（photon sphere）概念推广至非定常系统，揭示光环与时空焦散（spatiotemporal caustic）的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞光环共振此前缺乏直接的实验观测，且光子球的概念通常限于定常时空，难以应用于非定常的类比系统。该工作旨在建立黑洞光环、时空焦散与准简正模光谱学（quasinormal-mode spectroscopy）之间的实验联系。

Method: 理论方面，将光子球推广到非定常系统，证明光环对应时空焦散，并表明其复频率由该焦散的传播与发散行为决定。实验方面，在5公里长的色散位移光纤（dispersion-shifted fiber）中进行泵浦-探测（pump-probe）实验，以光学孤子（optical soliton）作为类比黑洞势，对两个不同的泵浦波长进行观测。

Result: 预测出特征谱平台，其边界由光环频率的实部设定，谱衰减由虚部决定。实验中在两个不同泵浦波长下均观测到这些特征，测得光谱与数值模拟及预期的光环模式 profile 一致，从而同时获取了共振的振荡与衰减特性。

Conclusion: 结果在色散类比系统中建立了黑洞光环、时空焦散与准简正模光谱学之间的直接实验联系，表明光环共振的复频率可通过光谱特征加以探测。

Abstract: We demonstrate the complete observation of a black-hole lightring resonance in an optical analogue. To do so, we generalize the concept of the photon sphere to non-stationnary systems by showing that the lightring is associated to a spatiotemporal caustic and that its complex frequency is determined by the propagation and divergence of this caustic. For an optical soliton acting as an analogue black-hole potential, we predict a characteristic spectral plateau whose boundaries are set by the real part of the lightring frequency, while its spectral decay is governed by the imaginary part. Using a pump-probe experiment in a 5-km-long dispersion-shifted fiber, we observe these signatures for two distinct pump wavelengths. The measured spectra agree with numerical simulations and with the predicted lightring-mode profile, providing direct access to both the oscillatory and decay properties of the resonance. Our results establish a direct experimental connection between black-hole lightrings, spacetime caustics, and quasinormal-mode spectroscopy in a dispersive analogue system.

</details>


### [211] [Locating critical solutions in numerical relativity using automatic differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01964)
*David Bambague,Miguel Bezares,Katy Clough,Steven Tobias*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作以标量临界坍缩（scalar critical collapse）为验证案例，展示自动微分（auto-differentiation）可高效定位数值相对论（numerical relativity）中的动态解，并大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值相对论中单次模拟计算代价高昂，且参数空间维度高、解对参数呈非线性敏感，导致定位感兴趣的结果十分困难。

Method: 将自动微分（auto-differentiation）应用于全非线性爱因斯坦方程（Einstein Equations）的初值问题求解，以标量临界坍缩这一经典问题作为原理验证。

Result: 在二维参数空间中用35次运行定位到一条临界解线；相比之下，二分法（bisection）定位10个临界点需270次运行。同时直接计算了不稳定模式（unstable mode），并确认其沿该临界解线具有普适性（universality）。

Conclusion: 该方法可直接推广至更高维参数空间，为强动态引力（strong dynamical gravity）区域中高效靶向更复杂解提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Numerical relativity is a term used to describe the solution of the full non-linear Einstein Equations as an initial value problem using numerical methods. Individual simulations are computationally expensive and in many applications the high complexity of the parameter space and non-linear sensitivity of the solutions makes locating particular outcomes of interest challenging. In this work, we demonstrate how auto-differentiation can efficiently find dynamical solutions of interest, using the classic problem of scalar critical collapse as a proof of principle. We locate a line of critical solutions in a 2D parameter space using 35 runs, compared to the 270 runs required to locate 10 critical points using bisection, and directly calculate the unstable mode, confirming its universality along the line. The method is straightforwardly extendable to higher-dimensional parameter spaces, offering a promising method to efficiently target more complex solutions in strong dynamical gravity regimes.

</details>


### [212] [Analytic Metric for Rotating Black Holes in Higher-Derivative Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02004)
*Jierui Hu,Dongjun Li,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作提出将度规重构（metric reconstruction）用作修改引力（modified gravity）中旋转黑洞的解析求解器，并在宇称偶立方引力（parity-even cubic gravity）中首次构造出耦合常数线性阶的解析旋转黑洞度规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接求解修改引力中耦合场方程以获取旋转黑洞解通常极为困难，且已有结果往往依赖自旋展开（spin expansion）或弱场展开（weak-field expansion）。因此需要一个能绕过直接求解、同时不引入此类近似限制的新途径。

Method: 方法核心是把度规重构（metric reconstruction）转化为解析求解框架，用以处理修改引力中的旋转黑洞，从而避免直接求解耦合场方程。具体在宇称偶立方引力（parity-even cubic gravity）中，于耦合常数的线性阶构造解析旋转黑洞度规，不施加自旋或弱场展开。

Result: 在宇称偶立方引力中获得了首个解析旋转黑洞度规（黑 hole 度规），阶数为耦合线性阶且无需自旋或弱场展开；并严格校验了场方程，同时复现了关于视界性质（horizon properties）的独立解析预言。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了在爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）之外构造旋转黑洞的新路线，并为强场检验（strong-field tests）奠定了基础。

Abstract: We turn metric reconstruction into an analytic solver for rotating black holes in modified gravity, bypassing the direct solution of coupled field equations. As an example, in parity-even cubic gravity, we construct the first analytic spinning black hole metric at linear order in the coupling, without spin or weak-field expansions. We stringently validate the field equations and reproduce independent analytic predictions for horizon properties. This establishes a new route to construct spinning black holes beyond Einstein gravity and lays the foundation for strong-field tests.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.SR'></div>

# astro-ph.SR [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Towards solar many-line inversions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01251)
*Johannes Hölken*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 该研究围绕太阳大气中小尺度磁流体动力学（magnetohydrodynamics, MHD）事件，先建立高分辨率偏振光谱观测的定标与图像重建理论基础，再研制FISS-SP原型仪器，并发展多谱线同时反演技术，实现对约49 km水平尺度结构的相干大气模型反演，最终用于分析太阳黑子孔（solar pore）边界对流特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳大气受水平尺度仅数十公里的小尺度事件显著影响，这些事件由磁流体动力学过程决定并起源于太阳大气最低层。为理解色球与日冕加热（chromospheric and coronal heating）、太阳发电机（solar dynamo）及其活动周，以及改进空间天气（space weather）事件预测，亟需提升对此类小尺度事件的认识。

Method: 工作分为三部分：一是对极高空间分辨率的偏振光谱观测进行定标，并构建MOMFBD图像重建算法数学证明的基础；二是描述FISS-SP原型仪器，其用于充分发挥1.6 m望远镜的分辨能力并为偏振光谱数据的图像重建提供合适数据；三是将多谱线同时反演的理论思路发展为实用技术，并应用于太阳黑子孔观测。

Result: 所获数据可在衍射极限（diffraction limit）下分辨小尺度特征。借助FISS-SP观测，多谱线同时反演方法得以实用化，可获得局地太阳大气的相干模型，分辨约49 km水平尺度的结构。对太阳黑子孔的分析识别出此前无法在偏振光谱上分辨的小尺度结构，其中典型的孔边界特征——带有亮点向孔内移动的暗纹——被判定为对流特征（convective features）。

Conclusion: 多谱线同时反演由理论构想发展为可操作技术，显著提升了对小尺度太阳大气结构的反演能力；太阳黑子孔边界的小尺度结构可被解释为对流现象，表明高分辨率偏振光谱观测与图像重建对研究太阳大气加热、发电机及空间天气具有重要价值。

Abstract: The Sun's atmosphere is significantly influenced by events that often measure only tens of kilometres in the horizontal direction. They happen in both quiet and active regions and are determined by magneto-hydro-dynamic processes. Their origins often lie in the lowest layers of the solar atmosphere. To improve the understanding of chromospheric and coronal heating, the solar dynamo and its activity cycle, or to improve the prediction of space weather events we must therefore enhance our understanding of such small-scale events. In this work I first describe the process of calibrating spectropolarimetric observations with very high spatial resolution and put together the building blocks for the mathematical proof the MOMFBD image reconstruction algorithm. In the second part I describe the FISS-SP prototype which is designed to fully exploit the resolution power of a 1.6m telescope. This instrument is built to provide suitable data for image reconstruction of spectropolarimetric data. The resulting data can resolve small-scale features down to the diffraction limit. The simultaneous interpretation of a few lines is still the norm for spatially highly resolved solar spectra. Theoretical works have shown that the simultaneous interpretation of multiple solar lines can greatly improve the robustness against noise. Using observations from FISS-SP I advance the theoretical idea into a practical technique. With this I can retrieve a coherent model of the local solar atmosphere, resolving structures down to a horizontal size of about 49~km. Finally, I use those new techniques to interpret an observation of a solar pore. In this last part I describe and analyse small-scale structures, which could not be spectropolarimetrically resolved before. Typical pore-boundary features - namely, dark striations with bright grains moving into the pore - are identified as convective features.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Microscopic Insights into the Quarkyonic Hadron--Quark Crossover: Lessons from Ultracold Fermi Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01117)
*Hiroyuki Tajima*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了通过类比冷原子费米气体与双色 QCD 中的 BEC–BCS 交叉，用“三重涨落”（tripling fluctuations）机制解释强子—夸克交叉（hadron–quark crossover）中声速峰与重子动量壳层等现象的近期工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高密度下重子物质如何演化为夸克物质，对中子星与致密 QCD 物理至关重要；连续强子—夸克交叉可调和核密度附近偏软的物态方程（equation of state）与支撑大质量中子星所需的快速硬化。近期中子星观测要求物态方程在中间密度区快速硬化，而 quarkyonic 物质图像在该区域呈现壳层状重子动量分布。

Method: 将强子—夸克交叉类比于超冷费米气体与双色 QCD 中的 BEC–BCS 交叉，重点关注配对涨落的三体对应物——与重子形成相关的三重涨落，并采用三重涨落的相移（phase-shift）表示进行分析。在具体实现上，考察一维三组分费米气体中的该机制、其相对论性推广，以及其与唯象 quarkyonic 物态方程的联系。

Result: 三重涨落中三体束缚态极点与散射连续谱的相互干涉抑制了低动量重子占据、产生重子动量壳层（baryonic momentum shell），并降低了密度磁化率（density susceptibility），从而在声速（speed of sound）中产生一个峰。

Conclusion: 该机制为强子—夸克交叉的微观描述提供了一致框架，并可与唯象 quarkyonic 物态方程建立联系，为解释中子星观测所要求的中间密度区快速硬化物态方程提供了可能途径。

Abstract: In neutron-star and dense quantum chromodynamics (QCD) physics, it is important to understand how baryonic matter evolves into quark matter at high density. A continuous hadron--quark crossover is one of the most attractive candidates because it can reconcile the comparatively soft equation of state around nuclear density with the rapid stiffening required to support massive neutron stars. Recent neutron-star observations motivate equations of state that stiffen rapidly across the intermediate-density regime, while quarkyonic-matter descriptions feature a shell-like baryon momentum distribution there. In this paper, we review our recent work on the microscopic description and interpretation of these phenomena by drawing an analogy with the Bose--Einstein condensate (BEC) to Bardeen--Cooper--Schrieffer (BCS) crossover in ultracold Fermi gases and two-color QCD. As pairing fluctuations play a crucial role in the BEC--BCS crossover, we discuss the role of its three-body counterpart, that is, tripling fluctuations associated with baryon formation in the hadron--quark crossover. In terms of a phase-shift representation of tripling fluctuations, the interplay between a three-body bound-state pole and the scattering continuum suppresses low-momentum baryonic occupation, generates a baryonic momentum shell, and reduces the density susceptibility, thereby producing a peak in the speed of sound. We review the demonstration of this mechanism in a one-dimensional three-component Fermi gas, its relativistic extension, and its connection to phenomenological quarkyonic equations of state.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [Stationarity and angular momentum conservation in pulsar spin noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01929)
*Reginald Christian Bernardo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于物理动机的双组分自旋游走模型分析脉冲星自旋噪声，并开发可扩展的高斯过程方法，利用模拟数据检验模型的区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星自旋噪声的平稳性（stationarity）尚待检验，需要一个具备物理基础的框架来区分不同角动量动力学假设下的自旋游走模型。

Method: 构建两种双组分模型：总角动量扩散的奇异非平稳模型，以及以守恒总角动量为锚点的极小平稳模型；并开发可扩展的高斯过程（Gaussian process）方法，基于模拟数据（mock data）进行评估。

Result: 在全状态观测（full-state observations，即同时独立获取壳层与超流体转动状态数据）下，两种模型完全可检验且可区分；但仅能观测单一组分时，稳健推断与预测较难实现，依赖先验与数据质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为检验脉冲星自旋噪声平稳性提供路径，有望通过联合连续引力波观测与脉冲星射电计时实现。

Abstract: We present an analysis of pulsar spin noise based on physically-motivated two-component models of spin wandering. We focus on two models, distinguished by their total angular momentum dynamics: a singular, nonstationary model with a diffusive total angular momentum, and a minimal, stationary model anchored on a conserved total angular momentum. We develop scalable Gaussian process methods and, using mock data, show that the two models are fully testable and distinguishable with full-state observations, i.e., simultaneous independent data on the crust and the superfluid rotational states. This paves a path to testing stationarity in pulsar spin noise, potentially achievable with joint continuous gravitational wave observations and radio timing of pulsars. However, robust inferences and predictions are harder to achieve, and depend on the priors and data quality, when only one component is observationally accessible.

</details>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [Impact of Gain Layer Doping Concentration on the Performance of 4H-SiC LGADs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00824)
*Tao Yang,Ben J. Sekely,Yashas Satapathy,Abraham Tishelman-Charny,Greg Allion,Gil Atar,Philip Barletta,Gabriele Giacomini,Carl Haber,Steve Holland,John F. Muth,Spyridon Pavlidis,Chengxi Yang,Haichen Wang,Stefania Stucci*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本研究考察了 4H-SiC 低增益雪崩探测器（low gain avalanche detector, LGAD）中增益层掺杂浓度对电荷收集与时间性能的影响，通过两种仅增益层掺杂不同的台面隔离器件对比，发现 25% 的掺杂差异可在相同偏压下造成约两个数量级的增益差别，并明确了实现最优时间分辨率的增益窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增益层掺杂浓度决定 LGAD 内部的电场分布，是其首要设计参数，但其对 4H-SiC LGAD 电荷收集与时间行为的具体影响尚待实验阐明。该工作旨在通过对照实验提供增益层设计的定量参考。

Method: 采用两个仅增益层掺杂不同（4×10^17 cm^-3 与 5×10^17 cm^-3）的台面隔离（mesa-isolated）器件，二者均使用冷 He 离子注入终端，并与同一晶圆的 PIN 二极管一起通过紫外瞬态电流技术（ultraviolet transient current technique, UV-TCT）表征；同时在 ^90Sr 贝塔源条件下以 Si LGAD 作为参考进行测量。

Result: 25% 的掺杂差异在相同偏压下导致约两个数量级的增益差别，增益范围为 3.7 至 216。高掺杂器件在 UV-TCT 下于约 20–80 的增益范围内出现时间分辨率最小值，符合从电子噪声主导的抖动（jitter）向倍增散粒噪声（multiplication shot noise）过渡的机制；在 ^90Sr 贝塔源下亦观察到非单调时间响应，高掺杂器件在 280 V 达到 51.7 ps 后性能退化，而低掺杂器件需超过 450 V。

Conclusion: 所测得的增益窗口为增益层设计的进一步优化提供了实验参考，表明存在一个兼顾时间分辨率与噪声权衡的最优增益区间。

Abstract: Gain layer doping concentration determines the electric field profile in a low gain avalanche detector (LGAD) and is therefore the primary design parameter. This work reports its influence on the charge collection and timing behavior of 4H-SiC LGADs. Two mesa-isolated devices with cold He ion implanted termination differ only in gain layer doping, $4\times10^{17}$~$cm^{-3}$ and $5\times10^{17}$~$cm^{-3}$, and are characterized together with a PIN diode from the same wafer using the ultraviolet transient current technique (UV-TCT). The 25\% doping difference produces a gain difference of about two orders of magnitude at the same applied bias, representing a gain range of 3.7 to 216. Under UV-TCT excitation, the higher-doped device exhibits time-resolution minima within a gain range of about 20--80 across the investigated laser intensities, consistent with the transition from electronic-noise-dominated jitter to multiplication shot noise. The non-monotonic timing response is also observed under a $^{90}Sr$ beta source with a Si LGAD reference: the higher-doped device reaches 51.7 ps at 280 V and then degrades, while the lower-doped device requires more than 450 V. This measured gain window provides an experimental reference for further optimization of the gain-layer design.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Quasi-static loading of granular media as a linear complementarity problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00152)
*Matthew R. Kuhn*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文将无速率依赖（rate-independent）耗散颗粒介质（granular media）的离散准静态（quasi-static）响应建模为线性互补问题（linear complementarity problem, LCP），借助既有 LCP 解的存在性、唯一性与稳定性理论，系统刻画颗粒系统的八类异常状态（含分岔与失稳）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统颗粒系统模拟多采用本质上动态的离散元（discrete element method, DEM）与不连续变形分析（discontinuous deformation analysis, DDA）方法，需处理加速度与阻尼；而准静态方法依赖颗粒间静力刚度关系，在有摩擦接触时刚度呈增量非线性且依赖加载方向，构成建模难点。

Method: 将颗粒系统响应表述为线性互补问题（LCP），并推导其对应形式，纳入接触处颗粒曲率引起的几何效应、摩擦接触刚度以及颗粒位移约束；同时以 LCP 框架下的规则界定八类异常条件。

Result: 八类异常条件在 LCP 语境下获得无歧义定义，其中包括离散分岔、连续-有界分岔（文献中尚未报道）、连续-无界分岔三类分岔，以及文献尚未报道的、数据连续变化时运动发生突跃不连续变化的现象；示例显示失稳与分岔在破坏附近普遍存在，破坏与软化对接触处颗粒几何轮廓敏感，且准静态系统可能进入需动态过渡才能解决加载路径缺失的状态。

Conclusion: 以 LCP 表述准静态耗散颗粒系统，可统一、明确地刻画其分岔与失稳等异常行为，并揭示接触几何对破坏与软化的影响，以及准静态描述在特定状态下的局限。

Abstract: The discrete quasi-static response of rate-independent dissipative granular media is addressed. Granular systems are conventionally simulated with methods that are intrinsically dynamic, such as the discrete element (DEM) and discontinuous deformation (DDA) methods, with the particles' accelerations and damping being essential aspects. In contrast, quasi-static methods derive from the static stiffness relationships among the particles. With frictional contacts, an assembly's stiffness is incrementally non-linear and dependent on the direction of loading. The paper resolves this difficulty by casting the response as a linear complementarity problem (LCP). The approach benefits from a foundation of past research on existence, uniqueness, and stability of LCP solutions, which are expounded in a concise set of rules. The LCP of a granular system is derived, accounting for geometric effects that arise from curvatures of particles at their contacts, frictional contact stiffnesses, and displacement constraints on the particles. The paper describes eight aberrant conditions (pathologies) of granular systems, in the forms of various bifurcations and instabilities. When placed in the context of an LCP, the eight conditions are unambiguously defined. These pathologies include three types of bifurcation: discrete, continuous-bounded (not yet reported in the literature), and continuous-unbounded. Not yet reported in the literature is an abrupt discontinuous change in movements upon a continuous change of the data. Methods and results are illustrated with examples of granular systems. Results show that instability and bifurcation are pervasive conditions near failure; that failure and softening are sensitive to the geometric contours of the particles at contacts; and that quasi-static systems can encounter states that require a dynamic transition to resolve a lack of paths consistent with loading.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Effects of Poisson Isocurvature Perturbations on Induced Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00989)
*Zi-An Zhou,Cheng-Jun Fang,Zong-Kuan Guo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究早期物质主导时代向辐射主导时代的突然转变（sudden transition）如何增强诱导引力波（induced gravitational waves, GWs），并以原初黑洞（primordial black hole, PBH）蒸发情景为例，分析泊松等曲率扰动（Poisson isocurvature perturbations）所诱导的引力波谱及其非高斯统计特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期物质主导到辐射主导的突然转变是增强诱导引力波的代表性机制；在 PBH 蒸发情景中，离散的 PBH 数密度涨落会自然产生泊松等曲率扰动，因此有必要考察该扰动的完整非高斯统计层级如何进入诱导引力波谱。

Method: 通过突然蒸发转变（sudden evaporation transition）评估由泊松等曲率扰动所诱导的引力波，并分析泊松等曲率源的内禀非高斯统计（intrinsic non-Gaussian statistics）的完整层级在诱导引力波谱中的体现。

Result: 张量功率谱（tensor power spectrum）仅保留标量源的两点关联（two-point correlations），而高阶关联（higher-order correlations）首先出现在张量双谱（tensor bispectrum）中；此外，不同双谱构型（bispectrum configurations）的相对振幅呈现非平凡的尺度依赖（scale dependence）。

Conclusion: 张量双谱中双谱构型相对振幅的尺度依赖可能为区分离散 PBH 情景与其他诱导引力波机制提供额外信息。

Abstract: A sudden transition from an early matter-dominated era to a radiation-dominated era provides a representative mechanism for enhancing induced gravitational waves (GWs). In the primordial black hole (PBH) evaporation scenario, discrete PBH number-density fluctuations naturally generate Poisson isocurvature perturbations. By evaluating the GWs induced by these perturbations through the sudden evaporation transition, we show how the full hierarchy of intrinsic non-Gaussian statistics of a Poisson isocurvature source enters the induced GW spectra. We find that the tensor power spectrum retains only the two-point correlations of the scalar source, while higher-order correlations first appear in the tensor bispectrum. We also find a nontrivial scale dependence in the relative amplitudes of different bispectrum configurations, which may provide additional information for distinguishing the discrete PBH scenario from other induced-GW mechanisms.

</details>


### [219] [Strongly mixed cosmological collider at unequal sound speeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02138)
*Javier Huenupi,Gonzalo A. Palma,Spyros Sypsas*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文针对双场暴胀系统（two-field inflationary system）在任意常数二次混合（quadratic mixing）与非等声速（unequal sound speeds）条件下的动力学，通过拉普拉斯表示（Laplace representation）将耦合方程化为二阶 Heun 方程，给出精确的晚期曲率功率谱（curvature power spectrum）及三点谱（bispectrum）解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有双场暴胀谱学分析多假定等声速或近似处理混合效应，缺少对任意混合参数与熵质量（entropy mass）、不等声速下曲率与等曲率微扰（curvature and isocurvature perturbations）耦合演化的精确刻画。本文旨在补齐这一解析缺口，明确声速比（sound-speed ratio）对功率谱与三点谱的影响。

Method: 推导曲率扰动 φ 与等曲率扰动 σ 的正则归一化模（canonically normalized modes），允许任意常数二次混合与不等声速 c_φ、c_σ。采用拉普拉斯表示将耦合动力学约化为二阶 Heun 方程，从而获得任意混合参数与熵质量下的精确晚期曲率功率谱；并以同一套归一化模计算三类代表性立方相互作用（cubic interactions）的树级三点谱（tree-level bispectrum），混合参数按精确处理。

Result: 声速比 r_c=c_σ/c_φ 控制两个声视界（sound horizons）的分离。在混合非零且 r_c≪1 时，功率谱依质量与混合呈现幂律增强、以 ln²(1/r_c) 增长、或在 ln r_c 中的有界振荡；当 r_c≫1 且质量与混合固定时，对无混合谱的修正以 ln r_c/r_c² 趋于消失。对重熵场（heavy entropy fields），不等声速改变碰撞振幅（collider amplitude）与相位，但保持由最终熵质量决定的严格压缩频率（squeezed frequency）；功率归一化振幅可随 r_c 非单调变化，声速层级（sound-speed hierarchy）会延迟趋近该渐近区。

Conclusion: 论文提供了含任意二次混合与不等声速的双场暴胀谱学精确解析框架，揭示声速比在功率谱增强/振荡及三点谱形状中的结构性作用，为不等声速双场模型的观测检验提供可计算的理论基础。

Abstract: We derive the canonically normalized modes of a two-field inflationary system with arbitrary constant quadratic mixing and unequal sound speeds $c_\varphi$ and $c_σ$ for the curvature and isocurvature perturbations, respectively. A Laplace representation reduces the coupled dynamics to a second-order Heun equation and gives the exact late-time curvature power spectrum for arbitrary values of the mixing parameter and entropy mass. The sound-speed ratio $r_c=c_σ/c_\varphi$ controls the separation between the two sound horizons. At fixed nonzero mixing and $r_c\ll1$, the power exhibits power-law enhancement, growth as $\ln^2(1/r_c)$, or bounded oscillations in $\ln r_c$, depending on the mass and mixing. For $r_c\gg1$, the correction to the unmixed spectrum vanishes as $\ln r_c/r_c^2$ at fixed mass and mixing. Using the same normalized modes, we compute the tree-level bispectrum for three representative cubic interactions, with the mixing parameter treated exactly. For heavy entropy fields, unequal speeds modify the collider amplitude and phase while preserving the strict squeezed frequency set by the final entropy mass. The power-normalized amplitude can vary nonmonotonically with $r_c$, and a sound-speed hierarchy can delay the approach to this asymptotic regime.

</details>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [CompMat-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents for Computational Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00636)
*Chenmu Zhang,Levi Felix,Jun-Jie Zhang,Xingfu Li,Xuelian Jiang,Tao Jiang,Subhendu Mishra,Xixi Qin,Boris Yakobson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CompMat-Bench 是一个包含 94 项任务的基准（benchmark），源自近期发表的计算材料学（computational materials research）研究，通过预先复现研究步骤并将输入准备与输出分析作为评测对象，避免在评测中重复执行昂贵模拟。其支持单任务与工作流、完整与简化方法指导共四种评测条件，并以固定规则而非 LLM 评审进行评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学研究任务上评测 AI 智能体（AI agents）受限于底层实验或计算所需的时间与资源。计算材料研究中重复执行相同的高成本模拟（expensive simulations）会使评测变得不可行，因此需要一个无需实际运行昂贵模拟即可评估智能体的方案。

Method: 构建 CompMat-Bench：从近期发表的计算材料研究中衍生出 94 项任务，每项任务要求智能体完成通向该研究科学目标的一个步骤；事先复现研究步骤，使评测聚焦于为昂贵模拟准备输入与分析输出。复现的输入与结果作为真值（ground truth），以固定规则而非 LLM 评审（LLM judge）评分。基准支持四种评测条件：单任务与由相关任务组成的工作流（workflows），各自搭配完整或简化的方法指导（methodological guidance）。

Result: 在单任务且提供完整指导的条件下，基于三个 LLM 的智能体展现出完成单个材料研究步骤的能力，94 项任务的通过率为 66.0–90.4%。更长的工作流与简化指导均可能限制智能体表现，但对不同智能体影响方式不同：二者会降低较弱智能体的通过率，而最强的智能体仅在长工作流与简化指导组合时表现下降。失败分析（failure analysis）将多数失败归因于科学错误而非软件使用错误。

Conclusion: CompMat-Bench 为在真实材料研究的步骤层面比较智能体、并分析其失败模式（failure modes）提供了基础。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents on scientific research tasks is constrained by the time and resources required for the underlying experiments or calculations. In computational materials research, repeating the same expensive simulations across agents and trials can make evaluation impractical. We introduce CompMat-Bench, a benchmark of 94 tasks derived from recently published computational materials studies, each asking agents to complete a step toward achieving the study's scientific goal. We reproduce the research steps in advance and assess agents on preparing inputs and analyzing outputs for expensive simulations, so expensive simulations can be avoided during evaluation. The reproduced inputs and results serve as ground truth for grading agents with fixed rules, without an LLM judge. The benchmark supports four evaluation conditions: single tasks and workflows composed of related tasks, each with full or reduced methodological guidance. With full guidance on single tasks, agents based on three LLMs demonstrate the ability to complete individual materials research steps, with pass rates of 66.0-90.4% across 94 tasks. Both longer workflows and reduced guidance can limit agent performance, but in different ways for different agents: they lower the pass rates of the weaker agents, whereas the strongest agent falls only when a long workflow is combined with reduced guidance. Failure analysis attributes most failures to scientific errors rather than to errors in software usage. CompMat-Bench provides a basis for comparing agents on the steps of real materials research and for analyzing agent failure modes.

</details>

### [221] [Global Strong Solutions for the Ternary Maxwell-Stefan Diffusion System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00162)
*Dieter Bothe*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了归一化等温、等压三元 Maxwell-Stefan 扩散系统在有界 $C^{2+\alpha_0}$ 区域上的整体强适定性，无需小性条件，允许任意正二元摩擦系数与相容非负初值。方法结合谱根分解、Euler-Poisson-Darboux 单侧截断、混合熵稳定、锥形 Schauder 估计与冻结极大 $L_p$ 正则性。结论指出正守恒质量分量在正时间严格为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三元 Maxwell-Stefan 扩散系统在一般摩擦系数与一般非负初值下的整体适定性，目标在于摆脱既有结果常见的小性条件限制。系统具有强耦合、退化与非线性特征，其全局可解性构成核心挑战。

Method: 对相异摩擦系数情形，采用迹--行列式变量导出两支分离的谱根，满足带反对称二次耦合的标量抛物方程；借助混合熵稳定的 Euler-Poisson-Darboux 单侧截断获得边界 De Giorgi 振荡衰减与正时间 Hölder 正则性。再由混合熵 Hessian 构造局部对称化子，结合锥形 Schauder 估计与冻结极大 $L_p$ 正则性建立一致控制。摩擦系数重复情形化为三角标量抛物系统处理。

Result: 在无小性条件下证明了归一化等温、等压三元 Maxwell-Stefan 扩散系统于有界 $C^{2+\alpha_0}$ 区域上的整体强适定性，适用于任意正二元摩擦系数与相容非负初值。获得正时间的 Hölder 正则性、一致 $C^{1+a}$ 控制与一致终端迹界，从而排除有限时间爆破。

Conclusion: 任一具有正守恒质量的组分在正时间严格为正，表明系统保持物理上合理的正性结构。整体强适定性和正性结果为三元 Maxwell-Stefan 扩散在一般参数与初值下的分析提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: We prove global strong well-posedness for the normalized isothermal, isobaric ternary Maxwell-Stefan diffusion system on bounded $C^{2+α_0}$ domains, for arbitrary positive constant binary frictions and compatible nonnegative initial data, without a smallness condition. For distinct frictions, trace--determinant variables produce two separated spectral roots satisfying scalar parabolic equations with antisymmetric quadratic coupling. Euler-Poisson-Darboux one-sided truncations, stabilized by the mixing entropy, yield boundary De Giorgi oscillation decay and positive-time Hölder regularity. The mixing-entropy Hessian then provides a local symmetrizer; conormal Schauder estimates give uniform $C^{1+a}$ control, and frozen maximal $L_p$-regularity yields a uniform terminal trace bound excluding finite-time breakdown. Repeated frictions reduce to triangular scalar parabolic systems. Every component with positive conserved mass is strictly positive for positive time.

</details>


### [222] [Global Strong Solutions for the Affine Darken Class of Maxwell-Stefan Diffusion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00166)
*Dieter Bothe*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文在已建立的多组分 Maxwell-Stefan 摩擦的 Stieltjes 坐标与 De Giorgi 正则性框架下，证明仿射 Darken 类（仿射 Darken class）在无小性假设下具有全局极大 \(L^p\) 强适定性，并刻画其为保持固定 Stieltjes 特征标架的唯一极大 Darken 类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bothe–Druet 的核心-对角闭包将多组分 Darken 扩散识别为热力学一致的 Maxwell-Stefan 模型，其本构数据为组分的示踪扩散系数（tracer diffusivities）。本文旨在将已有框架推广至更一般的 Darken 类，以处理系数依赖于组分且无需小性假设的情形。

Method: 基于近期预印本中为加法型 Maxwell-Stefan 摩擦引入的 Stieltjes 坐标（Stieltjes coordinates）与 De Giorgi 正则性框架，对具有组分依赖标量系数和一致正示踪扩散系数的仿射 Darken 类进行分析；同时对一般光滑一致正的 Darken 示踪扩散系数推导直接约束通量形式、精确混合熵对称化、Fisher 耗散与组分一致的法向椭圆性。

Result: 仿射 Darken 类获得无小性假设的全局极大 \(L^p\) 强适定性，且该恰为保持固定 Stieltjes 特征标架的最大 Darken 类；该类解与单纯形边界一致分离、正时间后成为经典解，并指数收敛到平衡态。一般光滑一致正情形亦给出直接约束通量形式、混合熵对称化、Fisher 耗散、组分一致法向椭圆性及局部强适定性。

Conclusion: 仿射 Darken 类在保持 Stieltjes 特征标架的约束下是可全局处理的极大类，其解具正则性与指数弛豫；常数乘性摩擦（含重复因子）可通过将相等因子物种分组纳入该框架。

Abstract: The core-diagonal closure of Bothe and Druet identifies multicomponent Darken diffusion as a thermodynamically consistent Maxwell-Stefan model in which the constitutive data are species tracer diffusivities. Building on the Stieltjes-coordinate and De Giorgi regularity framework introduced in our recent preprint for additive Maxwell-Stefan frictions, we prove global maximal-$L^p$ strong well-posedness, without smallness assumptions, for the affine Darken class with composition-dependent scalar coefficients and uniformly positive tracer diffusivities. We show that this is exactly the maximal Darken class preserving the fixed Stieltjes eigenframe underlying the global argument. For general smooth, uniformly positive Darken tracer diffusivities we also derive the direct constrained flux form, exact mixing-entropy symmetrization, Fisher dissipation, composition-uniform normal ellipticity, and local strong well-posedness. Solutions in the affine class remain uniformly separated from the simplex boundary, become classical for positive times, and converge exponentially to equilibrium. Constant multiplicative frictions, including repeated factors, are covered by grouping species with equal factors.

</details>


### [223] [Sharp lifespan results for plane-symmetric fluids on decelerated spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38118)
*Maximilian Ofner,Todd Oliynyk*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究空间平坦时空上相对论欧拉方程（relativistic Euler equations）的平面对称解，在减速膨胀背景下分析临界膨胀率附近的奇性形成与解的生命跨度（lifespan）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前结果已给出高于膨胀阈值（expansion threshold）时齐次各向同性解的非线性稳定性（nonlinear stability），本文旨在补足该阈值及以下的动力学行为，特别是小初值是否导致有限时间奇性（finite-time singularity）。

Method: 在状态方程 $p(\rho)=K\rho$（$0<K<1/3$）与幂律膨胀（power law inflation）$a(t)=t^\alpha$（$0\leq\alpha<1$）设定下，构造并分析平面对称解，结合激波型梯度爆破（gradient blowup）方法与基于能量估计（energy estimates）的分析。

Result: 证明在临界膨胀率 $\alpha_\text{crit}=\frac{2}{3(1-K)}$ 及其以下，存在任意小的初值导致激波型有限时间奇性形成，即解保持有界但梯度爆破；并给出生命跨度估计：低于阈值为多项式（polynomial）型，在阈值为指数（exponential）型。此外，能量估计定理给出小初值平面对称解生命跨度的下界，并可推广至 $3+1$ 维无对称性解。

Conclusion: 临界膨胀率刻画了稳定性与奇性形成的分界：高于阈值解非线性稳定，处于及低于阈值则出现激波型梯度爆破。生命跨度在阈值两侧由多项式过渡为指数标度，能量估计结果进一步扩展至无对称情形。

Abstract: In this article, we analyze plane-symmetric solutions to the relativistic Euler equations on spatially flat spacetimes with decelerated expansion. We consider a linear equation of state $p(ρ)=Kρ$ and power law inflation $a(t)=t^α$, where $0<K<\frac{1}{3}$ and $0\leqα<1$. Previous results imply nonlinear stability of the homogeneous and isotropic solution above an expansion threshold $α_\text{crit}=\frac{2}{3(1-K)}$. We prove that at and below this critical expansion rate there exist arbitrarily small data that exhibit finite-time singularity formation of the shock type, i.e., gradient blowup while the solution stays bounded. For this type of data, we establish lifespan estimates that are polynomial below and exponential at the critical threshold. In addition, we prove a theorem based on energy estimates that provides lower bounds on the lifespan of plane-symmetric solutions generated from small initial data and generalizes to solutions without symmetry in $3+1$-dimensions.

</details>


### [224] [Endpoint Mapping Properties of Wave Operators for Schrödinger Operators in Dimensions $n\ge3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00869)
*Han Cheng,Changxing Miao,Xiaohua Yao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathbb R^n$（$n\ge3$）上 Schrödinger 算子（Schrödinger operator）$H=-\Delta+V$ 低能波算子（wave operator）的端点映射性质，在四维证明零能共振（zero-energy resonance）破坏 $L^1$ 有界性，并对无共振的零能特征值给出各维度的低能 $L^p$ 完全分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能波算子在端点（如 $L^1$、$L^\infty$）处的映射性质通常比非端点情形更脆弱，且零能共振与零能特征值的存在会引入临界奇异性。该文旨在厘清这两类零能谱结构分别如何影响端点有界性，并给出统一分类。

Method: 在势能衰减条件 $|V(x)|\lesssim\langle x\rangle^{-\beta}$ 下分析低能波算子。四维情形取 $\beta>10$，一般维度取 $\beta>n+4$；通过识别有限秩障碍（finite-rank obstruction），并证明剩余特征值修正无法抵消其临界渐近剖面（critical asymptotic profiles）。

Result: 四维中，零能共振（无论零是否同时为特征值）均阻止 $L^1$ 有界性。对无共振的零能特征值，在所有 $n\ge3$ 下获得完整的低能 $L^p$ 分类；其中 $L^\infty$ 有界性等价于对每个零能特征函数 $\psi$，$V\psi$ 的第零、第一及调和第二矩（harmonic second moment）均消失。若相应高能界（high-energy bounds）可得，结论可推广至完整波算子。

Conclusion: 零能共振与零能特征值对端点映射性质的影响具有本质区别：前者在四维直接破坏 $L^1$ 有界性，后者的 $L^\infty$ 有界性由有限个矩条件完全刻画。相关结论在满足高能界时对完整波算子同样成立。

Abstract: We study endpoint mapping properties of low-energy wave operators for Schrödinger operators $H=-Δ+V$ on $\mathbb R^n$, $n\ge3$. In dimension four, we prove that a zero-energy resonance prevents $L^1$ boundedness, whether or not zero is also an eigenvalue, under $|V(x)|\lesssim\langle x\rangle^{-β}$ with $β>10$. For a zero-energy eigenvalue without a resonance, we obtain a complete low-energy $L^p$ classification in every dimension $n\ge3$ under $β>n+4$. In particular, $L^\infty$ boundedness is equivalent to the vanishing of the zeroth, first, and harmonic second moments of $Vψ$ for every zero-energy eigenfunction $ψ$. The proof identifies the finite-rank obstruction and shows that the remaining eigenvalue correction cannot cancel its critical asymptotic profiles. The same conclusions hold for the full wave operators when the corresponding high-energy bounds are available.

</details>
